### 1. [Discarded or Unreachable? Diagnosing and Repairing Spatial Relation Failure in Vision--Language Similarity](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05565v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05565

**作者：** Mahdi Mostajabdaveh、Zirui Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05565v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05565v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文诊断对比式视觉语言模型左右关系近随机水平的两种成因——池化丢弃几何信息，或原生图文相似度无法访问仍存在的信息——用线性探针与百万标题搜索在十种编码器上区分二者，并提出零参数的查询条件空间矩读出，从 patch 网格重算几何、作为向量与原生嵌入并列用于检索。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应「几何与实体关系是否保留在内部状态」「池化造成的信息坍缩」与「读出能否访问视觉证据」三条主线，其「诊断丢弃还是不可达 + 旁路读出」框架可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节是否被真正使用，但需注意其不涉及 latent 推理状态的生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Discarded or Unreachable? Diagnosing and Repairing Spatial Relation Failure in Vision--Language Similarity

**abstract/A1：** Contrastive vision-language models score left/right relations near chance, but the symptom has two causes that call for opposite repairs: the relation may survive in the pooled embedding yet be unreachable by native image-text similarity, or pooling may discard it while the dense features retain it. On clean two object photographs, across ten encoders, the lateral polarity is linearly decodable from the flip odd component of the pooled vector at 0.77-0.99, yet a search over a million captions leaves nine of the ten at chance under native scoring: an access failure. On controlled C4 orbits, where the relation is the only thing that varies, pooling keeps the arrangement axis at 0.998-1.000 and loses its polarity, which the patch grid retains: a pooling failure. Both sit downstream of the dense features, so one readout covers both. Our zero parameter, query conditioned spatial moment recomputes the geometry from the patch grid and stores it as a vector beside the native embedding. On a 118,287 image index it raises left/right pairwise accuracy against reversed geometry negatives from 0.51-0.53 to 0.81-0.96 and conditional R@1 among 21 category matched candidates from 0.05 to 0.37-0.75, at under a millisecond per batched query, while reproducing the native ranking exactly for queries that name no relation.

**comment/C1：** Code, stimulus generators, and all result files: https://github.com/mahdims/vl-spatial-moment

</details>

<a id="paper-2"></a>

### 2. [RI-Prune: Relation and Information Aware Visual Token Pruning for Multi-Image Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06047v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06047

**作者：** Dongjin Lee、Eunju Lee、Jinhee Jang、Yoonji Lee、Youngbin Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06047v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06047v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对多图 LVLM 视觉 token 预算膨胀问题，先用「Leave-One-In」与「Leave-One-Deletion」两个因果探针指出注意力显著性只是 token 重要性的弱代理，并提出免训练、免注意力的解码前剪枝器 RI-Prune，用查询相关性、跨图原型对应与图内代表性三类投影器输出信号打分，再做逐图 Top-K 选择。

**相关性简评：** 其因果探针直接检验视觉 token 对多图推理的实际贡献，与「视觉证据筛选」和贡献分配问题相关；基于投影器空间信号判断跨图对应与代表性，也可能为「内部状态应保留哪些视觉细节」提供可核查的选择依据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RI-Prune: Relation and Information Aware Visual Token Pruning for Multi-Image Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have recently been extended to handle multiple input images, but this extension dramatically increases the visual token budget, making visual-token pruning not merely an efficiency option but a practical necessity. Existing visual-token pruning methods, primarily designed for the single-image setting, rely on attention-derived saliency signals to estimate per-token importance. However, this attention-centric design transfers poorly to the multi-image regime: it causes substantial performance degradation on multi-image benchmarks, suffers from strong positional bias, and loses discriminative attention signals as the number of input images increases. To test whether attention-selected tokens are truly important for multi-image reasoning, we conduct two complementary causal probes Leave-One-In and Leave-One-Deletion, which measure token importance by restoring individual tokens to a zeroed visual sequence or removing them from the full sequence. Attention-based pruners provide only marginal gains over random selection, showing that attention is a weak proxy for visual-token importance in multi-image reasoning. Motivated by this gap, we propose Relation and Information Aware Pruning (RI-Prune), a training-free, attention-free pre-decoder selector for multi-image visual token pruning. RI-Prune scores each visual token using a z-normalized combination of three signals computed from projector outputs: query relevance, cross-image correspondence with compact per-image prototypes, and intra image representativeness. By integrating these signals, RI-Prune performs balanced per-image Top-K selection to preserve information across input images. Across multiple multi-image benchmarks and three LVLM backbones, RI-Prune achieves a stronger trade-off between token reduction and task performance than prior pruning methods, particularly under aggressive token budgets.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to CIKM 2026 Oral

</details>

<a id="paper-3"></a>

### 3. [Learning When to Think Aloud in Latent Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06108v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06108

**作者：** Zhehao Zhou、Tongrui Su、Shengyu Zhu、Da Li、Zeyu Liu、Jun Zhu、Xueqi Cheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06108v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06108v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VITAL 混合推理框架：用轻量 gating head 动态决定何时把隐状态转为显式 token 作为「推理锚点」，其余计算保持 latent；轨迹由显式 CoT 的 token 重要性估计构造，经课程式监督训练与兼顾正确性与简洁性的强化学习训练。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 THINK 边界问题——何处值得外显、何处留在 latent，可作为「选择性 verbalize」的收益/成本对照；但实验仅在 GPT-2、Llama-3.2-1B、Qwen3-1.7B 小模型上，gating 依据与长度泛化仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning When to Think Aloud in Latent Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Latent Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning improves efficiency by shifting intermediate computation into continuous hidden states. However, fully latent methods often struggle on precision-critical tasks such as mathematical reasoning, while existing hybrid approaches retain substantial token-generation overhead. We introduce VITAL, an importance-guided hybrid reasoning framework that selectively verbalizes informative tokens as reasoning anchors while keeping most computation latent. A lightweight gating head dynamically selects between explicit token generation and direct hidden-state feedback. VITAL constructs hybrid trajectories from explicit CoT using token- importance estimation and trains the model through curriculum-based supervised training, followed by reinforcement learning with a reward that balances correctness and conciseness. Experiments show that VITAL consistently out- performs existing fully latent reasoning methods, achieving relative average accuracy gains of 9.6% on GPT-2 and 5.1% on Llama-3.2-1B at comparable reasoning lengths. Compared with hybrid baselines, VITAL achieves higher reasoning accuracy with substantially shorter reasoning trajectories on Qwen3-1.7B.

</details>

<a id="paper-4"></a>

### 4. [ModalSearcher: Weaving Visual Evidence into Autonomous Cross-Modal Deep Search](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06131v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06131

**作者：** Zhihan Yin、Ruyi Li、Chengyu Fang、Yifeng Yao、Nonghai Zhang、Pengfei Xiong、Terry Zhang、Huishuai Zhang、Dongyan Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06131v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06131v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究多模态大模型在知识密集任务中的「自主跨模态搜索」：提出构造交错多模态知识图谱与多跳问题（text-only、图像起始、任意交错）的流水线，在真实 Web 环境中用 SFT 与 RL 训练搜索 agent，并构建 ModalSearch-Bench；8B 模型在五个多模态知识密集 QA 基准上平均提升 16.2 分。

**相关性简评：** agent 需在多跳中判断何时引入视觉证据、在推理／文搜／图搜／反查间切换，直接关联「LOOK 的收益与成本」和视觉证据筛选，可供核查其模态切换策略与停止决策的评价方式；但推理状态体现于外显搜索轨迹而非 latent 状态，与 LVR 主线仍有距离。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ModalSearcher: Weaving Visual Evidence into Autonomous Cross-Modal Deep Search

**abstract/A1：** Static and incomplete parametric knowledge limits the performance of multimodal large language models on novel-knowledge and knowledge-intensive tasks, especially those requiring multi-hop evidence acquisition. Existing multimodal agentic search methods often fix visual evidence at the input or a predefined trajectory position, making it difficult for agents to select search modalities as their information needs evolve and to handle complex real-world queries. We define this capability as autonomous cross-modal search: an agent chooses among direct reasoning, text-to-text search, text-to-image search, and reverse-image search, actively introduces visual evidence when appropriate, and switches between and integrates modalities across multiple hops. We propose ModalSearch-Pipeline, a scalable procedure for constructing an interleaved multimodal knowledge graph. It samples paths with diverse depths and modality compositions and uses backward question construction to obscure entity names and image targets that would expose reasoning shortcuts while retaining necessary disambiguating clues, yielding text-only, image-initialized, and arbitrarily interleaved multi-hop problems. We train the ModalSearcher Agent with supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning in a live Web environment, and construct ModalSearch-Bench. Experiments show that ModalSearcher-8B improves average accuracy by 16.2 points over Qwen3-VL-8B under the same agentic workflow across five multimodal knowledge-intensive QA benchmarks, with consistent gains on independently constructed active visual-search benchmarks.

**comment/C1：** 29pages

</details>

<a id="paper-5"></a>

### 5. [Entropogram-guided Visual Realignment Decoding: Capturing the Rhythm of Multimodal Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06415v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06415

**作者：** Jiaqi Deng、Zhe Fu、Yakun Chen、Dichang Zhang、Zonghan Wu、Xiaoshui Huang、Huan Huo、Guandong Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.MM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06415v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06415v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从解码期熵动态分析 MLLM 长链推理幻觉，识别高置信提前收敛与局部熵突变两种模式，并提出熵感知自省框架，在可疑状态触发视觉重对齐。

**相关性简评：** 该工作直接关联视觉证据筛选与 LOOK/STOP 决策；可供核查熵信号能否作为重看视觉证据或调整推理的触发依据，以及重对齐是否真正改变后续推理状态而非仅做输出修补。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Entropogram-guided Visual Realignment Decoding: Capturing the Rhythm of Multimodal Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable progress on cross-modal reasoning tasks, yet they continue to suffer from hallucinations in long-chain reasoning processes. In this work, we revisit hallucination in MLLMs from the perspective of reasoning generation dynamics. We argue that entropy is not merely a static measure of uncertainty but a structured signal that reflects how modality information is integrated throughout decoding. Through systematic analysis, we identify two characteristic entropy patterns associated with hallucination behaviors: (1) a generally high-confidence regime corresponds to premature convergence to inappropriate multimodal interactions; and (2) local abrupt entropy transitions are associated with reasoning instability and hallucination onset. Building on these observations, we propose an entropy-aware self-introspective framework for MLLM reasoning. Our method continuously tracks entropy dynamics during decoding and performs targeted visual re-alignment when suspicious hallucination states are detected, encouraging the model to re-examine salient visual evidence at critical reasoning stages. Extensive experiments across six multimodal reasoning benchmarks spanning nine distinct evaluation settings demonstrate that our approach delivers robust improvements in reasoning accuracy and hallucination robustness, while providing new insights into the generation dynamics underlying MLLM failures. The code is available at: https://github.com/jden4524/SelfRetrospectiveDecoding.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 9 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-6"></a>

### 6. [DreamRealm: Exploring and Materializing Hallucination Worlds for Trustworthy Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06433v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06433

**作者：** Benlei Cui、Chunyang Chai、Yiliang Zhang、Xiaoqian Xia、Chengwen Yao、Song Li、Longtao Huang、Yuwen Zhai、Haiwen Hong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06433v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06433v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DreamRealm 后训练框架：先从目标 MLLM 中挖掘已观察到与潜在的幻觉回答，用最小图像编辑将其「实体化」为有效图文对（RealmForgePipe、RealmSet），再以 RealmMatch 在图文组合似然矩阵上结合监督与双向对比损失训练模型把每个回答对应到其支撑图像。

**相关性简评：** 以模型自身的幻觉作为待检验的 latent 假设、并用编辑后的视觉证据做正负匹配，与关注细粒度视觉细节保留、视觉证据筛选和 latent 假设区分的兴趣直接相关；但其「latent」指幻觉回答而非推理期内部状态，需核查对比训练是否真正改变内部视觉表征。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DreamRealm: Exploring and Materializing Hallucination Worlds for Trustworthy Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remain prone to visual hallucinations when correct responses depend on subtle visual details. One line of post-training methods constructs visual contrasts through image retrieval, generation, or editing to improve fine-grained perception. However, these contrasts are not built around the model's own hallucinations, making it difficult to ensure that supervision targets its actual perceptual confusions. Another line suppresses hallucinated outputs through response correction or preference learning under fixed image conditions, but leaves the design of supervision signals on the image-input side underexplored. Inspired by how humans correct misconceptions, we introduce DreamRealm, a post-training framework that explores diverse latent hallucinated responses, minimally edits original images to make them valid, and trains the model to assign each response to its supporting image. Our data construction pipeline, RealmForgePipe, mines observed and latent hallucinations from a target model and materializes them through image editing. Using hallucinations mined from Qwen3.5-9B, we construct the Realized Hallucination Dataset (RealmSet). Our training objective, RealmMatch, constructs a likelihood score matrix across image--response combinations and combines supervised learning with bidirectional contrastive losses to reinforce correct matches and suppress mismatches. Compared with the original Qwen3.5-9B model, DreamRealm improves performance across six visual perception and hallucination benchmarks, including gains of 7.26 percentage points in HallusionBench accuracy and 11.34 percentage points in MMVP pair accuracy. Together with ablation studies on data construction and training methods, these results demonstrate that hallucination assignment is a promising paradigm for improving visual perception and mitigating hallucinations.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, including supplementary material. Code: https://github.com/Alibaba-VELLDEPTH/DreamRealm . Model: https://huggingface.co/Alibaba-VELLDEPTH/Qwen35-9b-DreamRealm

</details>

### 7. [EviSeek: An Evidence-Centric Agentic Framework for Embodied Active Information Seeking](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06434v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06434

**作者：** Jintao Li、Zhiyong Cao、Baihua Xiao、Yu Bai、Börje F. Karlsson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06434v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06434v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练的证据中心 agent 框架 EviSeek，把由问题导出的「证据需求」写成显式获取目标，用以驱动视角变换与物理交互动作，并选择性保留观测证据，在 ESI-Bench 的 3,436 题上提升了准确率与视觉支持加权准确率并降低平均 token 消耗。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应研究中「获取证据、视觉证据筛选、停止与动态计算」的关注点；其把交互的信息目的外显为目标的思路，可供核查能否用于 LVR 中判断 latent 状态缺少哪些视觉证据，而 token 下降究竟来自有效筛选还是启发式裁剪仍需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EviSeek: An Evidence-Centric Agentic Framework for Embodied Active Information Seeking

**abstract/A1：** Active embodied question answering requires agents to acquire evidence through viewpoint changes and physical interaction. The central challenge is to avoid undirected interaction: taking plausible actions without specifying what evidence they should reveal for answering the question. Action plans and accumulated histories alone can leave the informational purpose of an interaction implicit. We introduce EviSeek, a training-free framework that makes question-derived evidence demands explicit acquisition objectives, linking observation-conditioned actions and selectively retained evidence to the information the agent seeks. On 3,436 ESI-Bench questions, EviSeek improves accuracy over the Single-model agent by 7.1 percentage points with Qwen3-VL-32B and 3.7 points with Gemini 3.1 Pro, while improving visual-support-weighted accuracy and reducing mean token usage. These findings support making the informational purpose of interaction explicit, with action selection and evidence retention organized around what the agent needs to learn to answer the question.

</details>

<a id="paper-8"></a>

### 8. [Trick-or-Treat: Adversarial Evaluation of Multimodal Medical Reasoning with Synthetic Images](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06631v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06631

**作者：** Oishi Banerjee、Eujin Lee、Sung Eun Kim、Alexandra N. Willauer、Christine Y. Zhou、Zhizhou Yang、Pranav Rajpurkar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06631v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06631v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作用医学图像合成构建对抗性多模态评测：将真实胸片与合成临床照片配对，分为「相符」与「不符」两组，考察三个前沿模型在跨模态证据冲突下的诊断推理与幻觉行为。

**相关性简评：** 发现模型面对不符证据时倾向忽略冲突、反向虚构照片所见以迎合胸片结论，直接对应视觉证据筛选与证据使用问题；其内部机制及在其他域的可迁移性仍需读原文核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Trick-or-Treat: Adversarial Evaluation of Multimodal Medical Reasoning with Synthetic Images

**abstract/A1：** We used medical image synthesis to create a challenging multimodal evaluation framework that exposes hallucination behaviors in frontier models. Evaluating multimodal reasoning has historically been difficult because existing medical datasets rarely pair different image modalities, such as radiographs and external clinical photographs. To address this gap, we introduce Trick-or-Treat, an adversarial multimodal medical benchmark that pairs real chest radiographs with synthetic clinical photographs to challenge models' cross-modal reasoning. For each of a wide range of conditions, we created two pairs of images, where the pairs share a single chest radiograph but contain different external photographs. In matched pairs, the chest radiograph and photograph both contain findings suggesting the same disease (e.g., varicella pneumonia suggested by lung opacities and crusted skin lesions seen in chickenpox). In mismatched pairs, the photograph depicts an alternative clinical finding that would not lead to the same unifying diagnosis (e.g. replacing crusted skin lesions with ordinary acne). Synthetic photograph generation allows clinical findings to be varied while the person's features and photographic setting stay consistent, reducing confounding. In an investigation of three frontier models, we found that models hallucinated clinically significant photograph findings in 45-57.5% of mismatched pairs, up from 25-37.5% for the same photographs shown alone. When faced with mismatched pairs, models tended to ignore mismatches and instead hallucinated findings that better matched the radiograph (e.g. misreading acne as crusted skin lesions to justify diagnosing chickenpox). Trick-or-Treat both demonstrates concerning error pattern in frontier models' medical reasoning and provides a proof of concept for the use of synthetic images in adversarial multimodal evaluation.

</details>

<a id="paper-9"></a>

### 9. [Sekai: Incremental Event Memory for Generalized Reasoning Video Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06685v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06685

**作者：** Zhengtao Liu、Jinrong Xu、Shiu-Hong Kao、Huixin Sun、Yicong Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06685v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06685v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出广义推理视频分割任务 GRVS：目标集合随视频中累积的查询相关事件演化，可能从空集变为单/多目标；并构建带时序因果标注的 Isekai 基准，以及用结构化事件记忆维护目标状态、再由记忆引导解码器定位当前帧有效目标的 Sekai 在线框架。

**相关性简评：** 其「事件记忆＋目标有效性维护」直接对应 LVR 中的证据累积、视觉证据筛选与何时认定目标成立（类 STOP）问题，Isekai 的逐时刻因果标注可供核查状态更新与有效性判定是否真的被使用；但输出为密集分割而非 latent 推理迹，迁移空间仍需判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sekai: Incremental Event Memory for Generalized Reasoning Video Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Online reasoning video segmentation (Online RVS) requires a model to segment query-referred objects as a video streams in, using only the frames observed so far. Existing formulations largely assume a fixed target set throughout the video, overlooking a fundamental property of streaming reasoning: as new events occur, the objects that satisfy a query may change over time. To address this, we propose a new task, Generalized Reasoning Video Segmentation (GRVS), where the target set depends on query-relevant events accumulated over the observed video history. As new evidence accumulates, the target set can evolve from empty to one or multiple objects, requiring a model to continuously maintain target validity while localizing the valid objects currently in view. To evaluate GRVS, we build Isekai, a diagnostic benchmark of 60 videos and 197 queries across 11 temporal query families, with 12,569 scoring points whose target sets are causally annotated over time. We further propose Sekai, an online framework with two complementary components: an Event Memory Reasoner (EMR) that maintains query-relevant events and evolving target states in structured memory, and a Memory-Grounded Decoder (MGD) that identifies valid targets from this memory and localizes them in the current frame. Experiments show that Sekai significantly outperforms existing online RVS methods, achieving 50.33 J&amp;F and improving over the strongest baseline by 3.2 points. Code, example annotations, and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/zenastangbi/Sekai.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 6 figures. Code, example annotations, and evaluation scripts: https://github.com/zenastangbi/Sekai

</details>

<a id="paper-10"></a>

### 10. [Where It Breaks: Diagnosing Perception, Memory, and Decision Failures of a Vision-Language Agent in Pac-Man](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06704v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06704

**作者：** Xinglu Wang、Edward Wang、Yong Zhang、Zhenan Fan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06704v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06704v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文以 Qwen3.5-9B 驱动的 Pac-Man agent 为对象，通过静态截图感知检查与纯文本棋盘上的策略强化学习实验，分离并诊断感知、长程记忆和动作决策各自的失败。

**相关性简评：** 其「感知在交互中是否真的可用」及训练成功与整局验证脱节的发现，直接关联 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、状态保留和评估设计；可供核查其诊断方法，但任务为游戏智能体而非 latent 生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where It Breaks: Diagnosing Perception, Memory, and Decision Failures of a Vision-Language Agent in Pac-Man

**abstract/A1：** Training an agent to play Pac-Man with only up, down, left and right actions raises a basic question: when performance is poor, what should be improved? Difficulty can arise from reading the screen or from choosing actions over a long sequence. We examine these problems in a Qwen3.5-9B-based agent through separate perception checks and primitive-policy reinforcement-learning studies. In the policy study, which uses textual boards to remove image transcription from the decision interface, completion on eight held-out mazes increased from 0% before this optimization to 80.2% at a selected checkpoint, with three extra lives. The perception checks show that accurate static screenshot reading can coexist with errors during interaction. A separate policy comparison also shows that further optimization can accompany worse action choices. Together, these findings motivate concrete checks for post-training: evaluate perception in use, distinguish training success from full-game validation, and examine decisions and later performance rather than relying on one selected score. The study provides empirical lessons for improving future agent training and evaluation.

**comment/C1：** 35 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-11"></a>

### 11. [Turns Matter: Advancing Streaming Video RL via Turn-Level Credit](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06728v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06728

**作者：** Shuai Dong、Kanghui Tian、Linghan Chen、Wenqi Shao、Hongrui Wu、Haowen Hou、Yongfu Zhu、Xuehui Wang、Ziyue Wang、Wenpu Liu、Weichu Xie、Yuqi Xu、Congcong Wang、Kaiwen Tuo、Caoyuan Ma、Taiqiang Wu、Ji Zhao、Wenzheng Chang、Dianyi Wang、Siyuan Wang、Zhongyu Wei、Yu Qiao、Jiaqi Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06728v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06728v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TurnWise，针对流式视频理解中「作答还是继续观察」的逐轮决策，用 silence-completed prefix scoring 从每步剩余回报构造轮级 group-relative advantage，并在轮内、轮间对 token loss 取平均，以避免轨迹级共享优势带来的冗余回答或抑制后续追问。

**相关性简评：** 直接触及 STOP 式停止/继续观察决策及其训练信号分配：轮级信用分配与轮均值策略优化可供核查能否区分「等待」与「作答」两类决策的贡献，并评估其在流式视觉证据获取中的收益与成本权衡。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Turns Matter: Advancing Streaming Video RL via Turn-Level Credit

**abstract/A1：** Streaming video understanding requires models to repeatedly decide whether to respond or continue observing. Recently, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has been applied to this setting, with existing approaches emphasizing reward design while retaining a single trajectory-level advantage across turns. Such shared credit can reinforce redundant replies after earlier successes and discourage useful follow-ups after earlier failures. We propose TurnWise, a turn-level GRPO framework that aligns learning with individual streaming decisions. TurnWise addresses the mismatch between trajectory-level feedback and turn-level interaction, promoting informative responses and reducing redundancy. Specifically, turn-level credit assignment uses silence-completed prefix scoring to construct group-relative advantages from each decision's remaining return, separating its learning signal from earlier rewards. Turn-mean policy optimization averages token losses within each turn and then across turns, giving response and waiting decisions equal weight regardless of output length. These complementary designs improve streaming policy learning without a learned critic or additional rollouts. Extensive experiments show our method outperforms prior RL approaches for streaming video understanding.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-12"></a>

### 12. [ConceptARC at Scale: Studying Conceptual Understanding in Reasoning Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06732v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06732

**作者：** Philipp Mondorf、Adrien Deliège、Solim LeGris、Claas Beger、Ryan Yi、Barbara Plank、Brenden M. Lake、Todd M. Gureckis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06732v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06732v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出可批量生成 ARC 风格任务的 ConceptARC at Scale，并构建覆盖 16 个概念、1600 个任务的 ConceptARC-100，用于评测大推理模型及从零训练模型的概念泛化，并分析其内部表示。

**相关性简评：** 该基准与表示分析直接考察视觉抽象规则理解及内部状态是否按操作语义组织，可供核查 LVR/VLM 推理中任务必要信息的保留与跨任务泛化，但不涉及 latent 生成或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ConceptARC at Scale: Studying Conceptual Understanding in Reasoning Models

**abstract/A1：** Large reasoning models (LRMs) perform well on challenging benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether they form generalizable abstractions across conceptually related reasoning tasks. The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) provides a controlled testbed for studying abstraction, requiring solvers to infer transformation rules over two-dimensional grids from a small set of examples. While ARC evaluates rule abstraction within individual tasks, different tasks may share higher-level structure despite requiring different transformations$\\unicode{x2014}$for example, several tasks may involve reasoning about objects contained within other objects. Benchmarks such as ConceptARC capture this structure by grouping ARC-style tasks into recurring spatial and semantic concepts, but they often contain only a few manually constructed tasks per concept. We introduce ConceptARC at Scale, a generator for creating thousands of ARC-style tasks targeting a specific concept. Using this generator, we construct ConceptARC-100, a dataset of 1,600 new, manually reviewed tasks, with 100 tasks for each of the 16 concepts defined by ConceptARC$\\unicode{x2014}$ten times the size of the original benchmark. We evaluate a range of recent LRMs and models trained from scratch on ConceptARC-100 to characterize which concepts they solve reliably and where they struggle. We further analyze the models' internal representations and find clear semantic structure: task representations cluster according to the operations applied to objects, such as recoloring, removal, or translation, even when realized through substantially different rules and scenarios. Together, our results suggest that models form stable abstractions of transformation operations that generalize across diverse tasks.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 14 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-13"></a>

### 13. [Benchmarking Fine-Grained Visual Reasoning for Remote Sensing in Urban Management](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06770v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06770

**作者：** Jiawei Zhang、Rizhao Cai、Dayan Guan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06770v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06770v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建了基于无人机图像的细粒度城市管理遥感基准 UMBench，覆盖交通、城建、环境与灾害等任务，并据此诊断 MLLM 依赖场景先验、忽略细粒度视觉证据的问题，进而提出免训练的「证据优先」思维链提示方法。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及 VLM 细粒度视觉证据的保留与使用，其「场景先验替代证据」诊断和证据优先的 LOOK 式提示可供核查细粒度证据筛选与推理收益的联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Benchmarking Fine-Grained Visual Reasoning for Remote Sensing in Urban Management

**abstract/A1：** Fine-grained visual reasoning is essential for remote sensing in urban management, yet remains underexplored in the evaluation of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). We introduce UMBench, a fine-grained urban remote sensing benchmark built from unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) images for multiple urban management tasks such as traffic monitoring, urban construction, environmental quality, and disaster warning. Evaluations on UMBench reveal that current MLLMs often rely on scene-level priors and overlook fine-grained visual evidence. To address this issue, we further propose a novel evidence-first chain-of-thought method that directs models to inspect fine-grained visual evidence in a training-free manner. Experiments show that our method can improve the performance of both open-source and commercial MLLMs on UMBench.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by IEEE AP-GARSS 2026; 4 pages, 3 figures, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-14"></a>

### 14. [Transforming Integer Grids to Spatial Intuition: Perception, Not Planning, Governs ARC-AGI-3](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06793v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06793

**作者：** Harsidak Singh Banwait

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06793v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06793v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究 ARC-AGI-3 交互式视觉游戏中空间感知与规划的作用，提出零参数 O(HW) 感知引擎 SARBLOH，将整数网格帧转为对象清单、时序跟踪、几何简报与标注画布，并配合代码即动作运行时。

**相关性简评：** 「已证不变量」与「affordance 假设」的分离及主动验证探针，直接关联视觉证据筛选、THINK/LOOK/STOP 的成本与感知瓶颈诊断；但方法为外部符号管线而非 latent 状态，其结论对内部表示的适用性可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Transforming Integer Grids to Spatial Intuition: Perception, Not Planning, Governs ARC-AGI-3

**abstract/A1：** Sample-efficient fluid intelligence requires agents that rapidly form working models of unfamiliar interactive environments. ARC-AGI-3 tests this across uninstructed visual games scored by Relative Human Action Efficiency, which penalizes exploratory waste quadratically. When frontier models read raw integer grids, serialization destroys spatial topology, and they spend hundreds of actions rediscovering basic boundaries and symmetries. We introduce SARBLOH, an open-weight agent built on the premise that spatial perception, not planning capacity, governs interactive reasoning on ARC-AGI-3. A zero-parameter O(HW) perception engine turns each frame, in 35 ms, into an object inventory with bipartite temporal tracking, a geometric briefing that separates proven invariants from affordance hypotheses, and an annotated canvas. Paired with a code-as-action runtime, the agent writes algorithmic hypotheses instead of low-level moves. Across eight presentations, grounded perception raises the rubric score from 3/28 to 25/28 points while cutting token use by 89.4%. On 23 held-out games, geometric invariants generalize with 100% accuracy, while single-frame affordance guesses fall to 6-50%, so active verification probes remain necessary. On the public set, Run A (briefing off) solved 143 of 183 levels in 8.2 hours at 59.4% single-pass efficiency; Run B (briefing on) reached 63.5%. Within the nine-hour budget on one Kaggle RTX PRO 6000 GPU, SARBLOH scored 16.53 on the hidden Kaggle evaluation, 13.7x the first Milestone winner.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 17 figures, 8 tables. Submitted to the ARC Prize 2026 paper track. Code: https://github.com/Harsidak/SARBLOH

</details>

<a id="paper-15"></a>

### 15. [Where to Connect? Boosting MLLMs via Dynamic Gated Pathways across ALL ViT and LLM Layers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06844v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06844

**作者：** Yingying Yan、Jiaqi Tang、Wei Wei、Qianzhou Wang、Lei Zhang、Jianmin Chen、Yuyang Xia、Botong Geng、Jinjian Wu、Qifeng Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06844v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06844v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究多模态大模型中视觉信息应接入何处，提出 DGP：在全部 ViT 层与全部 LLM 层之间建立全连接路径，并让每个 LLM 层依据自身语义状态动态门控对视觉层的偏好。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及视觉细节如何进入并保留在 LLM 内部状态，且逐层路由分析可供核查不同深度所需的视觉粒度，与 LVR 中表示保留和视觉旁路问题联系明确。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where to Connect? Boosting MLLMs via Dynamic Gated Pathways across ALL ViT and LLM Layers

**abstract/A1：** A central yet under-explored question in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) is where to connect---how visual information from a hierarchical vision encoder should be wired into the layer-wise semantics of a large language model. Mainstream MLLMs inject a fixed single-layer visual representation into the LLM and treat all LLM layers as a single consumer, ignoring that different layers demand different visual granularities. Recent multi-layer fusion methods either compress hierarchical features on the encoder side or rely on predefined sparse or hierarchical layer-to-layer connections, and therefore stop short of modeling adaptive cross-layer interactions between the ViT and LLM hierarchies. We address this gap with DGP (Dynamic Gated Pathways), a routing mechanism that establishes all-to-all connections between every ViT layer and every LLM layer, with each LLM layer dynamically gating its preference over visual layers conditioned on its own semantic state. Built on top of LLaVA-1.5-7B and 13B, DGP delivers consistent gains over the LLaVA-1.5-7B baseline (e.g., +4.2 on SQA$^{I}$, +3.3 on MM-Vet, +3.1 on VizWiz and LLaVA$^{W}$) and surpasses prior multi-layer visual fusion methods on most benchmarks. Layer-wise routing analyses further show that different LLM layers do select different visual granularities on demand, supporting both the effectiveness and interpretability of the proposed dynamic gated pathways. The code is available: https://github.com/YingyingYan1/DGP.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages (10 pages main text + references + appendix), 15 figures. Accepted at the 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-16"></a>

### 16. [Visual Token Routing: Learning to keep tokens that matter](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06854v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06854

**作者：** Pranjal Aggarwal、Sean Welleck

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06854v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06854v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 Visual Token Routing，联合训练视觉语言模型与轻量时空路由器，按当前及前一张截图中的图像区域决定视觉 token 是否进入语言模型，以在动作预测性能基本不变的情况下减少视觉 token 和推理成本。

**相关性简评：** 其学习式视觉证据筛选与时空冗余判断直接关联 LVR 的「LOOK」成本和支持选择问题；但路由器由动作预测目标驱动，是否保留几何、实体关系等任务必要细节仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Visual Token Routing: Learning to keep tokens that matter

**abstract/A1：** Computer-use agents (CUAs) have recently achieved superhuman performance on several long-horizon benchmarks. However, their computational cost limits their use in long-horizon tasks and real-time domains such as video games. A key bottleneck is representing each screenshot as thousands of visual tokens, which accumulate into long contexts as agents reason over extended interaction histories. We observe that most of these visual tokens are either semantically irrelevant to the agent's next action or temporally redundant across screenshots. Building on this observation, we introduce Visual Token Routing (VTR), a joint training framework for learning sparse visual representations that preserve the information needed for action prediction. In VTR, we train the vision-language model together with a lightweight spatio-temporal router that examines each image region in the current and previous screenshots and decides whether its visual token enters the language model. We optimize the router using an adaptive loss to keep the number of visual tokens as small as possible while minimizing performance degradation. With just 2,000 training steps, VTR matches the performance of the full-resolution model on OSWorld, CUA-World, and video game action prediction, while using 2.5-5.8x fewer visual tokens. These savings reduce inference cost by up to 3.3x and speed up prefill by up to 2.9x. VTR also outperforms lowering the image resolution and other baselines for dropping tokens, by up to 12.8 points on OSWorld. Overall, VTR enables computer-use agents to process only the visual tokens they need, allowing them to be used in long-horizon tasks and real-time domains at a fraction of the computational cost. Code and models are available at https://pranjal2041.github.io/vtr

</details>

<a id="paper-17"></a>

### 17. [From Pixels to Actions - Towards Native Vision-Language-Action Policies](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06918v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06918

**作者：** Weichen Fan、Haiwen Diao、Penghao Wu、Siyuan Hu、Ziqi Huang、Tianqi Liu、Mingze Sun、Ziyi Wang、Zhongang Cai、Hanming Deng、Quan Wang、Lei Yang、Lewei Lu、Fangzhou Hong、Zhaoxi Chen、Dahua Lin、Ziwei Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06918v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06918v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出无独立视觉编码器的 NEO-VLA，将图像经轻量 patch 卷积 token 化并与语言在主干中联合演化，再由 flow-matching 动作专家读取不同深度的视觉表示，并用受控比较与逐层探测检验固定编码器终点的影响。

**相关性简评：** 直接关联视觉细节在内部状态中的保留与深度选择：逐层探测显示动作关键信号常早于最终层达峰，为「固定视觉终点导致信息坍缩」及按需读取中间视觉状态提供证据线索，值得核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Pixels to Actions - Towards Native Vision-Language-Action Policies

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language-action models are typically built on a simple premise: perception is first compressed by visual encoders, and action reasoning begins only afterwards. Yet manipulation often hinges on precisely the visual signals most vulnerable to this abstraction, e.g., local geometry, boundaries, depth, and subtle state changes. This raises a fundamental question: should action policies inherit a fixed visual endpoint, or directly access evolving visual computation? We introduce NEO-VLA, a structurally encoder-free vision-language-action framework. Images are tokenized by lightweight patch convolutional layers and evolve jointly with language throughout the backbone, allowing a flow-matching action expert to draw from instruction-conditioned visual representations at different depths. This simple change reveals a consistent pattern. Native architectures outperform encoder-based counterparts under controlled comparisons; full fine-tuning reduces but does not erase the gap, and additional scale alone cannot recover it. More strikingly, layer-wise probing shows that the action-critical signals often peak well before the final visual representation. Across diverse manipulation settings and distribution shifts, NEO-VLA consequently achieves stronger and more robust control. Together, these results suggest that embodied control benefits from access to visual representations across the computational hierarchy, rather than a fixed encoder output.

**comment/C1：** Code link: https://github.com/EvolvingLMMs-Lab/NEO/tree/main/VLAKit

</details>

<a id="paper-18"></a>

### 18. [Agents, Think Only When it Helps](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05709v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05709

**作者：** Min-Seon Kim、Seungdong Yoa、Sanghyu Yoon、Ye Seul Sim、Suhee Yoon、Dongmin Kim、Jung-Eun Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05709v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05709v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文通过独立扰动教师轨迹中的思考与动作，检验 LLM agent 蒸馏中 thought supervision 对最终动作性能的必要性，发现仅当思考信息未在动作中披露时，思考监督才明显重要。

**相关性简评：** 其「扰动或丢弃监督信号以做贡献分配」的诊断思路可供核查 LVR 轨迹训练中 latent 内容是否真被答案使用、何时可省略监督，但需迁移到视觉多模态场景验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Agents, Think Only When it Helps

**abstract/A1：** LLM agent distillation transfers agentic capabilities to student models by training on complete teacher trajectories that typically interleave thoughts and actions. We question how critical thought supervision is for agents' ultimate action performance. To answer this, we disentangle their impacts by independently perturbing teacher thoughts and actions during distillation. On widely adopted agent benchmarks across model scales, altering or discarding thoughts has little effect on student performance. We find, however, that thought supervision becomes important when information about the thought is not disclosed in the corresponding action during training. By identifying such discrepant cases, our findings challenge the common practice of distilling entire teacher trajectories for LLM agents, and offer a new insight that thought supervision can be a discernible choice for efficiency depending on whether the information of thought is disclosed in the action.

</details>

<a id="paper-19"></a>

### 19. [Routes of Reasoning: Reading and Reshaping Test-Time Compute in MoE Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05919v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05919

**作者：** Shengjie Wang、Mohsen Fayyaz、Nanyun Peng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05919v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05919v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把推理轨迹切成句级片段并标注六种推理行为（规划、执行、复述、事实、反思、结论），发现对 MoE 路由 logits 做线性探针即可近似预测行为标签，并在推理时选择性编辑专家路由分数以抑制过度思考、缩短生成长度。

**相关性简评：** 属纯文本工作且无视觉输入；但「从路由状态读出行为标签、再以无训练方式干预停止」为 LVR 的 THINK/STOP 成本控制与行为级状态读出提供可供核查的线索，能否迁移到多模态 MoE 的视觉证据筛选尚待验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Routes of Reasoning: Reading and Reshaping Test-Time Compute in MoE Models

**abstract/A1：** Reasoning models allocate test-time compute across distinct behaviors such as planning, deriving, and verifying. However, models frequently "overthink," continuing to verify and re-derive long after finding a correct answer. This wastes expensive compute and can even degrade performance. Existing interventions, such as token budgets or steering, primarily regulate reasoning duration rather than targeting specific behaviors. In this work, we demonstrate that Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routers provide a natural, training-free interface for behavioral control. We first split reasoning traces into sentence-level segments and label each with one of six reasoning behaviors (planning, execution, restatement, fact, reflection, and conclusion), so that every token carries the behavior of its segment. Linear probes on router logits predict this label nearly as well as probes on the residual stream, the model's full hidden state and the standard target for probing, while using about half as many features. These signals generalize: experts associated with a reasoning behavior on one dataset predict the same behavior on the others, across six datasets spanning five domains (math, code, science, knowledge, and commonsense). Building on this, we selectively edit expert router scores during inference to curb overthinking. Across four models and four benchmarks, this intervention reduces the number of generated tokens by a median of 30.4% (and up to 63.3%). Crucially, it encourages the model to stop once the problem is solved, rather than skipping essential reasoning steps. Accuracy never drops by more than 0.5 percentage points, and frequently improves. Ultimately, MoE routing offers both an interpretable lens into reasoning states and a lightweight mechanism for optimizing test-time compute.

</details>

<a id="paper-20"></a>

### 20. [Reasoning-Trace Overflow in Code Generation: An Empirical Study of Explicit Reasoning Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06095v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06095

**作者：** Lukas Twist、Helen Yannakoudakis、Jie M. Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06095v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06095v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在 7 个小型开源显式推理模型与 6 种代码基准上实证考察「推理轨迹溢出」：固定 32k 输出预算中有多少被思维链消耗、是否提升 pass@1，以及关闭推理、prompt 引导和预算式强制转换等推理期控制的效果。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本代码生成、不涉及视觉，但其把推理长度视为可控资源、用预算触发强制转换与停止的证据，可为 LVR 中 THINK/STOP 的成本权衡与停止决策提供可供核查的对照线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reasoning-Trace Overflow in Code Generation: An Empirical Study of Explicit Reasoning Models

**abstract/A1：** Explicit reasoning can help language models generate code, but it also introduces a practical risk: the model may spend so much of its output budget reasoning that it never produces usable code. We study this behaviour in relatively small open-weight reasoning models, framing it as reasoning-trace overflow. Across seven models and six code benchmark settings, we measure how much of a fixed 32k-token output budget is spent on reasoning, whether that reasoning improves pass@1, and whether lightweight inference-time controls can mitigate overflow. Our results show that the value of reasoning is highly conditional. Overflow is rare on simpler function-level tasks, but becomes common on harder code-generation benchmarks and for particular model families: on several model--benchmark pairs, more than half of generations exhaust the full 32k-token budget while still reasoning. Most complete overflow is associated with repetitive reasoning loops, although substantial long reasoning also occurs without looping. Disabling reasoning can therefore be competitive for overflow-prone models, simply because it ensures that an answer is produced. At the same time, reasoning remains valuable for stronger models on harder algorithmic and code-understanding tasks. Finally, prompt steering can substantially reduce overflow for some models but is inconsistent, while budgeted transitions provide more direct control and can improve both performance and token efficiency for overflow-prone models. These findings suggest that explicit reasoning should be treated as a controllable resource for code generation, rather than an always-on capability.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 6 figures, 1 table

</details>

<a id="paper-21"></a>

### 21. [Prism: Bidirectional Self-Teaching for Unified Multimodal Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06343v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06343

**作者：** Peng Zhang、Qian Chen、Xianyin Zhang、Yimin Tian、Ruite Fei、Dongyi Lv、Lifan Guo、Chi Zhang、Shuai Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06343v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06343v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Prism，一种无需外部验证器的双向自教学后训练方法：把模型自生成的交织轨迹拆成文本链与图像链，评估各自可靠性并据此构造训练目标，在 SenseNova-U1 与 BAGEL 上同时提升理解与生成指标。

**相关性简评：** 其按可靠性为自生成轨迹分链构造监督的思路，可能为 LVR 的轨迹训练与视觉/文本贡献分配提供可核查线索，但论文不涉及显式推理状态或 latent 生成，与主线的具体关联需读正文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Prism: Bidirectional Self-Teaching for Unified Multimodal Models

**abstract/A1：** Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) aim to support visual understanding and generation within a single model. Existing post-training methods either use understanding as a fixed teacher to improve generation, or rely on ground-truth trajectories to supervise both understanding and generation. Prism instead derives bidirectional supervision from self-generated interleaved rollouts, constructing training targets according to the reliability of their text and image chains. We propose Prism, a bidirectional self-teaching post-training method for UMMs. Like a prism decomposing mixed light, Prism decouples a coupled interleaved trajectory into a text chain and an image chain, assesses the reliability of each chain, and constructs a corresponding training target from their reliability states. Experiments on both SenseNova-U1 and BAGEL show that Prism improves both understanding and generation. On the generation side, GenEval improves from 85.8 to 89.2 (+3.4), and DPG improves from 86.1 to 87.3 (+1.2) on SenseNova-U1. On the understanding side, the total MME score improves from 2282.6 to 2311.6, and MMVP improves from 58.0 to 62.0. Consistent improvements are observed on BAGEL. These results highlight that Prism enables coordinated improvements in generation and understanding by constructing reliability-guided self-training targets from self-generated trajectories, without relying on an external verifier or externally annotated corrected trajectories during target construction.

</details>

<a id="paper-22"></a>

### 22. [Does Context Compression Preserve Refusal Alignment?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06380v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06380

**作者：** Anthony Nguyen、Wenjun Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06380v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06380v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察基于编码器的上下文压缩在重构保真度较高时是否仍保持指令微调模型的拒答对齐，发现压缩会系统性削弱拒答行为，并归因于解码器拒答方向上的激活衰减，进而提出在压缩表示空间做推理时 Memory Steering 以恢复拒答率。

**相关性简评：** 虽属纯文本压缩，但其「语义保真不等于行为保真」以及压缩表示空间的方向诊断与推理时干预，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 是否保留并实际驱动任务必要信息。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Does Context Compression Preserve Refusal Alignment?

**abstract/A1：** Context compression reduces inference cost by encoding inputs into compact representations while preserving semantic content. An open question is whether semantic preservation alone is sufficient to maintain downstream behaviours such as refusal alignment. We investigate this question and find that encoder-based compression systematically weakens refusal behaviour in instruction-tuned language models, despite high reconstruction fidelity. This effect persists across model families and compression architectures. Mechanistic analysis shows that compression attenuates activation along the decoder's learned refusal direction. We further explore Memory Steering, a lightweight inference-time intervention that restores refusal rates to near-baseline levels without retraining and operates entirely in compressed representation space. These results demonstrate that semantic preservation does not guarantee behavioural preservation under compression, highlighting the need to explicitly preserve alignment-relevant features in compression-aware systems.

**comment/C1：** Presented at Canadian AI 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-23"></a>

### 23. [GIST: Gibbs-Targeted Hierarchical Data Selection for Offline RLVR](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06395v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06395

**作者：** Xuliang Huang、Tianxiang Zhao、Chengwei Qin、Yao Shu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06395v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06395v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 offline RLVR 中「监督哪些响应与哪些 token」的问题：借助 KL 正则化 RLVR 的 Gibbs 最优策略，用双向 KL 度量策略与该目标的失配，并证明在参考策略处该失配可分解为正确响应的前向项与错误响应的反向项；据此提出 GIST 两级选择方法，用采样温度挑选正确/错误响应、用初始策略的 token 概率定位需监督的 token，在多个模型与目标上提升平均准确率并显著减少被监督 token 数。

**相关性简评：** 其按失配大小选择监督响应与 token 的思路，可能为 latent 推理轨迹中的贡献分配与稀疏监督提供可核查的线索：哪些推理步骤或状态值得被监督、如何用策略自身的概率定位关键位置；但该工作为纯文本 RLVR，未涉及视觉证据与内部状态表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GIST: Gibbs-Targeted Hierarchical Data Selection for Offline RLVR

**abstract/A1：** Offline reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) trains on a fixed pool of sampled responses and typically supervises every token of every response, although responses and tokens are not equally informative. We study which ones to supervise through the Gibbs-optimal policy of KL-regularized RLVR, which offline training targets, and measure the mismatch between a policy and this target with a bidirectional KL divergence. At the reference policy, this mismatch factorizes exactly and separates the two outcomes: correct responses contribute mainly through the forward term, concentrated on their low-probability tokens, and incorrect responses through the reverse term, concentrated on the responses the policy generates often and on their high-probability tokens. Based on this analysis, we propose GIST (Gibbs-Targeted Hierarchical Data Selection), a two-level selection method that approximates these principles with signals available before training. GIST uses the sampling temperature to select correct responses on-policy and incorrect responses near the policy's mode, and it uses the initial policy's token probabilities to supervise only the tokens where the mismatch lies. Applied to two offline training objectives, GIST raises the average accuracy over GSM8K, MATH-500, and MBPP for all three models under both objectives. In a controlled comparison on Qwen3-0.6B/GSM8K, it outperforms seven selection baselines that choose from the same candidates while supervising 66.6% fewer tokens than training on all candidates of the same prompts.

</details>

<a id="paper-24"></a>

### 24. [What Does Multi-Turn Reinforcement Learning Really Improve in LLM Agents?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06483v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06483

**作者：** Kejian Zhu、Zhuoran Jin、Shangqing Tu、Tianyi Men、Yupu Hao、Jiachun Li、Kang Liu、Jun Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06483v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06483v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用 Pass@(K,T) 从采样广度与交互深度两方面分析多回合 agentic RL 的收益来源，发现其优势集中在错误恢复与长程韧性且依赖充足回合预算，并提出训练中变化交互预算的 BudgetMix RL。

**相关性简评：** 其回合预算依赖分析与训练期预算混合，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益成本权衡、停止决策及预算或长度泛化提供可核查线索；但该工作针对纯文本 agent，与视觉状态和 latent 推理的联系尚待确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Does Multi-Turn Reinforcement Learning Really Improve in LLM Agents?

**abstract/A1：** While multi-turn agentic reinforcement learning (RL) excels on long-horizon tasks, its performance gains remain highly sensitive to interaction constraints. Evaluating across sampling breadth $K$ and interaction depth $T$ via $Pass@(K,T)$, we show that trajectory-level RL advantages depend on sufficient turn budgets, diminishing significantly when budgets tighten. Besides, agentic RL does not uniformly elevate single-step decision quality; rather, its strength concentrates in error recovery and long-horizon resilience. To mitigate the deployment risks caused by this budget dependence, we provide a preliminary discussion on the benefits and limitations of process supervision signals and introduce BudgetMix RL as an alternative that varies interaction budgets during training. Experiments demonstrate that BudgetMix RL achieves robust performance across diverse execution settings. Project Page: https://rethinking-agentic-rl.github.io/

**comment/C1：** Project Page: https://rethinking-agentic-rl.github.io/

</details>

<a id="paper-25"></a>

### 25. [Tools Need Not Speak in Text: Latent Context Compression for Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06500v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06500

**作者：** Yuetong Liu、Zhehao Zhou、Yitong Zhang、Haorui Chen、Jia Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06500v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06500v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Coca-Cola，把 agent 的工具观测压缩成连续 latent token 并直接嵌入输入嵌入空间，同时用 Action-Preserving Training 对齐完整观测与压缩观测下的下一动作分布，在搜索类 agent 上以更少 token 和更短运行时间完成任务。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本 agent 场景，但其以动作分布对齐作为监督、迫使 latent 保留决策关键信息的思路，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 内容是否真被下游使用、以及信息坍缩与监督信号设计问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Tools Need Not Speak in Text: Latent Context Compression for Agents

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents typically append raw tool outputs to their context. Over multi-step interactions, long and noisy observations incur substantial context overhead and dilute the agent's attention to task-relevant information. Existing token pruning and summarization methods remain constrained by discrete text representations, limiting the achievable compression ratios. In this work, we challenge the prevailing assumption that agents must consume tool observations as explicit text tokens. To this end, we propose Coca-Cola, a continuous latent context compression model for LLM agents. Coca-Cola compresses tool observations into latent tokens and embeds them into the input embedding space of the agent. We further introduce Action-Preserving Training, which aligns the agent's next-action distributions under full and compressed observations. This trains the compression model to learn action-sufficient latent tokens that retain decision-critical information. Experiments with web-search agents on four QA benchmarks and two model families show that Coca-Cola reduces the agent's token consumption by 70.01%-89.24% and end-to-end runtime by 24.45%-55.97%, while consistently improving task performance. These results suggest that Coca-Cola offers a promising approach for context compression in agents. Our code is available at https://github.com/THU-Agent/Coca-cola.

</details>

<a id="paper-26"></a>

### 26. [Verifier-Guided Self-Consistency: Test-Time Reasoning with Imperfect Generators and Verifiers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06606v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06606

**作者：** Ekaterina Fadeeva、Xueyan Li、Mrinmaya Sachan、Maxim Panov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06606v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06606v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VGSC 随机解码框架：每步由基座模型提出候选，结合步骤级正确性与未来结果估计做接受／重采样，同时保留多条轨迹做答案级自洽投票，并给出其近似采样「verifier-tilted」轨迹分布的概率解释。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本测试时推理方法，无视觉成分，但其逐步验证加重采样、多轨迹聚合的机制可能为 LVR 中的 latent 状态筛选、停止决策及 verifier 误差放大问题提供可核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Verifier-Guided Self-Consistency: Test-Time Reasoning with Imperfect Generators and Verifiers

**abstract/A1：** Large language models can solve many multi-step reasoning problems, but their reasoning remains brittle: plausible intermediate steps often lead to incorrect answers. Improving reasoning at test time requires balancing two imperfect signals: the generator, which provides diversity, and the verifier, which guides correctness. Existing approaches largely favor one side of this tradeoff. Verifier-weighted voting relies on trajectories sampled from the generator and cannot steer generation toward correct ones. By contrast, beam-style verifier-guided search can steer generation, but may over-concentrate on narrow trajectory sets and amplify verifier errors. We propose Verifier-Guided Self-Consistency (VGSC), a stochastic decoding framework that combines step-level correctness and future-outcome estimates to guide reasoning during generation. VGSC repeatedly proposes candidate next steps from the base language model and uses both signals in an acceptance/resampling procedure, while maintaining multiple trajectories for final answer-level self-consistency. We provide a probabilistic interpretation of VGSC as approximate sampling from a verifier-tilted trajectory distribution. Across multiple reasoning benchmarks, generator models, and verifier families, VGSC achieves higher average accuracy than representative baselines.

</details>

<a id="paper-27"></a>

### 27. [Where Search Agents Go Off the Rails: Graph-Grounded Step-Level Verification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06662v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06662

**作者：** Hyeseo Jeon、Seonho Yoo、Jay-Yoon Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06662v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06662v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 本文提出 GraphRail，将问题要求、证据与推理步骤拆成结构化 claims 并构建图进行对齐比较，在无 gold answer、无生成式评判器的条件下做步骤级验证，用于多跳 QA 中的中间错误检测与在线纠正。

**相关性简评：** 其「证据是否覆盖问题、推理是否扎根证据」的免参考步骤审计线索，可供核查迁移到 VLM 中视觉证据筛选与中间步骤验证；但材料仅涉及文本检索代理，未触及视觉状态表示或 latent 推理，迁移性尚待确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where Search Agents Go Off the Rails: Graph-Grounded Step-Level Verification

**abstract/A1：** Search-augmented reasoning systems can reach correct answers while making errors in intermediate steps. Reliable auditing must check whether evidence $D$ covers the question $Q$, reasoning $T$ addresses $Q$, and $T$ is grounded in $D$. We introduce GraphRail, which represents requirements, evidence, and reasoning as structured claims and compares them without a generative judge or gold answer. Across three multi-hop QA benchmarks and three reasoning frameworks, GraphRail improves error recall over generative verifiers while remaining stable under stochastic extraction. Its diagnoses can also guide online reasoning, providing a traceable way to detect and correct intermediate errors.

</details>

<a id="paper-28"></a>

### 28. [Context--Answer Metamodels: Forecasting LLM Activations Before the First Answer Token](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06837v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06837

**作者：** Tommy He、Thomas Jiralerspong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06837v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06837v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文训练「上下文—答案元模型」，用阅读上下文时的激活序列预测答案平均池化激活的点估计或分布，并考察随训练规模、跨域与跨模型迁移的表现。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本工作，但其在生成前预测未来内部状态与 rollout 分布的思路，可能为 LVR 中预判 latent 推理状态、评估上下文证据是否足以停止或分配计算提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Context--Answer Metamodels: Forecasting LLM Activations Before the First Answer Token

**abstract/A1：** Under stochastic decoding, a single context can elicit many different answers from a language model. Characterizing this variation typically requires many expensive autoregressive rollouts, each revealing only one realization. We ask whether the activations produced while reading the context can directly forecast the model's rollout activations. We train context--answer metamodels that map a context activation sequence to point or distribution forecasts of mean-pooled answer activations. We find that (i) the four metamodel families we study forecast mean-pooled answer activations with high fidelity, and the distribution metamodels also capture rollout variability; (ii) performance improves as the training set grows to 500,000 prompts, and the metamodels generalize zero-shot to out-of-domain datasets; (iii) their forecasts retain behavior-predictive signal; and (iv) context activations from Qwen2.5 predict mean-pooled answer activations from Qwen3.5 and Gemma 4, suggesting that activation forecasts can transfer across models. Together, these results suggest that context activation traces can support scalable forecasts of behaviors in LLM answers before the first answer token is generated.

</details>

<a id="paper-29"></a>

### 29. [How Finely Can Vision-Language Models See Biology? Bio-VQA: A Benchmark for Taxonomic, Morphological, Spatial and Individual Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05559v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05559

**作者：** S M Rayeed、Qian Ma、Charles V. Stewart、Yao Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05559v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05559v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** Bio-VQA 构建了覆盖层级分类、细粒度属性定位、定量空间推理与个体重识别四个方面的生物视觉问答基准（1.86M 问题、217K 图像），评测 18 个开源权重模型，并将对错评分扩展为分级评分。

**相关性简评：** 其失败诊断——计数显著弱于日常物体、细粒度属性与重识别接近或低于随机、15/18 模型不优于盲目基线、层级越细准确率越低——可供核查 VLM 是否真正使用视觉证据、细粒度细节是否保留在表示中；但属领域基准，与 LVR 状态生成无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Finely Can Vision-Language Models See Biology? Bio-VQA: A Benchmark for Taxonomic, Morphological, Spatial and Individual Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** In biodiversity monitoring, a single image raises several questions: which taxon, how many, which trait, and whether the individual has been seen before. Vision-language models (VLMs) could answer all of these from the image, but their ability is largely untested in biology, as existing benchmarks focus mostly on species identification within a few taxonomic groups. We present Bio-VQA, the largest biological visual question answering (VQA) benchmark and the only one to span four aspects of biological visual understanding: hierarchical taxonomic reasoning, fine-grained attribute grounding, quantitative spatial reasoning, and individual re-identification. Bio-VQA contains 1.86M questions over 217K images from 11 public datasets, and extends right-or-wrong scoring to graded scoring, so a near-miss counts for more than a distant one. Evaluating 18 open-weight models from 3.75B to 38.4B parameters, we find that general visual capabilities do not transfer to biological organisms: the best model counts animals exactly on 41.7% of images with 2 to 10 individuals, less than half its accuracy on everyday objects at the same counts. Taxonomic accuracy falls to one sixth, from 0.72 at phylum to 0.12 at scientific name, and scaling helps mostly at coarse ranks. The gap to a domain specialist widens accordingly, from 0.731 against BioCLIP-2's 0.874 at class (1.2x) to 0.269 against 0.544 at scientific name (2x). On trait size measurement, 15 of the 18 models do no better than an image-blind baseline that predicts one fixed number for every specimen, and on re-identification, 17 of the 18 models fall below chance under adversarial conditions. These failures persist across a tenfold range of model size. We hope Bio-VQA becomes a standard test of how well VLM capabilities transfer to the biological domain.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027, 8 pages main paper; 4 figures, 4 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-30"></a>

### 30. [How Do Artificial Agents Make Decisions in Light of Visual Data?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05671v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05671

**作者：** Keyty Loza、Shayan Monadjemi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05671v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05671v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究评估 VLM 智能体对可视化图形中不确定性的感知精度，以及其在图像形式视觉数据下与理性决策理论的一致性，并比较不同模型与图表类型。

**相关性简评：** 其「视觉信息是否真正进入决策」这一诊断视角与 LVR 中视觉证据使用、不确定性表征如何影响答案的问题相关，但工作停留在图表素养与决策对齐评测层面，未涉及内部状态或推理轨迹，可供核查视觉旁路问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Do Artificial Agents Make Decisions in Light of Visual Data?

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated significant advances in reasoning with visual data, which is particularly important given that much of human knowledge is encoded visually in scientific articles and other sources. However, a gap remains in understanding how artificial agents, powered by VLMs, perceive and navigate decision-making under uncertainty in the presence of visual data. This study explores this gap by evaluating and analyzing autonomous decision-making processes. Building upon prior work in visualization literacy and decision theory, we conducted two complementary studies: one examining how agents perceive and quantify uncertainty encoded in visualizations, and another investigating how artificial agents make decisions based on visual data in the form of images. We analyzed perception accuracy and decision performance across models and visualization types. Our results show that GPT-4o demonstrates the highest accuracy in perceiving uncertainty in pie and bar charts among the tested models. For decision-making, autonomous agents showed the best alignment with rational decision theory when uncertainty was represented through circle charts, icon arrays, or text.

**comment/C1：** Presented at the Workshop on GenAI, Agents, and the Future of VIS, held in conjunction with IEEE VIS, 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-31"></a>

### 31. [Do Saliency Maps Fail Dense Breasts? A Chance-Normalized, Confounder-Aware Audit of Explanation Fairness in Mammography](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05909v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05909

**作者：** Ahmed Jaheen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05909v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05909v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在1,345例CBIS-DDSM恶性视图上，用峰值是否命中病灶以及经随机基线归一化的病灶—背景显著性比（bits），检验三种CNN的Grad-CAM、Integrated Gradients与遮挡图在致密乳腺中定位病灶是否更差，并做病例组合调整及conspicuity、病灶依赖对照。

**相关性简评：** 这是对视觉证据归因的失败审计，直接触及任务必要视觉信息是否真被模型使用以及混杂因素与对照设计；其机会归一化审计口径可供核查能否用于LVR中视觉证据筛选与贡献分配的因果检验，但该文为单次分类解释，无latent状态或推理轨迹。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do Saliency Maps Fail Dense Breasts? A Chance-Normalized, Confounder-Aware Audit of Explanation Fairness in Mammography

**abstract/A1：** Saliency maps, offered as evidence that mammography classifiers use the cancer, are untested in dense breasts, which can hide cancers. We tested whether they locate cancers worse there, and why. On 1,345 malignant CBIS-DDSM views, we scored three CNNs' Grad-CAM, Integrated Gradients and occlusion maps by whether their peak hits the lesion and a chance-normalized lesion-to-background saliency ratio (bits; +1 doubles it). Unadjusted gaps were large: Grad-CAM peaks hit 44% of dense vs. 58% of non-dense lesions. Case mix, chiefly more calcifications in dense breasts, explained about half or more of each gap. Occlusion kept a significant average of -0.56 bit gap. Controls also implicate the classifiers: unadjusted gaps exceeded an image-only conspicuity map's, and models relied less on dense-breast lesions. We recommend reporting density gaps before and after case-mix adjustment, per classifier, with conspicuity and lesion-reliance controls.

</details>

<a id="paper-32"></a>

### 32. [SleuthVLM: Do We Really Need Audio-in-Video for Omni Video Understanding?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06083v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06083

**作者：** Peiran Wu、Fangfei Gou、Zhaoxi Mu、Changhao Zhang、Jian Liu、Weiqiang Wang、Yuntao Wang、Yuanchun Shi、Junxiao Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06083v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06083v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SleuthVLM：先把视频音频转写为文本，再让 VLM 在没有预设流程的情况下自主「侦查」长视频（最长 90 分钟），在四个 omni 基准上取得 75.0% 平均成绩，并据此主张瓶颈不是「听见音频」而是 agentic 能力。

**相关性简评：** 该工作涉及长视频中自主获取视觉证据与何时停止的决策，其「模态对齐不如 agent 能力关键」的诊断可供核查 THINK/LOOK/STOP 中证据筛选与工作区设计的收益来源；但状态完全在文本轨迹中、无 latent 表示，机制联系需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SleuthVLM: Do We Really Need Audio-in-Video for Omni Video Understanding?

**abstract/A1：** Omni models answer questions about videos by jointly processing sampled frames and audio in one turn, with progress driven largely by scaling model size and omni training data. We ask whether a model needs to hear the audio directly at all, and present SleuthVLM, an agent harness built on a VLM that never hears the audio directly: speech will be transcribed into text, and the VLM will sleuth the video on its own without any predefined workflow. On four omni benchmarks with videos up to 90 minutes, SleuthVLM achieves the best average accuracy: \\textbf{75.0%}, 2.6 points above Gemini 3.8 Flash, and it also tops the Video-MME-v2 leaderboard with \\textbf{77.8%}; with an open-source 27B VLM, it beats all open-source omni models. We draw three findings: SleuthVLM beats a model that glances; hearing is not the bottleneck, agentic ability is; omni understanding needs better agentic ability, not just more omni data for modality alignment. So we provide a simple but effective starting point for doing omni video understanding. Omni understanding needs better agents, not just bigger models that hear.

</details>

<a id="paper-33"></a>

### 33. [A Systematic Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Personalized Reasoning of Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06259v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06259

**作者：** Dohyun Kim、Junyong Lhim、Kwanyong Park

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06259v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06259v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MPRBench，一个用于系统评测多模态大模型个性化推理的基准，包含 12 个子任务并支持对代表性错误案例的可解释分析，实验发现模型在个性化概念识别和无关个人信息干扰下仍表现不佳。

**相关性简评：** 「个性化概念识别不准」与「对无关个人信息敏感」正对应 LVR 关心的视觉细节保留与证据筛选问题，可供核查是否能作为诊断任务；但论文聚焦基准评测，未涉及内部状态或推理期 latent 生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Systematic Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Personalized Reasoning of Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** People often make decisions by holistically reasoning over heterogeneous forms of personal information. In this paper, we study the relatively underexplored capability of multimodal personalized reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). To this end, we introduce \\texttt{MPRBench}, a novel and comprehensive benchmark specifically designed to evaluate personalized reasoning across a wide range of tasks and real-world challenges. \\texttt{MPRBench} consists of 12 sub-tasks and additionally supports interpretable analysis of representative error cases. Through extensive experiments, we find that current personalized MLLMs still struggle with personalized reasoning due to several key challenges, including inaccurate recognition of personalized concepts and sensitivity to irrelevant personal information. We hope that MPRBench will stimulate further research on personalized reasoning in MLLMs. Code and data are available at https://github.com/kkdh15/MPRBench

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-34"></a>

### 34. [Beyond Scene Shortcuts: Action-Centric Video-Text Alignment for Fine-Grained Action Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06302v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06302

**作者：** Zhiliang Zhu、Jiazheng Huang、Dawei Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06302v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06302v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文诊断视频 VLM 在细粒度动作理解中依赖静态场景与显著物体的「环境锚定动作幻觉」，并提出 STARR 训练框架，以动作—环境解离和注意力一致性奖励强化动作聚焦的时空证据对齐。

**相关性简评：** 其环境捷径诊断与注意力一致性约束可为 LVR 中视觉旁路、证据筛选和内部状态是否真正编码动作信息提供核查线索；但方法主要在输出与训练层面干预，尚需确认是否触及 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Scene Shortcuts: Action-Centric Video-Text Alignment for Fine-Grained Action Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Fine-grained action understanding requires vision-language models to ground what an agent does, how it acts, and why it acts in spatio-temporal evidence. However, richer supervision can backfire: fine-tuned models may generate detailed action descriptions grounded in static scenes and salient objects rather than motion, a failure we call Environment-Grounded Action Hallucination (EGAH). To mitigate EGAH, we propose Staged Attention Rectification and Reinforcement (STARR), an action-centric video-text alignment framework that breaks scene-action shortcuts through Action-Environment Dissociation and reinforces action-focused grounding with an Attention Consistency Reward within Group Relative Policy Optimization. We further construct FineActionR&amp;R, combining temporally localized captions with hierarchical and temporal-causal reasoning data. Built on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, FineActionVLM-R1 improves average MotionBench accuracy by 3.01 percentage points over its base model and achieves 91.50% on HAU-MCQ-Bench. Qualitative comparisons further indicate more faithful fine-grained action descriptions with fewer environment-induced hallucinations.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-35"></a>

### 35. [Traffic-VL: Reinforced Vision-Language Models for Generalizable End-to-End Traffic Signal Control](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06353v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06353

**作者：** Xingchen Zou、Maonan Wang、Kemou Jiang、Jie Li、Yiqi Chen、Xi Chen、Hai Yang、Yuxuan Liang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06353v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06353v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建了 SynTra-V 真实感多视角交通监控数据及语义审计流程，并训练 8B 的 Traffic-VL，将同步多视角图像与自然语言相位描述直接映射为信号决策，结合两阶段强化推理与闭环在线轨迹策略优化。

**相关性简评：** 它直接以视觉观测替代结构化中间状态并做闭环序列决策，与「视觉证据是否真正被用于决策、是否发生表示坍缩」及轨迹训练监督相关；但属交通控制专用系统，未剖析内部表示，可供核查视觉信息使用与训练贡献分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Traffic-VL: Reinforced Vision-Language Models for Generalizable End-to-End Traffic Signal Control

**abstract/A1：** Existing traffic signal control methods typically rely on structured traffic states produced by dedicated perception pipelines. Emerging visual approaches often use idealized simulator images and convert them into intermediate representations before control, limiting their ability to directly exploit roadside visual observations. To bridge both the visual-domain and perception-to-decision gaps, we introduce SynTra-V and Traffic-VL. SynTra-V converts physics-based traffic simulations into photorealistic multi-view surveillance imagery across diverse road topologies and sensing conditions, and automatic semantic auditing checks that control-critical geometry, vehicle movements, and lane semantics match the simulator state. Traffic-VL is an eight-billion-parameter vision-language model that directly maps synchronized multi-view images and natural-language phase descriptions to signal decisions without intermediate structured states. Building on Traffic-R1's two-stage reinforced reasoning framework, Traffic-VL adapts its vision encoder via offline reinforcement learning on SynTra-V and freezes it during online visual stepwise trajectory policy optimization in closed-loop simulation. Experiments show that Traffic-VL matches structured-state Traffic-R1 while outperforming image-based controllers and general-purpose vision-language models, including Gemini 3 Pro. Evaluations on unseen topologies, degraded sensing conditions, real surveillance footage, and a three-week deployment across ten intersections further demonstrate robust visual generalization and effective constrained online control.

</details>

<a id="paper-36"></a>

### 36. [BEAKER: An Expert-Curated Benchmark for Embodied Brains in Self-Driving Chemical Laboratories](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06911v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06911

**作者：** Fei Lin、Tengchao Zhang、Ziyang Gong、Xiaotong Yu、Bohan Zhang、Yifan Zhou、Qihao Yang、Ji Dai、Dong Li、Yining Jiang、Qinghua Ni、Jun Huang、Qiang Zhang、Yue Zhou、Zhihang Zhong、Yonglin Tian、Fei-Yue Wang、Xue Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06911v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06911v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。
