**内容简述：** 构建 BEAKER：面向自动驾驶化学实验室「具身大脑」的专家标注基准，含 1,000 条问答、11 类图像与视频任务，覆盖可供性定位、轨迹规划、器材与安全状态识别及步骤时序推理，并离线单轮评测 28 个 MLLM。

**相关性简评：** 其诊断发现（模型能跟上粗粒度流程却难以定位可操作区域、识别细粒度状态）与 VLM 内部是否保留任务必要的视觉细节和证据筛选相关，可作为视觉表示与证据使用的失败分析参考；但为离线单轮评测、无 latent 状态生成，联系仅在诊断层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BEAKER: An Expert-Curated Benchmark for Embodied Brains in Self-Driving Chemical Laboratories

**abstract/A1：** Self-Driving Chemical Laboratories (SDCLs) are moving chemical experimentation toward embodied automation, where machines must perceive laboratory scenes, reason about experimental states, and act under physical, procedural, and safety constraints. Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly considered as embodied brains for such systems, existing laboratory benchmarks mainly focus on safety, anomaly detection, or general scientific understanding, leaving their execution-oriented embodied capabilities underexplored. To fill this gap, we introduce BEAKER, a Benchmark for evaluating Embodied Actionability, Knowledge, Experimentation, and Reasoning in SDCLs. BEAKER contains 1,000 expert-curated question-answer samples across 11 image and video task types, covering key judgments required for chemical laboratory execution, including affordance grounding, trajectory planning, equipment, state, and safety recognition, apparatus setup, operation and step reasoning, and temporal procedure reasoning. All questions and annotations are manually created and reviewed by chemistry and embodied Artificial Intelligence (AI) experts, without AI-assisted generation or annotation. We evaluate 28 general-purpose, science-oriented, chemistry-specialized, and embodied MLLMs in an offline, single-turn setting. Results show that current models can follow coarse experimental procedures in videos, but still struggle to ground actions into operable regions and feasible trajectories, to recognize fine-grained laboratory apparatus, states, and safety norms, and to order steps in mid-length procedures. BEAKER thus reveals a clear gap between coarse procedural understanding and the precise, action-grounded perception required for laboratory execution, providing a diagnostic benchmark for future MLLMs in SDCLs.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by NeurIPS 2026 (Track on Evaluations and Datasets, Spotlight)

</details>

<a id="paper-37"></a>

### 37. [Hallucination Is Linearly Decodable from Mid-Layer Hidden States in Quantized LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02628v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2606.02628

**作者：** Aizierjiang Aiersilan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.ET、cs.NE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02628v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.02628v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文考察 4-bit 量化的 7–8B 指令微调 LLM 隐藏状态中真实性信号是否线性可分、在哪一层最强：在四个幻觉基准上逐层提取隐藏状态，比较线性/MLP 探针、EigenScore、自一致性与注意力熵，发现单个中层线性探针 AUROC 达 0.904–1.000，采样类方法不超过 0.541，且峰值层在模型族间落在一致的中层区间。

**相关性简评：** 其逐层线性探针与「中层深度带」分析范式可供核查量化压缩下 latent 视觉/任务必要信息是否仍线性可解码、以及深度位置与注意力熵是否反映证据使用；但研究对象是纯文本真实性而非视觉推理，联系需较多迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hallucination Is Linearly Decodable from Mid-Layer Hidden States in Quantized LLMs

**abstract/A1：** We investigate whether open-source LLMs encode a linearly separable truthfulness signal in their hidden states, and at which network depth this signal is strongest. Across three $7$B--$8$B instruction-tuned models (Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, Qwen2.5-7B) loaded in $4$-bit NF4 quantization, we extract per-layer hidden states on four hallucination benchmarks (TruthfulQA, HaluEval-QA, FEVER, and a controlled synthetic set) and compare four detection approaches: linear and MLP probes, INSIDE EigenScore, self-consistency, and attention entropy. A linear probe on a single mid-network layer achieves $0.904$--$1.000$ AUROC on held-out splits, while sampling-based detectors do not exceed $0.541$ AUROC under the same protocol. The truthfulness signal is approximately linear: MLP probes rarely surpass linear probes by more than $0.01$ AUROC. Peak probing layers fall in a consistent band across model families on natural-language benchmarks -- blocks~$13$--$18$ of~$32$ for Llama and Mistral, and blocks~$19$--$25$ of~$28$ for Qwen. First-block attention entropy provides a complementary signal in knowledge-grounded settings ($0.866$--$0.941$ AUROC on HaluEval-QA) at no additional inference cost. The low discriminability of sampling methods under this protocol reflects a structural mismatch between paired-label evaluation and the information these methods access, rather than an inherent limitation of those methods. Code and data are released for full reproducibility on a single $8$\\,GB GPU.

</details>

<a id="paper-38"></a>

### 38. [VAR-Q: Tuning-free KV Cache Quantization for Visual Autoregressive Image and Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05380v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05380

**作者：** Boxun Xu、Jiaji Lu、Yuxuan Yin、Zihu Wang、Ziyue Liu、Yu Wang、Zirui Liu、Peng Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.PF

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05380v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05380v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 分析视觉自回归生成（next-scale 与 next-frame 两种范式）的 KV cache 结构：少数超大值通道承载主要功能、离群值沿特征与块两个轴组织、并源于投影偏置与 RoPE 散射；据此提出免调优的分块分组低比特量化框架 VAR-Q，在九个模型上将 KV 显存最多降低 78.5%。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 推理机制无直接关联，但其对视觉 token 的 KV cache 离群通道与块级结构的刻画，可供核查多模态推理中缓存与注意力结构如何影响内部视觉状态的保真度与长上下文成本。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VAR-Q: Tuning-free KV Cache Quantization for Visual Autoregressive Image and Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Visual autoregressive generation spans two dominant paradigms: next-scale prediction for images and video, and next-frame prediction for video. Both decode block-by-block rather than token-by-token, yielding KV caches whose statistics diverge from those of language models. At scale, KV memory exceeds the weights and limits large-scale deployment caused by a large amount of visual tokens. Our analysis of these caches reveals three findings. First, a small set of massive-value channels carries the functional load: ablating these outlier channels collapses generation, whereas ablating a matched set of inlier channels leaves quality intact. Second, the outliers organize along two axes, feature and block, with magnitudes persistent within each cell and shifting across cells. Third, this structure arises from two independent mechanisms: projection bias and RoPE-induced scattering. Guided by these findings, we propose VAR-Q, a tuning-free KV-cache quantization framework that groups along the same two axes. The generation loop provides block boundaries for free, removing the need for outlier detection or calibration. Paired with bit-packing kernels for runtime memory reduction, VAR-Q delivers low-bit quantization across nine models spanning both paradigms, cutting KV memory by up to 78.5% with negligible quality degradation and enabling 6x larger inference batches.

**comment/C1：** A preliminary version of this work was presented at the ICCV 2025 BiVision Workshop

</details>

<a id="paper-39"></a>

### 39. [Who May Overrule the Agent? Evidence-Gated Authority in LLM Review Institutions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05390v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05390

**作者：** Fuad Ali

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05390v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05390v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把多智能体系统中审查者能否推翻上游决策形式化为「管辖权限」问题：在相同提议、任务信息与模型下，比较宽泛覆盖权与「证据门控」（审查者需给出可机械校验的约束违反记录与合规修复才允许替换），在两个本地开源模型、96 个空间政策任务和迁移基准上测量纠错收益与污染正确提议的代价，并用「保留/替换/升级」接口做前瞻验证。

**相关性简评：** 「须提供可校验证据方可覆盖既有决策」的门控机制可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据何时足以推翻语言先验答案、以及保留/替换/升级的决策授权条件；其「接口设计并非模型无关保障」的结论也可作为迁移时的警示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Who May Overrule the Agent? Evidence-Gated Authority in LLM Review Institutions

**abstract/A1：** Multi-agent language-model systems often add a reviewer to improve an upstream agent's decision. Yet a reviewer can both repair errors and replace decisions that were already correct. We study this as a problem of institutional jurisdiction: conditional on the same proposal, task information, model, and review output, when is the reviewer authorized to make its recommendation binding? We compare broad override authority with an evidence gate that permits replacement only after the reviewer produces a mechanically valid record of a binding-constraint violation and a compliant repair. Across two locally run open-weight models, 96 spatial-policy tasks, and a 96-task portfolio-transfer benchmark, the gate sharply reduces corruption of correct proposals while retaining most repairs of violations. The directions of this tradeoff are partly implied by the rule; its magnitude depends on reviewer transitions and upstream proposal states. A prospective validation then gives the reviewer an explicit choice to retain, replace, or escalate. This eliminates correct-proposal corruption for Qwen, but Mistral still replaces all 48 correct proposals; Qwen also declines every welfare-only repair that Mistral frequently attempts. Thus interface design changes reviewer behavior but is not a model-independent safeguard. The result is not that restricted review is universally better. It is that reviewer competence, action interface, and binding jurisdiction jointly determine both the correction domain and the corruption surface.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 3 figures, 3 tables. Code, frozen outputs, and reproduction instructions: https://github.com/tofuadmiral/institutional-representation-abm

</details>

<a id="paper-40"></a>

### 40. [Uncertainty-Aware Resource Orchestration for Agentic AI Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05392v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05392

**作者：** Masoud Shokrnezhad、Tarik Taleb

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.DC、cs.LG、cs.MA、cs.NI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05392v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05392v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把 agentic LLM 多步流程在调度时未知的算力、带宽与延迟需求形式化为不确定性感知编排问题，并提出基于 conformal prediction 的在线预留算法 CRO，用历史执行轨迹给出概率覆盖保证。

**相关性简评：** 其用 conformal 覆盖对动态计算需求做分布无关预算预留，可能为 LVR 的推理算力分配与 THINK/STOP 成本控制提供不确定量化线索，但论文不涉及视觉或 latent 推理状态，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Uncertainty-Aware Resource Orchestration for Agentic AI Systems

**abstract/A1：** Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) systems powered by large language models (LLMs) execute as autonomous multi-step pipelines that dynamically invoke tools, integrate observations into memory, and iterate until a task is resolved or a resource budget is exhausted. Unlike classical workflows with fixed graphs, the aggregate compute, bandwidth, and end-to-end latency demands of such pipelines are unknown at dispatch time: they emerge at runtime from intermediate observations and stochastic token sampling, yielding heavy-tailed, multi-modal random variables. This unpredictability forces infrastructure orchestrators into a dilemma: under-provisioning risks mid-execution throttling and deadline violations, while over-provisioning wastes scarce edge or cloud capacity. Existing schedulers require a fully known pipeline graph, while edge resource-management methods rely on parametric or stationary demand models, and neither fits the unknown, non-stationary profiles of agentic pipelines. We formalize this setting as the uncertainty-aware agentic orchestration problem, where the goal is to reserve, at dispatch time, compute and bandwidth allocations that cover actual pipeline demands, and to verify that the pipeline can meet its deadline, each with a prescribed probability. We then propose Conformal Reservation for Orchestration (CRO), an online algorithm that translates a sliding window of past execution traces into dispatch-time resource allocations with finite-sample, distribution-free coverage guarantees via conformal prediction. Simulations over heterogeneous task mixes with multi-modal and heavy-tailed profiles show that CRO is the only evaluated method that simultaneously satisfies target probabilistic coverage across all risk levels while, among coverage-feasible methods, attaining the lowest over-reservation, roughly halving the resource waste of the Chebyshev-bound baseline.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 2 figures. Accepted to IEEE MECOM 2026, Marrakech, Morocco, 2-4 November 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-41"></a>

### 41. [Challenge-Aware Knowledge Recruitment with Meta-Agent Supervision for Medical Multi-Agent Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05393v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05393

**作者：** Riccardo Riccio、Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde、Pietro Liò

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05393v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05393v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对医疗多智能体推理在难题上不如强单模型的问题，提出由外部 CHALLENGER 检查演进中的推理轨迹、识别具体事实或专长缺口，据此触发中途知识检索或专家补招；在 MEDAGENTSBENCH 硬子集上比较了检索时机、缺口信号与两种干预通道组合的效果。

**相关性简评：** 「依据推理轨迹中的缺口决定何时、为何引入外部资源」与 LVR 中动态决定是否重新获取视觉证据、停止或继续思考有结构相似性，而其发现的自触发不可靠、朴素组合检索与专家招募反而掉点，可供核查多通道干预（LOOK 与策略切换）如何协调。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Challenge-Aware Knowledge Recruitment with Meta-Agent Supervision for Medical Multi-Agent Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Large language models perform strongly on standard medical QA but degrade on harder reasoning tasks, where multi-agent debate often fails to beat strong single-model prompting. We argue that a key limitation is that existing systems select experts and retrieve knowledge from the question alone, rather than from gaps that emerge during reasoning. We propose challenge-aware adaptive recruitment: an external CHALLENGER inspects the evolving reasoning trace and identifies specific factual or expertise gaps. These signals trigger targeted knowledge retrieval or mid-discussion specialist recruitment. On the MEDAGENTSBENCH hard subset (862 questions across nine biomedical datasets), upfront retrieval provides little benefit, while challenge-aware in-between retrieval performs better; on a multi-specialist backbone it improves accuracy on 8 of 9 datasets and adds 6 percentage points over Challenger Only. Ablations indicate benefits from both retrieval timing and gap-specific query signals, and a live web-search experiment shows that the timing advantage persists across retrieval backends. For specialist recruitment, self-triggered adaptation is unreliable, whereas Challenger-triggered recruitment raises GPT-4.1-mini MedQA accuracy from 46% to 54%, with positive direction on all tested generalization datasets. Naively combining retrieval and specialist recruitment degrades performance, highlighting the need to coordinate adaptive intervention channels. These results show that when and why external resources are recruited is a key design choice in multi-agent medical reasoning.

</details>

<a id="paper-42"></a>

### 42. [PEEK: One-Step-Look-Ahead Exposure Bias Correction for Diffusion Sampling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05407v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05407

**作者：** Yunwei Bai、Ying Kiat Tan、Tsuhan Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05407v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05407v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PEEK，一种无需训练的确定性扩散采样插件：在若干去噪步先做一次试探性单步更新，在 look-ahead 状态上再评估一次去噪器，并与下一步预测插值形成新的噪声状态，以缓解训练‑测试输入分布不匹配造成的 exposure bias，同时用缓存加速插值调度搜索。

**相关性简评：** 虽属扩散图像生成、不直接研究 LVR，但其「先试探更新再评估并插值修正状态」的思路，可能与推理期 latent 状态漂移的诊断与逐步校正、以及多步状态更新的贡献分配有可供核查的类比线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PEEK: One-Step-Look-Ahead Exposure Bias Correction for Diffusion Sampling

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion image generators are successful due to accurate denoising networks and strong numerical samplers. Yet the two components are not fully matched. During sampling, the denoiser predicts for the next step based on flawed input that differ much from the real data. This train-test gap, or exposure bias, caps image generation quality (i.e., blurry, lacking in details or awkward in shape). To mitigate the problem, we propose PEEK, a novel training-free deterministic sampler plugin. At each eligible denoising step, PEEK first performs a provisional one-step update, evaluates the denoiser once at the look-ahead state, and interpolates it with the one-step-later prediction to form the next noisy state. By taking the additional step, we obtain a generally more-image-like prediction from the higher-signal noisy state, mitigating the exposure bias. To optimize the interpolation positions, PEEK searches for an interpolation schedule search made efficient by the prefix and baseline noise-schedule-rebasing caches. On 8 pixel-space and latent-space diffusion models with unconditional, label-guided, and text-guided settings across various output resolutions, PEEK consistently improves over NFE-matched baselines, with FID improvements up to 49.3\\% at minimal extra computation cost (e.g., the one-time-per-model calibration takes at most 2.2 minutes across our experiments.). Project website is at https://dachengruoque.github.io/PEEK-Video/.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to Neurips 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-43"></a>

### 43. [SurgTransfer: Multi-Step Latent Action Modeling for Action Transfer in Surgical World Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05443v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05443

**作者：** Saurabh Koju、Love Panta、Deepak Thapa、Rudra P. K. Poudel、Prajwal Ghimire、Angus Watson、Yash Raj Shrestha、Prashnna Kumar Gyawali、Binod Bhattarai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05443v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05443v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SurgTransfer 多步 latent action 学习框架，从无标注手术视频中学习上下文不变的隐动作表示并跨手术场景迁移，在 SurgToolLoc-2022 与 PitVis-2023 上用定量指标和外科专家定性评估验证。

**相关性简评：** 其多步、分层的隐动作抽取与跨情境不变性训练，可能为 LVR 中多步 latent 推理状态的构建及无标注轨迹监督提供可核查线索；但研究对象是手术动作迁移与世界模型，而非 VLM 推理，需先确认隐状态是否承载可解释的推理内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SurgTransfer: Multi-Step Latent Action Modeling for Action Transfer in Surgical World Model

**abstract/A1：** Surgical world models have the potential to provide intra-operative guidance, accelerate risk-free training and facilitate transfer of expert surgical maneuvers. However, the use of world models for transferring surgical expertise has been largely unexplored. Moreover, recent approaches in this domain have mainly relied on ground truth kinematics actions or expert annotations for specific tasks, limiting their applicability beyond the narrow range of tasks with ground-truth annotations. In contrast, existing works in the natural domain have shown promising results in learning latent actions solely from unlabeled videos and transferring the learned actions across contexts. We observe that this paradigm is short-sighted for the surgical domain, failing to capture the hierarchical and goal-directed structure of surgical procedures. Thus, we propose a novel multi-step action learning framework to learn context-invariant latent actions that are easily transferable across various surgical contexts. We perform extensive experiments on the publicly available datasets, SurgToolLoc-2022 and PitVis-2023, verifying our approach with both quantitative metrics and qualitative evaluation by surgical experts. We have published our code and the implementation details at https://github.com/bhattarailab/Surgical-Vision-World-Model.

</details>

<a id="paper-44"></a>

### 44. [SO(3)-Equivariant Learning on CAD Boundary Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05449v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05449

**作者：** Matteo Ballegeer、Dries F. Benoit

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05449v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05449v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SO3brep：在 B-rep 面邻接图上构建 SO(3) 等变图网络，节点特征在局部坐标系中计算后用 Wigner D 矩阵映射到共享坐标系，并通过 Clebsch-Gordan 张量积与相邻面间方向耦合，使消息传递保留相对朝向、输出保持 SE(3) 不变，在旋转测试的面分割与分类基准上取得最佳精度且参数量最少。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线关系较远，但其用等变消息传递在状态更新中显式保留几何相对朝向的做法，可供核查能否类比用于视觉几何关系在 latent 推理状态中的保持与不丢失。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SO(3)-Equivariant Learning on CAD Boundary Representations

**abstract/A1：** Learning on boundary representations (B-reps) requires robustness to the pose of a part, since CAD models are not stored in a canonical orientation. Existing B-rep networks either operate on world-frame coordinates and normals, and thus degrade under rotation, or encode each face in a local reference frame. Local frames yield invariance, but reduce inter-face messages to scalars and discard the relative orientation of adjacent faces. We propose SO3brep, an SO(3)-equivariant graph neural network on the B-rep face-adjacency graph. Node features are computed in local frames and mapped into a shared frame by Wigner D-matrices. Clebsch-Gordan tensor products couple these features with the directions between adjacent faces, so that relative orientation is preserved throughout message passing and the output remains SE(3)-invariant. Across three face-segmentation and two classification benchmarks with randomly rotated test parts, SO3brep achieves the highest accuracy among the compared B-rep networks and equivariant point cloud networks, with the fewest parameters. Its advantage is largest with few labeled parts, a setting typical of industrial CAD. Code is available at https://github.com/UGent-CVAMO/so3brep.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026 main track

</details>

<a id="paper-45"></a>

### 45. [From Tokens to Trajectories: Parameter-Efficient Continuous Adaptation of Unified Multimodal Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05451v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05451

**作者：** Gayatri Deshmukh、Onkar Susladkar、Adheesh Juvekar、Halil Kilicoglu、Ismini Lourentzou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05451v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05451v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 FlowShift，把预训练离散 token 的统一多模态模型后训练为连续 rectified-flow 生成器：用连续 latent 投影替换离散视觉接口、对注意力层加 LoRA，并以双目标速度向量蒸馏（DOVD）对齐连续教师与真值轨迹，在保持多模态理解能力的同时提升图像保真度。

**相关性简评：** 其「离散量化瓶颈导致细粒度视觉细节丢失」的判断与 LVR 中视觉证据是否保留在内部状态的问题相通，连续 latent 投影加轨迹蒸馏可能可供核查是否有助于缓解潜空间细节坍缩，但原文目标是图像生成质量而非推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Tokens to Trajectories: Parameter-Efficient Continuous Adaptation of Unified Multimodal Models

**abstract/A1：** While discrete tokenization in Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) establishes strong semantic reasoning, it introduces a strict quantization bottleneck that degrades fine-grained visual details, often resulting in structurally accurate but artifact-heavy images. We introduce FlowShift, a parameter-efficient post-training framework that bypasses this bottleneck by converting pre-trained discrete UMMs into continuous rectified-flow generators. Instead of discarding the backbone's semantic priors, FlowShift replaces discrete visual interfaces with continuous latent projections and applies LoRA to the attention layers. To efficiently boost visual quality without semantic drift, we propose Dual-Objective Velocity Vector Distillation (DOVD), which aligns the model with a high-fidelity continuous teacher and ground-truth continuous trajectories. This approach drastically improves perceptual quality while preserving prompt adherence and reasoning. When applied to leading discrete UMMs, FlowShift significantly improves COCO-30K FID and aesthetic scores, while retaining over 99.5% of multimodal understanding capabilities. Ablations confirm DOVD drives generation quality. Ultimately, FlowShift achieves high-fidelity continuous generation without sacrificing pre-trained semantic grounding.

</details>

<a id="paper-46"></a>

### 46. [Objective-Conditioned Active Preference Learning for Multi-Objective Alignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05506v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05506

**作者：** Duc Anh Vu、Thong Nguyen、Hoang Pham、Ponhvoan Srey、Luu Anh Tuan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05506v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05506v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 OGC-DPO，在多目标对齐中把每个「响应对—目标」组合视为独立查询，用策略自身的隐式梯度估算信息量并优先选择能扩大各目标梯度覆盖度的样本，以降低偏好标注成本，并指出冲突偏好也能提供有用训练信号。

**相关性简评：** 其按目标分组的梯度覆盖式选样与「冲突样本也有监督价值」的观察，可能可供核查用于多目标 latent 推理训练数据的筛选与监督分配；但论文不涉及视觉输入或推理期状态，联系仅停留在方法层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Objective-Conditioned Active Preference Learning for Multi-Objective Alignment

**abstract/A1：** Aligning large language models (LLMs) with several objectives, such as helpfulness, harmlessness, and honesty, typically relies on preference judgment for each objective, growing the annotation cost linearly with the number of objectives. Active preference learning can reduce this cost by selecting the comparisons whose labels are expected to change the policy most. Existing remedies either lack an objective-aware query selection mechanism for the multi-objective setting or regard conflicting preferences as harmful, therefore excluding them from the training data. We introduce OGC-DPO (Objective-conditioned Gradient Coverage DPO), an active learning framework that treats each (response pair, objective) combination as a separate query and measures its informativeness through the policy's own implicit gradient, without relying on an external reward model. OGC-DPO then selects the queries that most expand each objective's gradient coverage. Empirical experiments across varying numbers of objectives demonstrate the effectiveness of OGC-DPO over existing active preference learning methods under the same annotation cost. Further analysis demonstrates that informative conflicts, rather than being uniformly harmful, provide a useful policy training signal.

</details>

<a id="paper-47"></a>

### 47. [TSFM Meets LLM: Context as Covariates](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05515v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05515

**作者：** Hemanth Ram、Sai Shankar Narasimhan、Shubhankar Agarwal、Sandeep Chinchali

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05515v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05515v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CoCo 框架：用 LLM 将文本信息中的推理蒸馏为带数值的预测与置信度「协变量」，用以引导 TSFM 主干做时序预测；并先用 GRPO 以下游预测增益为奖励对齐 LLM，再在生成协变量上微调 TSFM，同时给出由 LLM 标注真实时序构造多模态语料的方案。

**相关性简评：** 把开放式文本推理压缩成少量数值协变量、并以「下游任务收益」作 RL 奖励做两阶段监督，可能为 latent 推理状态的信息保真与贡献分配、以及推理内容是否真被主干使用提供可供核查的线索；与时序预测主线本身无直接视觉关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TSFM Meets LLM: Context as Covariates

**abstract/A1：** Textual information is ubiquitous in real-world time series datasets, appearing as news, reports, and annotations that hold key predictive signals not deducible from numerical history alone. Large Language Models (LLMs) interpret text but lack numerical precision, while Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) are quantitatively accurate but cannot reason about the effects of text. Moreover, the lack of large-scale paired multimodal datasets limits progress in bridging this gap. In this work, we address this challenge by providing a scalable approach for generating realistic multimodal datasets by annotating real-world time series with LLMs. Building on this, we propose Context as Covariates (CoCo), a forecasting framework in which an LLM distills its textual reasoning into numerically grounded forecast and confidence covariates that guide a TSFM backbone, thereby exploiting the strengths of both LLMs and TSFMs. We align the two models in stages, first fine-tuning the LLM via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a reward tied to covariate-induced improvement in forecasting performance, and then fine-tuning the TSFM on covariates generated by the aligned LLM. Our experiments show that models trained on our synthetic multimodal corpus generalize to real benchmarks spanning finance, economics, weather, traffic, and security. Notably, CoCo with Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 as the LLM and Chronos2 as the TSFM achieves up to 12% MAE improvement over state-of-the-art TSFMs and LLM forecasters, such as GPT-4o. Code, checkpoints, and the evaluation datasets are publicly available.

</details>

<a id="paper-48"></a>

### 48. [From Activation to Intervention: Interpreting Sparse Visual Features through Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05522v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05522

**作者：** Jianyang Gu、Jacob Beattie、Mridul Khurana、Julia Chae、Xi Xiao、Cheng Zhang、Anuj Karpatne、Wei-Lun Chao、Sara Beery、Tanya Berger-Wolf

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05522v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05522v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 RecSAEI：沿 SAE 解码器方向干预视觉表征并重建图像输出，把特征解释从被动观察激活区域转为主动干预重建，用于消歧纹理与颜色等位置重叠的特征语义，并将 \[CLS\] 的 SAE 干预归因到稀疏的 patch 解码方向。

**相关性简评：** 「干预—重建」可作为核查视觉细节是否真正保留在内部状态的因果证据协议，供 LVR 中 latent 内容验证与视觉证据筛选借鉴；但该文仅在视觉编码器上验证，与推理期状态生成无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Activation to Intervention: Interpreting Sparse Visual Features through Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose visual representations into individual features, offering a promising basis for explaining model behavior. Interpreting these features typically relies on images or pixel regions that most strongly activate them. However, this evidence becomes ambiguous when multiple features with different semantics frequently activate in the same locations, such as texture and color in the same patterns. We propose Reconstructive SAE Intervention (RecSAEI), a framework that shifts feature explanation from passive observation to active visual reconstruction. By intervening on representations along SAE decoder directions and reconstructing the visual output, we provide direct evidence of a feature's semantic meaning. We demonstrate that our method successfully disambiguates feature meanings across multiple datasets and vision encoders. Furthermore, our framework grounds \[CLS\] SAE interventions in a sparse set of patch SAE decoder directions. Beyond stable target response, this attribution also exposes the compositional relationship between elementary visual patterns and high-level semantics. Our results advocate for intervention reconstruction as an essential complementary signal for explaining neural networks.

**comment/C1：** Demo and code available at https://imageomics.github.io/RecSAEI

</details>

<a id="paper-49"></a>

### 49. [MA-SafeDiffuser: Safe Multi-Agent Planning with Diffusion Probabilistic Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05524v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05524

**作者：** Kiran Ravish、Ankita Kushwaha、Preeti、Pawan Kumar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05524v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05524v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 将 SafeDiffuser 扩展到多智能体场景：把联合安全集定义为各智能体与成对 barrier 集的交集，推导集中式和去中心化（通信感知）的约束去噪过程并给出有限时间不变性证明，同时用时变规格与 liveness CBF 处理局部陷阱和死锁，并提供含多智能体 Maze2D 的轻量基准。

**相关性简评：** 把 CBF 约束嵌入反向扩散去噪、并加入 liveness/终止约束，可能为 latent 推理状态的受约束生成与停止决策提供可核查的技术线索；但任务域是多智能体轨迹规划，与视觉推理无直接关联，借鉴价值需阅读正文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MA-SafeDiffuser: Safe Multi-Agent Planning with Diffusion Probabilistic Models

**abstract/A1：** We propose MA-SafeDiffuser, a multi-agent extension of SafeDiffuser that equips diffusion-based trajectory planners with finite-time diffusion invariance guarantees for joint-agent safety specifications. Building on the single-agent construction that embeds control barrier function (CBF) constraints into reverse-diffusion updates, we: (i) formalize joint safe sets as intersections of per-agent and pairwise barrier sets; (ii) derive centralized and decentralized (communication-aware) constrained denoising procedures with provable invariance under mild assumptions; (iii) address local-trap and deadlock phenomena via time-varying specifications and liveness CBFs; and (iv) develop a lightweight benchmarking suite including a multi-agent Maze2D domain. Empirically, MA-SafeDiffuser reduces violation counts relative to unconstrained diffusion baselines, while retaining planning quality. We provide algorithms, proofs, and reference implementations.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at AAMAS 2026. 14 pages, including supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-50"></a>

### 50. [Towards Efficient Whole Page Ranking with One-Pass Inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05542v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05542

**作者：** Qi Xu、Zhigang Hua、Zijie Lei、Yan Xie、Jizhe Zhang、Lynn Lu、Delun Du、Sha Hua、Ren Mao、Ke Pan、Haomin Yu、Sandeep Pandey、Luke Simon

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05542v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05542v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出前缀感知的非自回归整页排序框架 MIS：每次请求一次性计算可复用表示，再在受限 beam search 中用轻量前缀条件打分，以兼得 AR 的顺序依赖与 NAR 的推理效率；在 KuaiRand-1K、RL4RS 及线上社交视频平台降低 CPU 开销并接近 AR 基线质量。

**相关性简评：** 非视觉推理工作，但「一次性编码 + 前缀条件轻量打分」避免逐步全模型推理的思路，可供核查能否迁移到 LVR 中 AR 与并行状态生成的效率–质量折衷及表示复用，属推测性借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Efficient Whole Page Ranking with One-Pass Inference

**abstract/A1：** Whole-page ranking jointly optimizes advertisements and organic content to balance business outcomes and user engagement under complex layout and business constraints. Existing non-autoregressive (NAR) approaches enable efficient page generation but typically score candidate items independently of previously selected items, limiting their ability to capture order-dependent interactions. Autoregressive (AR) methods address this limitation through sequential conditioning, but incur substantial inference overhead that challenges large-scale deployment. Bridging this quality-efficiency gap remains a challenge in industrial whole-page ranking. We propose Multi-Item Scoring (MIS), a prefix-aware NAR-style framework that incorporates sequential context into efficient multi-item scoring. MIS integrates global candidate interactions, localized prefix conditioning, and position-aware scoring within a shared representation architecture. By computing reusable representations once per request and performing lightweight prefix-conditioned scoring during constrained beam search, MIS captures important sequential dependencies without repeated full-model inference. Experiments on KuaiRand-1K and RL4RS demonstrate that MIS approaches the generation quality of an autoregressive CGA baseline while achieving 4.07x and 3.54x faster CPU decoding, respectively. In a randomized evaluation on a large-scale social-video platform, MIS improves the realization metric by 0.104% and watch time by 0.058%, while reducing ranking-service CPU consumption by 9.73%. These results demonstrate that prefix-aware multi-item scoring can narrow the quality gap between NAR and AR generation while retaining the efficiency required for industrial scalability.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-51"></a>

### 51. [AGORA: Reliability-Aware Aggregation of LLM Judges via Item-Response Theory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05556v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05556

**作者：** Junhyung Ahn

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05556v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05556v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 AGORA，用项目反应理论把评测条目放在单一潜在质量尺度上，以敏感度参数和有序评分阈值刻画各 LLM 评审者的可靠度，在无人工标签条件下联合学习可信权重并聚合多评审打分。

**相关性简评：** 其「小样本标签下按可靠度加权聚合多个噪声信号」及无需标签的离散度预判指标，可能可作为 VLM 推理中多验证器或多 latent 读数融合、以及何时信任某证据线索的参考，需核查其假设是否适用于推理链评分；论文本身不涉及视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AGORA: Reliability-Aware Aggregation of LLM Judges via Item-Response Theory

**abstract/A1：** LLM-as-a-judge is a common substitute for costly human evaluation, and aggregating several judges is the standard remedy for any single judge's bias. Judge pools mix judges of very different reliability, while the human-labeled sets that validate them hold a few hundred items or fewer. In this regime the per-item mean weights weak judges as heavily as strong ones, and confusion-table models such as Dawid-Skene must estimate a per-judge error table from too few items. We propose AGORA (Aggregation Of Reliability-weighted Assessments), an item-response-theory aggregator that places every item on one latent quality scale and describes each judge by a sensitivity parameter and ordered rating thresholds, so judge reliability is learned from all items jointly and without human labels. Across three benchmarks, AGORA matches or exceeds Dawid-Skene on large evaluation sets, and its advantage widens as the set shrinks toward a few hundred items. It remains accurate as pools grow more uneven, a label-free dispersion statistic indicates in advance when trust weighting helps, and its learned trust weights track per-judge human validity.

</details>

<a id="paper-52"></a>

### 52. [ChangeJEPA: Predict Before You Compare for Remote Sensing Change Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05570v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05570

**作者：** Buddhi Wijenayake、Dhananjaya Jayasundara、Vishal M. Patel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05570v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05570v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ChangeJEPA 把遥感变化检测重构为隐空间「后状态」预测：以变化前图像和部分变化后观测为条件，分别产生先验假设与经观测更新的假设，用 joint-embedding 预测损失对齐目标表示，再由多种表示间的不一致推断变化，并在三个基准上优于 Siamese 基线。

**相关性简评：** 「先验假设＋证据更新」的 latent 状态更新，以及把预测可靠性、先验惊讶度当作证据信号，可能为 LVR 的 latent 状态更新与视觉证据筛选提供可核查线索；但该工作面向像素级变化检测，不涉及语言推理或显式推理轨迹生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ChangeJEPA: Predict Before You Compare for Remote Sensing Change Detection

**abstract/A1：** Remote sensing change detection is typically formulated as Siamese feature comparison, where two images are encoded independently and a decoder determines which discrepancies correspond to meaningful change. This is challenging because the encoders preserve both true scene changes and nuisance variations such as illumination, seasonal, radiometric, and geometric differences. We introduce ChangeJEPA, a bitemporal joint-embedding predictive architecture that reformulates change detection as latent post-state prediction. Given a pre-change image and partial post-change evidence, ChangeJEPA predicts the full post-change representation under two complementary conditions: a prior hypothesis derived from the pre-change scene and an updated hypothesis conditioned on observed post-change context. A joint-embedding predictive loss aligns both hypotheses with the target latent representation, encouraging semantic consistency while suppressing transient appearance variations. Change is then inferred from the disagreement among the pre-change representation, the observed post-change target, and the predicted latent states, capturing complementary evidence of change, prior surprise, contextual update, and prediction reliability. Across SYSU-CD, LEVIR-CD+, and CDD, ChangeJEPA consistently outperforms its decoder matched Siamese baseline by up to 1.51 IoU points and achieves the best IoU and F1-score on all three benchmarks, demonstrating the advantage of latent post-state prediction over direct feature comparison. Page: https://buddhi19.github.io/ChangeJEPA

**comment/C1：** 15 pages

</details>
