### 53. [Multimodal Fusion Methods for Whole-Slide Images and Clinical Data in Inflammatory Bowel Disease](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05579v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05579

**作者：** Ankana Banerjee、Mark van der Giezen、Ole Gunnar Aasprong、Emiel A. M. Janssen、Øyvind Steinsbø、Tore Grimstad、Kjersti Engan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05579v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05579v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作构建模块化框架，将全切片图像的 slide-level 嵌入与就诊临床嵌入输入 15 种可替换的多模态融合方法，在炎症性肠病队列上预测缓解结局，并比较各融合策略与图像依赖程度。

**相关性简评：** 其「移除 slide 嵌入后平衡准确率下降」的图像依赖度量与融合方式对照，或可作为诊断 VLM 推理中视觉旁路与视觉证据贡献分配的核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multimodal Fusion Methods for Whole-Slide Images and Clinical Data in Inflammatory Bowel Disease

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal AI fusion of histology and clinical data is an active research area in computational pathology, but fusion methods are rarely compared under identical conditions and across different clinical endpoints. We present a modular framework in which slide-level embeddings, obtained by training an attention-based aggregator over patch embeddings from a frozen foundation model, and per-visit clinical embeddings are fed into 15 interchangeable fusion methods with a shared output dimension and prediction head (except decision-level methods). Using a prospective cohort of newly diagnosed inflammatory bowel disease patients, we predicted STRIDE-II-aligned remission outcomes from baseline or follow-up visits. The best fusion method reached macro-F1 of 0.57-0.75, outperforming both single-modality models in four of six scenarios, and differed by outcome and input. Image reliance, the drop in balanced accuracy when the slide embedding is removed, tended to follow the remission type - highest for histological and lowest for endoscopic remission; methods with similar accuracy relied on the slide to very different degrees.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, 4 figures, Submitted to the 2027 IEEE 24th International Symposium on Biomedical Imaging (ISBI)

</details>

<a id="paper-54"></a>

### 54. [Relevance Propagation as Entropic Optimal Transport on Filippov Neural Networks: Sinkhorn Propagation and Region Relevance in Image Classifiers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05594v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05594

**作者：** Olivier Gibaru、Eric Nyiri

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05594v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05594v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把 LRP 刻画为一个变分问题的解：前向激活流在活动计算图上的零相对熵、无散度流；用 Filippov 算子 DAG 统一 LRP$\_0$、LRP$\_{z^+}$、LRP$\_{α,β}$ 为 LRPN$\_{α,β}$，并提出按边取熵的 Sinkhorn Relevance Propagation；在 VGG-16 与 ConvNeXt-Tiny 上检验守恒、忠实性与定位。

**相关性简评：** 其「哪些隐藏单元携带证据」的精确归因记账与节点可变权重，可供核查 latent 状态中视觉证据的贡献分配与监督信号设计；但对象仅为图像分类归因，未涉及 latent 生成或 VLM 推理，迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Relevance Propagation as Entropic Optimal Transport on Filippov Neural Networks: Sinkhorn Propagation and Region Relevance in Image Classifiers

**abstract/A1：** Relevance propagation, which redistributes a network's prediction backward through the layers, is usually treated as a heuristic. We show that it is the solution of a single variational problem: Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) is the divergence-free flow on the active computational graph of zero relative entropy to the backward transition law of the forward activation flow; for a non-negative reference, this is a relative entropy between Markov measures on backward paths. The rules LRP$\_0$, LRP$\_{z^+}$ and LRP$\_{α,β}$ follow from this principle by the choice of a reference, and are instances of one rule, LRPN$\_{α,β}$, whose weights may vary from node to node, for instance with the node's activity, in the spirit of Hebb's principle. The principle rests on a measure-theoretic formalization of feed-forward networks as directed acyclic graphs of Filippov operators, which covers piecewise-smooth and discontinuous architectures (e.g. hard-routed Mixture-of-Experts) with a Clarke-Filippov generalized-gradient calculus; it also characterizes backpropagation as the adjoint equation of a Lagrangian relaxation. On networks of ReLU and max-pooling nodes, LRP$\_0$ is Gradient$\\times$Input, with an explicit jump across a kink where LRP$\_{z^+}$ stays continuous. Taking the entropy edge by edge yields a new rule, Sinkhorn Relevance Propagation. Aggregated over an input region, the flow of a non-negative reference tells, with exact accounting, which hidden units carry its evidence, the conditioned flow being a Schrödinger bridge. On VGG-16, the conservation identities hold to round-off, and the rules are told apart by the sign of their maps, the share of the output left to the bias nodes, faithfulness, localization and class dependence; on ConvNeXt-Tiny, LRPN$\_{α,β}$ conserves the output and its Hebbian weights give the highest ABPC of the rules tested.

**comment/C1：** 41 pages, 17 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-55"></a>

### 55. [CAPTOR: text-encoder-free grounding by counting caption co-occurrence over the visual words of a frozen encoder](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05599v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05599

**作者：** Elad Yoshai、Natan T. Shaked

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05599v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05599v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** CAPTOR 将冻结图像编码器的 patch token 聚类为 visual words，用带 caption 图像中各词的 patch 占比统计词—词表签名，从而在无文本编码器的情况下得到可用于 grounding 的 term-by-patch 打分矩阵，并在 COCO、VOC、Cityscapes 与病理切片上超过免训练基线。

**相关性简评：** 其「词—visual word 表 × 与查询无关的图像因子」提供了一个免训练的 patch 级证据打分图，可能可供核查 LVR 中按术语筛选视觉证据或做空间归因的线索，尤其适用于缺少语言对齐的领域编码器；但论文本身不涉及 latent 推理状态或停止决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CAPTOR: text-encoder-free grounding by counting caption co-occurrence over the visual words of a frozen encoder

**abstract/A1：** Grounding a text query in an image needs a matrix scoring every term against every patch. Vision-language models compute it with an aligned text encoder, which many image encoders, such as histopathology-related models trained on slides alone, lack. The Caption-Anchored Patch-Term Occurrence Representation (CAPTOR) estimates the matrix without a text encoder. It clusters a frozen image encoder's patch tokens into visual words and describes each captioned image by its share of patches in each word. A term signature is the standardized difference of those shares between images whose captions mention the term and the rest. The matrix factorizes into a term-by-word table and a query-independent image factor, so an image is encoded once and a new term adds one table row. Against every training-free baseline, CAPTOR leads on Common Objects in Context (COCO), Pascal Visual Object Classes (VOC) and Cityscapes. On COCO, scored on the cells one object owns, it reaches 0.565 mean intersection over union (mIoU) against 0.502. On all three, it is also level with a text alignment trained on the same backbone, which needs gradients, a text encoder and several times the captions. On held-out breast-cancer slides, it exceeds the strongest baseline scoring patches with a pathology text encoder by 0.160 mIoU. Read through CAPTOR, UNI, an encoder trained on slides alone, exceeds such baselines by 0.132 on a common grid. Counting can therefore give a text interface to any image encoder trained without language, wherever captioned images exist.

</details>

<a id="paper-56"></a>

### 56. [On the Generalization Limitation of On-Policy Distillation for Post-RL Consolidation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05609v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05609

**作者：** Yichen Wang、Yufa Zhou、Siqi Zhu、Haozheng Luo、Jingzhi Bao、Zhichao Liu、Zijun Wei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05609v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05609v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察多教师 on-policy 蒸馏（MOPD）在 RL 后能力整合中的泛化局限：用 Qwen3 在数学推理、代码、agent 与长上下文任务上评测外推和组合 OOD，发现 IND 分数可达教师的 96%–103%，而 OOD 反而低于基座，并将教师能力与数据覆盖视为瓶颈，进而分析更新几何并提出几何补丁与基座保留回放。

**相关性简评：** 属纯文本训练侧工作，但「蒸馏易在分布外丢失基座能力」与更新几何、基座回放的做法，可供核查 LVR/VLM 推理能力训练中监督分配与泛化保持的代价。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Generalization Limitation of On-Policy Distillation for Post-RL Consolidation

**abstract/A1：** Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) extends on-policy distillation (OPD) to consolidate capabilities from multiple teachers. Although prior work reports near-perfect in-distribution (IND) distillation performance, we find that this can mask incomplete capability consolidation beyond the supervised distribution. A preliminary study on synthetic reasoning shows that OPD does not fully transfer teachers' generalization capabilities. We then extend this investigation to real-world tasks, evaluating extrapolative and compositional generalization across mathematical reasoning, coding, agent, and long-context capabilities using Qwen3 models. Across extrapolative settings, OPD and MOPD reach 96.04-102.73% of their teachers' IND scores. Yet their out-of-distribution (OOD) scores remain 2.45-16.25% below the base model's. Across three compositional settings, MOPD improves IND performance over the base model, but its performance on composed tasks as OOD remains 18.19-24.34% below that of the best teachers. To understand these gaps, we examine teacher capability and data coverage as two potential bottlenecks. Jointly alleviating these bottlenecks eliminates OOD forgetting in our experiments and suggests the potential for generalization beyond the teacher. (M)OPD also reduces OOD forgetting on average relative to its reinforcement learning (RL) baselines. Analysis of the associated update geometry reveals smaller and more spectrally concentrated updates on average than those of the RL baselines, along with greater shared update structure under MOPD. Motivated by these findings, we propose geometry-based patching and base-preserving replay to improve OOD performance while largely preserving IND performance. Our findings identify a blind spot in capability consolidation via (M)OPD, highlighting the need for algorithms that explicitly support OOD retention and generalization.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages, 12 figures. Project page: https://yichenzw.com/opd-generalization.github.io/

</details>

<a id="paper-57"></a>

### 57. [Hybrid Spark: Adaptive Speculative Decoding That Lets the Content Choose the Drafter](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05611v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05611

**作者：** Ekagra Ranjan、Bharat Venkitesh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05611v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05611v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Hybrid Spark 框架，在每一步同时运行基于模型与无模型的 drafter，并以「每个待验证 draft token 在 batch 其他位置可换得的机会收益」作为统一价格，配合校准后的接受率估计，为每条请求挑选更优的草稿链且保持异步调度。

**相关性简评：** 其按 token 估计接受概率并据此分配验证预算、把计算机会成本显式定价的思路，可能为 LVR 中动态计算分配与「STOP」式验证权衡提供可核查线索；但论文本身面向纯文本 LLM 服务吞吐，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态，迁移可行性需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hybrid Spark: Adaptive Speculative Decoding That Lets the Content Choose the Drafter

**abstract/A1：** Speculative decoding (SD) speeds up large language model (LLM) serving, but its gain depends on the workload's \\emph{shape}, such as batch size and context length, and on its \\emph{content}. A model-based drafter proposes a short block on any content, while a model-free drafter such as n-gram lookup proposes long copies at almost no cost when the output repeats its input, as in code editing. Adaptive verification spends a batch-wide verification budget on the draft tokens most likely to be accepted, but it supports only one model-based drafter. Model-free drafters provide no per-token confidence, so they cannot take part in budget allocation, and deployments must either fix one drafter or split their GPUs between drafters. We present \\sys, a framework that runs a model-based and a model-free drafter on every step and gives each request the better of their chains, within adaptive verification and entirely on the GPU, which keeps asynchronous scheduling intact. The key idea is a price per verified draft token: the goodput that slot would earn elsewhere in the batch. Once the model-free drafter's tokens carry calibrated acceptance estimates, fit once on general text, this price puts both drafters' chains on one scale. Instantiated with \\dspark and an n-gram drafter in vLLM, \\sys achieves 1.10--1.62\\x the output throughput of \\dspark on code editing at batch size~4 and 1.04--1.27\\x at batch size~128, while staying at 0.97--1.01\\x of it on chat at temperature~0. A single deployment can therefore serve both workloads.

**comment/C1：** Speculative Decoding

</details>

<a id="paper-58"></a>

### 58. [LACS: Distribution-Sensitive Label-Agnostic Confidence Selection for Dynamic Data Pruning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05616v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05616

**作者：** Heejin Choi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05616v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05616v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** LACS 提出一种训练期动态数据剪枝规则：以最大预测置信度的分布相对阈值（log-mean-exp 参考加 margin）同时自适应调整阈值与保留比例，无需标签和逐样本梯度，并分析其分数敏感性、无剪枝条件，在神经网络、语言建模与 boosting 等七个设定上评估计算节省。

**相关性简评：** 其按分数分布自适应确定保留量、无需标签的选择规则，可供核查是否用于 LVR 训练轨迹或中间推理步的筛选与计算分配；论文强调置信度高既可能是对也可能是自信错误，这一点也与「用置信度做证据筛选或停止决策是否可靠」相关，但直接联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LACS: Distribution-Sensitive Label-Agnostic Confidence Selection for Dynamic Data Pruning

**abstract/A1：** Training on large datasets motivates selecting which examples receive expensive training computation. Fixed quotas determine the amount of pruning in advance, while absolute confidence cutoffs depend on the score scale. We study a population-relative alternative that adapts the threshold and retained count together. We introduce LACS (Label-Agnostic Confidence Selector), a training-time rule based on maximum predictive confidence, requiring neither labels at selection time nor per-example gradients. LACS removes examples at or above a log-mean-exp reference plus a margin. We characterize its sensitivity to score gaps and mass, and derive an exact no-pruning condition. For $0&lt;ρ&lt;1$, the removed fraction is at most $ρ$ and at least one example remains. The unsigned score permits both confidently correct and confidently wrong examples to be removed. Seven evaluation settings include neural training, language modeling, and boosting. Neural execution studies reduce backward-active rows by 10-35%; on CIFAR-10, 29% fewer backward rows accompany accuracy means of 89.77% versus 89.82% for full CE. At a matched backward budget and number of optimizer updates, LACS is 1.0 pp above small-batch training and 1.2 pp above random selection over five seeds. Across 14 tabular datasets, quadratic boosting reduces mean test error from 23.36% to 22.96%. Its full training replays use about 30% fewer fitting rows; these counters span a different horizon from validation-selected accuracy. In one RBF-SVM case study, elapsed time falls by 37% with slightly lower error. These findings separate selective-work reduction from realized acceleration: the current exact-current-score neural implementation uses an additional scoring forward, so its backward sparsity does not yet translate into wall-clock acceleration. This overhead is an execution choice rather than a requirement of the selection rule.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-59"></a>

### 59. [Route to Remember: Adaptive Memory Selection for LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05636v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05636

**作者：** Zeyu Fang、Jun Yin、Yongsheng Mei、Xu Han、Chaitali Joshi、Zhongkai Sun、Jie Hao、Bin Guo、Chengyuan Ma、Benjamin Z. Yao、Chenlei Guo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05636v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05636v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究如何用 on-policy self-distillation 把用户事实类记忆内化进模型权重（weight memory），并训练 verify-then-regenerate 路由器在权重记忆与文本检索记忆之间分配查询；在 HaluMem 上以更少读取 token 达到或超过文本记忆基线，并分析不同训练配方的影响。

**相关性简评：** 其「内部参数记忆直接作答 vs 转向外部检索」的路由与验证机制，可能为 VLM 中凭内部状态作答还是再取视觉证据的 LOOK/STOP 决策提供可供核查的线索，无上下文学生的内化效果也可参照 latent 信息坍缩问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Route to Remember: Adaptive Memory Selection for LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** The memory of production agents is increasingly becoming a bottleneck and is built almost exclusively from text: key points are extracted from out-of-limit context and retrieved into context at query time. Therefore, every new query incurs an additional cost that grows with the accumulated history. Weight memory is a complementary alternative that internalizes knowledge into model parameters and answers with no context at all, and has recently proven effective in self-evolving agents for internalizing skills and behaviors. However, its application in episodic and semantic memory, such as user-specific facts, remains comparatively unexplored, and a gap remains between earlier attempts at weight memory and text-based systems regarding recall performance. We study how to make weight memory competitive as a long-term factual store by applying on-policy self-distillation (OPSD). It trains a context-free student from a teacher, which is the same model conditioned on the user's memory, together with a verify-then-regenerate router that lets weight and text memory each cover the queries they handle best. On the HaluMem benchmark, OPSD matches or surpasses strong text-memory baselines, including full-context prompting and retrieval systems, while reading fewer tokens per query. We further conduct an extensive study that characterizes the behavior of weight memory and analyzes how different training recipes affect its performance. As agent harnesses move toward persistent, multi-session deployments, these results position weight memory as a practical, low-cost component of agent memory and provide insights into feasible training and evaluation protocols for combining it with retrieval.

</details>

<a id="paper-60"></a>

### 60. [Quantifying Trait-State Hierarchy in EEG Foundation Model and Signal Processing Embeddings](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05652v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05652

**作者：** Anusha Madapura、Aditya Kommineni、Shrikanth Narayanan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05652v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05652v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作在 EEG 数据集上用层级度量与 kNN 纯度量化信号处理与基础模型嵌入中的「特质—状态」层级，发现多数嵌入呈宏观特质层级、损害状态可分性，并以归一化削弱特质信息使状态分类平衡准确率提升约 10%，且微调会使嵌入空间转为宏观状态层级。

**相关性简评：** 其层级度量和去特质归一化提供了诊断潜表示中任务相关状态与个体/干扰信息混杂的具体手段，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 内容筛选、表示坍缩与线性可读性问题；但属 EEG/BCI 领域，跨到视觉多模态推理的适用性需验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Quantifying Trait-State Hierarchy in EEG Foundation Model and Signal Processing Embeddings

**abstract/A1：** Electroencephalography (EEG) measures brain activity through recording electrical potentials of the scalp. EEG recordings capture two distinct sets of characteristics in the signal: (1) \\emph{Traits} -- information that remains relatively constant over the course of a recording session, such as subject identity, age, and sex; (2) \\emph{States} -- signatures that encode responses to external stimuli, including visual, auditory, and motor information. There is limited understanding of how different EEG modeling features (signal processing and foundation models) encode state and trait characteristics. In this work, we quantify state- and trait-level features captured by EEG embeddings using a hierarchy metric and kNN purity on Brain-Computer Interface dataset trials, and find that a majority of EEG features form a macro-trait hierarchy (embeddings form trait-level clusters with micro-states within). Our linear probe results indicate that this hierarchy structure leads to suboptimal state distinguishability, negatively impacting BCI performance of EEG foundation models. To mitigate this, we explore normalization techniques that reduce trait-specific information in embeddings and provide a $\\sim$ 10\\% improvement in balanced accuracy for state classification. Additionally, we observe that when fine-tuning models for downstream state decoding, embedding spaces transform from macro-trait to macro-state hierarchies, which in turn lead to better state decoding.

</details>

<a id="paper-61"></a>

### 61. [When Are Two SAE Latents the Same Feature?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05655v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05655

**作者：** Alexandra Suvorikova、Ilgam Latypov、Anton Trukhan、Yuriy Dorn

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05655v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05655v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一个能量加权最优传输框架，在指定相似度几何下连接 SAE 特征对应、字典级差异度量及其按 latent 的贡献分解，并给出考虑有限数据的渐近推断与 bootstrap 同时置信带。

**相关性简评：** 其 decoder、激活与一阶因果三种相似度及传输分解，可能为判定 LVR 中两个 latent 状态是否「同一」并量化差异来源提供可核查的度量与不确定性工具；但该文针对纯文本 SAE 特征匹配，与视觉推理仅有间接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Are Two SAE Latents the Same Feature?

**abstract/A1：** Comparing sparse autoencoders (SAEs) requires connecting feature correspondence, differences between whole dictionaries, and uncertainty from finite evaluation data. We develop a framework that connects these tasks through an energy-weighted optimal-transport plan under a specified similarity geometry. The same plan defines a dictionary-level discrepancy, decomposes it into latent contributions, and quantifies how each latent's mass is distributed across counterparts. We characterize relationships between decoder, activation, and first-order causal similarities; idealized models clarify the interpretation of splitting and absorption. For fixed trained SAEs, we derive joint asymptotic inference for transport costs and fractions, accounting for activation similarities and both energy marginals estimated from the same documents. A joint document bootstrap gives asymptotically valid simultaneous confidence bands. Experiments across four language models locate cross-seed discrepancy and separate context-dependent changes.

</details>

<a id="paper-62"></a>

### 62. [Revisiting Critic Estimation in Soft Actor-Critic Algorithms](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05666v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05666

**作者：** Jan Felix Kleuker、Koen Ponse、Aske Plaat、Thomas M. Moerland

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05666v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05666v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在 SAC 中重新考察三种 critic 设计选择（soft advantage 替代 soft Q、multi-step 回报目标、从回放数据训练 multi-step critic），在 14 个任务上以整轮超参搜索比较六种变体，发现仅在回放轨迹片段上训练 λ-return critic 能稳定提升性能，且无需重要性加权或 trace cutting。

**相关性简评：** 其「无修正的 multi-step 目标 + 轨迹片段回放」可用于核查 LVR 中多步推理轨迹的贡献分配与监督方式，但仅在 RL 值估计中验证，向 latent 推理的迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Revisiting Critic Estimation in Soft Actor-Critic Algorithms

**abstract/A1：** Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) is a widely used deep reinforcement learning algorithm that combines entropy regularization with an actor-critic architecture. Standard implementations rely on one-step temporal-difference learning of Q-functions, yet alternative critic design choices remain underexplored. In this work, we revisit three such choices: learning soft advantages instead of soft Q-values, the use of multi-step return targets, and the training of multi-step critics from replay buffer data. Keeping the policy objective fixed, we compare the six critic variants these choices generate: (i) advantage and (ii) dueling-advantage critics learned from one-step transitions, and $λ$-return and soft Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE) critics learned either (iii, iv) on-policy or (v, vi) from replayed trajectory segments. Each variant is evaluated across an entire random hyperparameter search on fourteen tasks, treating the hyperparameters as part of the algorithm. Learning soft advantages instead of soft Q-values does not improve performance, and the soft GAE critic adds nothing over the simpler $λ$-return critic. What does improve performance, most clearly on continuous control, is training a $λ$-return critic on replayed trajectory segments, even without importance weighting or trace cutting. This extends earlier evidence that uncorrected multi-step targets work well under replay to the full soft $λ$-return, entropy terms included. By design, the underlying ideas studied here extend beyond SAC, and our empirical findings aim to encourage their application to a broader class of reinforcement learning algorithms.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-63"></a>

### 63. [Toward an Optimal Configuration for Tree Species Classification from Bark Images: A Leakage-Safe Ablation Study](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05680v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05680

**作者：** Maeve Côté

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05680v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05680v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 BarkNet 1.0 树皮图像上以 ConvNeXt-V2 做系统性消融，考察 patch 大小、patch 预测聚合方式、单/两阶段训练、骨干容量、整图基线与推理时 patch 数量对树种分类精度的影响，并给出推荐配置。

**相关性简评：** 其「patch 级预测如何聚合为图像级判断」的学习式聚合与多数投票对比，以及推理 patch 数增加后收益饱和的现象，可能为 LVR 中局部视觉证据的汇总方式与证据获取/停止决策提供可核查线索，但任务本身与多模态推理相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Toward an Optimal Configuration for Tree Species Classification from Bark Images: A Leakage-Safe Ablation Study

**abstract/A1：** Background: The automatic identification of tree species from bark photographs is of practical interest for forestry inventory and the wood-processing industry, yet the design choices that govern the accuracy of deep-learning approaches have not been studied systematically, and some prior results may be inflated by leakage of images of the same trunk across training and test partitions. Methods: A systematic ablation study is conducted on the public BarkNet 1.0 dataset (20 species) under the established tree-level, leakage-safe partitioning scheme. Using ConvNeXt-V2 backbones, the following factors are varied in turn and evaluated by five-fold cross-validation: patch size, the strategy by which per-patch predictions are aggregated into an image-level decision, the training regime (single- versus two-stage), backbone capacity, the whole-image baseline, and the number of patches available at inference. Results: Per-patch accuracy rises monotonically with patch size (from 75.6% at 160 px to 89.6% at 512 px), and image-level accuracy rises in step, plateauing near 94.9% around 512 px. Learned aggregation exceeds majority voting by at most 0.7 pp, an advantage that shrinks as patches grow and attention becomes near-uniform. Backbone capacity (pico to tiny) alters accuracy by less than a point, and two-stage training offers no advantage over one-stage. Whole-image classification (91.9%) trails patch-based aggregation, since it admits no bag to average over; accuracy increases with the number of patches at inference, saturating around 64. Conclusions: The study yields a simple recommended configuration: patches as large as possible while leaving enough to average over (around 512 px here), with a nano backbone. It also quantifies the second-order role of learned aggregation and model capacity, providing a reproducible reference point for practitioners and future research

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-64"></a>

### 64. [TRUST-Stereo: Teacher-Assisted Robust Uncertainty-Guided Search and Transfer for Fast Stereo Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05689v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05689

**作者：** Pouya Sohrabipour、Yihong Feng、Chaitanya Kumar Reddy Pallerla、Tuan Dang、Ngan Le、Dongyi Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05689v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05689v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TRUST-Stereo 快速立体匹配方法：用不确定性引导残差搜索模块（UGRSM）估计传播视差的可靠性并预测残差偏移以重定位局部代价体中心，另用两级师生蒸馏把大基础模型的单目表示迁移到轻量骨干，在多个基准上取得较强零样本泛化与约 50 ms 推理。

**相关性简评：** 其「先评估传播状态可靠性、再预测修正量以重定位局部搜索范围」的粗到细迭代机制，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态误差累积的纠正与视觉证据的局部重筛选提供可核查的方法线索，但立体匹配任务与多模态推理相距较远，迁移价值需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TRUST-Stereo: Teacher-Assisted Robust Uncertainty-Guided Search and Transfer for Fast Stereo Matching

**abstract/A1：** Coarse-to-fine stereo matching enables efficient disparity estimation but remains sensitive to errors in propagated disparity, which may place ground-truth correspondences outside subsequent local search ranges. We propose TRUST-Stereo (Teacher-Assisted Robust Uncertainty-Guided Search and Transfer for Fast Stereo Matching), which addresses this limitation through an Uncertainty-Guided Residual Search Module (UGRSM). UGRSM estimates the reliability of propagated disparity and predicts residual offsets to adaptively relocate local cost-volume centers. Additionally, we introduce a two-stage Senior-Junior Teacher Assistant knowledge distillation strategy to transfer rich monocular representations from a large foundation model to a lightweight backbone to achieve fast stereo matching performance. Experiments on KITTI 2012, KITTI 2015, ETH3D, and Middlebury demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization. Using only 1.4M synthetic stereo pairs, TRUST-Stereo achieves state-of-the-art results on several benchmark metrics, outperforming competing efficient methods trained with substantially larger datasets, including real-world pseudo-labeled samples. Specifically, it achieves a KITTI 2015 EPE of 0.93, ETH3D Bad 1.0 of 2.13% and Middlebury Bad 2.0 of 6.79% while maintaining inference at approximately 50 ms per stereo pair.

</details>

<a id="paper-65"></a>

### 65. [Anchored Slice Propagation with Boundary Transport for 3D CT Segmentation from Three Orthogonal Annotated Slices](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05693v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05693

**作者：** Tushar Kataria、Md Rakibul Haque、Shireen Y. Elhabian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05693v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05693v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向仅有三个正交切面标注的3D CT分割，用固定预训练亲和力骨干做切片间掩码传播：在传播链与另一标注平面相交处用已知标签校正预测（anchoring），并据其与这些标签的一致性加权融合多条链，同时比较SDF与轮廓两种边界几何传输方式。

**相关性简评：** 其「传播链漂移校正、按与已知标注的一致性为多链分配可信度、以及用边界距离弥补二值掩码的信息丢失」可能为LVR的多条latent推理轨迹纠错、融合加权与几何细节保留提供可供核查的线索，但与VLM推理无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Anchored Slice Propagation with Boundary Transport for 3D CT Segmentation from Three Orthogonal Annotated Slices

**abstract/A1：** Sli2Vol propagates a binary mask through affinities between adjacent slices, but an error can persist because each prediction becomes the input to the next step. Three orthogonal annotations reduce dependence on any one chain, yet they offer more than three starting masks: where a chain crosses another annotated plane, the correct label is already known. Those labels could correct drift during propagation and indicate how much to trust each chain at fusion. Between annotated planes, the information carried by the chain may matter as well. A binary mask records foreground membership but discards distance to the boundary, a cue that could help follow gradual shape changes between slices.We address these two sources of error with a fixed pretrained affinity backbone. We anchor each chain by correcting its prediction where it crosses another annotated plane, then use its agreement with those labels to weight fusion. We compare this anchored mask with two ways of transporting boundary geometry: a truncated signed-distance field (SDF) propagated by dense affinity averaging, and a contour whose vertices follow motion cues from the same affinity. The contour tracker starts from a hand-crafted rule and learns a motion correction from supervoxel pseudo-objects in 506 CT volumes, without manual organ labels. On 2,091 held-out structure instances from approximately 403 CT scans spanning 17 abdominal structures, anchoring raises mean Dice from 0.737 with orthogonal mask propagation to 0.769 and improves every structure. Beyond the anchored mask, learned contour tracking improves Dice by 5.7\\% for the esophagus and 5.9\\% for the postcava, while SDF transport improves right-adrenal Dice by 2.8\\%. The anchored methods have similar mean Dice (0.769--0.772); contour tracking performs worse on the kidneys, stomach, and hepatic vessel tree.

</details>

<a id="paper-66"></a>

### 66. [GeoDIVE: Geospatial Descent Informed by Value Estimation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05697v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05697

**作者：** Hamza Iqbal、Dan Cher、Eric Xing、Pranavi Kolouju、Abby Stylianou、Robert Pless、Nathan Jacobs

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05697v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05697v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** GeoDIVE 把多分辨率卫星影像库组织成树，用 best-first 遍历并由轻量模型对子树中隐藏的最优查询相似度做乐观估计，从而决定编码哪些瓦片；在图像与开放词表文本查询下以约 10% 编码成本达到穷举检索效果，并发布 Git-10M 上的多尺度检索查询。

**相关性简评：** 其用可学习价值估计引导分层遍历、分配编码预算的做法，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与 LOOK/计算分配提供方法线索；但对象是外部图库检索而非 VLM 内部推理状态，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GeoDIVE: Geospatial Descent Informed by Value Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Finding specific objects, structures, and spatial patterns supports critical work in agriculture, infrastructure planning, environmental monitoring, and disaster response. These features, however, are often dispersed across very large regions and span dramatically different spatial scales, which defines a search space that is costly to embed, maintain, and search naively. We introduce GeoDIVE, a model that treats a multiresolution satellite image gallery as a forest and uses best-first traversal to determine which tiles to encode. At each visited node, a lightweight model then predicts an optimistic estimate of the best query similarity hidden among its descendants using the query and subtree representations. In evaluations with image and open-vocabulary text queries, GeoDIVE matches exhaustive search at 10% of the encoding cost for image queries and leads all tested baselines at every budget in both modalities. To support future evaluation of multiscale geospatial search, we also release object-level image and text queries for Git-10M, constructed from Microsoft building footprints and OpenStreetMap polygons with VLM-generated captions. Source code and models will be made publicly available.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027. Includes supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-67"></a>

### 67. [CoReFlow: Consistency-Relaxed Flow Maps for Probabilistic Traffic Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05715v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05715

**作者：** Pengnan Chi、Zhiguo Zhang、Xiaoliang Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05715v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05715v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向概率交通预测，提出 CoReFlow 模型，利用 flow map 作为概率流 ODE 的解算子实现一步生成，并用有限时间区间插值端点的简洁目标进行训练；相比自蒸馏、MeanFlow Identity 等保持轨迹一致性的做法，改为以条件均值限制一致性，在四个真实数据集上取得更高精度并降低推理成本。

**相关性简评：** 其「放宽轨迹一致性、以条件均值约束换取少步/一步生成」的训练目标，可能为 diffusion 或 parallel 类 latent 推理状态的生成与更新、以及在推理步数与精度间取舍提供可供核查的线索；但论文场景是交通预测，未涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoReFlow: Consistency-Relaxed Flow Maps for Probabilistic Traffic Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Probabilistic traffic forecasting is essential for reliable Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS), as it captures uncertainties in complex road networks. Recent diffusion- and flow-based generative models have facilitated uncertainty modeling through a multi-step sampling process. However, beyond slow inference, this process is designed to capture complex multimodal distributions, which misaligns with the limited complexity of the conditional traffic data distribution, causing models to underperform deterministic baselines. To address this, we introduce the \\underline{Co}nsistency-\\underline{Re}laxed \\underline{Flow} Map (CoReFlow) model, presenting the first effort to leverage the one-step generation capability of flow maps, known as solution operators of probability-flow ordinary differential equations. Besides, we improve accuracy using a concise training objective that supervises the model via interpolation endpoints over finite time intervals. Unlike existing methods (e.g., self-distillation and MeanFlow Identity) that maintain trajectory consistency to model multimodal distributions, our objective leverages conditional mean to restrict the consistency, thereby achieving higher accuracy in the probabilistic traffic forecasting task. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets show CoReFlow achieves state-of-the-art forecasting accuracy. It surpasses recent baselines, provides competitive uncertainty modeling, and reduces inference costs compared to existing generative baselines.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-68"></a>

### 68. [Give the Drafter a Second Chance to Guess: Parallel Reconsideration for Speculative Decoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05724v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05724

**作者：** Hoang Anh Duy Le、Huibing Dong、Alireza Khadem、Ziteng Sun、Xiang Song、Yizheng Huang、Soroush Radpour、Anshumali Shrivastava、Ye He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05724v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05724v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对投机解码中 drafter 单次前向对所有 token 一律给出草稿的问题，该方法依据 drafter 自身不确定性，用与草稿并行运行的额外前向重新猜测其犹豫的 token，并以配套训练配方与服务期算法把重猜结果并入候选树，提升接受长度与加速比。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本推理加速工作，不涉及视觉或多模态 latent 状态；但其「不确定性触发的并行额外计算＋候选树合并＋接受验证」机制，可供核查是否能迁移为 LVR 中按不确定度分配计算、并对候选推理轨迹做投机验证的思路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Give the Drafter a Second Chance to Guess: Parallel Reconsideration for Speculative Decoding

**abstract/A1：** Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference with a small drafter, whose per-guess acceptance rate determines the achievable speedup. Feature-level drafters share a design choice: every token, easy or hard, is drafted under a single forward pass. Many of the tokens the target rejects are ones the drafter is itself unsure about yet the target answers decisively. To address this, we introduce Parallel Reconsideration: guided by its own uncertainty, the drafter reconsiders its hesitant guesses with extra passes that run in parallel with the one-pass draft, adding negligible latency. A co-designed training recipe and serving-time drafting algorithm append these reconsidered guesses to the original draft tree as refined candidates. Across models and benchmarks, on average, Parallel Reconsideration improves over the baselines by 5.3% to 10.2% on acceptance length and by 3.5% to 9.2% on speedup.

</details>

<a id="paper-69"></a>

### 69. [Mitigating Spurious Invariance in Contrastive Learning: A Probabilistic Self-Supervised Learning Framework for Medical Image Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05734v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05734

**作者：** Wei Wei、Yading Yuan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05734v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05734v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对医学图像对比学习中的虚假不变性问题——数据增强会抑制细微但关键的医学特征——将特征嵌入建模为概率分布而非固定向量，并提出统一多种自监督方法的概率框架。

**相关性简评：** 以分布替代单点向量来承载特征，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态如何避免细节与任务必要信息坍缩、以及用不确定性表示推理状态提供线索，但该文无多模态推理实验，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Mitigating Spurious Invariance in Contrastive Learning: A Probabilistic Self-Supervised Learning Framework for Medical Image Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Owing to the prohibitive cost of manual annotation for enormous medical images, self-supervised learning (SSL) has gained substantial attention and shown promise in various medical imaging tasks. Among SSL approaches, contrastive learning has emerged as a prominent one, encouraging models to encode semantic information that remains invariant between different augmented views. However, this invariance can be spurious for medical images, as subtle but critical medical features can be suppressed during augmentations. In this paper, we mitigate this issue by encoding the learned feature embeddings as probability distributions rather than fixed vectors, thus capturing a richer and more nuanced spectrum of information. Furthermore, we propose a unified probabilistic framework that seamlessly integrates multiple SSL approaches. Our method achieves superior performance over the state-of-the-art SSL methods on various downstream tasks across multiple datasets, including medical image segmentation and classification. We further validate the method through additional analyses of robustness to stronger augmentations, label efficiency under limited fine-tuning labels, and computational trade-offs. The code is released at https://github.com/wwzjer/ProbSSL.

**comment/C1：** Published in IEEE Transactions on Medical Imaging (TMI), 2026. 17 pages

</details>
