### 70. [Self-Supervised Learning of Universal Latent Spaces for Operator Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05735v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05735

**作者：** Sepehr Mousavi、Levi Lingsch、Siddhartha Mishra、Laura De Lorenzis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05735v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05735v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在合成解析函数上无标签预训练一个函数自编码器，得到对任意点云可用的、对平移/缩放/幅度精确等变的latent空间，冻结后跨PDE与多类物理任务复用，并在缓存嵌入上训练神经算子。

**相关性简评：** 其冻结的通用latent空间、一次性编码缓存与latent rollout，可供核查LVR中预训练潜空间工作区、状态更新与推理计算复用的问题；但领域相隔较远，等变与测地插值结构能否迁移到视觉推理仍属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Self-Supervised Learning of Universal Latent Spaces for Operator Learning

**abstract/A1：** Neural operators fit their representation of functions to the task they are trained on, so every new task rebuilds a reduced space from labeled data. Following the self-supervised pre-training that made representations shareable in natural language processing and computer vision, we decouple representation learning from operator learning. A functional autoencoder is pre-trained on analytic functions synthesized on the fly, with no solver and no labels, to represent functions on arbitrary point clouds, and the latent space it induces is frozen and reused across tasks. The representation is exactly equivariant to translation, dilation, and amplitude, and reconstructs solutions of partial differential equations (PDEs) and natural images on geometries it never saw. Being task-agnostic, the latent space can be interpreted in its own right. The whole space is used and decodes to valid fields, it preserves the ordering of distances between physical fields, its geodesics interpolate physical evolutions, and edit directions transfer across fields. With frozen representation maps, a dataset is encoded once and a neural operator is trained entirely on cached embeddings, which saves memory and training time. On 28 tasks in fluid dynamics, solid mechanics, acoustics, and the Helmholtz and Poisson equations, this paradigm can exceed the accuracy of state-of-the-art end-to-end architectures, with up to an order of magnitude higher throughput and memory savings that fit models nearly two orders of magnitude larger on consumer-grade GPUs. At inference, latent rollouts run up to an order of magnitude faster than regular rollouts, and chunked inference processes functions of over half a billion points on a single consumer-grade GPU.

</details>

<a id="paper-71"></a>

### 71. [Learning Hierarchical Patch Splitting Policies for Faster Vision Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05742v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05742

**作者：** Aditya Gupta、Jean Sebastien Dandurand、Kai Qiu、Rohan Choudhury、Laszlo A. Jeni

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05742v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05742v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SPLIT，用学习到的分层 patch 切分策略，依据模型判定的任务相关信息分配 token 分辨率，在减少约 30% token 的情况下保持分类与稠密预测精度。

**相关性简评：** 其按语义相关性分配视觉计算可能为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选与动态计算预算提供线索，但属于输入 tokenization 而非 latent 状态生成，需核查细粒度证据是否被牺牲。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Hierarchical Patch Splitting Policies for Faster Vision Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Vision Transformers (ViTs) are bottlenecked by the length of their input token sequence. Standard patchification fixes this length with a single patch size applied across the image, regardless of regional content. Prior work on adaptive patch sizing reduces token count by assigning coarser patches to visually simple regions, but relies on noisy pixel-level heuristics such as local entropy to make patchification decisions. We argue that this compute allocation should be grounded in the model's interpretation of task relevant information, not in raw pixel statistics. We introduce Split Policy Learned Image Tokenization which learns to allocate token resolution based on what the model finds meaningful, producing shorter token sequences that are better matched to task-relevant content. SPLIT matches the accuracy of full-resolution ViT classification baselines while using 30% fewer tokens. The same gains hold on dense prediction: SPLIT achieves competitive accuracy on COCO and ADE20K, demonstrating that semantically aligned patchification generalises well beyond classification.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-72"></a>

### 72. [Optimizing Recourse Under Predictive Multiplicity](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05774v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05774

**作者：** Shahin Jabbari

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05774v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05774v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究预测多重性（predictive multiplicity）下算法追索的稳健性，将跨竞争模型集合的最坏情况有效性损失表述为预算约束下的最小化问题，提出 oracle-efficient 算法 ORPM 并给出收敛条件，在多类模型组成的竞争集合上评测，并量化要求共同动作对成功率及群体差异的影响。

**相关性简评：** 其「竞争模型集合下预算约束的最坏情况优化」及不同模型对同一样本判断分歧的刻画，可能为多 latent 假设/多解码器聚合与有限计算预算下的假设筛选提供可核查的优化与评价线索，但领域相距较远，需人工判断可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Optimizing Recourse Under Predictive Multiplicity

**abstract/A1：** Algorithmic recourse identifies modifications that can change an unfavorable model prediction into a favorable one. Under predictive multiplicity, similarly performing models can disagree on individual predictions, so recourse valid for one model may fail for another. We formulate robust recourse under predictive multiplicity as a budget-constrained minimization of the worst-case validity loss across a competing set of models, which can include models with different architectures or from different hypothesis classes. We propose an oracle-efficient algorithm called ORPM and prove sufficient conditions for its convergence. We evaluate ORPM on one synthetic and five real datasets, and competing sets consist of linear, neural, mixed, and decision-tree models. Our experiments show decreasing optimization-gap bounds and high validity in many tested settings, with favorable performance against baselines designed for specific model classes or architectures. We also quantify how requiring a common action across competing models changes recourse success at a given budget and examine disparities in this success across demographic groups.

</details>

<a id="paper-73"></a>

### 73. [FlyHash for LLM Agent Memory: An Empirical Validation and a New Application](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05791v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05791

**作者：** Mohan Bheemadolu、Chrisogonas O. Odhiambo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05791v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05791v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把果蝇嗅觉回路的 FlyHash（稀疏随机投影加 winner-take-all）用于 LLM agent 的外部记忆检索，在匹配比特数的协议下检验其邻域保持优势，并进一步比较延迟与内存开销、以及真实记忆相关性标签下的表现（结论显示实用收益远窄于质量结论）。

**相关性简评：** 其「稀疏投影 + WTA」哈希编码与比特级相似度成本核算，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据检索和 latent 相似度缓存、获取成本的设计提供核查线索；但任务面向文本 agent 记忆，与多模态推理状态的距离需较多判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FlyHash for LLM Agent Memory: An Empirical Validation and a New Application

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external memory stores to retain information across conversations, typically via dense vector embeddings searched with approximate nearest-neighbor methods. The fruit fly's olfactory circuit computes a locality-sensitive hash of sensory input using a strikingly different mechanism - sparse random projection followed by winner-take-all competition - and prior work (Dasgupta et al., 2017) showed this scheme, dubbed FlyHash, preserves neighbor structure better per bit than conventional random-hyperplane hashing (SimHash) on several benchmarks. We apply FlyHash, to our knowledge for the first time, to LLM agent memory retrieval, and ask three questions a practitioner actually needs answered: does the quality advantage hold under a correctly matched-bit protocol (it does, robustly, across 10 seeds and four datasets spanning two modalities, with one specific, previously undocumented comparison pitfall identified and corrected along the way); does that advantage translate into real memory and latency savings, including against the dense-vector indexing (HNSW) practitioners default to today (no: at equal bits FlyHash's codes are larger and slower to compare than SimHash's, and HNSW dominates both hash methods at ordinary scale; a sparse-set comparison we introduce recovers most but not all of a naive latency gap, leaving hashing's practical case narrower and more specific than the quality result alone implies); and does the advantage survive genuine, non-circular agent-memory relevance labels rather than the nearest-neighbor-by-embedding proxy this literature otherwise relies on (it does). We report all three answers precisely, including the two that narrow rather than flatter the headline result, and release our implementation, a working agent-memory demo, and a full account of the methodological pitfalls we found and corrected.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages. Code, data and interactive demo: https://github.com/mohanji/flyhash-agent-memory (archived at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23903302)

</details>

<a id="paper-74"></a>

### 74. [ReTailor: A Residual Feature Tailoring Framework for Real-Time Cross-Sensor Pansharpening Adaptation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05811v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05811

**作者：** Jing-Ze Liang、Wei-Jie Xiao、Zi-Han Cao、Yang-Dong Li、Tian-Yi Hu、Liang-Jian Deng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05811v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05811v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ReTailor，一种面向跨传感器全色锐化的实时序贯自适应框架：仅优化轻量残差 Feature Tailor，在自监督物理约束下纠正传感器偏移，并结合内容感知 patch 选择与跨图像流的滚动残差自举。

**相关性简评：** 任务本身是图像融合，与 VLM 推理无直接关系；但「少量信息量 patch 参与优化」「滚动自举跨样本继承状态」「轻量残差模块不动原有先验」可供核查其作为视觉证据筛选与在线 latent 状态更新的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReTailor: A Residual Feature Tailoring Framework for Real-Time Cross-Sensor Pansharpening Adaptation

**abstract/A1：** Recent pansharpening models achieve strong fusion performance on trained sensors, yet often generalize poorly to unseen sensors. Existing cross-sensor adaptation is limited by target-sensor retraining, which requires target-sensor training pairs and a dedicated training stage, and by per-image zero-shot adaptation, which is computationally expensive due to whole-network optimization for each test image. To address these problems, we propose ReTailor, a real-time sequential cross-sensor adaptation framework. Only a lightweight residual Feature Tailor is rapidly optimized under self-supervised, physics-aware constraints to correct cross-sensor shifts without modifying the well-learned fusion prior. For efficient and effective deployment, ReTailor combines content-aware patch selection, which identifies a few informative patches per image for optimization at negligible cost to improve both quality and efficiency, with rolling residual bootstrap, which inherits sensor-related knowledge across the image stream to further improve quality. Extensive experiments on cross-sensor adaptation settings show that ReTailor mitigates cross-sensor degradation both effectively and efficiently. Moreover, we compare ReTailor with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), a widely used method for fine-tuning, to demonstrate the advantages of our approach. We further evaluate the core principle of ReTailor on two additional remote sensing tasks: super-resolution pansharpening and image segmentation, where it consistently improves performance. In general, ReTailor provides a highly effective and efficient strategy for deploying pansharpening models under real-world cross-sensor degradation, while also offering useful insights into managing cross-sensor degradation in other remote sensing tasks. Code will be released at https://github.com/JingZe-Liang/ReTailor .

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 12 figures, including appendices. The first three authors contributed equally

</details>

<a id="paper-75"></a>

### 75. [Pathwise-Budgeted LLM Routing: Online Allocation with K-Bit Tail-Cost Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05835v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05835

**作者：** Hanzhang Qin、Sophie H. Yu、Yuewei Zhang、Zhi-Hai Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05835v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05835v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向固定预算下的 LLM 调用问题，提出上下文 bandit 策略 ADP-HT，用分位数网格上的粗粒度长度标签构建保守成本估计，并权衡预测成功率与成本以决定调用哪个模型及是否重试。

**相关性简评：** 其「成本事后才揭示、按剩余预算调惩罚」的在线决策框架，可供核查是否迁移到 LVR 中继续推理或重试的 STOP 成本建模与预算分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Pathwise-Budgeted LLM Routing: Online Allocation with K-Bit Tail-Cost Learning

**abstract/A1：** Firms using large language models under fixed budgets must decide which model to call and whether to retry a failed task. Because a call's charge is revealed only after completion, one long response can exhaust the remaining budget and leave later tasks unserved. Our objective is to maximize the number of tasks solved before service stops. We develop a contextual bandit policy, ADP-HT, which learns each model's task-specific success probability and constructs conservative cost estimates from coarsened response-length labels on a quantile grid with $2^K$ intervals. The policy weighs predicted success against these costs, adjusting the cost penalty to realized spending and the budget available per remaining task. Under calibration, local regularity, and grid-coverage conditions, we bound the excess allowance in the policy's conservative cost estimates. At a fixed sample size, increasing $K$ reduces the discretization term but increases the estimation allowance. At the tightest tested budgets, ADP-HT solves more than twice as many MATH500 and GPQA tasks as the best implemented baseline. It achieves the highest mean cumulative score at ten of fourteen RouterBench budgets. On LiveCodeBench, it outperforms a deployed commercial router in both default and cost-saving modes at the three lowest test budgets.

</details>

<a id="paper-76"></a>

### 76. [PEARL: Prediction Error-Adaptive Regularized Learning for Selective Forgetting under Partial Preference Drift](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05842v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05842

**作者：** Rashmi Gangadharaiah、Balakrishnan Narayanaswamy

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05842v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05842v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究部分偏好漂移下的非平稳情境 bandit，提出 PEARL：在预测显著错误的特征方向上反转 Gram 更新以实现选择性遗忘，并在 LLM 响应选择基准上验证其优于均匀遗忘与神经 bandit 基线。

**相关性简评：** 按预测误差定向重置内部置信、保留仍有效维度的机制，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的选择性更新与陈旧视觉证据剔除提供方法线索，但需核查其向推理状态表示的迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PEARL: Prediction Error-Adaptive Regularized Learning for Selective Forgetting under Partial Preference Drift

**abstract/A1：** User preferences shift as needs, context, and tastes evolve. This matters for personalized recommendation, LLM response generation, and RLHF-trained reward models, which can become miscalibrated as preferences drift. Existing non-stationary bandit methods forget uniformly across all dimensions, but real preference shifts are typically partial: some dimensions go stale while others remain valid. Neural contextual bandits accumulate confidence monotonically via the Gram matrix, with no mechanism to direct uncertainty where predictions have gone wrong. We propose PEARL (Prediction Error-Adaptive Regularized Learning), which reverses the Gram update along feature directions where predictions are surprisingly wrong, while leaving confidence intact elsewhere. We prove that (i) any non-adaptive policy suffers linear regret after a preference shift, (ii) no fixed exploration rate achieves low regret in both stationary and non-stationary regimes, and (iii) PEARL's cumulative regret scales with the number of shifted users N\_s, not the total population N. On PRISM, a personalized LLM response selection benchmark (1,500 users, 21 LLMs), PEARL beats greedy by +4.0 points and the best neural bandit baseline by +2.5 points (p&lt;0.001), while performing on par with greedy when preferences are stable. It beats sliding-window and discounted approaches by 3 to 10 points, showing that selective forgetting outperforms uniform forgetting. The selection policy is a ~100K-parameter MLP that runs in milliseconds on CPU, leaves LLM weights frozen, and works with any API-based model.

**comment/C1：** 36 pages, 6 figures, 10 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-77"></a>

### 77. [On Convergence of Gradient Temporal-Difference Learning with Regularized Corrections and Eligibility Traces](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05869v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05869

**作者：** Jingyi Jin、Martha White、Hao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05869v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05869v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文为带正则化修正的梯度 TD 学习建立收敛理论：在递减步长、非 i.i.d. 离策略数据下证明约束 TDRC(λ)（含状态依赖与历史依赖 λ）收敛，并提出 TDRC(λ)-Huber，保持原不动点同时降低对大 Bellman 误差的敏感性。

**相关性简评：** 离策略、多步 eligibility trace 下的收敛与稳定性条件，可能为 latent 推理多步轨迹训练中的贡献分配与监督稳定性提供可核查的理论线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On Convergence of Gradient Temporal-Difference Learning with Regularized Corrections and Eligibility Traces

**abstract/A1：** Temporal-difference (TD) learning underlies many prediction and control algorithms, but can diverge when combined with off-policy learning, bootstrapping, and function approximation. TD with Regularized Corrections (TDRC) is theoretically sound and practically simple, but existing convergence theory covers only the one-step case under i.i.d. sampling, while the Huber Bellman-error formulation has not been extended to TDRC or eligibility traces. In this work, we establish convergence of constrained TDRC($λ$) with diminishing stepsizes and non-i.i.d. off-policy data, for state-dependent and history-dependent $λ$-parameters. We also propose TDRC($λ$)-Huber, which preserves the TDRC($λ$) fixed point while reducing sensitivity to large Bellman errors. For both algorithms, we apply an ODE-based weak-convergence method from stochastic approximation theory. For the nonlinear mean dynamics of TDRC($λ$)-Huber, we give an integral small-gain condition that guarantees uniqueness and global asymptotic stability of the target equilibrium. We then establish convergence of the constrained algorithm, derive computable sufficient conditions for convergence, and identify a regularization-based convergence mechanism shared by linear TDRC($λ$) and TDRC($λ$)-Huber. When the mean TD matrix is positive stable, both constrained algorithms converge under sufficiently strong regularization without requiring positive definiteness.

**comment/C1：** 66 pages, 1 figure. Submitted to the Journal of Machine Learning Research

</details>

<a id="paper-78"></a>

### 78. [RECAP: Context-Aware Prediction Recycling with Request-Local and Persistent Memories for Multimodal Speculative Decoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05894v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05894

**作者：** Hengjie Zhu、Dayan Wu、Xinze Liu、Peng Fu、Zheng Lin、Weiping Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05894v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05894v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RECAP：把目标模型预测按一、二、三 token 键存储，请求内上下文记忆隐式绑定当前图像、token 级记忆跨请求保留覆盖，再用熵-置信加权融合检索结果，经 best-first 搜索组成候选树并由一次目标前向在贪心解码下验证，实现免训练的多模态推测解码加速。

**相关性简评：** 其「按前缀长度区分图像敏感解码状态、并跨请求缓存预测」的键粒度与融合加权，可能为 latent 状态缓存、相似度检索及 THINK/STOP 额外计算成本核算提供可核查线索，但本身不研究视觉推理中的 latent 状态生成与使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RECAP: Context-Aware Prediction Recycling with Request-Local and Persistent Memories for Multimodal Speculative Decoding

**abstract/A1：** Recycling a target model's own predictions offers a training-free source of proposals for speculative decoding. In vision-language models, however, existing recycling indexes predictions by the last token alone, conflating predictions computed under different images and prefixes; we find that such reuse fails most often at image-sensitive decoding states. Longer keys reduce this aliasing but match fewer states. We introduce \\method, which stores target predictions under one-, two-, and three-token keys: contextual memories live only for the current request and are thus implicitly conditioned on its image, while token-level memory persists across requests to retain coverage. At each lookup, entropy-confidence weighting fuses the retrieved predictions according to their key specificity and concentration. Best-first search composes the fused transitions into a candidate tree, supplemented by suffix-matched continuations, and one target pass verifies the tree under greedy decoding. Without training or an auxiliary draft model, \\method\\ achieves mean speedups over autoregressive decoding of $1.66\\speed$ and $1.56\\speed$ across seven single-turn benchmarks on Qwen2.5-VL-7B and LLaVA-1.6-7B, exceeding the strongest training-free baseline, Token Recycling ($1.51\\speed$ and $1.37\\speed$). Our implementation is available in https://github.com/Senmo996/RECAP-official-code.

</details>

<a id="paper-79"></a>

### 79. [EchoKV: Self-Corrective KV Cache Eviction via Iterative Query Feedback](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05903v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05903

**作者：** JianKun Zou、Tongtong Yuan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05903v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05903v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** EchoKV 指出 KV cache 驱逐中「工作区」与驱逐查询相互耦合，早期工作区误差会导致后续查询与驱逐决策被扭曲；它用未来位置提议初始化工作区，并借早期解码查询做迭代修正。

**相关性简评：** 其「保留的状态影响后续查询、进而影响证据选择」的闭环失败模式，可能与 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与状态更新耦合、误差自增强的问题同构，可供核查；但对象是 KV 驱逐而非 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EchoKV: Self-Corrective KV Cache Eviction via Iterative Query Feedback

**abstract/A1：** KV cache eviction is not merely a token-ranking problem: the workspace retained for generating eviction queries also determines the quality of those queries. Consequently, an early workspace error can distort later query observations and reinforce an incorrect final eviction decision, particularly when evidence is dispersed across a long context. We present EchoKV, which discovers and addresses this failure mode with iterative correction. It initializes a feedback-compatible bootstrap workspace using proposals at future decode positions, then uses early decode-generated queries to recover the workspace before the remaining lookahead steps. It uses the same number of workspace forward passes as LAQ while adding a tensor re-scoring operation for workspace repair. On LongBench (16 tasks, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2 and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct), EchoKV shows notable improvements over LAQ on several tasks requiring dispersed evidence, including multi-hop cross-document QA and long single-document comprehension. Across budgets and backbones, EchoKV selectively improves evidence-intensive tasks while maintaining comparable performance on the remaining LongBench subsets. These results identify query--workspace coupling as a practical failure mode of query-driven KV eviction.

</details>

<a id="paper-80"></a>

### 80. [DecoupAttn: Decoupled Sparse Attention for Training-Free Acceleration of Video Diffusion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05917v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05917

**作者：** Yanjin Li、Bingbing Qiu、Qiang Huang、Rui Qian、Yanfeng Li、Jian Wang、Xiaoying Tang、Mingda Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05917v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05917v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DecoupAttn：一种免训练的稀疏注意力加速框架，用语义聚类补偿全部 key，并仅保留少量位置固定的精确块纠错；配合 Distortion Budget 在时间步、层、头间分配精确算力，以及 Reassign Cache 逐步重分配漂移的 key。

**相关性简评：** 其「缓存簇/掩码过期引起跨步误差累积」与「按失真在各层各步分配精确计算」的机制，可能可供核查多步 latent 推理中的状态缓存刷新、注意力稀疏结构与计算分配；但对象是视频扩散加速，迁移可行性需判读。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DecoupAttn: Decoupled Sparse Attention for Training-Free Acceleration of Video Diffusion

**abstract/A1：** Sparse attention accelerates few-step video diffusion transformers by computing a few key blocks exactly and compensating for the rest. However, existing sparse attention still faces key challenges, including identification overhead from finding critical blocks at every denoising step, suboptimal budget allocation that sets sparsity budgets uniformly or by local metrics, and cross-step error accumulation as reused masks or clusters go stale. Observing that the errors left on skipped blocks cancel each other once every key is compensated, we propose DecoupAttn, a new paradigm of decoupled sparse attention: semantic clusters compensate every key, and exact attention corrects their error on a few blocks fixed once offline by position. To support this paradigm, we introduce Distortion Budget, a rate-distortion objective for budget allocation that places exact computation across timesteps, layers, and heads where it removes the most distortion that compensation leaves in the final latent. Built on this budget, we further propose Reassign Cache, in which each step reassigns the drifting keys to cached centroids and refreshes compensation while the budgeted blocks stay fixed. Moreover, DecoupAttn is training-free: a few calibration clips compile one plan offline, and inference runs standard block-sparse kernels with one fused compensation kernel. Our code will be released after submission.

**comment/C1：** 35 pages, 16 figures, 35 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-81"></a>

### 81. [Split-KV: Efficient Context Parallelism for Training Diffusion Drafters](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05926v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05926

**作者：** Geng Zhang、Fangzhao Zhang、Cong Xie、Tiantian Fan、Yang You

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05926v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05926v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 diffusion drafter 训练中「query 短、上下文长」的非对称性，提出 Split-KV 上下文并行方法：KV 分片本地保存，每层注意力通信只涉及 query 规模张量、与上下文长度无关，并用按实际注意力代价的 LPT 策略均衡 KV 分块。

**相关性简评：** 其 block-sparse 跨注意力中少量 query 读取长 KV 的通信压缩与负载划分线索，可能可供核查 LVR 里少量 latent 查询长视觉证据时的缓存与并行设计；但该工作属文本投机解码训练系统，非视觉推理研究。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Split-KV: Efficient Context Parallelism for Training Diffusion Drafters

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion drafters accelerate speculative decoding by denoising multiple draft tokens in parallel. Their training uses anchor sampling to construct compact draft queries and performs cross-attention with a block-sparse mask over a much longer context. We empirically show that acceptance length remains stable as context scales with a fixed query budget, while increasing the query budget yields diminishing quality gains, jointly establishing a practical regime with queries far shorter than the context. No existing context parallelism (CP) method jointly exploits this query--context asymmetry and meets the nonlinear, sample-dependent load-balancing requirement created by anchor sampling. We present Split-KV, an exact CP method that keeps key/value shards local and limits per-layer attention communication to query-sized tensors, making this communication independent of context length. Its global query view further enables a cost-aware longest-processing-time-first (LPT) strategy that balances context key/value chunks by their realized attention cost. At a CP size of 64, Split-KV retains 85--91\\% weak-scaling efficiency and achieves up to $3.67\\times$ speedup over the fastest available CP baseline. LPT achieves $&lt;$1\\% imbalance and plans three orders of magnitude faster than the best load-balancing baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/zxgx/verl.

</details>

<a id="paper-82"></a>

### 82. [Modality-Aware Expert Pruning for MoE-Based Multimodal Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05927v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05927

**作者：** Donggeun Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05927v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05927v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出无训练的模态感知专家剪枝框架 MAEP，将 MoE-MLLM 中专家重要性按视觉与语言模态分解，用 ΔH 度量移除专家后的输出变化，并以低注意力视觉 token 作为视觉侧剪枝信号，从而压缩专家权重。

**相关性简评：** 其按模态分解的专家级贡献归因与「视觉侧信号可作为裁剪依据」的做法，或可供核查 VLM 内部视觉信息究竟由哪些专家承载、是否存在视觉旁路；但论文目标仅为压缩与显存，未涉及 latent 推理状态或推理期计算分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Modality-Aware Expert Pruning for MoE-Based Multimodal Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) enables multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to scale capacity efficiently, but expert parameters dominate memory even though only a few experts are activated per token: they account for over 90% of model size in modern MoE-MLLMs (e.g., 54 GB out of about 58 GB in BF16 for InternVL3.5-30B-A3B and Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B). Expert pruning has emerged as an effective compression strategy, but existing methods aggregate importance uniformly across tokens, ignoring that the same expert may serve different roles for vision versus language processing. To address this, we propose MAEP (Modality-Aware Expert Pruning), a training-free framework that decomposes expert importance by modality in a single forward pass. MAEP introduces (1) Delta-H Output ($ΔH$), a redistribution-aware metric measuring the MoE output change when an expert is removed, and (2) low-attention vision tokens as a vision-side pruning signal that is dataset-stable: its expert-importance ranking remains stable across different datasets. Text-side importance serves as a preservation signal while vision-side importance serves as a pruning signal, and experts are ranked globally across layers. Experiments on three MoE-based MLLMs across multimodal and text benchmarks show that MAEP outperforms modality-agnostic baselines on the majority of multimodal benchmarks while remaining competitive on text-only tasks, reducing the BF16 expert weight footprint by about 27 GB (about 47% of total memory) so that the full weight footprint of an approximately 58 GB model fits within a single 40 GB GPU. Code is available at https://github.com/nota-github/MAEP.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-83"></a>

### 83. [Reward Budgeting Reduces Premature Convergence in Reinforcement Learning for LLM Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05942v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05942

**作者：** Mengni Jia、Mengyu Zhou、Xiaoxi Jiang、Guanjun Jiang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05942v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05942v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文分析 LLM 推理 RL 中策略分布过早锐化导致的性能平台，提出 sharpness factor κ 与 reward budgeting：为每个 prompt 分配固定总奖励预算的 advantage 构造，并在数学推理基准上提升多种 RL 基线。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本 RL 研究，不涉及视觉或 latent 状态；但其奖励预算与 advantage 缩放可能为 LVR 轨迹训练中的贡献分配、抑制策略过早收敛提供线索，迁移到多模态 latent 推理尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reward Budgeting Reduces Premature Convergence in Reinforcement Learning for LLM Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** RL for LLM reasoning often reaches an early performance plateau as the policy distribution becomes increasingly sharp. Recent work has studied policy entropy as both a diagnostic and a control signal for this behavior. In our experiments, however, increasing entropy alone does not reliably change the eventual plateau. We analyze RL through its implicit target policy and derive a sharpness factor $κ$, together with an effective counterpart $κ\_{\\mathrm{eff}}$, that describes how strongly reward or advantage gaps are translated into probability-ratio gaps. Motivated by this view, we introduce \\emph{reward budgeting}, a plug-in advantage construction that assigns a fixed total reward budget to each prompt. For binary rewards and independent on-policy rollouts, its unclipped score-function estimator follows the gradient of an exact finite-$G$ concave objective with diminishing returns, while its advantage gap decreases as more rollouts become correct. For general rewards, a shifted log-mean interpretation holds asymptotically under the stated regularity conditions. These results motivate, rather than establish exact objective equivalence for, the clipped implementations. On Qwen3-8B and Llama3.2-3B-Instruct, reward budgeting consistently improves a range of RL baselines, with gains up to +12.50 on AMC23 and +10.00 on AIME24 and AIME25. Overall, our results support an update-dynamics view of premature convergence and show that strong gains can be obtained without explicitly preserving entropy.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-84"></a>

### 84. [FLINT: Coupling Proximal Initialization and Bounded Stochasticity for Flow-Matching Inverse Problems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05945v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05945

**作者：** Junseo Bang、Daewon Choi、Dong Ju Mun、Se Young Chun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05945v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05945v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 flow-matching 逆问题的后验采样，推导将初始噪声接近度与累积采样随机性耦合的稳定性界，并据此提出两阶段框架 FLINT：先用少量迭代的流形约束近端目标获得高保真起点，再在有界随机性调度下进行引导采样。

**相关性简评：** 其「近端初始化＋有界累积随机性」的稳定性分析可供核查是否适用于 LVR 迭代 latent 状态：初始状态质量与后续随机注入预算如何共同决定细节保留或被侵蚀，但原文属成像逆问题，与多模态推理无直接联系，迁移性需较多验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FLINT: Coupling Proximal Initialization and Bounded Stochasticity for Flow-Matching Inverse Problems

**abstract/A1：** Flow matching models have emerged as a dominant paradigm for generative posterior sampling in imaging inverse problems. Existing solvers follow two distinct lines of work: trajectory-space methods, which refine sampling dynamics from random initial noise, and source-space methods, which optimize the initial noise itself. While most prior work treats these as independent design choices, we show that they are inherently coupled. We derive a unified stability bound that links initialization proximity and cumulative sampling stochasticity through a multiplicative envelope, complemented by two empirical observations. First, we identify a sufficiency regime in which a moderately optimized proximal source initialization is enough to attain the performance plateau, rendering exhaustive source-space optimization unnecessary. Second, this initial advantage is fragile: it is eroded by excessive stochasticity accumulation during guided sampling, necessitating a mechanism that bounds the cumulative noise injection. These findings motivate FLINT, a two-stage framework that operationalizes this coupling. FLINT establishes a high-fidelity starting point via a manifold-constrained proximal objective in only a few iterations, then performs guided sampling under a bounded stochasticity schedule that helps prevent erasure of the initialization advantage. Across diverse inverse problems, FLINT delivers state of the art perceptual quality and competitive distortion metrics while maintaining superior computational efficiency.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026, Project Page: https://junseo88.github.io/FLINT/

</details>

<a id="paper-85"></a>

### 85. [CLEAR: Color-Texture Latent Alignment for Single Image Reflection Removal](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05969v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05969

**作者：** Tu Vo、Sheir A. Zaheer、Chan Y. Park

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05969v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05969v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** CLEAR 研究单图玻璃反光去除，以预训练 latent diffusion 骨干作自然图像先验，构建确定性单步非迭代框架：物理引导的分离器一次前向输出透射、反光与残差三层的粗 latent，再由混合特征对齐模块（HFAM）把输入 skip 视为受污染的层混合特征，用与干净透射对齐的 color query 校正其高频内容。

**相关性简评：** 其「输入高频特征即受污染混合特征」的假设与用干净目标对齐查询做注意力式整流的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、旁路注入有害细节的抑制提供可供核查的方法线索，但论文本身不涉及多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CLEAR: Color-Texture Latent Alignment for Single Image Reflection Removal

**abstract/A1：** Single Image Reflection Removal (SIRR) seeks to recover the transmitted scene from a photograph captured through glass. Recent latent restoration methods use pretrained generative priors, but many recover detail by feeding high-frequency features from the degraded input back into the decoder. This works for degradations such as blur, noise, or haze, where input detail mostly belongs to the scene. It is less suitable for SIRR, where reflections add a second structured layer: directly reusing the input's skip connections can sharpen the image while also bringing reflection edges back. Latent compression also introduces color drift and fine-detail loss. We propose CLEAR (Color-Texture LatEnt Alignment for Single Image Reflection Removal), a deterministic, single-timestep, non-iterative latent framework that uses a pretrained latent diffusion backbone as a natural-image prior. A physics-guided separator organizes the prediction around the transmission, reflection, and residue formation model and outputs coarse layer latents in one forward pass. The Hybrid Feature Alignment Module (HFAM) then treats input-derived skips as contaminated layer-mixture features. It rectifies their high-frequency content with a clean-transmission-aligned color query, learned from the input features themselves under ground-truth feature supervision, keeping transmission-consistent texture while attenuating reflection-colored detail. CLEAR achieves the best average across fidelity and perceptual metrics on six real-world benchmarks and the lowest LPIPS on every dataset, with the largest gains on the hardest benchmarks.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-86"></a>

### 86. [On the Design of Hyper-Connections: Simplification, Stability, and Scalability](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05984v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05984

**作者：** Xiangdong Zhang、Xiaohan Qin、Sunan Zou、Tuo Dai、Bizhao Shi、Zhuo Xia、Yebin Yang、Yizhou Chen、Yuliang Liu、Yu Cheng、Junchi Yan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05984v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05984v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 系统拆解 Hyper-Connections 架构，发现「无 mixing 的学习式读写门控」与「固定门控的无约束 mixing」各自即可达到 mHC 水平，并指出对扩展残差状态的重复访问是主要训练开销，据此提出去掉残差 mixing、改用共享内存访问与延迟写的 SimpleHC，在 15B–30B MoE 上保持可比性能且额外训练时间仅 2.4%。

**相关性简评：** 多流残差状态与读写门控决定内部状态如何跨层携带和更新，其对重复状态访问的成本拆解可能为 LVR 的 latent 状态更新结构与计算开销核算提供核查线索；但属纯文本架构研究，未涉及视觉证据或推理期 latent 生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Design of Hyper-Connections: Simplification, Stability, and Scalability

**abstract/A1：** Hyper-Connections (HC) architectures are increasingly adopted in frontier language models, yet their components remain insufficiently understood and introduce non-negligible training overhead. In this work, we systematically study the HC architecture to understand its components and identify simpler, more efficient designs. We find that learned read/write gating without mixing and unconstrained mixing with fixed gates form two effective interaction groups: either alone achieves performance comparable to mHC. We also identify repeated access to the expanded residual state as a major source of training overhead, even without mixing. These findings lead to SimpleHC, which simplifies the architecture by removing residual mixing and simplifies execution through shared memory access, using the resulting additive structure and shared gate inputs. Layer-specific gates share a group-entry state, enabling batched reads and deferred writes; scalar corrections exactly preserve preceding sublayer output contributions. Our analyses support both the retained residual structure and the design of shared memory access. SimpleHC incurs only 2.4% extra end-to-end training time over a vanilla residual baseline at the largest tested scale. It maintains performance comparable to mHC when scaling from 15B to 30B MoE models, exhibits smoother gradient dynamics during standard training, and remains stable under stress. We hope SimpleHC will encourage a broader pursuit of simplicity in foundation model design, making performance gains more accessible and practical at scale.

**comment/C1：** Code: https://github.com/aHapBean/SimpleHC

</details>

<a id="paper-87"></a>

### 87. [A Comparative Study of Importance Attribution to Columns in Deep Learning over Relational Databases](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05986v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05986

**作者：** Hila Levi-Yosefi、Shunit Agmon、Ester Livshits、Agapi Rissaki、Benny Kimelfeld

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DB

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05986v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05986v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文系统比较关系数据库深度学习模型的列级重要性归因方法（Monte Carlo Shapley、Kernel SHAP、单属性影响、mask learning），从属性排序与分数分布两方面分析差异，并考察其与 fidelity 的关系。

**相关性简评：** 其「单属性扰动/mask learning 可低成本替代 Shapley」的比较框架，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据或 latent 状态的贡献分配与廉价归因监督提供核查线索，但对象是表格属性而非多模态输入，迁移有效性待验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Comparative Study of Importance Attribution to Columns in Deep Learning over Relational Databases

**abstract/A1：** Deep learning over relational databases has led to increasingly effective prediction models, motivating a growing need to explain their behavior. We study explanation methods that assign importance scores to individual database attributes, focusing on a fundamental question: to what extent does the choice of importance-attribution method matter? We develop a methodology to systematically compare importance-attribution methods and analyze their similarities and differences. In particular, we study two aspects of the explanations: which attributes they deem important, captured by differences in attribute rankings, and how importance scores vary across attributes, captured by differences in score distributions. We further investigate whether these differences are reflected in the conventional measure of explanation quality, fidelity. We apply our methodology in an extensive empirical study of four methods: Monte Carlo Shapley, Kernel SHAP, single-attribute impact, and mask learning. We experiment with multiple datasets and tasks using two RDL architectures: heterogeneous GraphSAGE and Relational Graph Transformers. Our experiments show that explanation methods behave differently depending on the prediction type: regression or classification. In regression, most methods exhibit high agreement in attribute rankings, whereas classification yields considerably less consensus. Notably, while mask learning frequently diverges in its importance distributions, Shapley-based methods and single-attribute impact often converge to similar explanations. This suggests that, in many settings, single-attribute impact can replace Shapley at substantially lower computational cost. Overall, our study provides methodological tools for comparing importance-attribution methods, and empirical evidence that their choice has important consequences for interpreting relational deep learning models.

</details>

<a id="paper-88"></a>

### 88. [Decouple to Reuse: Fine-Grained Knowledge Augmentation for Time Series Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05993v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05993

**作者：** Yuqian Wu、Qingxiang Liu、Yuxin Guo、Weifeng Su、Jian Li、Pengfei Xu、Xi Chen、Yutao Yue、Yuxuan Liang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05993v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05993v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文针对时间序列基础模型的域偏移问题，提出两阶段细粒度知识增强框架：先用双分支残差码本分别解耦趋势与季节模式，再以任务自适应聚合和门控融合将检索到的细粒度模式动态注入冻结的模型表示。

**相关性简评：** 其「解耦检索＋门控融合注入冻结骨干」的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据的细粒度筛选与写入内部状态的机制提供核查线索，但领域相距较远，需确认门控是否真正按样本选择性使用检索内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Decouple to Reuse: Fine-Grained Knowledge Augmentation for Time Series Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Time series forecasting is critical for demand prediction and resource management in modern Web services, yet applying pretrained Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) to downstream tasks remains challenging due to domain shifts. Existing retrieval-augmented approaches typically retrieve coarse-grained window contexts and may introduce context-specific noise. To address this problem, we argue that temporal patterns should be reused at a decomposition level, as effective trend and seasonal dynamics may originate from different contexts. We propose \\method, a two-stage \\underline{F}ine-grained \\underline{K}nowledge \\underline{A}ugmentation framework that decouples and consolidates pre-training knowledge into compact, task-shared residual codebooks to enhance frozen TSFMs. Specifically, Stage I constructs dual-branch residual codebooks for decoupled trend and seasonal signals, while Stage II dynamically retrieves and merges these fine-grained patterns into target representations via task-adaptive aggregation and gate fusion. Extensive experiments across eight real-world benchmarks with four representative TSFM backbones demonstrate the superior effectiveness and computational efficiency of \\method, while further validating the strong transferability of the residual codebooks across different backbones. The source code is available at the anonymous repository: https://github.com/yuppielqx/TSFM-Component-Token-Adapter

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 10 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-89"></a>

### 89. [FedIA: Instance-Aware Cross-Modal Representations for Federated Prompt Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05994v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05994

**作者：** Xuanjing Zhang、Qi Guo、Yanan Qiao、Yong Han、Junge Bo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05994v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05994v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FedIA 联邦提示学习框架：以全局文本提示作为类别语义锚点，为每个候选类筛选相关图像 patch，并据此条件生成实例级局部文本表示，再通过自适应全局-局部融合按图像-类别对校准两者贡献。

**相关性简评：** 其类别条件化的视觉证据筛选与输入级自适应融合，可能为 LVR 中「视觉证据筛选」和 latent 贡献分配提供线索；但该方法面向联邦参数高效微调、无推理期状态生成，需核查其选择机制能否迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FedIA: Instance-Aware Cross-Modal Representations for Federated Prompt Learning

**abstract/A1：** Federated Prompt Learning (FPL) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of pre-trained Vision-Language Models across decentralized clients without exchanging raw data. To cope with cross-client heterogeneity, existing FPL methods typically pair a globally shared prompt with a client-specific local prompt. However, this design personalizes only at the client level: the same local prompt is applied to all samples of a client, and the global and local prompts are combined in an input-agnostic, fixed manner. Consequently, it overlooks the substantial visual variations among samples within the same client and cannot adjust the balance between shared and personalized knowledge to each input. To address these limitations, we propose Federated Prompt Learning with Instance-Aware Cross-Modal Representations (FedIA), an FPL framework that learns instance-aware cross-modal representations. Specifically, FedIA first uses global text prompts as client-invariant semantic anchors to select image patches most relevant to each candidate class, suppressing distracting regions. The selected visual evidence then conditions the generation of instance-aware local text representations. Finally, an adaptive global-local fusion module calibrates the contributions of local and global text representations for each image-class pair. In this way, FedIA shifts personalization in FPL from the client level to the instance level while preserving the semantic knowledge shared across clients. Extensive experiments under domain shift, pathological label skew, and Dirichlet-based label skew show that FedIA outperforms FPL baselines, with maximum accuracy gains of 1.44\\%-4.37\\% over the latest baseline across the three settings. Ablation studies and analyses further verify the complementary contributions of instance-aware representation generation and adaptive global-local fusion.

</details>

<a id="paper-90"></a>

### 90. [How Far Can You Steer? Behavior-Capability Frontiers in Contrastive Activation Addition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06001v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06001

**作者：** Deepanshu Raj

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06001v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06001v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文围绕对比激活引导（CAA）研究行为收益与能力损伤的权衡，提出“行为—能力前沿”框架，在四个指令微调模型、七种行为和 1%/3%/5% 能力损失预算下筛选干预，并检验行为效应泛化、表示几何对跨行为迁移的预测力以及未测能力的副作用。

**相关性简评：** 把内部激活方向的施加视为有明确代价预算的约束型干预，并用表示几何预测因果迁移，这可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态干预的收益—成本刻画与表示相似度解读，但尚未涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Far Can You Steer? Behavior-Capability Frontiers in Contrastive Activation Addition

**abstract/A1：** Activation steering can modify language-model behavior without parameter updates, but behavioral efficacy alone does not determine whether an intervention is usable. Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA) steers targeted behaviors using activation directions derived from contrasting examples, potentially degrading unrelated model capabilities in the process. We therefore study CAA through a behavior-capability frontier, selecting interventions that maximize behavioral gain subject to an explicit bound on capability degradation. We evaluate this framework across four instruction-tuned models, seven behaviors, and capability-loss budgets of 1%, 3%, and 5% (absolute percentage points). Validation-selected behavioral effects generalize strongly to held-out data ($ρ= 0.945$), while joint behavior-capability survival among nonzero operating points rises from 37.5% at the 1% budget to 72.7% at 3%, with limited additional improvement at 5%. Control experiments indicate that these effects depend on learned behavior directions rather than generic activation perturbations. Representational geometry is significantly but only modestly predictive of causal cross-behavior transfer. Finally, secondary capability, out-of-distribution, and open-ended evaluations show that interventions satisfying protected benchmarks can still degrade untested capabilities or transfer unevenly to free-form generation. These results characterize activation steering as a constrained intervention whose usable range depends on model, behavior, direction, layer, and evaluation budget, rather than a universal control knob.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 18 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-91"></a>

### 91. [ChanSFormer: A Channel Agnostic Vision Transformer for Multi-Channel Cell Painting Images](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06015v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06015

**作者：** Jingwei Zhang、Srinivasan Sivanandan、Dimitris Samaras

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06015v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06015v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 ChanSFormer，用解耦的空间—通道注意力和独立的通道 CLS token 替代可学习通道嵌入，使 ViT 在未知通道配置下仍能同时保持通道身份与跨通道信息流，并在多通道生物成像与遥感数据上评测分类与迁移性能。

**相关性简评：** 其「每通道独立 token + 解耦通道/空间注意力」的结构化表示可能为 LVR 中异构视觉证据拆分、避免多源细节在内部状态中坍缩提供可核查的架构线索，但本文本身不涉及推理或 latent 状态生成，迁移性需另行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ChanSFormer: A Channel Agnostic Vision Transformer for Multi-Channel Cell Painting Images

**abstract/A1：** High-content multi-channel imaging, from Cell Painting assays to remote sensing, is central to modern scientific pipelines. Because these channels are highly heterogeneous and experimental configurations frequently evolve, a practical vision backbone must be fundamentally channel-agnostic. Current channel-adaptive vision transformers rely on global self-attention with learnable channel embeddings, which can be computed only for previously known channels. Attempts to overcome this limitation of new channels either discard the embeddings, losing channel identity, or process each channel independently, thereby eliminating critical cross-channel interactions. To address this, we introduce ChanSFormer, an efficient Vision Transformer that replaces rigid embeddings with disentangled spatial-channel attention and individual channel CLS tokens. This architecture natively preserves both distinct channel identities and cross-channel information flow. Furthermore, the individual channel class token design unlocks more informative feature representations for self-supervised learning and enables representation-based channel sampling while remaining channel-agnostic. We evaluate ChanSFormer on two biology multi-channel datasets, CHAMMI, JUMP-CP and a satellite dataset, So2Sat. Experimental results show that ChanSFormer outperforms state-of-the-art methods by 3.93%-4.27% on JUMP-CP in classification accuracy. It also demonstrates exceptional cross-dataset transferability under a self-supervised setting, outperforming previous methods by up to 2.72% in accuracy in the same classification tasks. Furthermore, the disentangled attention reduces the quadratic complexity of channel-by-spatial sequence length of the global attention baseline, thus improving throughput by 55%-313%. Code is available at https://github.com/cvlab-stonybrook/ChanSFormer.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-92"></a>

### 92. [USI-Brain: Enhancing Underwater Spatial Intelligence via Spatial Data-Driven Vision-Language Modeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06040v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06040

**作者：** Zhiliang Zhu、Zhiqiang Zhao、Xie'an Jiang、Yingxin Lai、Kangye Zheng、Dawei Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06040v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06040v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建水下空间数据引擎，通过图像增强、2D与几何辅助问答生成及专家过滤得到4.27M QA，用于训练USI-Brain，并提出以门控方式修正视觉特征的水下先验引导整流器UPR，同时发布USI-Bench评测空间能力。

**相关性简评：** UPR对受散射、色偏破坏的视觉特征做门控修正，可能为「任务必要的几何与视觉细节能否保留在内部状态」提供视觉证据校正线索，但论文本身不涉及latent推理状态的生成，具体效果与机制可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** USI-Brain: Enhancing Underwater Spatial Intelligence via Spatial Data-Driven Vision-Language Modeling

**abstract/A1：** Underwater spatial reasoning depends on object boundaries and geometric cues that are obscured by scattering, color distortion, and low visibility. Existing underwater resources capture these cues through images, annotations, and depth maps, but provide fragmented supervision for vision-language models. We present a spatial data engine that connects visual appearance with object-level geometry through enhancement, 2D and geometry-assisted question generation, and expert filtering. It produces 4.27M QA pairs across five capability groups. We use this corpus to train USI-Brain and introduce an Underwater Prior-guided Rectifier (UPR) that applies gated corrections to visual features. USI-Bench evaluates the resulting capabilities with 6,525 held-out QA pairs. On its 15 task metrics, USI-Brain and USI-Brain + UPR improve over Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct by 5.76 and 7.63 points on average, respectively, using signed differences on 100-point scales. Ablations show that geometry-assisted supervision improves depth and ordering by 4.8 and 6.1 points with UPR disabled, while UPR adds 18.0 points in scene description under matched training data. Both variants use raw images at evaluation. Across NautData, UWBench, and MarineEval, USI-Brain achieves the best or joint-best score on 28 of 32 metrics. Model weights and USI-Bench will be released at https://github.com/SKY-Zhu-91/USI-Brain.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 4 figures, 10 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-93"></a>

### 93. [PRISM-AMC: Replay-Free Continual Learning for Automatic Modulation Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06066v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06066

**作者：** Usman Akram、Matthew Graham、Haris Vikalo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06066v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06066v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PRISM-AMC，一种无回放的持续学习框架，通过梯度投影记忆保留中间表示中与任务相关的子空间以缓解灾难性遗忘，并在与 SNR 相关的梯度噪声模型下分析投影随机梯度更新，在调制分类数据集上评估。

**相关性简评：** 其「对中间表示做任务子空间投影、不重放原始数据」的训练机制，可能可供核查用于 LVR/VLM 多阶段训练中保护视觉证据相关子空间、避免能力被覆盖；但领域为无线信号分类，与视觉推理主线无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PRISM-AMC: Replay-Free Continual Learning for Automatic Modulation Classification

**abstract/A1：** Automatic modulation classification (AMC) is a key component of cognitive wireless systems, enabling spectrum awareness and adaptive communication. Most deep learning approaches to AMC assume a fixed set of modulation classes and access to previously observed training data. In practice, modulation classes and channel conditions may evolve over time, while storage or data-retention constraints may preclude replay of raw baseband I/Q samples. We propose PRISM-AMC, a replay-free continual learning framework that mitigates catastrophic forgetting by preserving task-relevant subspaces of intermediate representations via gradient projection memory. We further analyze projected stochastic-gradient updates under an SNR-dependent gradient-noise model. PRISM-AMC is evaluated in both class-incremental and domain-incremental settings. Experiments on the MIGOU and DeepSig 2018.01A datasets show substantially reduced forgetting relative to sequential fine-tuning and elastic weight consolidation, while remaining competitive with or outperforming replay-based RSD-AMC in class-incremental settings, without storing raw I/Q samples.

</details>
