### 94. [Which One Is Sounding? Binaural Sounding Instance Disambiguation for Audio-Visual Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06074v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06074

**作者：** Haoze Ma、Yidi Li、Yu Ma、Pengyuan Zhao、Yi Shi、Bin Ren、Yanwen Guo、Dimitrios Kanoulas

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SD

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06074v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06074v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 BiSID-AVS：先用音频提示定位同类候选区域，再把双耳音频的耳间差异投影为图像平面空间响应图，并在特征与解码器两级施加空间条件，从多个同类候选里挑出真正发声的实例。

**相关性简评：** 「先得候选、再用辅助空间线索筛选真正证据」的流程可能与 LVR 的视觉证据筛选相关，其线索到响应图的投影与双层条件化可供核查是否用于候选门控；但原文不做 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Which One Is Sounding? Binaural Sounding Instance Disambiguation for Audio-Visual Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Existing audio-visual segmentation methods mainly predict sound-related regions or category masks and often fail to identify the sounding instance when multiple same-class candidates coexist. Recent audio-visual instance segmentation methods address instance-level prediction but rely largely on motion and cross-frame association, which may become unreliable under sparse observations or inconspicuous motion. To this end, we introduce binaural audio, whose interaural differences encode source-direction information, to provide spatial constraints for distinguishing the sounding instance among same-class visual candidates. We propose Binaural Sounding Instance Disambiguation for Audio-Visual Segmentation (BiSID-AVS), which progressively refines category-level candidate responses into a mask of the true sounding instance. BiSID-AVS first grounds category-consistent candidate regions through audio prompting, then projects binaural cues into an image-plane spatial response map, and finally refines the true sounding instance through dual-level spatial conditioning at the feature and decoder levels. Experiments on the binaurally extended AVS-Synthetic benchmark and Habitat-AVS dataset for robotic indoor perception show that BiSID-AVS achieves the best performance on the multi-candidate subsets, improving instance-level mIoU by 35.19 and 41.21 percentage points, respectively, while substantially reducing false activation on non-sounding same-class regions.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 5 figures, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-95"></a>

### 95. [Shallow Encoding Emits Deep Memories](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06084v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06084

**作者：** Mingyuan Ma、Yangmin Li、Harris Nover、Zheyu Fu、Po-Han Huang、Alex Settle、Ashwin Nanjappa

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06084v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06084v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对混合注意力/状态空间语言模型的推理成本，论文发现深层记忆的写入可由浅层残差流经「emitter」预测，且循环状态能量集中在固定方向的秩一项上，据此提出 SEED，将 prefill 变浅、把循环状态压缩为 sink 加低秩因子。

**相关性简评：** 深层状态可由浅层表示预测、以及状态呈「sink 加低秩工作记忆」的结构，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的跨深度预测、压缩存储与相似度中深度/缓存因素提供核查线索，但需确认压缩是否损失推理所需信息。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Shallow Encoding Emits Deep Memories

**abstract/A1：** Hybrid language models interleave a few softmax-attention layers with many linear-attention or state-space layers, so every layer keeps a memory of earlier tokens: a key-value cache or a fixed-size recurrent state. Serving them spends more compute and high-bandwidth memory on these memories than the output requires. The prefill runs every layer for every prompt token, and every decode step loads and stores whole recurrent states, which on Qwen3.8-Flash-Next take as many bytes per request as the attention cache of thousands of tokens. We find two properties of trained hybrids that locate this excess. First, writes are easier than reads: writing a deep layer's memory of a token needs little of the deep computation that predicting the next token needs. Trained copies of the deep layers' write paths, which we call emitters, therefore write a prompt's deep memories from the residual stream of a shallower anchor layer, and all prompt tokens but the last run as few as 63% of the layers. Second, recurrent states have a state sink: most of their energy lies in a rank-one term along a fixed direction of each head, and the rest is a low-rank working memory. SEED builds on both: it makes the prefill shallow in depth and the decode state shallow in representation. Each recurrent state is stored as its sink plus low-rank factors, which the Gated DeltaNet, Kimi Delta Attention and Mamba-2 updates extend exactly; we prove that the open-loop error of the periodic compressions back to low rank is bounded independently of their number. SEED keeps the original weights frozen, compresses no attention cache, and runs all layers for every generated token. On six checkpoints of five production hybrids of 30B to over 300B parameters, it is nearly lossless on eleven official exams. Served with SGLang on NVIDIA GB300 GPUs on an agentic-coding trace, it raises the per-GPU throughput of Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 1.2-1.4x.

**comment/C1：** 47 pages. Mingyuan Ma and Yangmin Li contributed equally

</details>

<a id="paper-96"></a>

### 96. [GeoBiaset: A Counterfactual Benchmark for Demographic Bias in World-Level Geolocalization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06085v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06085

**作者：** Niccolò Niccoli、Silvia Dani、Marco Mistretta、Andrew D. Bagdanov、Marco Bertini、Lorenzo Seidenari

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06085v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06085v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 GeoBiaset，一个以反事实方式在带地理坐标的背景图中插入七类不同族裔人物前景、固定真值坐标的基准（9,541 张图像），并给出 POPI 指标衡量地定位模型与多模态大模型预测是否被拉向所插入群体的关联区域；结合遮挡、无地标条件与梯度热图分析模型依赖的视觉线索。

**相关性简评：** 其反事实前景编辑加「预测是否被拉向插入内容」的度量方式，可供核查 VLM 答案与 latent 状态是否真正依赖特定视觉证据线索，而非仅靠背景或先验；但研究对象是地定位偏差，未涉及推理期 latent 状态，迁移需自行设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GeoBiaset: A Counterfactual Benchmark for Demographic Bias in World-Level Geolocalization

**abstract/A1：** World-level image geolocalization is an increasingly important task, yet the biases of current models remain poorly understood. Prior work has identified coarse dataset imbalances noting, for instance, that some cities are absent from training sets and that models tend to perform better in wealthier regions. However, no one has yet examined which visual cues drive these disparities. To do so, we introduce GeoBiaset, the first benchmark designed to measure how geolocalization models are influenced by the apparent race of a person inserted into the image foreground. The dataset consists of 9,541 images, pairing existing geotagged background images with counterfactual edits spanning seven races, holding the ground-truth coordinates fixed. Controlled masking experiments show that occlusion and background drift do not fully explain geolocalization degradation. Alongside the benchmark, we introduce the Person-Origin Perturbation Indicators (POPI), novel metrics that quantify model resilience to these demographic perturbations by measuring whether predictions are pulled toward the region associated with the inserted group. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art geolocalization and multimodal large language models reveals a 16-47% drop in continent-level performance when a subject from a demographically mismatched group is inserted. A landmark versus no-landmark analysis and gradient-weighted heatmaps show that models preferentially attend to geographic landmarks when present, but shift attention to facial features in their absence.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at Neurips 2026, E&amp;D track. Project page: https://miccunifi.github.io/GeoBiaset/

</details>

<a id="paper-97"></a>

### 97. [Drift-adaptive Representation Preservation for Domain-Incremental Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06093v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06093

**作者：** Hyunmin Kong、Gyutae Oh、Jitae Shin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06093v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06093v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究域增量学习中的遗忘问题：按「域-类」概念分别追踪其表示相对固定锚点的漂移量，把漂移当作遗忘风险的可测代理，用它同时调节保持损失强度与原型构建权重；为在不保存旧数据的情况下获得该信号，改造 JEPA，仅存紧凑上下文摘要并重跑上下文到目标的关系来重算漂移。

**相关性简评：** 以预测架构重算表示相对锚点的漂移并据此分配保持力度，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态漂移、表示坍缩的监测与训练期监督分配；但该工作面向纯视觉分类的持续学习，未涉及多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Drift-adaptive Representation Preservation for Domain-Incremental Learning

**abstract/A1：** Pretrained vision models provide strong general-purpose representations, but in Domain-Incremental Learning (DIL), where the input distribution changes sequentially, adapting to new domains often degrades performance on previously learned ones. We observe that forgetting is not uniform across domains and classes and is not explained by training order alone. This motivates us to track each past domain-class concept individually. We use representation drift, defined as the deviation of a past concept's current representation from a fixed anchor, as a measurable surrogate for forgetting risk. The measured drift then serves two complementary roles. A consolidation loss assigns stronger preservation to high-drift pairs, while prototype construction down-weights highly drifted domains to reduce their influence on the class reference. Preservation is thus allocated by measured drift rather than assumed importance. To obtain this signal during training without storing past raw data, we repurpose a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA). For each domain-class pair, we store only a compact context summary in the backbone feature space and rerun it through the learned context-to-target relation to measure its drift from the fixed anchor. Across DomainNet, Office-Home, CORe50, and CDDB-Hard, our method sustains a strong accuracy-forgetting balance on every benchmark, using substantially less training memory than accuracy-competitive methods and the smallest storage among compared methods.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to ACCV 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-98"></a>

### 98. [XuanJian-DINOSAR: A SAR Foundation Model Based on Global and Local Semantic Consistency](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06105v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06105

**作者：** Chunxiao Wu、Huixin Huang、Jialei Pan、Qiang Liang、Tao Wu、Cheng Wu、Long Huang、Yuanji Li、Qiang Cheng、Aifang Liu、Linghao Xia、Rui Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06105v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06105v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建约36万张SAR图像，将光学预训练的DINOv2适配到SAR，结合DINO全局自蒸馏、iBOT局部掩码建模与KoLeo正则，并加入针对斑点噪声的局部语义一致性分支做自监督预训练。

**相关性简评：** 其对空间对应位置patch token施加余弦一致性损失、以抵抗强度与斑点扰动的做法，可供核查作为保持视觉latent状态稳定与局部细节的训练约束；但论文只做SAR表示预训练，无推理过程或latent推理，联系停留在表示训练层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** XuanJian-DINOSAR: A SAR Foundation Model Based on Global and Local Semantic Consistency

**abstract/A1：** Self-supervised pre-training on synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imagery enables transferable representations, but the optical-SAR domain gap and speckle perturbations limit representation stability. Most SAR foundation models use masked image modeling (MIM) through pixel reconstruction or structural feature prediction, which is sensitive to speckle or relies on predefined operators. Joint modeling of cross-view semantics and perturbation stability at corresponding spatial locations remains underexplored. We propose XuanJian-DINOSAR, a SAR foundation model based on global and local semantic consistency. We curate over 360K publicly available SAR target chips, detection images, and scene images spanning diverse targets, scenes, and sensors, and adapt an optically pre-trained DINOv2 model to SAR through self-supervised learning. The framework combines DINO global semantic self-distillation, iBOT local masked modeling, and KoLeo feature-distribution regularization to learn globally discriminative and locally structure-aware representations. We introduce a local semantic consistency branch tailored to SAR intensity statistics and coherent imaging characteristics. Shared geometric transformations produce spatially aligned teacher weak views and student strong views. Intensity transformations and Gamma multiplicative speckle perturbations are applied to student views, while a cosine consistency loss aligns patch tokens at corresponding locations, promoting stability under radiometric perturbations. This objective complements the cross-region and cross-scale semantic consistency of DINOv2 multi-crop training, jointly enforcing global and local semantic consistency. Experiments on multiple downstream classification and detection datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on most evaluation metrics, validating the generality and transferability of the learned representations.

</details>

<a id="paper-99"></a>

### 99. [Beyond Risk Cues: A Mechanism-Level Benchmark and Verifiable Debate for Scam Video Detection on the Web](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06110v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06110

**作者：** Yifan Wang、Junyu Lu、Shun Zhang、Chaozhuo Li、Fanliang Bu、Rong Yin、Lingbin Bu、Changrong Min、Peng Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06110v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06110v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 MechScam 基准（6,614 个视频，含机制解释、视频证据标注及由真实证据构造的误导性论证），并提出 ScamCourt：控辩双方分别立论、裁判依据引文经核验的辩论记录作出判断，以区分诈骗视频与相似的广告等非诈骗内容。

**相关性简评：** 其「引文先核验再进入决策」的辩论与裁判机制，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、证据是否真正支撑答案的判定提供借鉴线索，但论文任务是视频诈骗检测，与 latent 状态生成的关系尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Risk Cues: A Mechanism-Level Benchmark and Verifiable Debate for Scam Video Detection on the Web

**abstract/A1：** Online scam videos pose significant threats to individuals and society. Existing studies primarily focus on binary classification, offering limited insights into how scams work and the reasoning behind detection decisions. Moreover, they often overlook the challenge of distinguishing scam videos from superficially similar non-scam content, such as advertisements, and the robustness of detection models against misleading arguments. These limitations hinder reliable scam video detection. To address these limitations, we introduce MechScam, a benchmark comprising 6,614 videos covering diverse scam mechanisms and non-scam categories. MechScam provides explanations supported by video evidence, annotations of potential scam elements and their context, and misleading arguments constructed from genuine evidence. Recognizing that genuine evidence can still be used to justify misleading conclusions, we further propose ScamCourt, a debate framework in which trained prosecution and defense debaters present opposing arguments, while a trained judge makes decisions based on a transcript with verified citations. Extensive experiments show that state-of-the-art MLLMs struggle to distinguish scam videos from similar non-scam content. ScamCourt outperforms larger MLLMs and same-backbone fine-tuned baselines, reducing false positives and showing stronger resistance to misleading arguments. Together, MechScam and ScamCourt contribute to more reliable and interpretable scam video detection. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/SuperYFan/MechScam.

</details>

<a id="paper-100"></a>

### 100. [Semantic Prior-Guided Incomplete Cross-Modal Retrieval with Noisy Labels](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06113v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06113

**作者：** Jiali Chen、Ruitao Pu、Yingke Chen、Dezhong Peng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06113v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06113v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SPIRN 框架，以固定语义先验作为稳定参照，结合质量感知鲁棒学习、全局标签校准与置信度驱动的缺失模态补全，处理跨模态检索中模态缺失与标签噪声并存的问题。

**相关性简评：** 其用跨模态记忆库补全缺失模态、并以递增阈值过滤不可靠补全的机制，可能为 LVR 中缺失或不可靠视觉证据的恢复与筛选提供可核查的线索，但论文本身不涉及推理期 latent 状态或生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Semantic Prior-Guided Incomplete Cross-Modal Retrieval with Noisy Labels

**abstract/A1：** Cross-modal retrieval (CMR) aims to bridge the semantic gaps between modalities by learning shared representations. Unfortunately, due to the dynamism and inconsistencies in real-world data acquisition, multimodal datasets frequently encounter severe modality incompleteness. Existing incomplete CMR methods adhere to a label-driven paradigm, relying heavily on categorical annotations as the semantic benchmark. However, label noise is difficult to avoid and often coexists with modality incompleteness. To address this challenge, we propose a novel semantic prior-guided framework for incomplete cross-modal retrieval with noisy labels, termed SPIRN. Specifically, SPIRN uses fixed semantic priors as a consistent reference for robust representation learning, noisy-label handling, and missing-modality completion. Based on this reference, Semantic Priors Guidance (SPG) establishes a stable category-level semantic space, Quality-aware Robust Learning (QRL) dynamically evaluates sample quality to distinguish clean samples from noisy ones and handles noise via reverse learning, Prior-guided Global Label Calibration (PGLC) rectifies corrupted annotations for refined supervision, and Confidence-driven Selective Imputation (CSI) recovers missing modalities from a cross-modal memory bank while filtering unreliable imputations with an increasing threshold. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first framework to jointly tackle the dual challenges of modality incompleteness and label noise in the CMR community. Extensive experiments on four widely used benchmark datasets demonstrate that SPIRN outperforms 11 state-of-the-art methods in various scenarios involving both missing modalities and noisy labels.

</details>

<a id="paper-101"></a>

### 101. [ReactWorld: Structuring Interaction Responses for Embodied Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06120v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06120

**作者：** Hanwei Liu、Huijia Zhang、Hanzhang Wang、Guangyu Liu、Hanbei Zhan、Linjie Hu、Bin Xiao、Xuan Zhou、Dong Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06120v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06120v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ReactWorld 面向具身视频生成，在生成主干特征空间中预测交互响应场（参与者角色、未来运动响应强度、局部运动变化），并通过反馈适配器把预测位移转成时序注意力的位置偏置，以引导对象特征更新与跨帧信息检索。

**相关性简评：** 其「先预测结构化中间状态、再反馈调制生成特征与注意力」的结构可供核查是否能为 LVR 的 latent 状态注入与更新提供线索；但属视频世界模型，非直接研究 VLM 推理，预测内容是否真正被使用仍需验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReactWorld: Structuring Interaction Responses for Embodied Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Embodied video world models predict scene changes caused by robot actions, yet generated object responses may remain inconsistent with task intent and contact dynamics. Critical responses in embodied scenes often concentrate in local regions and specific time intervals. Inspired by chain-of-thought reasoning, we introduce ReactWorld, an embodied video generation model guided by structured interaction anticipation. In the generation backbone's feature space, an interaction response field predictor anticipates how the interaction will unfold, explicitly describing participant roles, future motion response intensity, and local motion changes. A response feedback adapter forms increments to object features from the difference between the object branch's outputs with predicted and zeroed local dynamics inputs. It also converts predicted displacements into positional biases for temporal attention. These two mechanisms guide object feature updates and information retrieval across frames, respectively. Through interaction anticipation and generative feedback, ReactWorld achieves an average score of 77.51 on RBench, outperforming PhysisForcing by 3.29 points with improvements in all nine categories. On EZS-Bench, it achieves a Domain score of 86.98 and an average score of 81.87.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 7 figures. Project page: https://metaengine-world.github.io/ReactWorld/

</details>

<a id="paper-102"></a>

### 102. [RDT-3D: Resolution-Decoupled Training for High-Fidelity 3D Geometry Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06141v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06141

**作者：** Jiale Zhang、Yang Liu、Chengyang Li、Jin Yuan、Qianxi Jia、Taicheng Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06141v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06141v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向高保真 3D 几何生成，指出粗到细框架中训练时坐标引导与几何监督对齐、而推理时却由粗坐标解码高分辨率几何的训练—推理分辨率错配，提出将 RoPE 引导锚定在 512、监督放在 1536 的解耦训练方案，并构建 512/1536 对齐的 30 万点云数据集。

**相关性简评：** 其「位置编码引导的锚定尺度与监督尺度分离」可作为一种具体线索，供核查位置/RoPE 结构如何影响 latent 推理状态的分辨率与长度外推，但与 VLM 推理本身无直接关系，需人工判断可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RDT-3D: Resolution-Decoupled Training for High-Fidelity 3D Geometry Generation

**abstract/A1：** High-fidelity 3D geometry generation requires maintaining global structural coherence alongside fine-grained surface details. While recent coarse-to-fine frameworks leverage Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) to condition local queries, we reveal a critical training-inference resolution discrepancy: coordinate guidance and geometric supervision are aligned during training, whereas inference decodes high-resolution geometry from coarse spatial coordinates. This mismatch introduces a severe distribution shift that degrades fine details. To overcome this, we propose Resolution-Decoupled Training (RDT), which anchors RoPE guidance at 512 resolution while supervising geometry at 1536, enabling robust detail extrapolation. Additionally, to eliminate high-resolution data bottlenecks, we present Dual3D-300K, a large-scale paired dataset comprising 300K watertight 3D point clouds explicitly aligned across 512 and 1536 resolutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RDT substantially improves sharp geometric regions, while Dual3D-300K offers a scalable benchmark for high-resolution 3D generation.

</details>

<a id="paper-103"></a>

### 103. [PTQ4SAM3: Semantics-Preserving Post-Training Quantization for Segment Anything Model 3](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06143v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06143

**作者：** Xi Xiao、Yunbei Zhang、Lin Zhao、Janet Wang、Xinru Jiang、Yanshu Li、Chenrui Ma、Pan Wang、Tianyang Wang、Xiao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06143v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06143v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对 SAM 3 部署成本高，作者发现扰动低方差特征方向会明显改变文本提示下的目标选择，称其为「提示对齐漂移」，并提出离线语义保持量化 SPQ：给概念敏感参数更高校准权重、保留残差特征、校正视频记忆的均值误差。

**相关性简评：** 「低方差方向扰动即改变目标选择」这一诊断可供核查任务必要视觉证据在内部状态中的保留程度与敏感方向，视频记忆的均值误差校正也提示多帧 latent 状态的漂移累积问题，但论文目标为压缩效率而非 LVR 机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PTQ4SAM3: Semantics-Preserving Post-Training Quantization for Segment Anything Model 3

**abstract/A1：** Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) can segment objects from text prompts and track them across video, but its memory and computation costs make deployment difficult. Post-training quantization (PTQ) reduces these costs by using fewer bits. However, a quantized model can select the wrong object or lose track of its target across frames. We call this prompt-alignment drift. Controlled tests show that perturbing low-variance feature directions strongly affects target selection. We propose Semantics-Preserving Quantization (SPQ), which gives more calibration weight to concept-sensitive parameters, preserves residual features, and corrects the mean error in video memory. Fixed text concepts guide calibration without task annotations. All three components operate offline and add no inference operators. With a W4A8 image encoder, SPQ improves RefCOCO Pr@0.5 from 42.1 to 72.5 over PTQ4SAM while retaining 54.8 COCO mask AP. Experiments on LVIS, RefCOCO variants, and SA-V show consistent gains. A CLIP study also shows improved image-text retrieval.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 4 figures. Accepted to WACV 2027. Includes supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-104"></a>

### 104. [SafeStart: Mitigating the Overlooked Safety Cost of Active Expert Reduction in MoE LLM Serving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06147v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06147

**作者：** Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo、Saeid Shokoufa、Seyedarmin Azizi、Arya Fayyazi、Souvik Kundu、Mehdi Kamal、Massoud Pedram

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06147v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06147v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出 MoE serving 中的专家削减虽保持准确率，却削弱模型对有害请求的拒答能力，且该损失主要产生于处理 prompt 与前几个生成 token 阶段；据此提出 SafeStart，在 prompt 和前八个生成 token 上运行全部专家、之后才削减，并用融合 kernel 保留大部分解码加速。

**相关性简评：** 其「削减内部专家带来的能力损失集中在生成前段、可用分阶段全量计算恢复」这一发现，可能为 LVR 中推理步计算预算分配与 THINK/STOP 收益成本分析提供线索，可供核查 latent 状态削减对早期推理的影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SafeStart: Mitigating the Overlooked Safety Cost of Active Expert Reduction in MoE LLM Serving

**abstract/A1：** Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models send each token through a few of many expert subnetworks. Serving methods make these models faster by running even fewer experts per token, and they are validated on accuracy benchmarks. We show that these methods can weaken the model's refusal of harmful requests, and we introduce SafeStart, which largely restores refusal while keeping most of the speedup. In vLLM on one RTX A6000, keeping about two thirds of the selected experts speeds up offline decoding by 15 to 28% at batch size 8. We find that, at this setting, under a jailbreak that tells the model not to refuse, the share of harmful requests that the model answers rises by 18.4 percentage points on Qwen1.5-MoE, 9.5 on OLMoE, and 6.6 on DeepSeek-V2-Lite, the three of nine models where it rises significantly, while MMLU accuracy drops by less than two points. We discover that most of this increase arises while the model processes the prompt and generates its first few tokens. SafeStart therefore runs all experts for the prompt and the first eight generated tokens, and the reduced set of experts afterwards, through a fused kernel in vLLM. With experts removed, SafeStart keeps at least 85% of the offline decode speedup at batch sizes 8 and 32 and brings the change under this jailbreak to between -2.1 and +1.3 points for three expert-reduction methods on these three models. On 889 held-out requests, in an analysis fixed before generation, truncation raises harmful compliance by 5.8 to 21.4 points, and SafeStart leaves a rise of 0.6 to 2.6 points, non-inferior to the original model on two of the three models. Our results show that faster MoE serving need not cost safety: a serving system can keep most of the gain from running fewer experts by running all of them at the start of each response, and it should test refusal, not only accuracy, before it enables such a method.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages, 13 figures, 41 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-105"></a>

### 105. [Grounding in the Dark: Text-Only Training for Zero-Shot Video Temporal Localization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06149v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06149

**作者：** Divyanshu Mishra、Sabine Sternig、Rakshith Shetty、Erhan Gundogdu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06149v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06149v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作研究只用文本视频描述训练一个无视觉预训练的 LLM 做零样本视频时序定位：构造 134K 文本序列语料，用 LoRA 适配 Qwen3-8B 到 CLIP/LLM2CLIP 文本嵌入，并提出免训练的 Nearest Entity Replacement，用最近文本实体嵌入的归一化均值表示每一帧，以缩小推理时的模态鸿沟。

**相关性简评：** 其核心是文本嵌入空间与视觉帧嵌入的错位及免训练映射，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据如何映射到语言表示空间、在无配对监督下筛选证据提供一条可供核查的方法线索，但不涉及 latent 推理状态生成或停止决策，故只作方法借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Grounding in the Dark: Text-Only Training for Zero-Shot Video Temporal Localization

**abstract/A1：** Video-LLMs are typically trained on paired video-text data for temporal tasks, yet collecting precise temporal annotations and training on large-scale video datasets are both costly. We investigate whether a text-only LLM, with no vision-language pretraining, can be trained on textual video descriptions alone to perform temporal grounding and receive video only at inference time. This setting removes paired video data from training altogether and is a step toward training multimodal models without paired annotations. We generate a 134K-example corpus of textual sequences with up to 100 time steps, adapt Qwen3-8B with LoRA on CLIP or LLM2CLIP text embeddings, and evaluate on Charades-STA and QV-Highlights without training on videos from either benchmark. LLM2CLIP, whose text encoder supports longer inputs, represents detailed descriptions better than CLIP (61.54 versus 53.86 mtIoU on Temporal-TextVid) and transfers them better to video. Still, replacing text with frame embeddings at inference yields only 4.99 mtIoU on Charades-STA and 11.12 on QV-Highlights, revealing a large modality gap. To bridge it, we propose Nearest Entity Replacement (NER), a training-free mapping that represents each video frame by the normalized mean of its nearest text-entity embeddings, so that the model receives the kind of input it was trained on. NER reaches 14.45 mtIoU on Charades-STA and 20.72 on QV-Highlights, outperforming the strongest of four families of modality-gap baselines by 5.00 and 1.71 points. NER thus enables partial transfer from text to video, while more reliable grounding will likely require encoders with better-aligned text and visual representations.

</details>

<a id="paper-106"></a>

### 106. [CARM: Conformal Adaptive Risk Masking for Reliable Scientific Super-Resolution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06151v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06151

**作者：** Guangtao Zhang、Haonan Wu、Junxuan Gao、Zhengyang Liu、Zhaoxi Zeng、Zhihao Li、Jianguo Huang、Zhiqiang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG、math.PR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06151v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06151v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CARM，在时序分布漂移下对科学超分辨率做自适应空间风险控制：用多个异质更新速率的专家在线聚合阈值，将空间不确定性分数转成可信掩膜，并证明掩膜条件风险的时均收敛率为 O(1/T)。

**相关性简评：** 其「不确定性分数→受共形风险约束的可信区域掩膜」与延迟反馈下的阈值自适应，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选及 LOOK/STOP 可靠性门控提供可核查的方法线索，但研究对象是地球观测超分而非 VLM 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CARM: Conformal Adaptive Risk Masking for Reliable Scientific Super-Resolution

**abstract/A1：** Deep learning-based super-resolution (SR) can reconstruct fine-scale Earth-observation fields from coarse observations, but reconstruction accuracy alone does not provide a calibrated rule for identifying reliable spatial regions. Conformal Mask Calibration (CMC), built on conformal risk control, converts a spatial uncertainty score into a trust mask and controls the expected worst-case local distortion under exchangeability. However, its fixed threshold can become miscalibrated in Earth-observation streams affected by seasonality and episodic events. We propose $\\textit{CARM}$ (Conformal Adaptive Risk Masking), a statistical framework for adaptive spatial risk control under temporal distribution shift. CARM maintains experts with heterogeneous update rates and aggregates their threshold proposals online using an asymmetric surrogate loss, balancing rapid adaptation to distribution changes against stability during relatively stationary periods. All updates are causal and use only mature feedback available after a fixed delay, allowing CARM to operate with any SR model that provides a spatial uncertainty score. We prove that the time-averaged mask-conditioned risk converges to at most $α$ at an $O(1/T)$ rate, without distributional assumptions and for any choice of aggregation weights across experts. On a daily South China Sea chlorophyll-$\\textit{a}$ SR stream, CARM reduces the empirical violation rate from $43.49\\%$ to $9.38\\%$ and the moving-average exceedance frequency from $47.29\\%$ to $1.22\\%$ at $α=0.10$, while retaining a non-trivial trusted region. Experiments on ERA5 wind and OSTIA sea-surface temperature further show consistent relative improvements across multiple SR backbones.

</details>

<a id="paper-107"></a>

### 107. [Neural Stochastic Forecasting for Lateral Molecular Diffusion Simulations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06157v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06157

**作者：** Jacob Ede Levine、Mario Hidalgo-Soria、Chen Yun Wen、Yun Lyna Luo、Sai Chandra Kosaraju

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06157v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06157v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出神经随机（NS）模型，用 transformer 编码粗粒化 MD 的蛋白构型、多时间尺度循环网络与带学习记忆的随机隐过程建模演化，再由 cross-attention 解码器在几何与周期边界条件下生成相互关联的蛋白位移，用于长时膜蛋白集合扩散的预测与统计评估。

**相关性简评：** 其「随机隐过程＋多尺度记忆更新＋条件解码」的长程状态推演与分布统计检验方式，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的持续更新、长程 rollout 稳定性与生成结果评估提供方法线索；但该隐状态是否承载可复用的任务相关信息尚待核查，与视觉多模态推理的直接联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Neural Stochastic Forecasting for Lateral Molecular Diffusion Simulations

**abstract/A1：** Predicting the long-time diffusion of membrane proteins is challenging because molecular dynamics (MD) simulations are computationally expensive, while experimental single-particle tracking is often limited by finite observation times. Here, we introduce a neural stochastic (NS) model for forecasting the collective diffusion of membrane proteins from coarse-grained MD trajectories. The model combines a transformer-based representation of protein configurations, recurrent networks operating at multiple temporal scales, and a stochastic latent process with learned memory. A cross-attention decoder then generates correlated protein displacements while accounting for the instantaneous system geometry and periodic boundary conditions. We evaluate the model in two membrane systems: seven interacting PIEZO channels and a mixed system containing seven PIEZO and ten TREK channels. For the seven PIEZO system, the NS model reproduces the anomalous diffusion exponent measured in MD simulations $α\\sim 1$, substantially improving on a previously developed variational autoregressive model that exhibits superdiffusive behavior. The model also generates trajectories extending to 40,000 frames and captures the distinct diffusion characteristics of PIEZO and TREK populations in the mixed system. Comparisons of time-averaged mean-square displacements, Van Hove displacement distributions, and non-Gaussian parameters show that the model preserves key statistical features of the simulated dynamics. These results establish neural stochastic modeling as a promising approach for generating long trajectories of multi-protein membrane diffusion and extending the statistical analysis of molecular dynamics simulations.

</details>

<a id="paper-108"></a>

### 108. [Selective Prediction Intervals: Jointly Learning to Predict, Bound, and Abstain](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06158v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06158

**作者：** Lidor Rubi、Gilad Katz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06158v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06158v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SelPI，在回归任务中联合训练区间、点估计与弃权三个头，用两个分别基于区间损失和点值损失的 gate 加 Fréchet 惩罚，在给定接受率预算下决定是否弃权。

**相关性简评：** 其「区间宽度与区间未命中反相关」的发现提示不确定度信号不可跨目标通用，双门控加预算约束的形式可供核查是否迁移为 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的独立门控与停止判据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Selective Prediction Intervals: Jointly Learning to Predict, Bound, and Abstain

**abstract/A1：** Many high-stakes regression decisions ask three things of a model at once: a point estimate to act on, an interval that bounds the risk of acting, and the option to abstain when neither can be trusted. Deep learning provides these in isolation: prediction-interval methods return an interval, and at best a value estimate inside it, for every input, while selective-prediction methods learn when to abstain, but only for point predictions and with no statement of how far the truth may lie from the estimate. We introduce SelPI, which trains interval, value and abstention heads jointly under a single loss with an explicit acceptance-rate budget. A natural objection is that interval width already tells us when to abstain. We show that it does not: across 18 tabular benchmarks, width predicts large point errors well (AUROC 0.72) but is anti-correlated with interval misses (AUROC 0.38), so abstaining on wide intervals discards precisely the inputs the interval got right. SelPI therefore selects with two gates, one trained on the interval loss and one on the value loss, coupled by a Fr'echet penalty that ties their separate budgets to the joint acceptance rate they deliver. With the base predictor held fixed, the learned gate beats width thresholding on a proper interval score on all 18 tabular datasets, closing a median 87% of the gap to an oracle selector, and it also beats selection by deep-ensemble disagreement on every dataset. The advantage grows with the heteroscedasticity of the task, and we report the settings in which it disappears.

</details>

<a id="paper-109"></a>

### 109. [DSRS: Teaching MLLMs to Perceive, Reason, and Segment in Complex Driving Scenes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06160v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06160

**作者：** Sicen Guo、Zhengfei Song、Kuanhsun Chen、Xu Huang、Ping Zhong、Wei Ye、Shaoyi Du、Rui Fan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06160v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06160v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出驾驶场景推理分割任务 DSRS 及配套基准，要求模型理解隐含指令、推断空间关系与交通规则并输出分割掩码，并提出 DSRS-Net，在 MLLM 多模态融合中注入几何特征以提升推理分割效果。

**相关性简评：** 其「几何特征注入以缓解 RGB-only 预训练造成的模态表示失配」可供核查 LVR 中几何与任务必要视觉细节是否保留在内部状态的问题，但方法本身面向分割输出，未涉及 latent 推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DSRS: Teaching MLLMs to Perceive, Reason, and Segment in Complex Driving Scenes

**abstract/A1：** Existing reasoning segmentation frameworks typically employ multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with broad world knowledge to extract semantics in a human-like manner and generate corresponding segmentation masks. Nevertheless, these frameworks lack the necessary scene-specific awareness. This paper introduces driving scene reasoning segmentation (DSRS), a scene-specific task that requires models to interpret implicit instructions, infer spatial relations, detect potential risks, handle challenging conditions, and comprehend underlying traffic rules, ultimately generating accurate segmentation masks. Meanwhile, we establish a benchmark dataset to facilitate future research on the DSRS task. To pioneer this task, we propose DSRS-Net, a novel architecture with advanced reasoning segmentation capability for automated driving. Specifically, DSRS-Net seamlessly infuses geometric features for deeper reasoning segmentation, without compromising the original multimodal fusion mechanism. This strategy effectively mitigates the modality representation mismatch in MLLMs caused by RGB-only pretraining, while enhancing the integration of geometric, contextual, and linguistic features. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of DSRS-Net in both driving scene reasoning segmentation and other language-driven segmentation tasks across various public datasets. The source code and dataset will be released upon publication.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages and 19 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-110"></a>

### 110. [FaCoDiff: Factor-Correlated Diffusion for Multivariate Probabilistic Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06166v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06166

**作者：** Jiaxuan Li、Yao Liu、Yuewei Xue、Shijie Dong、Zixiang Jin、Xiaoyu Guo、Qiao Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06166v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06166v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FaCoDiff，用紧凑的条件统计估计器产生历史条件因子载荷，并配合低秩结构化去噪、边际方差对角补偿与几何感知目标，实现多变量概率预测，避免稠密协方差存储与分解。

**相关性简评：** 其低秩因子隐式状态、因子特征调制去噪器以及前向扰动与反向采样共用同一条件几何的设计，可能为 diffusion 式 latent 推理的状态表示与几何一致性提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FaCoDiff: Factor-Correlated Diffusion for Multivariate Probabilistic Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Multivariate probabilistic forecasting requires modeling cross-variable dependence alongside marginal uncertainty. The challenge is to learn this dependence while controlling marginal noise scales and avoiding dense covariance costs. We propose Factor-Correlated Diffusion (FaCoDiff), which couples a compact conditional statistical estimator with structured denoising. History-conditioned factor loadings use distinct coefficients for forecast-center correction, scale adjustment and noise dependence. Diagonal compensation preserves the estimated marginal noise variances, while low-rank operators enable residual Gaussian likelihood evaluation and noise transport without dense covariance storage or factorization. Factor-derived feature modulation explicitly conditions the denoiser, and a geometry-aware objective accounts for errors after noise transport. The same conditional geometry governs forward perturbations and reverse sampling. Across nine datasets, FaCoDiff achieves the lowest mean variogram score on eight datasets and ranks among the top two on 43 of 54 dataset-metric combinations in the reported comparisons. Matched ablations on Solar and Weather show that factor covariance improves mean Energy and Variogram scores under all four combinations of center and scale correction. Disrupting cross-variable sample pairings while preserving marginal trajectories worsens joint and aggregate scores on both datasets, demonstrating the predictive value of generated cross-variable dependence.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-111"></a>

### 111. [Annotator-Free Object Detection via Foundation Model Collaboration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06195v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06195

**作者：** Yasiru Ranasinghe、Vishal M. Patel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06195v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06195v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作提出免标注目标检测框架，用分割与视觉语言基础模型协作生成候选框和语义标签，再通过自精炼训练循环与基于地标的聚类分摊 LVLM 查询，迭代训练轻量开放集检测器。

**相关性简评：** 按图像聚类摊销 LVLM 标注查询、并把多模态推理结果蒸馏进轻量检测器的做法，可能为降低 LVR 中反复获取视觉证据的查询成本与缓存复用提供核查线索，但原文不涉及 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Annotator-Free Object Detection via Foundation Model Collaboration

**abstract/A1：** Object detection research remains constrained by high annotation costs and the closed-set assumptions of supervised models, limiting scalability to novel categories. We present a target-dataset annotation-free object detection framework that eliminates the need for human labeling on the target dataset by leveraging the complementary strengths of pretrained foundation models, including segmentation and vision-language systems, combined with a self-refining training loop. To further reduce reliance on expensive LVLM queries, we introduce a landmark-based clustering mechanism that amortizes labeling across image clusters, improving label consistency while lowering computational cost. Our method autonomously generates segmentation proposals, assigns semantic labels through multimodal reasoning, and iteratively improves both localization and classification using a lightweight open-set detector. This modular pipeline enables adaptation to completely unseen object classes and domains without any human-provided target-dataset annotations, while maintaining compatibility with existing detection architectures. Evaluations on COCO, LVIS, ODinW-35, and Roboflow-100 demonstrate substantial performance gains over state-of-the-art open-vocabulary and long-tail detection methods, achieving 60.8 AP on COCO and 47.4 AP on LVIS under zero-shot settings. Beyond strong benchmark results, our approach provides a scalable foundation for real-world detection systems in unknown environments, where annotation is infeasible.

</details>

<a id="paper-112"></a>

### 112. [Rubric Curriculum RL: Exploiting the Generation-Verification Gap in Non-Verifiable Domains](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06244v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06244

**作者：** Sumeet Ramesh Motwani、Tejas Krishnan、Alistair Letcher、Charles London、Suhaas Bhat、Huitian Jiao、Philip Torr、Riashat Islam、Christopher Summerfield、Jakob Foerster、Christian Schroeder de Witt、Qilong Gu、Shital Shah

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06244v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06244v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Rubric Curriculum RL（RcRL），在无真值开放域中把 rollout 之间的成对偏好与 rubric 准则课程结合，构造更具区分度且非平稳的奖励信号，避免策略对固定评判器过拟合并保持输出熵，在创意写作与 HealthBench 上优于基线。

**相关性简评：** 其利用「生成难、验证易」的差距、并以 curriculum 缓解固定评判器被 reward hack 的思路，可能为 LVR 中难以逐步核验的 latent 轨迹监督与验证器过拟合提供训练信号设计线索，尚需核查能否迁移到多模态视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rubric Curriculum RL: Exploiting the Generation-Verification Gap in Non-Verifiable Domains

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has led to significant gains in math and code generation. Extending these gains to open-ended domains remains challenging: ground-truth verification is unavailable, human annotation is expensive, and learned reward models are prone to reward hacking. The generation-verification gap, where judging good outputs is easier than producing them, offers a potential path to improvement. However, rubric-based judges are often imprecise and stationary, so policies can overfit or reward hack them. We introduce Rubric Curriculum RL (RcRL), which exploits this gap without new data, human annotations, or stronger teacher models. We combine pairwise preferences over rollouts with a curriculum over rubric criteria, yielding a more discriminative signal than absolute scoring and a non-stationary objective. Whereas baselines plateau within a few hundred steps, RcRL preserves output entropy and keeps improving past 1000 steps. On creative writing, RcRL achieves a 70.5% human win rate against the base model, with gains across benchmarks and judges. On HealthBench, RcRL improves over the base model by 14.6% (full set) and 25.4% (consensus subset), outperforming all training baselines, including instance-specific rubrics.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at ICML 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-113"></a>

### 113. [CAPE-JEPA - Cross-modal Adversarial Probabilistic Embedding JEPA](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06247v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06247

**作者：** Aniket Junghare、Biplab Banerjee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06247v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06247v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CAPE-JEPA，用于 SAR、多光谱与 RGB 遥感图像的跨模态检索：以跨注意力高斯预测器输出逐 patch 的均值与方差并按负对数似然训练，配合基于 Epps-Pulley 特征函数检验的跨模态对抗式分布正则以及训练期对比学习。

**相关性简评：** 其概率化 latent 预测与不确定性估计可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的置信度刻画、视觉证据筛选与停止决策提供可核查线索，但该文面向检索对齐，未涉及推理轨迹生成或答案对 latent 内容的使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CAPE-JEPA - Cross-modal Adversarial Probabilistic Embedding JEPA

**abstract/A1：** Cross-modal remote sensing image retrieval (RS-CMIR) requires representations that are simultaneously modality-invariant and semantically discriminative, despite the profound physical gap between heterogeneous sensors such as Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) and multispectral imagery. Recent JEPA-based Predictive self-supervised approaches such as X-JEPA formulate cross-modal alignment as a deterministic latent forecasting task, implicitly assuming uniform patch predictability and leaving cross-modal distributional alignment unaddressed. We propose CAPE-JEPA, a probabilistic cross-modal Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture with three contributions: (i) a cross-attentional Gaussian predictor outputting per-patch parameters (mu, sigma^2) conditioned on opposite-modality context, trained via negative log-likelihood to model inherent cross-sensor prediction uncertainty; (ii) adversarial distributional regularization combining unimodal isotropic Gaussian regularization with a novel cross-modal adversarial variant that aligns modality distributions via worst-case Epps-Pulley characteristic function tests without explicit sample pairing; and (iii) in-training contrastive learning with attention-pooled heads for scene-level discriminative grounding. Evaluated on BigEarthNet-14K (BEN-14K) and fMoW across SAR, multispectral, and RGB modalities, CAPE-JEPA surpasses X-JEPA by up to 13.74 points on BEN-14K and 15.59 points on fMoW, outperforming all baselines including SkySense and CROMA across both unimodal and cross-modal directions while using fewer parameters (407.30M vs. 432.72M).

**comment/C1：** Accepted for publication at the British Machine Vision Conference (BMVC 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-114"></a>

### 114. [Learning Noise Optimization for Reward Alignment in Few-Step Generative Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06255v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06255

**作者：** Hongeun Kim、Jeongsol Kim、Jong Chul Ye

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06255v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06255v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 LeNO，用 Local GRPO 与标量奖励学习一个以锚点为条件的残差传输，把奖励引导的初始噪声优化摊销进模型，从而在冻结预训练少步生成器的情况下避免推理期逐样本搜索，并针对高斯先验给出 KL 上界作为正则目标。

**相关性简评：** 其「用标量奖励把测试期搜索摊销为一次学习式状态修正」与「对原始先验的 KL 上界约束」可能可供核查 latent 推理中状态更新训练及分布漂移约束的线索，但不涉及视觉推理本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Noise Optimization for Reward Alignment in Few-Step Generative Models

**abstract/A1：** Reward alignment for diffusion- and flow-based generative models is often formulated over stochastic trajectories, making it less suitable for few-step deterministic generators. Initial noise optimization provides a trajectory-free alternative, but existing gradient-free methods require costly per-sample search at inference. We introduce LeNO (Learning Noise Optimization), which amortizes reward-guided initial noise optimization into a learned residual transport while keeping the pretrained generator frozen. LeNO learns an anchor-conditioned distribution over noise corrections using Local GRPO and scalar rewards, eliminating iterative test-time optimization. For Gaussian initial noise with norm-preserving projection, we derive a tractable upper bound on the KL divergence between the corrected noise distribution and the original prior, providing a principled regularization objective. We evaluate LeNO on few-step text-to-image and protein structure generation, including expensive black-box designability rewards. Across both domains, LeNO improves target rewards with a single noise correction at inference while largely preserving generation quality and diversity.

</details>

<a id="paper-115"></a>

### 115. [CrossPressor: Scalable Cross-View Fusion for Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06258v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06258

**作者：** Yuxi Hu、Wenhao Dong、Jun Zhang、Friedrich Fraundorfer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06258v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06258v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对前馈 3D Gaussian Splatting 在长多视角序列中锚点固定、单个锚点需聚合越来越多视角的「表示—融合失配」，提出免训练的 CrossPressor，用覆盖轮转的证据路由把保留的空间证据分散到各支持视角与融合层，并降低融合开销。

**相关性简评：** 其固定容量瓶颈下强约束「所有支持视角都被表示」的证据路由思路，可能为 LVR 在有限 latent 预算内筛选视觉证据、避免单一视角主导与信息坍缩提供可核查的方法线索；但任务属 3D 重建，迁移价值需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CrossPressor: Scalable Cross-View Fusion for Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting

**abstract/A1：** Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS) becomes increasingly difficult to scale to long multi-view sequences. Anchor-based compression methods such as ZPressor alleviate representation growth by decoding Gaussians only from a compact set of anchor feature maps, but keeping these anchors fixed forces each one to aggregate an increasing number of support views. We identify this representation--fusion mismatch and introduce \\textbf{CrossPressor}, a training-free method for scalable anchor-based fusion. CrossPressor preserves all decoder-facing anchor queries while constraining the view-growing support sequence through coverage-cycled evidence routing, distributing retained spatial evidence across support views and fusion layers while keeping all support views represented. Routing before K/V projection, together with joint context packing, further reduces support-side fusion cost without retraining. CrossPressor maintains reconstruction quality close to dense fusion while achieving up to $26.12\\times$ faster cross-view fusion and $1.43\\times$ end-to-end acceleration on long input sequences. Our project page is at: https://yuhsihu.github.io/CrossPressor.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

### 116. [Consistency Learning and Distillation for One-Step MIMO-OFDM Channel Estimation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06271v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06271

**作者：** Fredrik Nguyen、Qiwen Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06271v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06271v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究 MIMO-OFDM 信道估计中把迭代神经估计器压缩为一步推理，提出观测锚定的一致性学习与 Consistency Mid-Training，利用流匹配教师轨迹的中间状态蒸馏出紧凑学生模型，在显著减少参数与计算量下降低误符号率。

**相关性简评：** 「从轨迹中间状态学习端点预测」的一致性蒸馏思路，可能为 LVR 中把多步迭代推理压缩为少步或一步 latent 状态更新、以及轨迹监督的贡献分配提供方法线索，但任务属无线信道估计，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Consistency Learning and Distillation for One-Step MIMO-OFDM Channel Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Iterative neural channel estimators can improve recovery under pilot contamination, but repeated network evaluations increase inference cost. This paper develops observation-anchored consistency and Consistency Mid-Training (CMT) estimators for one-step channel estimation in multiple-input multiple-output orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (MIMO-OFDM) systems. Both estimators refine pilot-derived least-squares channel estimates in the delay-beam domain. Consistency learning couples endpoint predictions along noisy-to-clean paths. CMT then refines a compact consistency-trained student to predict a flow-matching teacher's endpoint from intermediate trajectory states. Across the evaluated SNR range, the compact CMT student reduces uncoded symbol error rate (SER) by 9.6-20.2% relative to the one-step flow-matching baseline (0.44-0.98 dB in logarithmic SER reduction), while using 74.5% fewer parameters and 73.0% fewer network multiply-accumulates.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables

</details>
