### 117. [MonoLaDN: Language-Guided DeNoising for Monocular 3D Object Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06282v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06282

**作者：** Kiet Dang Vu、Cao Le Minh Khoa、Nguyen Viet Tri Pham、An Nguyen Gia、Tam Le Thuc Ho、Duc Dung Nguyen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06282v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06282v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MonoLaDN，在训练期用冻结 VLM 对每个目标的遮挡、深度、尺寸与角度生成结构化推理，将其编码为 query 并加噪，让单目 3D 检测器学习重建干净推理；推理时丢弃该路径，部署模型仍为纯视觉。

**相关性简评：** 其「把外部推理写入 query、再以去噪重建监督使其内化」的训练机制，可供核查 LVR 中推理轨迹的监督方式与丢弃推理路径后 latent 是否真被使用、是否发生信息坍缩；但任务属单目 3D 检测，与 VLM 推理期状态生成无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MonoLaDN: Language-Guided DeNoising for Monocular 3D Object Detection

**abstract/A1：** Monocular 3D object detection offers a low-cost route to 3D perception, but it remains fundamentally ill-posed, since depth must be inferred from a single view. Recent transformer-based detectors learn geometry purely from image features and 3D box annotations, lacking the high-level semantic priors that vision-language models (VLMs) acquire from large-scale pretraining. VLMs offer such priors, but deploying one directly as a detector is impractical due to its heavy inference cost. We propose MonoLaDN, a language-guided denoising framework that transfers the geometric reasoning of a VLM into a monocular 3D detector while keeping the deployed model purely vision-based. During training, a frozen VLM produces structured reasoning about each object's occlusion, depth, dimension, and angle from the image and its ground-truth boxes. We encode this reasoning into queries, perturb them with noise, and train the detector to reconstruct the clean reasoning. Reconstructing the corrupted reasoning forces the detector's depth-guided features to internalize the VLM's geometric priors, so this knowledge remains available even after the reasoning path is discarded at inference, adding no latency or parameters to the deployed model. Experiments on KITTI 3D and nuScenes show that MonoLaDN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art detectors and generalizes well to new domains.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at ACCV 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-118"></a>

### 118. [STEK: Structural-Terrain-Enhanced Koopman-Inspired Cross-Channel Regularization for Retrospective Battery Multi-Target Estimation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06299v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06299

**作者：** Jing Wang、Shiyuan Piao、Fugee Tsung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06299v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06299v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 STEK 框架，把电池单体级生命周期 dQ/dV「地形」与端点曲线派生的结构代理经质量代理门控融合，并用有界 FiLM、有效性门控残差头和类 Koopman 的跨通道一致性目标做回溯式多目标估计。

**相关性简评：** 其「保留原曲线通路、以有效性与质量门控注入辅助结构」的设计以及辅助描述符缺失时可平滑回退的检验，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据的条件性注入与旁路失效诊断提供门控与一致性正则线索，需核查原文机制；论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** STEK: Structural-Terrain-Enhanced Koopman-Inspired Cross-Channel Regularization for Retrospective Battery Multi-Target Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Retrospective battery characterization across heterogeneous cells is challenging because cycling records vary substantially with chemistry, protocol, cell format, capacity, and operating conditions. An endpoint charge--discharge curve captures the local condition of an evaluated cycle, while a cell-level lifecycle terrain summarizes structural evolution across the archived trajectory. We propose STEK, a structural-terrain-enhanced framework for retrospective battery multi-target estimation. STEK combines a cell-level lifecycle (dQ/dV) terrain with an endpoint-derived structural proxy through quality-proxy-conditioned gating. Bounded FiLM and a validity-gated residual head refine the endpoint-curve representation while preserving a curve-based prediction pathway. Battery-type and optional material-text representations further condition an identity-centered low-rank linear map, providing a Koopman-inspired cross-channel consistency objective without modeling temporal dynamics or performing rollout. Under the fixed seven-output MIX-battery protocol, the full retrospective setting improves aggregate performance over endpoint-only learned baselines across multiple temporal backbones; this comparison reflects both the additional archived context and the modeling design. Under matched auxiliary inputs, STEK outperforms direct concatenation and quality-unaware FiLM, while descriptor-removal tests show graceful fallback to the endpoint pathway. Deterministic rules remain preferable for directly recoverable capacity summaries. The method targets retrospective archive harmonization rather than online estimation from cycle t alone.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 4figures

</details>

<a id="paper-119"></a>

### 119. [NanoKV: High-Ratio KV Cache Compression for Autoregressive Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06377v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06377

**作者：** Jiacheng Lu、Haoyi Zhu、Sipei Yi、Enze Xie、Cheng Zhuo、Yu Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06377v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06377v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** NanoKV 提出一种冻结主干的高倍率 KV cache 压缩框架：将跨 token 与注意力头的共享内容存入聚类中心，把联合头—通道残差编码为低位系数，并针对 Key 与 Value 在注意力中的不同角色分别设计拟合目标；另提供复用这些系数做混合维度注意力以降低计算的 NanoKV-Fast 变体。

**相关性简评：** 该文面向视频生成而非 VLM 推理，但其「为 Key 保留下游块输出、为 Value 保真重建」的差异化拟合目标与混合维度注意力，可能为 LVR 中历史 latent 状态的缓存压缩、按贡献分配监督及计算量动态削减提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NanoKV: High-Ratio KV Cache Compression for Autoregressive Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Autoregressive video models rely on historical key-value (KV) caches to preserve scene consistency over extended rollouts, making KV storage and repeated attention over history a growing memory and computational bottleneck. Aggressive compression can degrade generation quality, while reconstructing full-dimensional KV leaves attention computation largely unchanged. We introduce NanoKV, a frozen-backbone framework for generation-aware high-ratio KV compression. NanoKV exploits redundancy across tokens and attention heads by storing shared content in cluster centers and encoding joint head-channel residuals into low-bit coefficients. Crucially, NanoKV uses different fitting objectives for Keys and Values according to their distinct roles in attention: Key decoders are optimized to preserve downstream block outputs, whereas Value encoders and decoders are fitted for faithful reconstruction through the deployed quantization path. Building on the same compressed representation, the optional NanoKV-Fast variant reuses the stored coefficients for mixed-dimensional attention, reducing computation without modifying the video backbone. Across three autoregressive video backbones, NanoKV consistently improves the quality-storage trade-off. On HY-WorldPlay, NanoKV achieves $26.09\\times$ effective KV compression, nearly twice that of KVTC, while improving PSNR by 1.0 dB. NanoKV-Fast retains $20.53\\times$ effective compression and achieves an approximately $1.15\\times$ end-to-end speedup over KVTC.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 13 figures. Project page: https://jiacheng-lu03.github.io/NanoKV/ . Code: https://github.com/jiacheng-lu03/NanoKV

</details>

<a id="paper-120"></a>

### 120. [LegalEvolver: Agentic Legal Judgment Prediction via Rubric-Guided Self-Evolution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06381v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06381

**作者：** Xinyu Yang、Jiejun Tan、Chenlong Deng、Chenghao Zhang、Tongyu Wen、Zhicheng Dou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06381v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06381v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LegalEvolver：面向法律判决预测的检索查询优化框架，用启发式方式生成初始优化查询作为种子以提高搜索轨迹采样效率，并把 rollout 经验总结为评分细则（rubric），通过迭代的细则优化与 rubric 引导的强化学习实现自我进化，提升多步推理与外部法条检索的表现。

**相关性简评：** 虽属纯文本法律 agent，但其「由潜在问题推导检索查询」与「rubric 迭代作为训练监督信号」的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据获取（LOOK）的查询构造与轨迹级贡献分配提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LegalEvolver: Agentic Legal Judgment Prediction via Rubric-Guided Self-Evolution

**abstract/A1：** As LLM-based agents develop, they have been increasingly applied in the legal domain. Legal Judgment Prediction~(LJP) requires models to conduct multi-step reasoning and external legal evidence retrieval. Existing legal search agents' performance is often constrained by the quality of retrieval queries. They often formulate queries directly from the initial user request, overlooking latent legal issues and legally salient details. To address this issue, we propose \\emph{LegalEvolver}, a query optimization framework specifically designed to generate legally informative queries for LJP-oriented retrieval. Sampling high-quality optimized queries and their corresponding search trajectories is challenging, as relying solely on unguided high-temperature rollouts is both inefficient and constrained by the model's initial capabilities. We therefore heuristically optimize queries as seeds, improving the efficiency of trajectory generation. Furthermore, we summarize rollout experience into rubrics and use them to guide multi-round iterative self-evolution. Iteratively performing rubric optimization and rubric-guided RL enables the model to surpass its initial performance ceiling. End-to-end performance on extensive benchmarks demonstrates the effectiveness of query optimization on LJP. Comparisons with direct SFT followed by RL demonstrate the effectiveness of rubric guidance. Evolving rubrics further outperform fixed rubrics, confirming the benefits of self-evolution.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-121"></a>

### 121. [Unaligned Image Guided Denoising via Cross-modal Conditional Flow Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06422v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06422

**作者：** Runmin Zhang、Linshan Li、Si-Yuan Cao、Zhu Yu、Lingyu Zhu、Huaqi Zhang、Hui-Liang Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06422v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06422v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 UGD-FM，将跨模态（未对齐、大位移）引导的图像去噪表述为渐进式 cross-modal conditional flow matching 过程，使复原与引导聚合相互促进，并用面向关键阶段、训练—推理一致的策略实现一至两步推理。

**相关性简评：** 其「渐进 flow matching 状态更新 + 阶段聚焦且训练—推理一致」的少步推理训练思路，可能为 diffusion/flow 式 latent 推理状态的更新与压缩迭代提供可核查线索，但任务本身是图像复原，与 VLM 推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Unaligned Image Guided Denoising via Cross-modal Conditional Flow Matching

**abstract/A1：** This work presents UGD-FM, a novel unaligned image guided denoising framework based on cross-modal conditional flow matching. Unlike previous approaches that assume spatially aligned inputs or only handle small-baseline rectified pairs, we address a more challenging setting where severe target noise and large-range two-dimensional cross-modal misalignment coexist. Specifically, we formulate guided denoising as a progressive flow matching process, allowing image restoration and aligned guidance aggregation to mutually reinforce each other. To support efficient few-step inference, we introduce a stage-focused and inference-consistent training strategy that focuses on task-critical denoising stages, and mitigates the training-inference mismatch. We further propose adaptive sparse guidance propagation for efficient and reliable large-range matching, regularized by perceptual and geometric auxiliary losses. Experiments demonstrate that UGD-FM achieves state-of-the-art denoising performance with only one or two inference steps, and is compatible with modern single-image restoration backbones.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-122"></a>

### 122. [D3D: Decoupled Diffusion Drafting for Asynchronous Speculative Decoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06425v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06425

**作者：** Baoyou Chen、Ruiqiao Mei、Guancong Lin、Tao Song、Weihao Yuan、Qun Liu、Siyu Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06425v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06425v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 D3D：一个独立的扩散式草稿模型，用块级去噪加辅助自回归监督，在不依赖目标模型隐状态的条件下并行生成候选 token 块，从而让草稿与验证异步并行，并按对接受长度的影响剪枝层以降低开销，在多个基准上取得约 5 倍解码加速。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关系，但其「解除轮次间隐状态依赖、并行生成整块」及按下游接受度剪枝的线索，可供核查 LVR 中 parallel 推理状态更新与视觉/目标信息旁路依赖的取舍。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** D3D: Decoupled Diffusion Drafting for Asynchronous Speculative Decoding

**abstract/A1：** Target-conditioned diffusion drafters predict candidate tokens in parallel, yet each new block must wait for updated target-model hidden states. This forces drafting and verification to run sequentially, limiting speedup even though each block is generated in one pass. We introduce D3D, a standalone diffusion drafter that removes this inter-round dependency. D3D combines blockwise denoising with auxiliary autoregressive supervision to achieve long accepted prefixes without target hidden states. This independence lets D3D draft successor blocks during verification in one packed pass, enabling asynchronous speculative decoding. We progressively prune layers according to their effect on acceptance length to reduce the cost of packed drafting. Removing five of 36 layers retains over 99\\% of mean acceptance length across three benchmarks with Qwen3-8B as the target. Across seven benchmarks, D3D averages 5.00$\\times$ and 5.71$\\times$ decoding speedup with Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-32B targets, compared with 4.03$\\times$ and 3.67$\\times$ for DFlash. On GSM8K with Qwen3-32B, speedup reaches 7.48$\\times$. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/fudan-generative-vision/D3D.

</details>

<a id="paper-123"></a>

### 123. [Understanding the Out-of-Distribution Generalization of Chain-of-Thought Reasoning in LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06438v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06438

**作者：** Nuo-Jing Liang、Xiao-Tong Yuan、Pan Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06438v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06438v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文将 CoT 视为组合式 OOD 泛化，在仅提供分布内原子步骤数据的设定下，分析共享单步预测器组合推理的风险界与估计误差，并用 Atom-Task 和 GSM8K 实验检验短到长及组合泛化。

**相关性简评：** 其对推理步数 T、每步 token 数和样本量如何影响误差累积，以及显式状态跟踪重要性的结论，可为 LVR 的长度泛化、轨迹训练与状态更新提供可核查的分析线索，但并非视觉或多模态 latent 推理研究。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Understanding the Out-of-Distribution Generalization of Chain-of-Thought Reasoning in LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning substantially improves Large Language Models (LLMs), yet its out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization mechanism remains theoretically underexplored. We study CoT as compositional OOD generalization: the target task lacks complete reasoning trajectories or direct input-output pairs, while training provides only in-distribution (ID) atomic step data. We show that CoT generalizes by repeatedly applying a shared single-step predictor to compose reusable ID transitions. Theoretically, let $T$ denote the number of reasoning steps in the target task, $n$ the number of training samples per sub-task, and $k$ the number of output tokens per step; then the OOD task risk is controlled by the sum of the ID sub-task risks, scaled by a coefficient of order $\\mathcal{O}(k)$ that is independent of $T$, although the summed risk itself may still grow with $T$. A Rademacher-complexity analysis further shows that the estimation term is of order $\\mathcal{O}(\\sqrt{T/n})$, rather than linear in $T$, due to predictor sharing under a uniform capacity control on the shared class. For autoregressive Next-Token Prediction, the final-task risk is bounded by the best-in-class step-level risk scaled by the same coefficient of order $\\mathcal{O}(k)$, plus an estimation term of order $\\widetilde{\\mathcal{O}}\\big(k\\sqrt{T/n}\\big)$. A parity-coupled construction further shows that, for the sub-task empirical risk minimizer, the expected final-task risk is at least the best in-class risk plus $Ω(\\sqrt{T/n})$ when $T\\le n$. Experiments on Atom-Task and GSM8K support the theory: CoT enables short-to-long and compositional generalization, while explicit state tracking is crucial. Our code is available at https://github.com/maxliang114514/CoT-OOD.

</details>

<a id="paper-124"></a>

### 124. [SUMONE: A Simple and Efficient Summarizer for Plug-and-Play Agent Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06445v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06445

**作者：** Jizhe Zhang、Ang Li、Chenheng Zhang、Yisen Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06445v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06445v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SumOne：一个独立的摘要器，用冻结的重构器从摘要中恢复被随机掩码的原始轨迹片段，以重建质量加长度惩罚作为任务无关奖励，通过 GRPO 训练，用于长程 agent 历史的推理期上下文压缩。

**相关性简评：** 其「掩码重建 + 长度惩罚」训练信号是一种可直接借鉴的信息保留监督线索，可能用于缓解 LVR 中压缩潜在状态的信息坍缩，并绕开下游任务奖励；但论文面向纯文本 agent 记忆压缩，与视觉证据保留的关联需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SUMONE: A Simple and Efficient Summarizer for Plug-and-Play Agent Memory

**abstract/A1：** Long-horizon language-model agents accumulate increasingly large interaction histories, making context compaction essential for efficient execution. Existing learned compaction methods often rely on downstream task outcomes or solver behavior, coupling training to specific tasks or agents. We introduce SumOne, a standalone summarizer trained to preserve information in agent histories through masked reconstruction. Given a summary of the expired history, a frozen reconstructor recovers randomly masked portions of the original trajectory; reconstruction quality, combined with a length penalty, provides a task-agnostic reward for optimizing the summarizer with GRPO. This yields self-supervision directly from raw trajectories, without reference summaries or downstream task execution. At inference time, only the lightweight summarizer is retained, enabling plug-and-play context compression for otherwise unchanged agents. We conduct a systematic evaluation of SumOne in long-horizon search and software-engineering tasks. SumOne reduces estimated inference cost by approximately 78\\% on DeepSearchQA and 75\\% on SWE-bench Verified relative to operating with full context, with absolute decreases of 0.04. The same checkpoint also transfers across downstream models and agent harnesses without additional fine-tuning.

</details>

<a id="paper-125"></a>

### 125. [The Alignment Tax Is Graded: Task-Selective Capability Loss Under Length-Normalized DPO with SFT Retention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06476v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06476

**作者：** Ye Yuan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06476v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06476v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 一项纯文本的经验刻画研究：在金融申报结构化任务上用稀疏 LoRA 与长度归一化 DPO 加 SFT 保留项微调 Qwen3.5-4B，追踪 reward margin 增大时下游能力的变化，发现「对齐税」是分级的、任务选择性的（抽取基本保持、json 小降、router 严重退化）。

**相关性简评：** 虽无视觉输入与 latent 状态，其「margin 增长—任务选择性退化」的诊断口径（等价检验、退化窗口定位、chosen/rejected 奖励分解）可供核查是否可迁移到评估 LVR 训练是否损伤对视觉证据的使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Alignment Tax Is Graded: Task-Selective Capability Loss Under Length-Normalized DPO with SFT Retention

**abstract/A1：** Setting. This is an empirical characterization study; we propose no new method and run no comparisons against alternative objectives. We fine-tune Qwen3.5-4B with sparse LoRA and length-normalized DPO plus an SFT retention term (the RPO objective) on structured financial-filing tasks, and track downstream capability as the implicit reward margin grows to $\\sim 7$ $β$-scaled units (nf4 training; $\\sim 3$ under fp16 measurement on the unquantized base). Question. Does margin growth degrade capability uniformly across tasks, or selectively? Findings. In a pre-registered study (8 seeds, 6 unseen at freeze) with artifact-aware scoring and TOST equivalence testing, the alignment tax is graded and task-selective: extraction is preserved ($Δ= -0.55$ pts, equivalent at $\\pm5$ pts), json loses little ($Δ= -3.52$ pts, equivalence fails on a clustered item-level interval), and router degrades severely ($Δ= -30$ pts). Router's loss concentrates in a margin 1-3 damage window containing a transient format-break spike. The margin is accounted for almost entirely ($\\geq 96\\%$) by suppression of rejected completions, with chosen reward approximately flat, consistent with the retention term anchoring it; we do not attribute the behavioral damage to any single gradient channel. Gold-logp deflation is task-selective (extraction $-0.15$, json $-1.04$, router $-1.47$ nats/token under fp16), with gold selectively protected relative to incorrect controls on extraction (8/8 seeds). QLoRA reward margins differ up to $3.4\\times$ between nf4 training and unquantized fp16 measurement, with an axis-dependent gap. Scope. Length-normalized rewards with SFT retention, sparse LoRA on 8/32 layers, one 4B model, easily-separable synthetic preferences, three structured tasks. A no-retention control and a measured format-alignment metric are left to future work.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 3 figures, 23 tables. Code: https://github.com/yuan-phd/delta-filing

</details>

<a id="paper-126"></a>

### 126. [Nowcasting Future Performance for Population Pruning in Survival-of-the-Fittest Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06512v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06512

**作者：** Rico Städeli、Léo Meynent、Boris Knyazev、Damian Borth

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06512v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06512v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究能否用中间训练检查点预测未来的测试精度，以支撑候选模型种群的提前剪枝；nowcaster 以准确率与损失历史、权重统计以及 SANE 表示（将检查点编码为 latent token 序列的 transformer 自编码器）为输入，预测五个 epoch 后或训练结束时的精度，并在 Survival-of-Fittest 训练中据此排序、停止靠后的候选。

**相关性简评：** 其「用 latent token 编码模型状态并预测后续表现」的做法可供核查是否能迁移到 LVR 的推理状态表示与停止决策（预判继续计算是否值得），但研究对象是训练期种群剪枝，与视觉或多模态推理本身无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Nowcasting Future Performance for Population Pruning in Survival-of-the-Fittest Training

**abstract/A1：** Training high-performing neural networks often involves evaluating populations of candidate models with varying hyperparameters, architectures, or random seeds. Training every candidate to completion is computationally expensive, yet stopping seemingly weaker runs early risks discarding models that would ultimately perform better, as learning curves can cross. To address this challenge, we investigate whether intermediate training checkpoints can predict future model accuracy and enable more informed pruning decisions. Our nowcasters predict test accuracy five epochs ahead or at the end of training. They are trained with accuracy and loss histories, weight statistics, and SANE representations as inputs. SANE is a transformer autoencoder that encodes a checkpoint as a sequence of latent tokens. We then use the nowcasters in Survival-of-the-Fittest (SOTF) training. SOTF repeatedly ranks runs during training by predicted accuracy and stops the lowest-ranked ones. Our results show that weight statistics work best for five-epoch forecasts, and SANE representations work best for final accuracy prediction. For evaluation, we compare SOTF with pruning the population by current accuracy. In homogeneous model zoos such as the CNN zoos, pruning by current accuracy already retains a final model only slightly worse than the best. Model zoos with a larger spread in performance, such as the ResNet-18 and ViT zoos, are more difficult to prune by current accuracy alone. In these zoos, SOTF with nowcasted final accuracy almost closes the gap to the best model.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at: 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026). Workshop: Neural Network Artifacts as a New Data Modality

</details>

<a id="paper-127"></a>

### 127. [EvoOPD: Evolving Privileged Information Strategies for Black-Box On-Policy Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06542v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06542

**作者：** Ximo Zhu、Ruiqi Liu、Yi Han、Zhengan Wu、Gengsheng Li、ZiAn Wang、Zhu Yuantian、Jin Jiang、Wenhao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06542v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06542v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** EvoOPD 将黑盒教师的文本反馈当作特权信息，经 PI 条件化的教师模型转化为学生自身轨迹上的稠密监督，并把「向教师索取何种 PI」本身当作优化问题，用测得的修复效用演化 PI 策略库，在数学与代码任务上以 Qwen3 学生验证。

**相关性简评：** 其按学生状态动态调整反馈焦点、类型与粒度的做法，可能为 LVR 轨迹训练中的监督构造与贡献分配提供借鉴；但属纯文本蒸馏，需核查效用度量能否迁移到视觉推理的 latent 状态筛选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EvoOPD: Evolving Privileged Information Strategies for Black-Box On-Policy Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Proprietary language models exhibit strong reasoning and coding capabilities, but their output distributions are inaccessible, making fine-grained distillation methods that rely on teacher logits difficult to apply directly to black-box models. We observe that rich textual feedback from a black-box teacher can be treated as privileged information (PI) and converted, through a PI-conditioned privileged teacher, into dense supervision signals on the student's own trajectories. However, what PI should be acquired from the teacher to most effectively support student learning remains unclear. PI strategies that differ in where they focus, what information they request, and how much detail they provide can have substantially different effects on the current student, and their relative effectiveness can further change as training progresses. Based on this observation, we propose Evolving Privileged Information Strategies for Black-Box On-Policy Distillation (EvoOPD), which treats PI acquisition itself as an optimization problem and maintains a PI Strategy Bank that evolves with the student state. EvoOPD uses the measured repair utility of PI on the current student to adapt the focus, information type, and granularity of teacher feedback, allowing the acquired information to continually match the student's learning needs. We systematically evaluate EvoOPD on mathematical reasoning and code generation with Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B students. EvoOPD achieves the best overall performance in all six model-task settings, improving over the strongest baseline in each setting by an average of 2.14 percentage points in mathematics and 1.30 points in code. Further analysis shows that the relative effectiveness of PI acquisition strategies changes with student state, making a fixed strategy difficult to sustain as the best choice throughout distillation.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-128"></a>

### 128. [Using Experience Twice: Dynamic Process Rubrics for Privileged Token-Level Credit Assignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06543v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06543

**作者：** Shumao Wu、Kongcheng Zhang、Jianqiang Huang、Baisheng Lai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06543v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06543v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 Online Rubric Self-Distillation（ORSD）：在每步由当前策略基于同一组轨迹自生成多个自然语言过程评分量表，用冻结验证器按与任务成功的对齐度选出其一，再以「有/无该 rubric」条件下的 token 对数似然差作为过程信用，叠加到 GRPO 的轨迹级优势上，实现 token 级信用分配，并在 ALFWorld、WebShop 上验证效果。

**相关性简评：** 该文属纯文本智能体的训练期监督方法，但其「以自生成过程信号提供 token 级贡献分配、与结果级奖励加性融合」的思路，可能为 LVR 中轨迹训练与信用分配、以及 latent 内容是否被答案实际使用这一问题提供可核查的对照线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Using Experience Twice: Dynamic Process Rubrics for Privileged Token-Level Credit Assignment

**abstract/A1：** Agent trajectories often contain both successful decisions and failed attempts, yet optimization with sparse trajectory-level rewards struggles to identify which critical decisions should be reinforced or suppressed. We observe that meaningful distinctions among on-policy trajectories can be distilled into natural-language process rubrics and used as process supervision signals. Based on this insight, we propose Online Rubric Self-Distillation (ORSD), which leverages self-derived process rubrics for dense token-level supervision, thereby augmenting outcome-centric reinforcement learning. Specifically, the current policy independently generates multiple rubric bundles at each step according to the same group of trajectories, extracting candidate process rubrics from cross-trajectory behavioral differences. A frozen verifier then evaluates all rubric items against each trajectory in the group, and selects one candidate bundle based on bundle-level alignment between rubric satisfaction and task success. ORSD treats the selected rubric bundle as privileged information during training, then compares the log-likelihoods of the original tokens with and without the rubric. The resulting likelihood gap provides token-specific process credit, which is added to the original trajectory-level GRPO advantage through additive advantage shaping. ORSD thus bridges trajectory-level outcome supervision and fine-grained process supervision, enabling token-level credit assignment using process signals. Extensive experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show that ORSD accelerates policy improvement and boosts final task performance for both Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B under the same environment-interaction budget. Our code is available at https://github.com/AnnaXChun/ORSD.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 3 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-129"></a>

### 129. [MKD2World: A Typed Spatial MarkDown World Model For Web Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06544v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06544

**作者：** Zhihao Nan、Sifan Zhou、Yiming Cheng、Jinkai Zheng、Zheng Zhu、Si Shi、Ming Li、Fei Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06544v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06544v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Typed Spatial Markdown 文本状态表示，按空间关系序列化桌面网页 UI 元素的类型、视觉语义、显式位置与层级，构建 AtomBlock-WebUI 数据集训练截图到该表示的渲染器，并训练两阶段的动作条件下一状态预测世界模型，推理时由 agent 抽取任务相关证据。

**相关性简评：** 其空间化状态序列化与「digest」证据抽取步骤可供核查：显式编码位置与层级的状态表示、预测状态中的视觉细节保留程度及预测证据如何筛入决策，可能与 LVR 的内部状态表示和视觉证据筛选问题相通，但对象是外部文本世界模型而非 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MKD2World: A Typed Spatial MarkDown World Model For Web Agents

**abstract/A1：** Graphical user interface (GUI) agents can improve decision-making with world-model foresight, provided that predicted states accurately reflect post-action interfaces. Existing state representations fail to jointly capture interface layout, UI semantics, and explicit locations. We introduce Typed Spatial Markdown (TSM), a Markdown-style textual state representation for desktop web interfaces. TSM serializes block-level and atomic-level UI elements according to their spatial relationships, jointly encoding their types, visual semantics, explicit locations, optional control states, and hierarchical membership. To train a screenshot-to-TSM renderer, we construct AtomBlock-WebUI, a dataset with precise annotations at both granularities. By injecting element-type labels into HTML and directly extracting bounding boxes after rendering, we avoid annotation errors caused by post-hoc inference from webpage structures. Building on TSM, we propose MKD2World, a desktop web world model trained in two stages for current-state description and action-conditioned next-state prediction. During inference, the web agent queries MKD2World as needed and performs a \`\`digest'' step, in which it interprets each predicted state and extracts task-relevant evidence for selecting the best action. Experiments show that MKD2World-9B achieves an EleF1 score of 26.50 on the out-of-domain test set, outperforming GPT-5.6-Sol(14.98) by 11.52 points. Across Online-Mind2Web tasks, MKD2World-9B improves the success rate of the Qwen3.5-35B web agent from 22.94% to 37.61%, achieving competitive performance among leading open-source GUI world models. Code is available at https://github.com/OmniAI-Lab-CUHKSZ/MKD2World.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-130"></a>

### 130. [SpikePose: Lightweight Video Human Pose Estimation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06594v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06594

**作者：** Yunhao Huang、Kaushik Bhargav Sivangi、Tianyi Yu、Fani Deligianni

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.NE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06594v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06594v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SpikePose，将帧重置的单步脉冲编码器用于逐帧空间证据提取，并配运动对齐热图记忆（MAM）估计关节位移、对齐历史热图并与当前预测自适应融合，在 MPII 与 NTU60 子集上比较定位精度、运动误差和估算能耗。

**相关性简评：** 其 MAM 先按估计位移对齐历史热图再加权融合，可供核查 LVR 中跨推理步 latent 状态的对齐、历史证据保留与筛选；其解析能耗/精度权衡也可供参考迭代式 THINK/LOOK 的成本度量，但任务为视频人体姿态估计、无显式推理与答案使用机制，迁移仅为推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SpikePose: Lightweight Video Human Pose Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Efficient video human pose estimation must balance localisation accuracy, motion quality, and energy cost. We present SpikePose, a lightweight video pose estimator that pairs a frame-reset, single-step spiking encoder with motion-aligned heatmap memory (MAM). The encoder supplies per-frame spatial evidence, while MAM estimates joint displacement, aligns historical heatmaps, and adaptively fuses them with current predictions. By confining temporal reasoning to a compact, lightweight module outside the spiking backbone, SpikePose helps bridge the gap between spiking and classical Artificial Neural Networks (ANN) on large-scale, non-event-driven video tasks. We evaluate spatial accuracy, motion errors, and estimated arithmetic energy on MPII and a fixed NTU60 Cross-Subject subset. Against the architecture-matched ANN with MAM, SpikePose cuts estimated arithmetic energy by 91.4% at a cost of just 1.12 points in head-bone-normalised PCK at 0.5 threshold (PCK\_HB(0.5)). Within SpikePose, adding MAM reduces velocity and acceleration errors by 28.13% and 31.46% while slightly improving localisation, at a cost of only 0.003M parameters and 0.0023 analytical GFLOPs per frame. Code will be released on GitHub upon acceptance.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, submitted to ICASSP 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-131"></a>

### 131. [The Behavioral Rate of Fine-Tuning: Compression as a Measure of Useful Information](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06598v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06598

**作者：** Joey David、Paul Caillon、Alexandre Allauzen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06598v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06598v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出操作性的「行为速率」R\*，即以重新载入冻结基座后仍能保留固定比例微调增益的最小压缩适配器序列化大小来度量微调写入的有用信息，发现该速率由数据集与基座模型共同决定，并观察到压缩能滤除集中在适配器主导奇异方向上的噪声，使损坏数据训练的适配器反而在压缩后超过基座模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「以压缩后行为保持量衡量有用信息、区分任务必要成分与噪声」的操作化思路，可能为量化 latent 推理状态或适配增量中的有效信息与信息坍缩提供测量线索，可供核查；但原文限于纯文本 LoRA，迁移到视觉推理状态还需较多验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Behavioral Rate of Fine-Tuning: Compression as a Measure of Useful Information

**abstract/A1：** Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapts a frozen language model through a compact learned update, making it a natural setting for asking how much information fine-tuning needs to write. Yet adapter rank, parameter count, or numerical reconstruction error do not directly tell us how many bits are required to preserve the acquired behavior. We therefore define an operational behavioral rate $R^\\star$ as the smallest serialized size of a compressed adapter that, once reloaded into the frozen base model, retains a fixed fraction of its held-out fine-tuning gain. Across LoRA fine-tuning experiments, this rate increases with distinct training content at fixed optimization budgets, but is not determined by intuitive metrics like source description length, fine-tuning gain, or weight-space reconstruction error. Most importantly, the same datasets exhibit substantially different, and even reordered, rates across frozen base models, showing that fine-tuning compressibility is a property of the dataset--receiver pair rather than of the dataset alone. However, we show that correction spectra measured on the data are able to rank these rates within a receiver. Finally, we study a striking noise-reducing effect of compression: adapters trained on deliberately corrupted datasets fall far below the frozen model, yet after compression score above it (23.7% to 82.9% on GSM8K, against 77.6% for frozen Qwen2.5-7B), consistently across tested families and sizes. The damage is unevenly concentrated in the adapter's leading singular directions, which compression appears to filter. Away from its training task, the compressed corrupted adapter also generalizes better than a clean fine-tune: on harder math it retains 97% of base accuracy, versus 44% for the clean adapter.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-132"></a>

### 132. [Time-Division Multiplexing: The Operational Rhythm and Temporal Organization Layer of Digital Life](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06603v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06603

**作者：** Haibin Tong、Luming Guo、Nana Cui、Jiang Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、q-bio.NC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06603v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06603v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一个“时间组织层”：以带相位标签的微时间片、可观测占空比与三层嵌套时钟，将代理的候选探索与长期承诺分开调度，并给出占空比更新的下降性保证、噪声项与 busy/idle 执行契约，配合五组合成实验及 Qwen3-8B 编排原型验证。

**相关性简评：** 其相位化时间片与嵌套时钟的调度契约，可能为 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的顺序编排与动态计算分配提供可核查的机制线索；但该工作面向通用持续代理且明确无任务基准证据，能否迁移到 latent 推理状态尚待确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Time-Division Multiplexing: The Operational Rhythm and Temporal Organization Layer of Digital Life

**abstract/A1：** Continual agents must handle urgent interaction while retaining alternatives, replaying errors, and committing safe long-term changes. A previous digital-life framework distinguishes online (busy) and offline (idle) channels, but leaves open whether these are separate programs or phases of a common process and how their operations should be ordered. Motivated by recent observations of rapidly alternating perceptual representations in primate cortex, we develop a temporal organization layer with phase-tagged micro-epochs, an observable duty cycle, independent content and resource controls, and three nested clocks separating candidate exploration from durable commitment. We give a projected duty-cycle update, state its deterministic descent guarantee and the additional noise term required for a stochastic guarantee, and specify coupled busy/idle execution contracts. Five synthetic experiments (64 seeds each) examine descent and noise, objective misalignment, gain asymmetry, resource coupling, and dropped replay logs. In the gain/noise model the best tested gain ratio is 1.55; decoupled scheduling yields 26.6% versus 11.1% success on a constructed urgent/high-load subset. A frozen-backbone Qwen3-8B prototype further checks orchestration, but its proxy outcomes are not task-benchmark evidence. The results establish a testable scheduling design and identify its conditions of validity, not machine consciousness or general-purpose agent superiority.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-133"></a>

### 133. [From Reconstruction to Imputation: Test-Time Optimization for Missing Time-Series Channels](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06612v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06612

**作者：** Björn Ludwig、Alexander Windmann、René Heesch、Oliver Niggemann

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06612v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06612v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究整条传感器通道缺失下的时间序列插补：不重训模型，而在测试时把缺失时刻的取值作为优化变量，固定观测值并在冻结的重构模型上反传，仅用观测坐标的重构误差做自洽优化，并用跨雅可比刻画该细化何时有效，必要时加时间平滑先验。

**相关性简评：** 其「冻结 backbone + 测试时优化未观测变量 + 观测自洽目标」可能为 LVR 的推理期 latent 精化与视觉证据筛选提供线索，跨雅可比判据也可供核查「何时值得继续更新 latent」的启发性信号；但该文属时间序列领域，与多模态推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Reconstruction to Imputation: Test-Time Optimization for Missing Time-Series Channels

**abstract/A1：** Complete sensor-channel failures create a difficult form of time-series missingness: local interpolation is unavailable, and deploying a separately trained imputer for every failure pattern may be impractical. We ask whether a sequence reconstructor trained without artificial mask augmentation and then frozen can be converted into an imputer at inference time. Our method treats the missing entries of each test window as optimization variables, clamps all observations, and backpropagates through the frozen model to minimize reconstruction error only on observed coordinates. The resulting observed-self-consistency objective requires neither artificial mask augmentation nor full-channel dropout during reconstructor training. We characterize when this refinement is informative through the cross-Jacobian from missing inputs to observed residuals, and use an optional temporal-smoothness prior when that coupling is weak. Across five real-world datasets with exactly one channel synthetically masked per test window and four reconstruction architectures, no-prior test-time refinement reduces MAE relative to one-pass reconstruction in 19 of 20 dataset-backbone combinations. Compared with mask-aware imputers trained under either standard structured masks or matched full-channel dropout, the best refined reconstructor achieves the lowest MAE on two datasets and trails the strongest baseline on three. These gains require substantially more inference time and are sensitive to cross-channel coupling. Our results establish test-time self-consistency as a conditional inference-time adapter for frozen reconstruction models that complements mask-aware imputation without retraining the reconstructor.

</details>

<a id="paper-134"></a>

### 134. [Uncertainty-Driven Efficient Communication for UAV-Based Collaborative Perception](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06635v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06635

**作者：** Jiuwu Hao、Yuting Wan、Liguo Sun、Zao Zhang、Yueyang Wu、Pin Lv

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06635v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06635v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 UCP 框架，用观测不确定性（传感器受限区域）与判别不确定性（决策边界附近的模型歧义）驱动空间请求/供给图，并通过不确定性引导的交叉注意力 U-Fusion 聚合消息，在 3D 检测与轨迹预测上改善带宽-性能权衡。

**相关性简评：** 以不确定性筛选应传递的视觉证据并决定获取区域，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与「LOOK」式获取决策提供线索；但任务是协同感知而非推理，需核查其选择机制是否具备状态化、可停止的推理语义。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Uncertainty-Driven Efficient Communication for UAV-Based Collaborative Perception

**abstract/A1：** Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-based collaborative perception enables multiple agents to share complementary perceptual information, overcoming limitations such as occlusion and limited sensing range. Communication efficiency is a critical bottleneck, as UAVs operate under stringent bandwidth and computational constraints. However, existing spatial selection methods focus predominantly on foreground regions to reduce missed detections, neglecting perceptually ambiguous regions and leaving false detections unresolved. We propose UCP, an uncertainty-driven framework that characterizes collaborative value through observational uncertainty, identifying sensor-limited regions, and discriminative uncertainty, capturing model-level ambiguity near the decision boundary. These estimates drive spatial request and supply maps for efficient inter-agent communication, and the received messages are aggregated via U-Fusion, an uncertainty-guided cross-attention module. Experiments on three benchmarks across 3D object detection and trajectory prediction demonstrate a superior performance-bandwidth trade-off with high interpretability and strong generalizability.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to IEEE SMC 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-135"></a>

### 135. [Human Motion Prediction via Continual Prior Compensation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06637v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06637

**作者：** Jianwei Tang、Jian-Fang Hu、Tianming Liang、Xiaotong Lin、Jiangxin Sun、Wei-Shi Zheng、Jianhuang Lai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06637v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06637v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向人体运动预测，提出时序持续学习框架 CPC：把不同预测时长拆成多个子任务并分阶段渐进训练，同时引入可学习的 Prior Compensation Factor 显式度量先验知识损失，并扩展出逐子任务估计损失的 CPC++。

**相关性简评：** 其「分阶段训练 + 可学习先验补偿因子度量知识损失」的机制，可能为 latent 推理轨迹的分阶段训练、先验遗忘与贡献分配提供可核查的训练侧线索；但研究对象是姿态序列预测，与视觉多模态推理仅有方法层面的间接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Human Motion Prediction via Continual Prior Compensation

**abstract/A1：** Human Motion Prediction (HMP) aims to predict future human poses at different moments according to observed past motion sequences. Previous approaches mainly treated the prediction of different temporal moments as a single prediction task and learned the predictions of varied moments simultaneously, which would encounter a main limitation: the learning of short-term predictions (referring to \`\`near-future'' prediction) could be hindered by the predictions of long-term (referring to \`\`far-future'' prediction) motions. In this paper, we develop a novel temporal continual learning framework called Continual Prior Compensation (CPC) to progressively train HMP models, in which we divide the prediction task of motions corresponding to varied temporal moments into several subtasks and train the model in a multi-stage manner. To mitigate the prior information forgetting in the progressive training, we further introduce a learnable random variable Prior Compensation Factor (PCF) to explicitly measure the prior knowledge loss. We theoretically show that the PCF can be efficiently learned together with the model parameters by minimizing a reasonable upper bound of the objective function. The proposed CPC is further enhanced to estimate the prior information loss for each subtask and a new framework called Continual Prior Compensation++ (CPC++) with Fine-Grained Prior Compensation Factor (FGPCF) is finally developed. Our CPC and CPC++ frameworks are quite flexible and can be easily integrated with different HMP backbone models and adapted to various datasets and applications. Extensive experiments on three HMP benchmark datasets using multiple SOTA HMP backbones (PGBIG, siMLPe, MotionMixer, and LTD) demonstrate the effectiveness and flexibility of our frameworks.

</details>

<a id="paper-136"></a>

### 136. [Dynamical State-Space Diffusion for Spatiotemporal Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06652v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06652

**作者：** Woojun Lee、Hankyeol Kim、Pilsung Kang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06652v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06652v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DSSD：由稳定对角状态空间模型的阻尼振荡模态构造时间相关噪声算子（取其平稳协方差的 Cholesky 因子），使扩散加噪过程本身携带多尺度时间结构，去噪器结构不变，并在科学场与机器人视频数据上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其状态空间时间算子及「Kalman 新息形式」的可逆因果混合，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态转移与噪声结构的建模提供线索；但论文面向像素级时空预测，能否用于推理状态尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dynamical State-Space Diffusion for Spatiotemporal Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion models for temporal prediction typically corrupt each future frame with independent noise, leaving temporal dependence entirely to the denoiser. We propose Dynamical State-Space Diffusion (DSSD), which learns the temporal structure of the corruption process itself. DSSD builds a temporal kernel from the damped oscillatory modes of a stable diagonal state-space model, whose learned decay rates and frequencies let the corruption capture multiple timescales and oscillatory correlations, while the denoiser architecture remains unchanged. Running this model on a finite clip, however, requires the state before the first frame, which is unobserved; starting from a zero state leaves the early frames with less noise than the diffusion schedule prescribes. We avoid this by using the Cholesky factor of the model's stationary covariance as the temporal operator. We prove that this operator is causal, exactly invertible, keeps each frame's noise variance equal to that of standard diffusion, and reduces to the identity at the clean end of the process, and that it is exactly the Kalman innovations form of the state-space model. The denoiser is trained to predict the temporally correlated noise, and sampling removes and reapplies the temporal mixing at each step. On scientific-field and robot-video datasets, DSSD outperforms diffusion baselines that share its denoiser, and the evolution of its learned modes during training shows that the temporal operator adapts to each dataset.

</details>

<a id="paper-137"></a>

### 137. [Don't Replace, Complement: A Detector-Agnostic Subspace Signal for Near-OOD Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06688v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06688

**作者：** Wonseok Shin、Songkuk Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06688v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06688v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PLUS：对每个类别，从稀疏自编码器（SAE）中选出最活跃的 m 个神经元，将其解码方向正交化构成低维子空间，用该子空间内白化平方马氏距离作为近 OOD 打分，并与任意已校准的基检测器以固定权重相加，在多种骨干与 18 个检测器上稳定提升近 OOD AUROC。

**相关性简评：** 其「按类选取内部特征方向并构造低维子空间后度量距离」可作为一种表征探针，供核查 LVR 的 latent 状态是否保留任务必要方向、以及能否据此类子空间得分做证据充分性或停止判断，但迁移可行性需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Don't Replace, Complement: A Detector-Agnostic Subspace Signal for Near-OOD Detection

**abstract/A1：** Post-hoc out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is mature on far-OOD yet remains weak on near-OOD. These two settings differ in their use of class-characteristic feature directions: far-OOD inputs largely fail to activate those of their predicted class, whereas near-OOD inputs activate them much as in-distribution samples do. Because the axis separating far-OOD leaves near-OOD almost indistinguishable from ID, a score optimized for one setting is misaligned with the other; where a detector already saturates far-OOD, added near-OOD sensitivity is bought partly against it. This divergence motivates a targeted near-OOD signal designed to complement existing scores. We propose PLUS (Per-class Low-dimensional sUbspace Signal). For each class, PLUS selects the $m$ most class-active neurons from a sparse autoencoder, orthonormalizes the corresponding decoder directions to form a low-dimensional subspace, and scores an input by its per-class whitened squared Mahalanobis distance $z$ within the subspace of its predicted class. Designed as a dedicated complement rather than a standalone detector, the signal $z$ is fused additively with any calibrated base score at a single, fixed weight. This weight is a single global constant: no value is adjusted per backbone, per OOD dataset, or per deployment, and all calibration statistics are estimated from ID validation data alone. When applied to 18 existing detectors spanning diverse method families, $z$ consistently improves near-OOD AUROC in all 126 detector--backbone combinations. Far-OOD performance is broadly maintained or improved, with only marginal declines where the base score is near-optimal, making $z$ a robust, detector-agnostic complement across backbones.

</details>

<a id="paper-138"></a>

### 138. [Energy-Guided Transport for Projection-Free Physics-Informed Flow Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06711v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06711

**作者：** Giuliano Galadini、Aurelio Uncini、Danilo Comminiello

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、physics.comp-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06711v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06711v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Energy-Guided Transport：把非线性物理约束写成光滑势能，在 flow matching 采样中对中间生成状态持续施加耗散力，训练-free 地把样本导向物理可行流形，避免刚性投影并保持 O(N) 开销。

**相关性简评：** 其在生成过程中对中间状态做连续能量引导、而非离散纠偏的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理状态的约束与偏航修正提供线索；待核查的是该外力是否只是外部修正，未与模型自身状态更新真正结合。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Energy-Guided Transport for Projection-Free Physics-Informed Flow Matching

**abstract/A1：** Deep generative models, particularly Flow Matching frameworks, have emerged as powerful tools for synthesizing complex physical systems. However, standard architectures remain purely statistically driven and agnostic to the underlying governing equations, often violating fundamental physical constraints during inference. Existing zero-shot approaches designed to enforce these constraints typically rely on rigid algebraic projections. While effective for sparse boundary conditions, applying such discrete geometric corrections to dense, full-domain PDEs may disrupt the probability path, \\rev{potentially deviating from the learned manifold and introducing substantial computational overhead}. To address these challenges, we introduce Energy-Guided Transport, a novel training-free framework that formulates non-linear physical constraints as a smooth potential energy landscape. Rather than interrupting the ODE integration, our method applies a continuous dissipative force directly to the intermediate generative states, steering them toward the physical manifold. By evaluating this force implicitly via reverse-mode automatic differentiation, our framework preserves the natural dynamics of the flow while maintaining an efficient $\\mathcal{O}(N)$ algorithmic complexity. We extensively evaluate our framework across various 1D and 2D physical systems, including the chaotic Navier-Stokes equations. Our method achieves stable generation without structural divergence, significantly improves the stability-accuracy trade-off compared to state-of-the-art methods, and substantially accelerates inference with respect to exact projection strategies. Code available at: https://github.com/ispamm/Projection-free-energy-guided-flow-matching.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-139"></a>

### 139. [Template Gap Reconstruction for Shell Output Simulation with Small Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06731v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06731

**作者：** Jarrod Ragsdale、Rajendra Boppana

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06731v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06731v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 template gap reconstruction（TGR）：从小规模模板库中选出可复用输出骨架，用 sentinel 与自回归生成的 gap 交错拼接，以小型语言模型模拟 shell 输出，从而减少生成 token 并改善编辑相似度。

**相关性简评：** 「外部检索骨架＋模型补全残差」的交错生成与模板选择器，可能为 LVR 中把可复用结构或缓存外置、让模型只生成关键细节提供方法线索，可供核查其是否牺牲细节保真与动态内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Template Gap Reconstruction for Shell Output Simulation with Small Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Small language models offer a compact basis for shell simulation without executing submitted commands, but full-output decoding requires them to predict recurring utility structure alongside content that varies across executions. We introduce template gap reconstruction (TGR), which supplies reusable output literals interspersed with sentinels to be interleaved with autoregressively generated gaps. A template selector chooses which template to be reconstructed, with the empty template retaining full-output decoding. We construct a template bank from 18.7 million outputs collected across heterogeneous execution environments and train a template selector and five reconstruction models initialized from various small language model checkpoints on a target environment excluded from bank construction. Evaluation demonstrates that TGR improves output edit similarity (O-ES) over full-output decoding (FOD) when the optimal template covers more than 25\\% of the template, while performing similarly for lower-coverage outputs. Additionally, TGR outperforms FOD for shorter gap sequences, with O-ES values of 0.964 and 0.812, respectively, while maintaining similar performance for longer sequences. TGR reduces the number of tokens generated by 26.3--38.3\\% across used tokenizer families. These results support using a reusable output structure as a practical design choice for small shell simulators, improving response fidelity and shortening generation targets while keeping template selection and residual prediction central to response quality.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 7 figures, 12 tables, conference preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-140"></a>

### 140. [TailSteer: Trajectory-Tail Steering for Drift-Resistant Multi-turn Image Editing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06741v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06741

**作者：** Ruikang Li、Zixuan Chen、Jiarui Wu、Mingde Yao、Yiyuan Zhang、Yujin Wang、Hanming Deng、Lewei Lu、Tianfan Xue

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06741v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06741v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TailSteer 末段训练框架：保持早期去噪动态贴近基座模型，把保真修正集中到去噪轨迹末端，并用编辑模型自身的 on-policy 去噪 rollout 与未改动源内容作参考来学习修正，以减少多轮图像编辑中的累积漂移。

**相关性简评：** 其「轨迹末段施加修正、避免扰动早期动态」的干预位置选择与 on-policy 轨迹自监督，可能为 LVR 中迭代推理状态在何处更新、如何沿轨迹分配监督提供可核查线索，但属图像编辑场景，与 VLM 视觉推理的联系尚需判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TailSteer: Trajectory-Tail Steering for Drift-Resistant Multi-turn Image Editing

**abstract/A1：** Strong single-turn editing performance does not guarantee reliable multi-turn interaction: as edited outputs are repeatedly reused, small unintended changes can accumulate into severe visual drift. This long-term drift is often mitigated through training on multi-turn data or cross-turn rollouts, potentially altering pretrained priors, or through specialized inference-time mechanisms that are difficult to scale to foundation models. We introduce TailSteer, a final-stage training framework that improves multi-turn robustness while retaining the generative and editing capabilities acquired through earlier training. TailSteer follows a simple principle: keep early denoising dynamics close to those of the base model and concentrate preservation corrections near the trajectory tail, where fine-grained deviations can be suppressed. These corrections are learned from the editor's own on-policy denoising rollouts, using unchanged source content as a preservation reference without requiring curated ground-truth editing pairs. Experiments across the FLUX, SenseNova, and Qwen families show that TailSteer substantially reduces cumulative drift over 15-20 editing turns while largely preserving base-model performance on extensive text-to-image and single-image editing benchmarks. These results demonstrate that long-horizon editing robustness can be unlocked in foundation models after TailSteer, rather than by relearning their existing capabilities.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 9 figures. Project page: https://lyricccco.github.io/tailsteer/

</details>

<a id="paper-141"></a>

### 141. [Semantic Residual Vector Quantization for Sign Language Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06781v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06781

**作者：** Chang Liu、Ke Han、Niccolò Biondi、Gloria Isotton、Elisa Ricci、Davide Talon、Nicu Sebe

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06781v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06781v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SRVQ，将文本到手语动作生成拆为语义与运动学两级：首个 residual 量化层编码手语内容，后续层细化姿态、手形与轨迹，并用词级条件的分层码生成器完成 SMPL-X 动作合成。

**相关性简评：** 残差量化的分层 latent（语义层对应「说什么」、后续层对应「怎么做」）与词级对齐监督，可能为 LVR 中按内容/细节分级组织 latent 状态及层级监督提供核查线索，但任务是动作生成，与 VLM 推理相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Semantic Residual Vector Quantization for Sign Language Generation

**abstract/A1：** Sign language generation (SLG) maps written sentences into signing motion while preserving both lexical content and fine-grained articulation. This requires a model to decide what signs should be expressed and to realize them through precise handshape, trajectory, facial expression and body coordination. Existing pose-based systems and reconstruction-only motion tokenizers often entangle these two decisions, leaving the generator without an explicit text-aligned intermediate target for what to sign before refining how to sign it. To address this issue, we introduce Semantic Residual Vector Quantization (SRVQ), a framework for SLG that frames generation as a two-level process moving from semantics to kinematics. The newly introduced SRV Quantizer learns a hierarchy in which the first residual level encodes sign content and subsequent levels refine pose, handshape, trajectory, and body coordination, all within a single quantizer and without separate semantic representations or external planning stages. Through word-aware conditioning for hierarchical code generation, a masked semantic generator predicts semantic codes and a kinematic generator completes the residual codes for full SMPL-X motion synthesis. We evaluate using retrieval and FID in a shared text-motion embedding space, alongside standard geometric metrics. On CSL-Daily and Phoenix-2014T, SRVQ achieves R@1 of 82.3 and 70.3, outperforming the previous SOTA by 30 and 46 points, respectively.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-142"></a>

### 142. [Task-Efficient Agent Evaluation Using Cached Traces](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06803v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06803

**作者：** Ben Johnson、Hayden Helm

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06803v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06803v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用 LLM 将 agent 执行轨迹抽成结构化摘要并文本嵌入，据此构建参考系统的低维几何表示，由少量已执行任务预测新系统在 SWE-bench Verified 与 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上的整体得分，以降低评测成本。

**相关性简评：** 其「保留任务与正确性信号、抑制模型身份与 harness 信号」的轨迹表示与去混杂思路，可供核查 latent 状态相似度中内容、系统与实现差异的分离及小样本评价设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Task-Efficient Agent Evaluation Using Cached Traces

**abstract/A1：** Efficient benchmarking estimates a system's full benchmark performance from a small number of task executions. Standard approaches rely solely on the binary correctness of executions from a collection of already-evaluated reference systems. An agentic system, however, produces a rich trace for each execution that can reveal substantially more about its capabilities. Leveraging this information requires useful trace representations. We propose representing a trace by extracting a structured summary of it with an LLM and embedding the summary with a standard text encoder. These representations preserve correctness- and task-related signal while minimizing the presence of system identity-, model family- and harness-related signals that dominate raw trace embeddings. Given this representation for each task execution from all reference systems, we construct low-dimensional representations of the systems and predict a new system's score from its position among the reference systems. On SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.0, augmenting an item-response model with this geometry-based prediction reduces mean absolute error from 0.13 to 0.08 (Wilcoxon $p &lt; 10^{-4}$) and from 0.14 to 0.12 (Wilcoxon $p &lt; 10^{-3}$), respectively, when provided a single random executed task. Further, given access to the full set of reference systems, our approach dominates baselines for any given number of executed tasks on both benchmarks.

</details>

<a id="paper-143"></a>

### 143. [Rules, Traces and Routing Statistics: Training and Calibration Data for Open Models on ARC-AGI-2](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06827v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06827

**作者：** Nguyen Minh Thang、Trinh Thi Phuong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06827v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06827v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在四张 L4、离线 12 小时的约束下，比较规则语料、自身推理轨迹与合成任务等训练／校准数据，以及「思考上限」、答题后程序校验等推理流程对三个开源模型 ARC-AGI-2 成绩的影响，并报告按路由统计剪枝 MoE 专家以扩展 KV cache 的做法。

**相关性简评：** 其长首答＋思考上限＋程序校验的推理流程，以及按路由统计控制专家与缓存容量，可能为 LVR 的停止决策、证据验证和缓存／注意力结构对 latent 相似度的影响提供可核查线索；但研究任务是 ARC 网格题而非视觉 latent 推理，联系需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rules, Traces and Routing Statistics: Training and Calibration Data for Open Models on ARC-AGI-2

**abstract/A1：** We study which training and calibration data help three open models solve ARC-AGI-2 tasks when the solver runs offline on four L4 GPUs for at most 12 hours. We compare data offline on public tasks and report 189 runs on the 120 hidden semi-private tasks. For the NVARC Qwen3-4B grid model, we built a corpus of 12,000 executable rules in a typed language and 95,993 tasks; a rule is admitted only if its behaviour on a fixed probe set differs from that of every admitted rule, and a task only if its pairs refute a shortlist of nearest competing rules and defeat 12 shortcut solvers. Continued fine-tuning on 1,920 real task records with revised four-GPU scheduling raised the semi-private score from 28.96 (mean of the released model) to 33.06 (mean of five runs, best 34.86); no checkpoint trained with synthetic tasks, even all 95,993 of them, improved on the real-task model. For the Qwen3.8-27B reasoning model, we fine-tuned on 1,036 of its own reasoning traces with correct final answers and compared sets that add traces of other models and synthetic tasks. With an inference procedure built around the model (a thinking cap enforced in the serving engine, a Python program checked on the demonstrations after each answer, a long first attempt per input), the score rose from 26.81 to 44.03, the level the ARC Prize Foundation measured for the base model without a time limit (42.36); most of the gain came from the inference procedure. For the Qwen3.8-Flash-Next mixture-of-experts model (125B parameters, 6B active), GPU memory limited concurrency. Pruning from 512 to 384 experts per layer by router-weighted activation saliency grew the key-value cache from 0.55M to 1.39M tokens; a 296-expert list adapted with ARC routing statistics, keeping experts for program tokens, plus an FP8 indexer cache, reached 1.96M. With a tool that checks programs, the model scored 56.67, our best result.

**comment/C1：** 39 pages, 7 figures, 23 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-144"></a>

### 144. [Systolic Linear Attention: Co-Designing Tile-Grouped Gated Delta Attention for TPUs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06828v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06828

**作者：** Gagik Amirkhanyan、Nuojin Cheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06828v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06828v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文面向 Google TPU 提出 Systolic Linear Attention（SLA），把 gated delta 线性注意力的键维度加宽到 256、将衰减门分为四个 64 通道分组，并用块矩阵乘在单个融合 kernel 内完成 chunk 求解，从而提升训练速度与困惑度表现。

**相关性简评：** 其分块、门控分组的循环状态更新实现，可能为 LVR 中逐 token 更新 latent 状态的实际成本与 kernel 组织提供工程参考，但该文完全是语言模型硬件协同优化，未涉及视觉证据或推理状态内容，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Systolic Linear Attention: Co-Designing Tile-Grouped Gated Delta Attention for TPUs

**abstract/A1：** Gated-delta linear attention layers such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN), Kimi Delta Attention (KDA), and GDN-2 are tuned for GPUs, where small tensor-core tiles and fast vector registers make per-channel decay rescaling and sequential in-block solves cheap. On Google TPUs, which concentrate compute in wide $256 \\times 256$ systolic matrix units backed by a slower vector unit, porting these layers directly leaves half of each matrix tile empty and bottlenecks on vector operations. We present \\textbf{Systolic Linear Attention (SLA)}, which reshapes gated delta attention around the matrix unit by widening keys to $256$ with grouped value heads, grouping decay gates into four $64$-channel tiles, and computing the chunk solve entirely with block matrix products in a single fused kernel. On TPU v7x, a full \\texttt{SLA-R4} layer runs $1.6\\times$--$2.5\\times$ faster than the open-source Tokamax KDA kernel ($1.4\\times$--$2.4\\times$ with exact chunk inversion), driven primarily by head sharing and kernel fusion. Across a $2{,}048$-chip TPU v7x pre-training study of sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models from $6.5\\text{B}$ to $63.1\\text{B}$ parameters, SLA trains $11\\%$--$24\\%$ faster than KDA at similar perplexity, and reaches slightly lower perplexity than KDA in smaller matched-seed runs.

</details>

<a id="paper-145"></a>

### 145. [Context-Aware Autoregressive Image Generation for Emerging Reasoning Properties](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06838v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06838

**作者：** Jixuan Ying、Haoyu Liu、Timing Yang、Tingyu Zhu、Predrag Neskovic、Anand Bhattad、Cihang Xie、Zeyu Zheng、Alan Yuille、Feng Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06838v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06838v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出上下文感知的自回归图像生成框架，让模型依据全局上下文与当前局部信息动态决定下一个 token 的位置，取代固定的 next-patch、随机顺序或单步生成，并在 masked AR 与 scale-wise AR 模型上报告结构鲁棒性与指令遵循的改善。

**相关性简评：** 其「按上下文动态选择下一步位置」的机制或可为 LVR 中 LOOK 步的视觉证据选取与推理状态更新顺序提供借鉴线索；但论文聚焦图像生成本身，未涉及多模态推理或 latent 状态，实际迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Context-Aware Autoregressive Image Generation for Emerging Reasoning Properties

**abstract/A1：** Autoregressive image generation has recently emerged as a competitive visual generation paradigm, yet its generation order is often predefined or heuristic, such as next-patch prediction, random-order prediction, or one-step generation for specific scales. We observe that these strategies can cause models to overlook important structural dependencies in images, thereby limiting both structural robustness and reasoning capability. In this work, we propose a context-aware autoregressive generation framework, where the model dynamically determines the next token location based on global context and current local information. Rather than following a fixed or random order, our method allows the generation process to adapt to image structure and semantic dependencies. Extensive experiments show that context-aware generation significantly improves structural robustness across different image AR paradigms, including masked autoregressive and scale-wise autoregressive models. Beyond quantitative improvements, we further observe emerging reasoning properties: the model can better understand complex semantic instructions and generate images that more faithfully follow the given guidance. These findings suggest that generation order is a critical yet underexplored factor in image autoregressive modeling, and that context-aware decoding can unlock stronger reasoning-aware visual generation capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/Hpesojyjx/Saccades-AR.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 16 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-146"></a>

### 146. [Learning to Decode Linear Codes with Generalized Belief Propagation Network](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06874v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06874

**作者：** Samira Malek

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06874v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06874v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 GBPNet，在保留 Tanner 图消息传递对称性的前提下，用级联变量节点更新与可学习的逐元素消息缩放增强神经译码器，并引入渐进训练与高 SNR 训练两种策略，在多种线性码上取得更低误码率。

**相关性简评：** 其迭代消息传递中的状态更新、逐元素缩放与渐进／高信噪比训练，可能为 LVR 的循环推理状态更新与轨迹式监督提供可核查的借鉴线索，但领域为信道译码，与视觉推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Decode Linear Codes with Generalized Belief Propagation Network

**abstract/A1：** Error correcting codes are a fundamental component of communication systems, enabling reliable data transmission over noisy channels. Recently, neural decoders have demonstrated notable performance gains over classical iterative algorithms. In this paper, we propose a Generalized Belief Propagation Network (GBPNet) that preserves message-passing symmetry while enhancing the decoder's learning capability. The architecture uses cascaded variable-node updates and learned element-wise message scaling while retaining the Tanner graph structure. We also introduce two training strategies, progressive training and high-SNR training, that improve decoding accuracy. Experimental results on several linear codes show that GBPNet achieves lower bit error rates than conventional belief propagation and the evaluated state-of-the-art neural decoders.

</details>

### 147. [Motion-guided Contrastive Regularization For Latent World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06901v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06901

**作者：** Yiwen Chen、Qiqi Hou、Jisoo Jeong、Yinhao Zhu、Huaizu Jiang、Amir Said

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06901v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06901v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出 JEPA 式潜世界模型存在「静态坍缩」：编码器保留回合特定的静态内容却压制操控相关的状态变化，并提出以光流相似度为软目标的运动引导对比正则，在 OGBench-Cube 与 LIBERO-Object 上提升规划成功率。

**相关性简评：** 该失败模式与 LVR 关注的潜状态信息坍缩、静态线索挤占任务必要视觉证据高度相通；其用同回合候选观测构造潜转移对比目标的做法，可供核查能否用于检验潜推理状态是否真正编码状态变化。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Motion-guided Contrastive Regularization For Latent World Models

**abstract/A1：** JEPA-style world models learn action-conditioned latent dynamics without reconstructing observations, but distributional regularization alone does not ensure that their representations preserve information needed for control. We identify static collapse, a failure mode in which the encoder preserves episode-specific static content while suppressing manipulation-relevant state changes. Because static content is temporally predictable yet diverse across episodes, this failure can persist despite regularizers such as SIGReg. A controlled study on OGBench-Cube shows that introducing randomized backgrounds substantially degrades planning performance while leaving the underlying manipulation task unchanged. To address this problem, we propose motion-guided contrastive regularization. Our approach compares predicted latent transitions with transitions to candidate observations from the same episode, reducing the usefulness of static background identity for distinguishing action outcomes. Optical-flow similarities provide soft contrastive targets, allowing candidates with similar motion to receive similar supervision. Combined with latent prediction and SIGReg, our objective encourages representations to preserve within-episode state distinctions and similarities between related transitions. Our method improves planning success over LeWorldModel (LeWM) from 52% to 78% on OGBench-Cube with randomized backgrounds and from 14% to 54% on LIBERO-Object, while maintaining comparable performance on four other visual-control benchmarks.

</details>

<a id="paper-148"></a>

### 148. [Agent Harness Awareness: Turning Verified Telemetry into Operational Control for Tool-Using Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05391v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05391

**作者：** Manu Agrawal、Shrey Nagpal

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05391v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05391v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Agent Harness Awareness：在关键动作前由宿主向工具调用 agent 注入小规模、新鲜且经验证的状态快照，并用 ToolRoute 隔离基准及三个模型上的 216 次决策实验检验其对决策后悔和单次完成率的影响。

**相关性简评：** 其「宿主提供经验证状态、保留测量与最终放行权」的信任分离设计，可能为 LVR 中如何向推理过程注入可信外部证据、以及依据证据做停止或放行决策提供概念参照，但论文无视觉或多模态内容，迁移性尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Agent Harness Awareness: Turning Verified Telemetry into Operational Control for Tool-Using Agents

**abstract/A1：** Tool descriptions tell an agent what an action can do, but they do not necessarily reveal whether that action is healthy or suitable at the moment of use. Execution harnesses often possess this information through monitors, traces, and recent failures while the model choosing the next action does not. We call this gap operational blindness. We introduce Agent Harness Awareness, a trust-separated design in which the host supplies a small, fresh, verified state snapshot before a consequential action while retaining measurement, enforcement, evaluation, and final dispatch authority. We test its tools-and-network instance with ToolRoute, a controlled isolation benchmark that holds the task and candidate semantics constant and varies the operational information available to the model. In a 216-decision ablation across GPT-5.6 Sol, Claude Sonnet 5, and Gemini 3.7 Flash, verified tool-health state reduced mean observable policy regret by 96.9% against a same-shape neutral control (4,252 ms to 131 ms) and raised single-dispatch completion from 69.4% to 95.8%. In a separate 27-decision end-to-end replication with live local TCP/HTTP execution, OpenTelemetry client spans, and Toxiproxy-injected faults, verified state reduced mean regret from 8,956 ms to 0 ms (zero excess observable operational cost) and completed the live action in 9/9 model-by-fault cases versus 5/9 for the neutral control. These studies establish the tools-and-network instance of Agent Harness Awareness: selected host telemetry materially improves pre-dispatch operational choice. Runtime state, hardware pressure, economics, retry-aware recovery, and heterogeneous selection remain open empirical extensions of the same architecture.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 3 figures

</details>

### 149. [Augmentation with Reinforcement Transformation for Low-textured Scientific Sketch Image Retrieval](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05408v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05408

**作者：** Zhiqiang Wang、Sheri L. Barton、Jennifer H. Johnson、Yi He、Xingquan Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05408v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05408v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ART，将旋转、翻转、缩放等增强变换的选择建模为多臂老虎机问题，按奖励自适应构建视图库，再用于低纹理科学素描图像的自监督微调与检索。

**相关性简评：** 其「按奖励挑选有效变换/视图」的选择机制，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据或视图的筛选与计算分配提供线索；但任务本身是检索而非多模态推理，属较远领域的可核查启发。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Augmentation with Reinforcement Transformation for Low-textured Scientific Sketch Image Retrieval

**abstract/A1：** Low-textured sketch images are widely used in scientific applications, including wildlife conservation, archaeological illustration, and forensic composites. Learning from and retrieving such images is challenging because they lack detailed surface textures and visual variations commonly exploited by modern deep learning models. This paper proposes Augmentation with Reinforcement Transformation (ART) to fine-tune pretrained models to learn effective representations for accurate sketch image retrieval. Our key observation is that sketches are particularly sensitive to image transformations, such as rotation, flipping, zooming, and stretching. While some transformations preserve their structural integrity, others may introduce distortions that hinder representation learning. Instead of randomly choosing transformations to construct fine-tuning views, ART formulates augmentation selection as a multi-armed bandit (MAB) problem, where transformations are treated as arms and their effectiveness is continuously evaluated through rewards. This enables ART to adaptively construct reward-guided view banks before self-supervised fine-tuning; the subsequent representation loss does not feed back to ART. On Florida manatee sketches provided by Mote Marine Laboratory, ART achieves 71.40% average Top-1 accuracy for transformed-scar retrieval, compared with 68.83% for random augmentation selection, and 55.84% for location-aware sketch retrieval, compared with 18.18% for frozen CLIP.

**comment/C1：** IEEE BigData 2026

</details>
