### 150. [Learning Implicit Solves for Dynamic Simulations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05439v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05439

**作者：** Chawit Kaewnuratchadasorn、Jiaji Wang、Xin Nie

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05439v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05439v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 INOS：先在静态平衡解上预训练神经算子，再把它作为 Newmark-β 时间积分每一步内层「有效平衡问题」的求解器，在没有动态轨迹监督的情况下预测全场响应，并展示零样本优于动态训练的基线、跨空间与时间离散化复用以及百万步稳定推演。

**相关性简评：** 与 VLM 推理无直接关系，但其「预训练算子充当内层隐式求解、外层固定积分格式推进状态」的混合结构，可能为 latent 循环中的求解式状态更新模块与长程稳定 rollout 的评价方式提供类比线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Implicit Solves for Dynamic Simulations

**abstract/A1：** High-fidelity simulation of dynamical systems is central to scientific discovery and engineering design, yet remains computationally demanding. Here we introduce the Implicit Neural Operator Solver (INOS), which learns the implicit solve and encodes a time integrator. INOS pretrains a neural operator from static equilibrium solutions and reuses it within Newmark-$β$ integration. At each time increment, the integrator forms an effective equilibrium problem, the pretrained neural operator infers the next-step solution field, and the Newmark relations advance the dynamic state. Numerical examples in linear and nonlinear elastodynamics demonstrate accurate full-field predictions without dynamic trajectory supervision. Comparative results show that INOS, evaluated zero-shot, achieves higher predictive accuracy than neural solver baselines trained on dynamic simulations. In addition, INOS delivers over 4,600$\\times$ speedup in nonlinear simulations over sequential classical finite-element solvers. INOS natively accommodates changes in dynamic conditions and enables reuse of the pretrained operator across spatial and temporal discretizations. Further evaluation demonstrates a stable million-step rollout. These findings establish INOS as a reusable neural implicit solver for accurate, efficient, and robust dynamic modeling.

</details>

<a id="paper-151"></a>

### 151. [Towards Robust Long-Lived Agents: Benchmarking Experience Lifecycles in Evolving Environments](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05450v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05450

**作者：** Shijue Huang、Hangyu Guo、Hengheng Zhang、Guo An、Zhitao He、Qi Tian、Yiren Feng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05450v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05450v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 ReScopeBench，覆盖 18 个交互环境，把经验分为结构经验与规则经验，并设计以经验为中心的任务流与环境静默变化，在 20 个模型上同时考察任务表现和已存经验的保留、修订与丢失。

**相关性简评：** 虽为非视觉 agent 记忆基准，但其「经验是否被保留、是否被有效使用、环境变化下是否过时」的诊断方式，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的信息保存与答案是否真正使用内部内容提供评价思路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Robust Long-Lived Agents: Benchmarking Experience Lifecycles in Evolving Environments

**abstract/A1：** As agents become more capable, they are moving beyond one-off tasks toward sustained assistance across successive user goals. Such assistance requires agents to discover how unfamiliar environments work, reuse what they learn across tasks, and keep that experience current as environments evolve. Existing benchmarks increasingly study experience reuse and adaptation, but provide limited insight into which parts of accumulated experience agents revise, retain, or lose. To address this gap, we introduce ReScopeBench, a benchmark for the experience lifecycle across 18 interactive environments. It distinguishes structural experience about how an environment is organized and accessed from rule experience about reusable principles and constraints that guide decisions across tasks. To study how experience is acquired, reused, and revised, we propose an experience-first construction approach. It builds task streams around shared experience and introduces silent environmental changes. We evaluate task performance and track changes in saved experience throughout each stream. Across 20 models, we find that retained experience improves task performance, but agents struggle to keep it current as environments change. Even when the required experience is correctly recorded, agents do not always complete tasks successfully. These findings identify selective maintenance and effective use as complementary challenges for long-lived agents.

</details>

<a id="paper-152"></a>

### 152. [Can Large Language Models Review the Scientific Literature? A Test Against a 102-Article Benchmark](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05459v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05459

**作者：** Abdullah Al Kalbani、David M. Schultz、Alexandra K. Anderson-Frey、Zhonghua Zheng、Catherine McGuire

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.DL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05459v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05459v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文以一篇关于 Spanish plume 天气型的已发表综述及其 102 篇文献为基准，评测六种 AI 工具在检索文献、解读、跨文献综合与提出未来方向四个阶段的整流程表现，并由两位领域专家盲评综合质量，另给出八条使用建议。

**相关性简评：** 其区分「实质讨论」与「顺带提及」的证据筛选任务，以及综合中无依据论断与引文错误的检查，可能为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选和 latent 内容对证据使用的核查提供评价思路，但该工作为纯文本、不涉及内部状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can Large Language Models Review the Scientific Literature? A Test Against a 102-Article Benchmark

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support scientific literature reviews, yet evaluations often examine individual tasks rather than the review process as a whole. We assess six AI tools across four stages: finding relevant articles, interpreting them, synthesizing across them, and proposing future research. The benchmark is a published review of the Spanish plume weather pattern and its 102-article corpus. In the initial searches, five tools collectively identified 57 articles, with the best individual tool finding 42. Institutional library access increased discovery from 42 to 82 articles for Codex and from 15 to 79 for Claude Code. Given the complete corpus of 102 PDFs, three tools correctly identified all 18 articles discussing the Spanish plume substantially, but incorrectly classified 40% to 79% of the 84 passing mentions as substantial. The same three tools produced syntheses evaluated blindly by two domain experts. Although largely accurate, the syntheses provided limited insight and sometimes contained unsupported claims, quotation errors, and exaggerated statements of consensus. Finally, all three tools proposed research directions broader than the Spanish plume literature, although two approached questions identified in the benchmark review. No single tool performed best across all tasks. We provide eight recommendations for researchers using AI tools in literature reviews. Our findings suggest that LLMs can assist with discovering and interpreting scientific literature, particularly when institutional access is available, but cannot replace expert judgment in evaluating evidence or synthesizing research.

**comment/C1：** 34 pages, 7 figures, 6 tables. Data, prompts, analysis scripts and results: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23849039 (GitHub: https://github.com/abx40/spanish-plume-llm-literature-review)

</details>

<a id="paper-153"></a>

### 153. [SlackBench: Benchmarking Agents on Collaborative Projects Grounded in Real Code Repositories](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05512v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05512

**作者：** Mingxuan Li、Huanzhi Mao、Ruitao Zheng、Jarod Stanbury James、Yunze Li、Natanya Anderson、Joseph E. Gonzalez、Alex Dimakis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05512v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05512v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** SlackBench 基于真实科研项目的隐私保护重演，构建含多方对话、代码仓库与文档的可复现协作环境，提出 140 个跨源检索、弃答、目标导向摘要与情境推断查询，并评测 33 个前沿模型与 agent 框架。

**相关性简评：** 不涉及视觉或 latent 状态，但其「定位相关对话—核查仓库状态—调和冲突或过时证据—对不可答问题弃答」的证据筛选与停止判断流程，可能为 LVR 中何时获取何种证据、何时停止提供概念参照，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SlackBench: Benchmarking Agents on Collaborative Projects Grounded in Real Code Repositories

**abstract/A1：** AI Agents are becoming digital coworkers that can browse the web, write code, run experiments, and assist humans over extended horizons. Yet existing benchmarks fall short of real knowledge work: many rely on synthetic data or isolated tasks, missing the complex, multi-party dynamics of collaborative projects, where critical information is scattered across conversations, code, and evolving decisions. Even simple workplace questions may require locating relevant channels and direct messages, recovering context from long discussions, inspecting repository state, reconciling stale or conflicting evidence, and synthesizing across sources. We introduce SlackBench, a benchmark for evaluating agents on realistic research and engineering workflows that require joint reasoning over workplace communication and evolving repository state. Grounded in privacy-preserving reenactments of real academic research projects, SlackBench provides reproducible environments with realistic multi-party dialogue, evolving decisions, and corresponding code changes grounded in GitHub repositories and Google Docs. It contains 140 queries spanning heterogeneous-source agentic search, abstention on unanswerable or false-premise questions, goal-directed summarization, and circumstance inference over long conversations. These tasks are not reducible to static corpus question answering: agents must determine which conversations matter, traverse threads, inspect repository state, and synthesize evidence across communication and code artifacts. Across 33 frontier models and agent harnesses, the best system scores around 80% and most agents score below 66%, highlighting substantial headroom for improvement. SlackBench thus provides an extensible framework for evaluating agents in collaborative work environments. The SlackBench dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/HuanzhiMao/SlackBench .

</details>

<a id="paper-154"></a>

### 154. [Recasting Visual Language Models as EEG Foundation Models for Clinical Neurodiagnostics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05538v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05538

**作者：** Xin Zhou、Elena Sergeeva、Nan Bi、Wenyu Song、Lijun Yin、Hao Deng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05538v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05538v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作将 EEG 信号以可视图像形式输入通用 VLM，在多个临床神经诊断任务上评测，并与无标注 EEG 大规模预训练得到的专用基础模型比较 few-shot 适应能力及随模型规模扩展的表现。

**相关性简评：** 它提供了「通用 VLM 视觉表示能否直接承载非自然、细粒度信号证据」以及「能否以 few-shot 替代领域大规模预训练」的对照线索，可供核查与 LVR 中视觉细节保留、训练监督成本权衡的关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Recasting Visual Language Models as EEG Foundation Models for Clinical Neurodiagnostics

**abstract/A1：** Historically automated electroencephalography (EEG) systems was done using task-specific supervised models trained on purposely annotated data. A more recent modeling paradigm relies on creating a unified representation, or a 'foundation model', for multiple modality-specific tasks through large-scale domain-specific pretraining on the unannotated EEG signal. The quality of this unified representation directly depends on the availability of the modality-specific datasets. At the same time, generalist Vision Language Models (VLMs) exhibit broad few-shot learning capacities as well as good fine-tuning adaptability to different visual and language tasks. Can VLMs learn how to read and interpret EEG visually, emulating to a degree the way human clinicians inspect the EEG patterns? In this work we make a case that they can: we show that generalist VLMs without large-scale EEG pretraining perform competitively across a collection of clinical neuro-diagnostic tasks, can adapt to different tasks in a few-shot learning setting better than specialist foundation models, and exhibit better performance with model scaling when modality-specific models fail to exhibit.

</details>

<a id="paper-155"></a>

### 155. [Role-based Effectiveness of an Epistemic Uncertainty Estimate in Training Models for Semantic Segmentation of 3D LiDAR Point Clouds](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05569v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05569

**作者：** Krishnakumar N、Jaya Sreevalsan-Nair

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05569v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05569v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 本文在四个大规模 LiDAR 点云分割基准上，将认知不确定性估计分别用作额外输入特征与训练损失的样本置信权重，并与基线 ANN、PointNet++、RandLA-Net 对比 mIoU、少数类预测及误差—不确定性对齐。

**相关性简评：** 论文把不确定性当作可反复利用的内部信号而非单纯提分手段，可能为 LVR 中按视觉证据可靠性筛选信息与决定何时停止提供外部参照；但其任务是 3D 分割而非多模态推理，迁移线索需核查。｜DSML｜ parameter&gt;

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Role-based Effectiveness of an Epistemic Uncertainty Estimate in Training Models for Semantic Segmentation of 3D LiDAR Point Clouds

**abstract/A1：** Point cloud segmentation is a fundamental task in 3D data analysis, with applications across robotics, augmented and virtual reality, and remote sensing. However, the inherent sparsity, noise, and unordered nature of point clouds introduce significant challenges, particularly in achieving reliable classification. While the state-of-the-art rely on training models for explicit modeling of uncertainty, there is a gap in exploring different roles that estimated uncertainty using generative approaches can play. This work investigates the impact of deterministic uncertainty in point cloud segmentation by explicitly modeling epistemic uncertainty and using it in different roles, namely, as a feature and in loss function. We evaluate a baseline artificial neural network (ANN) and two uncertainty-aware variants (uncertainty as an additional input feature and as a per-sample confidence weight in the training loss) on four large-scale LiDAR benchmarks spanning airborne and mobile acquisition, namely ISPRS Vaihingen, AHN3, Paris-Lille-3D, and Toronto-3D, together with PointNet++ and RandLA-Net references and their uncertainty-augmented counterparts under canonical spatially disjoint train and test splits. Under this protocol, uncertainty-aware modeling improves mean Intersection over Union, recovers minority classes that the baseline ANN never predicts at all, and in half of the configurations improves the alignment between prediction error and estimated uncertainty as measured by Patch Accuracy versus Patch Uncertainty. Our findings indicate that uncertainty can play multiple roles in the segmentation pipeline rather than as a device for improving accuracy alone.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 7 figures, 10 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-156"></a>

### 156. [Verified but Vacuous: Three Failure Surfaces of LLM-Driven Verified Code Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05590v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05590

**作者：** Aditya Vempaty、Himaneesh Sompalle、Manan Tayal、Mowafak Allaham、Prasenjit Dey、Ashish Jagmohan、Ravi Kokku

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05590v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05590v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文主张 LLM 生成代码的「已验证」应拆成三个可分离层面——验证器接受、证明义务与 spec 的语法绑定、spec 对非形式需求的语义保真，并用 Dafny 作为中间表示的管线在 Python 与 Rust 基准上给出 A≠B、B≠C 的实证。

**相关性简评：** 其「通过检查≠真正绑定到需求」的分层审计思路，可能类比 LVR 中「答案正确但未实际使用 latent 状态或视觉证据」的鉴别性评价，可供核查；但论文不涉及视觉输入、推理状态或训练监督，联系仅为评价概念层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Verified but Vacuous: Three Failure Surfaces of LLM-Driven Verified Code Generation

**abstract/A1：** What should \`\`verified'' mean when evaluating LLM-generated code? Many existing pipelines report a single verification number. We argue this collapses three distinct properties measured in a conditional audit sequence: verifier acceptance (Surface A), \\emph{syntactic specification binding (Surface B: does the proof obligation bind to the spec?), and \\emph{semantic specification fidelity} (Surface C: does the spec capture the informal requirement?). Two empirical demonstrations show the surfaces separate. \\textbf{First ($A \\neq B$), via Li@Opus (a same-model reproduction of \\citet{li2025dafny}'s Dafny-as-intermediate-representation (IR) pipeline under Claude Opus 4.7) on three Python benchmarks}: it reaches \\textbf{99.1\\% verifier acceptance} on HumanEval while only \\textbf{1.9\\%} carry a certificate binding to a spec-defined predicate or function. \\textbf{Second ($B \\neq C$), via VCG on two Rust benchmarks}: we use VCG (which is our own in-house Dafny-as-intermediate pipeline) on Rust because the Rust target lets us enforce fully-verifiable output (every component ships as a Dafny certificate, ensures-binding gate passing by construction). Even inside that construction, \\textbf{11.48\\% (MBPP-Rust) and 14.82\\% (VeriContest LC slice) of structurally-verified samples still fail hidden tests}. On the LC slice of VeriContest, \\textbf{VCG's reported End2End rate is 7.79\\% (535/6870).} The cited external rates are 5.51\\% (GPT-5.5) and 1.88\\% (single-shot Opus 4.7). These give descriptive differences of 2.3 percentage points and 4.14$\\times$, respectively; task-budget, repair and harness differences prevent a matched-performance claim.

</details>

<a id="paper-157"></a>

### 157. [GaussianRenderFormer: Transformer-based Rendering of 3D Gaussian Splats](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05593v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05593

**作者：** Shahaf Valencia Levy、Sagie Benaim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05593v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05593v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GaussianRenderFormer，用 Transformer 取代光栅化器做 3D Gaussian splatting 渲染：每个 Gaussian 作为视角无关编码器的一个 token，视角相关解码器让相机射线小块通过注意力读取这些 token 直接输出像素，并诊断出视角解码器存在瓶颈。

**相关性简评：** 其「信息本可推断却未被解码器利用」的诊断（显式给出 Gaussian 投影足迹仅增益 1.4 dB）可能与 LVR 中视觉细节留存于内部状态却未被答案真正使用的问题同构，可供核查；但任务本身是渲染，不含推理轨迹或停止决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GaussianRenderFormer: Transformer-based Rendering of 3D Gaussian Splats

**abstract/A1：** Can a neural network learn to render 3D Gaussian splats on its own, with no rasterizer in the renderer? We present GaussianRenderFormer, a transformer that takes a set of Gaussians and a camera and outputs the image directly. Each Gaussian becomes one token in a view-independent encoder, and a view-dependent decoder turns small patches of camera rays into pixels by attending to those tokens. Trained on 26,820 Objaverse objects pruned to 20,000 Gaussians each, GaussianRenderFormer renders 300 unseen objects at 38.08 dB PSNR against the full, unpruned splats, while rasterizing the same pruned input reaches 44.02 dB. The gap is not one of generalization, and our diagnostics are consistent with a bottleneck in the view decoder: telling it the projected footprint of each Gaussian, which it could in principle infer, adds 1.4 dB. Under-training is not ruled out. Code, trained models and videos: https://svlwoof.github.io/gaussianrenderformer

**comment/C1：** Under review at TMLR. 32 pages. Project page: https://svlwoof.github.io/gaussianrenderformer

</details>

### 158. [Transport along Local Annealing Paths: Wasserstein Geometry and Multimodal Sampling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05605v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05605

**作者：** Han Chen、Sifan Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.CO、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05605v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05605v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文分析几何退火路径在 Wasserstein 空间中的几何性质，指出模式间质量迁移会使路径远长于端点距离并拖慢优化，进而提出分量退火传输（CAT），以模式先验的混合参考构造目标的精确混合分解、分别学习各分量的传输映射，并用「传输努力」指导自适应退火调度。

**相关性简评：** 其混合分解、模式间质量迁移代价与按传输努力分配退火算力的思路，可能可供核查 latent 多假设表示的模式坍塌、假设间状态迁移以及动态计算/停止决策的评价方式，但需较多迁移论证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Transport along Local Annealing Paths: Wasserstein Geometry and Multimodal Sampling

**abstract/A1：** Variational approaches to learning transport maps for multimodal sampling can suffer from mode collapse. One way to mitigate this problem is to learn transport along a prescribed path of intermediate distributions defined by geometric annealing. We show that annealing can take a substantial detour in Wasserstein space: when mass moves between modes, the path can be much longer than the distance between its endpoints. We further link path geometry to transport learning, showing that long annealing paths can slow population optimization, while sufficiently regular paths permit rapid convergence. Motivated by these findings, we propose componentwise annealed transport (CAT) to reduce the need for mass transfer between modes. Starting from a mode-informed mixture reference, CAT constructs an exact mixture decomposition of the target and learns separate transport maps along componentwise annealing paths. We estimate the component weights separately and show that these estimates remain consistent even when the learned flows are inexact. We also develop adaptive annealing schedules guided by transport effort. Experiments on multimodal synthetic distributions and Bayesian inference problems demonstrate improvements in transport learning and sampling efficiency.

**comment/C1：** 36 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-159"></a>

### 159. [Assurance-Conditioned Continuous Authorization for Autonomous AI Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05632v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05632

**作者：** Mehdi Shajari

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05632v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05632v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ACCA：一种与框架无关的自主 agent 持续授权机制，区分证据有效性与上下文适用性，借助有类型依赖图对授权相关变化做选择性重评估，用 authority epoch 标记有效权限变化，并在效果时刻拒绝陈旧动作，仅在交易域回归套件中做了实现级验证。

**相关性简评：** 「证据有效但上下文已不适用」以及按依赖结构选择性重评估、在动作生效前拒绝陈旧依据的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据何时失效、何时需重新看与停止决策的判据；但原文属 agent 授权治理，未涉及视觉或多模态推理，迁移仅为概念类比。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Assurance-Conditioned Continuous Authorization for Autonomous AI Agents

**abstract/A1：** Autonomous software agents can act through tools, delegate work, and change their models or execution environments while earlier authorizations remain in flight. We present Assurance-Conditioned Continuous Authorization (ACCA), an approach designed to be harness-independent, for maintaining operational authority according to currently justified assurance. It distinguishes evidence validity from contextual applicability, derives assurance claims from eligible evidence, and resolves a multidimensional effective-authority envelope within a principal-declared ceiling and applicable policy. A typed dependency graph supports selective re-evaluation of authorization-relevant changes. An authority epoch changes when effective authority changes; a distinct optional authorization-basis version supports revalidation of action-specific authorization conditions. Protected effect-time enforcement can reject stale actions before a consequential resource change, subject to the available commit mechanism. A compositional Domain Governance Profile expresses domain-specific semantics without granting authority. Controlled scenario tests implemented as a regression suite using a trading profile exercise default-deny rules, evidence-conditioned contraction and recovery, policy divergence, selective non-response, and stale-action rejection at a local gateway before the simulated-broker boundary. The study establishes implementation-level evidence in one domain, not a cross-domain guarantee of harmful-behavior prevention.

**comment/C1：** 81 pages, 3 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-160"></a>

### 160. [Data-Efficient Scaling-Law Discovery for Recommendation Systems via Coupled Hyperparameter Scaling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05643v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05643

**作者：** John Martabano Landy、Louis C. Tiao、Yao Xuan、Luc Kien Hang、Rupinder P. Khandpur、Arvind M. Vepa、Srinidhi Raghavendran、Anirudh Agrawal、Xiaoyi Leo Liu、Jiang Liu、Peggy Yao、Alireza Vahdatpour、Djordje Gligorijevic

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05643v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05643v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「scaling layer」把排序/推荐模型的原始架构参数按语义分组，映射到低维超超参数空间，再由 Axia 拟合代理模型、用后验采集准则选配置，以少量试验得到复杂度—质量缩放律与 Pareto 前沿。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理主线无直接关系，但其小样本下的缩放律拟合与不确定性驱动的算力分配，可能可供核查 LVR 中「计算量—准确率」权衡的预测及 THINK/STOP 预算决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Data-Efficient Scaling-Law Discovery for Recommendation Systems via Coupled Hyperparameter Scaling

**abstract/A1：** Large-scale ranking and recommendation models of Web platforms benefit from increased compute much as large language models do, and their scaling behavior is broadly power-law in form. Yet ranking models have heterogeneous architectures which makes their scaling law less straightforward than that of LLMs. We address how to discover the scaling law of an arbitrary ranking model efficiently. We introduce a scaling layer, which groups primitive architecture parameters into semantically coherent groups and defines how they co-vary, inducing a mapping from a low-dimensional space of hyper-hyperparameters into the full architecture space. On top of it, Axia fits scaling-informed surrogates, selects configurations by a posterior-based acquisition criterion, and returns a Pareto frontier from which per-model operating points are validated -- replacing exhaustive sweeps with a small number of informative runs. A fleet-level evaluation protocol, computed on a fixed representative model set under matched trial budgets, makes refinements to either component measurable. Our contribution is an automated system that learns model-specific complexity-quality relationships and gives uncertainty-aware guidance for allocating compute across a heterogeneous model ecosystem. Across 34 model iterations in two development cycles on Meta's ads ranking and retrieval system, the workflow yielded a mean 0.66% offline normalized-entropy improvement and 0.36% cumulative online lift.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 10 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-161"></a>

### 161. [ARIDA - Active railway infrastructure dataset annotation using neural network predictions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05646v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05646

**作者：** Maximilian Dornik、Christian Sieberichs、Simon Geerkens、Alexander Braun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05646v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05646v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ARIDA 铁路半自动标注流水线：将实例分割预测转换为包围盒、多边形、折线等几何标注，用置信度阈值与轮廓一致性质量分数作为验收门，并以不确定性排序的主动学习循环自适应调节每轮人工查询量。

**相关性简评：** 其「不确定性选样＋质量门控＋何时停止标注」的组合，可能与 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 STOP 的成本收益权衡形成概念对应，可供核查；但工作面向数据标注流水线而非推理期 latent 状态，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ARIDA - Active railway infrastructure dataset annotation using neural network predictions

**abstract/A1：** Modern railway perception requires large comprehensive image datasets, but creating them is slowed by manual labeling, especially for track polylines and other complex infrastructure geometry. This paper presents the Active Railway Infrastructure Dataset Annotation framework, a railway-specific semi-automated pipeline that converts instance predictions from a mask scoring instance segmentation network into bounding-boxes, polygons, polylines, and paired polylines. To keep automatic track geometry annotation reliable without ground truth, it applies confidence thresholds and an unique contour-agreement quality score as an acceptance gate. An active learning loop ranks remaining images by uncertainty and adapts the number of manual queries per cycle. This is achieved through a novel quantity control mechanism, in which easy images are auto-accepted and human effort targets hard cases. Experiments on disjoint, diverse imagery and time-sequential, redundant imagery quantify effort reduction and accuracy trade-offs, achieving about two thirds reduction in the disjoint regime and up to 93% in the sequential regime. The study also provides guidance for initial label budgets and stopping to avoid over-annotation.

</details>

<a id="paper-162"></a>

### 162. [Attributing Agent Failures to Their History Needs an Executed Counterfactual: Toward a Reporting Standard](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05650v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05650

**作者：** Egor Pakhomov、Erik Nijkamp

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05650v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05650v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文主张对「agent 失败可归因于其历史」这一论断应提供干预验证的证据，提出需冻结可执行的中途状态、只改变 agent 所看到的历史再继续执行，并给出可复现的 replay certificate；文中还报告了两个警示性测量，说明快照恢复的保真度问题。

**相关性简评：** 其「冻结状态—仅改历史—继续执行」的干预范式与可复现性度量，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态与视觉证据的因果贡献分配、以及轨迹回放/缓存一致性核查提供评价思路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Attributing Agent Failures to Their History Needs an Executed Counterfactual: Toward a Reporting Standard

**abstract/A1：** Published explanations of why long-horizon agents fail are commonly taxonomies labeled over completed trajectories: a judge reads a transcript and names the failure. We argue that a claim attributing an agent's behavior to its history should carry intervention-verified evidence, per instance or via a validated estimator: freeze an executable mid-trajectory state, alter only the history the agent sees, and continue - executed rather than inferred from the transcript, at a state whose restoration has been measured, with a replay certificate matched to the estimand. Each of the four manipulation classes we name has a precedent, and several methods already execute interventions from checkpoints, re-executed prefixes or recorded conversation states (for example DoVer, REFLECT, Min et al., Who&amp;When Pro, Causal Agent Replay), but none reports a measured replay certificate. Two cautionary measurements, neither applying the standard, follow: a carrier taxonomy whose "concentration" could not be separated from benchmark construction (adjusted odds ratio 1.15, interval crossing 1), and hash-identical snapshots that, restored into identically seeded processes, agreed with one another in 24 of 24 states while differing from the live continuation in 9 and 20 of 24. Restoring the captured position of a random stream made all 24 agree with the live run.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the IAB Workshop (Interpreting Agent Behavior) at NeurIPS 2026 (non-archival). 20 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-163"></a>

### 163. [Beyond Accuracy: Information-Theoretic Decoder Evaluation Under Latent Intent](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05658v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05658

**作者：** Tasha Kim、Benjamin Ballyk、Anson Lei、Ingmar Posner、Oiwi Parker Jones

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05658v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05658v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 将脑机到机器人的辅助控制形式化为 POMDP，证明以最差目标对 Chernoff 信息 Γ\_min 为边界存在遗憾的相变，并给出可由混淆矩阵在 O(K²) 内算出的闭式遗憾预测器 U\_id；在真实 EEG 上展示同准确率 35.1% 的两解码器留出遗憾相差 9.6 倍。

**相关性简评：** 其「潜在意图可区分性决定下游序列决策表现、单步准确率只是代理」的论证与信息论诊断，可能为 LVR 中判断潜在视觉证据是否足以支撑多步推理与停止决策提供可核查的评价思路，但领域较远，迁移需验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Accuracy: Information-Theoretic Decoder Evaluation Under Latent Intent

**abstract/A1：** A central challenge in assistive brain-to-robot control is decoding a user's latent intent from complex and noisy brain signals. Prior approaches evaluate decoders as single-trial classifiers with accuracy-based metrics, but this neglects the inherently sequential nature of assistive control. Since a planner must integrate intermittent, noisy decoder outputs over time, single-step accuracy can only be a proxy when predicting downstream task performance. We formalize brain-to-robot control as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), and prove that there is a sharp phase transition: regret is uniformly bounded across the horizon as long as pairs of goals are all decoder-distinguishable, but grows linearly otherwise. We show that a single channel statistic, the worst-pair Chernoff information $Γ\_{\\min}$ sets this boundary. Then, we derive a closed-form regret predictor $U\_{\\mathrm{id}}(C)$, that can be computed from any confusion matrix in $O(K^2)$ time. Across two domains spanning orthogonal information channels, we show that $U\_{\\mathrm{id}}$ predicts cumulative planning regret beyond what accuracy can explain. We then highlight a pathological accuracy trap, in which two decoders have identical validation accuracy 35.1% on real EEG data (BNCI2014001 subject 5) but result in a difference of $9.6\\times$ in held-out regret, which $U\_{\\mathrm{id}}$ predicts. Same-signal control confirms $U\_{\\mathrm{id}}$'s advantage with decoders that span independent feature channels. Ultimately, our work provides a drop-in diagnostic to evaluate and select sensors and decoders in sequential assistive control.

</details>

<a id="paper-164"></a>

### 164. [Combining Relevant Expert Knowledge Using Mixtures of Feedback Fuzzy Cognitive Maps](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05664v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05664

**作者：** Olaoluwa Adigun、Akash Kumar Panda、Bart Kosko

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IR、cs.SC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05664v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05664v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文把法律证据理论中的相关性（兼具证明力与实质性）形式化地用于神经符号系统，通过比较各专家反馈模糊认知图与混合 FCM 的极限环预测差异来估计答案的证明力，再用类贝叶斯去混合得到查询相关的混合权重，并在合成样本与一个 13 节点缺血性中风风险 FCM 上演示。

**相关性简评：** 其“按查询只取相关来源”、以预测差异衡量各来源贡献的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与多分支 latent 的贡献分配提供概念性参照，但领域相距较远，迁移可行性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Combining Relevant Expert Knowledge Using Mixtures of Feedback Fuzzy Cognitive Maps

**abstract/A1：** A neuro-symbolic system should combine only relevant answers from expert agents or other knowledge sources. Using only relevant experts for a given input query becomes ever more important for accuracy and scalability as the knowledge network grows and connects ever more knowledge sources. We extend the standard definition of relevance from the legal theory of evidence: An answer is relevant if and only if it is both probative and material for the matter at hand given the knowledge available. We apply this relevancy scheme to mixing the feedback fuzzy cognitive maps (FCMs) of real or artificial experts. The expert FCM directed causal graphs mix or combine into a common knowledge network. We compare the equilibrium limit-cycle predictions or answers of the experts to the answers of the mixture FCM and then estimate the probative status of answers by their difference or variation. A new Bayes-like theorem de-mixes the knowledge mixture into corresponding posterior-like FCMs that also allow relevancy-based comparisons. The result is a final set of relevancy mixing weights that produces a relevancy-mixed FCM knowledge network for the given query. So different queries produce different relevancy-mixed FCMs. We demonstrate this relevancy approach using generated synthetic FCMs from an actual 13-node expert FCM that models the causes and feedbacks of ischemic-stroke risk.

**comment/C1：** 8 page, 8 figures, 2 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-165"></a>

### 165. [From Type to Token: Petri Nets, Individual Token Philosophy, and Individualized Causal Effects](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05703v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05703

**作者：** Fernando Delbianco、Fernando Tohmé、Gustavo Bodanza

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05703v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05703v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文批评以 ATE/CATE 为代表的类型层因果估计在效应异质性无界时失效，提出用「个体 token 哲学」下的 Petri 网形式化因果机制，并给出从 Bayesian Network 到 Petri Net 的翻译，再叠加 conformal prediction 层以有限样本覆盖保证量化个体因果效应。

**相关性简评：** 其 token 层因果结构与个体化效应量化的思路，可能为 LVR 中单条推理轨迹的步骤贡献分配与因果核验提供概念工具，但原文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，可供核查迁移空间。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Type to Token: Petri Nets, Individual Token Philosophy, and Individualized Causal Effects

**abstract/A1：** Structural Causal Models and Bayesian Networks are the dominant framework for representing causal relations. They are fundamentally type-level formalisms since their estimands, Average Treatment Effects and Conditional Average Treatment Effects, are properties of distributions over populations, not of individual units. When treatment effects exhibit unbounded heterogeneity, these estimands collapse. What persists is the structural, token-level core of causation. We argue that Petri Nets under the Individual Token Philosophy are the natural representation of these token-level causal structures. We develop a formal translation from Bayesian Networks to Petri Nets, providing a two-layer account of individualized causality. The PN layer provides a structural representation of causal mechanisms that does not rest on distributional assumptions. In turn, a conformal prediction layer quantifies causal effects with finite-sample coverage guarantees. Together they constitute a complete account of individual causal effects.

</details>

<a id="paper-166"></a>

### 166. [Do Language Models Know Which Constraints Belong Together?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05726v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05726

**作者：** Junbo Jacob Lian、Kaixuan Chen、Hanzhang Qin、Huiling Chen、Chaoyu Zhang、Chung-Piaw Teo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.DS、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05726v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05726v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究语言模型能否指出 Dantzig-Wolfe 重整中「只有联合才强化界」的约束族分组，提出 JointDW 用分阶段搜索验证 LLM 预测并与 LP 对偶前瞻交错；同时给出仅观察界值时所需评估次数的覆盖数下界，并以预测节省的评估量衡量预测器掌握的信息。

**相关性简评：** 用「预测节省的搜索评估次数」量化预测所含信息、以及返回上一级联合才生效的隐藏组合，可能为核查 latent 状态的非边际信息贡献与 THINK/STOP 预算分配评价提供概念线索，但属运筹优化背景，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do Language Models Know Which Constraints Belong Together?

**abstract/A1：** Dantzig-Wolfe reformulation strengthens the linear relaxation of a mixed-integer program by convexifying a chosen set of constraint families, and every candidate costs a column generation run. The choice is hard when families strengthen the bound only together, because a search that adds one family at a time then stops at the linear relaxation. The classical inequalities of lot sizing, network design and facility location rest on such zero-marginal groups. We prove that when each evaluation convexifies at most $s$ of $k$ families, any method that observes only bounds needs a covering number of evaluations, of order $k^w$, to find a hidden group of $w$ families, also on actual Dantzig-Wolfe relaxations. A prediction that misses $e$ members reduces the cost to $Θ(k^e)$, through one staged search that does not need to know $e$, so the evaluations a prediction saves measure what the predictor knows. We study two sources of predictions. A one-pass lookahead at an optimal LP dual is provably blind to groups whose families share no variable, and a language model reads the names and text of the model. JointDW verifies the prediction of the language model with the staged search and interleaves it with the lookahead at a bounded loss. Groups that strengthen the bound only jointly appear in a quarter of the MIPLIB-NL models whose bound can be improved. There the strongest language models identify them about as often as the lookahead, their predictions rest on the names for textbook models, and reasoning about doubles the accuracy of the same model. On larger models with distracting families, the lookahead finds the group in one evaluation of three families. Language models given only the text seldom name it, while one reasoning model given every coefficient names it in all but one answer and then needs fewer evaluations than the lookahead for pairs of families.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 4 figures, 15 tables. Code and data: https://github.com/junbolian/JointDW

</details>

<a id="paper-167"></a>

### 167. [RESHPart: Feedback-Reshaped Spectral Embedding for Hypergraph Partitioning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05729v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05729

**作者：** Rongjian Liang、Zhuo Feng、Brucek Khailany

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05729v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05729v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文从谱嵌入几何角度重审超图划分，指出电路超图存在射线状嵌入结构，并通过拉普拉斯设计与超边重加权、几何反馈与搜索反馈迭代重塑嵌入，提出 FRESHPart，在 Titan23 上相对 hMETIS 取得明显切割改进且单 GPU 120 秒内完成。

**相关性简评：** 与 VLM/LVR 无直接关联，但其「表示几何可被目标设计与反馈回路系统地塑形」的思路，可能为 latent 状态的几何调控与反馈式状态更新提供类比线索，可供核查迁移价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RESHPart: Feedback-Reshaped Spectral Embedding for Hypergraph Partitioning

**abstract/A1：** With scalable Laplacian solvers now available, the next frontier is to exploit them more effectively for high-quality hypergraph partitioning. In this work, we revisit spectral hypergraph partitioning from the perspective of spectral embedding geometry. Through a systematic analysis of circuit hypergraphs and controlled synthetic (hyper)graphs, we identify a ray-like embedding structure that helps explain the effectiveness of projection-based partitioning. We further show that the geometry of spectral embeddings can be systematically influenced through Laplacian design and hyperedge reweighting. Guided by these insights, we propose FRESHPart, a feedback-reshaped spectral partitioning framework that refines the spectral embedding through Geometry Feedback and Search Feedback, producing embeddings that are better aligned with projection-based partitioning. On the Titan23 benchmark, FRESHPart achieves average cut reductions over hMETIS of 13.09% at $ε=2\\%$ and 25.21% at $ε=20\\%$, surpassing the strongest previously reported results (12.16% and 22.53%, respectively), while completing every benchmark in under 120 s on a single GPU.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to ASP-DAC 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-168"></a>

### 168. [Text-Only Domain Adaptation for Low-Resource Medical ASR: Shallow Fusion and Density Ratio Rescoring for Hausa](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05744v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05744

**作者：** Oche David Ankeli、Hassan Adelani Luqman、Theodora Ngozi Egbunike、Joshua Gottschalk、Bonaventure F. P. Dossou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05744v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05744v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究豪萨语医疗语音识别的纯文本域适应，在冻结的 MMS-1B 声学模型上用 4-gram KenLM 做 Shallow Fusion 与 Density Ratio（DRA）解码期融合，WER 从 29.56% 降至约 23.3%，但与仅用通用语言模型的融合相比增益有限。

**相关性简评：** 该方法不涉及视觉或 latent 推理状态，但「密度比」以通用模型为源先验扣除、只保留目标域特有增益，可能为 LVR 中区分通用先验与推理内容实际贡献（贡献分配）提供一种可核查的度量思路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Text-Only Domain Adaptation for Low-Resource Medical ASR: Shallow Fusion and Density Ratio Rescoring for Hausa

**abstract/A1：** Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems often struggle with specialised medical vocabulary, especially in low-resource languages where domain-specific speech data are scarce. We investigate text-only domain adaptation for Hausa medical ASR using the fixed MMS-1B-FL102 acoustic model and two decoding-time language-model fusion methods: Shallow Fusion (SF) and the Density Ratio Approach (DRA). A 4-gram KenLM trained on 19,421 Hausa health sentences serves as the medical target model, while a 75,372-sentence general-domain model provides the source prior for DRA. Evaluation uses 250 recordings of 25 physician-reviewed prompts from 10 speakers across six northern Nigerian states. Under prompt-disjoint five-fold cross-validation, SF and DRA reduce WER from 29.56% to 23.55% and 23.33%, respectively. Both significantly outperform greedy decoding (p &lt; 0.001), while their difference is not significant (p = 0.423). However, general-LM-only SF achieves 23.96% WER, indicating that the medical LM's additional improvement over a general LM is modest. Medical Term Error Rate also improves under fusion, but does not establish a distinct medical-domain advantage. These findings demonstrate that lightweight, text-only language-model fusion can improve Hausa medical ASR without additional domain-specific speech collection.

</details>

<a id="paper-169"></a>

### 169. [Toward a Compositional Knowledge Architecture for a Shared Organizational Cortex](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05753v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05753

**作者：** Andrew Miracle、Phyl Georgiou、Christine Okoth、Ese Monday

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05753v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05753v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Signals、Domains、Claims、Dimensions 四层组织知识架构，用真值维护网络随证据变化修订解释的成立状态，并在检索实验中报告 Dimension 项与完整配置的 nDCG@10 提升。

**相关性简评：** 其把解释连同依据一起持久保存、并随新证据修订信念状态的做法，可能与 LVR 中多步视觉证据整合后的内部状态更新和证据筛选形成概念类比，可供核查；但属组织知识管理场景，与 VLM 推理无直接实验联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Toward a Compositional Knowledge Architecture for a Shared Organizational Cortex

**abstract/A1：** Organizations accumulate evidence in conversations, documents, decisions and relationships, but they act on interpretations: judgements assembled from several observations, resting on assumptions, and revised as circumstances change. Existing retrieval, knowledge graphs and agent memory keep the records and lose the interpretation. We propose a four-layer architecture, Signals, Domains, Claims and Dimensions, in which interpretations persist with their justifications and standing, a truth-maintenance network revises that standing as evidence changes, and validated, versioned Dimensions position interpretations on named axes. We call the result a shared organizational cortex. In a registered retrieval experiment, the Dimension term gains 0.028 nDCG@10 (p = 0.002). In system-level comparisons, the full retrieval configuration improves nDCG@10 by 0.081 over Signals-only and 0.053 over graph-augmented Signals-only retrieval (both reported p &lt; 0.001). The architecture gives organizations a persistent, revisable basis for shared judgement.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 10 figures, 10 tables. Evaluation and sources in https://github.com/withrobinhq/geometric-knowledge-paper-evals (tag registered-plan)

</details>

<a id="paper-170"></a>

### 170. [Decompose the Distillation: Interpretable Single-Pass Guidance for Diffusion Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05755v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05755

**作者：** Wuyang Zhang、Xiaolin Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05755v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05755v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CtrlFlow：在冻结骨干上挂两个轻量 LoRA，用单次前向同时预测 base flow 与 guidance direction，使 CFG 的引导强度可在推理时任意缩放而无需重训，并以多尺度蒸馏损失、尺度自适应调制与时间网格变换对齐训练与推理误差。

**相关性简评：** 将预测显式分解为「基础流 + 可缩放引导方向」，并让损失对齐推理期复合误差，可能与 LVR 中把 latent 状态拆成基础表示与可调推理更新方向、以及贡献分配式监督形成概念类比，可供核查；但论文仅在图像扩散生成中验证，未涉及视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Decompose the Distillation: Interpretable Single-Pass Guidance for Diffusion Models

**abstract/A1：** Classifier-Free Guidance is a widely used inference-time guidance rule for conditional image generation with diffusion models, which exposes a guidance scale that adjusts conditioning strength after training. This flexibility requires conditional and unconditional model evaluations at every sampling step, so an N-step trajectory takes 2N forward passes. Single-pass alternatives reduce such cost, yet most collapse CFG's two components into one opaque guided prediction, losing the separable base flow and guidance direction that support per-step control and diagnostics, even when the scale remains adjustable. This work introduces CtrlFlow, a single-pass guidance method that preserves CFG's separable base-flow and guidance-direction interface. To preserve both components at one-pass cost, CtrlFlow attaches two lightweight LoRA adapters to a frozen backbone, each adding 2.5% parameters, and predicts the base flow and guidance direction from one pass of the shared frozen backbone. Because the guidance scale multiplies the guidance direction only after the forward pass, one evaluation yields the guided velocity at any scale, so a single trained checkpoint remains adjustable at inference without retraining. Preserving two outputs introduces a training mismatch: inference amplifies guidance-direction errors by the chosen scale, while naive component-wise losses weight both outputs equally. To align training with this composed inference-time error, CtrlFlow uses a unified multi-scale distillation loss. Residual guidance errors can still persist at extreme scales, where even small guidance-direction errors are amplified by the guidance scale. To reduce this inference-time error, CtrlFlow applies scale-adaptive modulation to damp velocity-field errors and time-grid warping to concentrate solver evaluations where the trajectory changes most rapidly.

**comment/C1：** The Fortieth Annual Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems

</details>

<a id="paper-171"></a>

### 171. [PAYWORTHY: Can LLMs Predict Which Freelance Jobs an AI Agent Will Complete?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05759v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05759

**作者：** Mohammad Reza Rezaei、Dingxian Wang、Brett Levert、Celeste Fejzo、Andrew Rabinovich、Zhao Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05759v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05759v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 PAYWORTHY 基准：包含 1,494 条真实自由职业招聘帖，智能体仅凭帖文单次尝试完成任务，由 LLM 扮演客户判定接受或拒绝；再让 18 个模型在尝试前预测成功概率，用 AUC 衡量其排序能力，发现预测质量强烈依赖是否告知智能体的能力边界。

**相关性简评：** 「告知智能体不能做什么」才使成功率预测有效，这一现象可能为 LVR 中判断是否继续思考或获取证据、何时停止，以及模型对自身能力边界的认知提供线索，可供核查；但论文不涉及视觉输入或多模态 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PAYWORTHY: Can LLMs Predict Which Freelance Jobs an AI Agent Will Complete?

**abstract/A1：** Before an AI agent takes on a paid job, someone has to judge whether it is likely to succeed. We test whether frontier language models can make this call before any attempt. We propose PAYWORTHY which contains 1{,}494 real freelance job postings, sampled from 18.8M postings made between 2020 and 2026 on a freelancing platform. An agent attempts each job from the posting alone in a single run, and an LLM judge acting as the client decides whether to accept the work. We ask 18 models to forecast how likely the agent is to succeed and measure how well each model's forecasts rank the accepted jobs above the rejected ones (AUC, where 0.5 is a random ranking). The answer depends on how the agent is described. Told what the agent cannot do, every model ranks the jobs well (AUC 0.66--0.86). Told instead that it is a generic agent with a browser and code execution, the same models rank the same jobs about as well as a random ordering (0.45--0.56). These results hold under judges from four other model families (Claude, GLM, Kimi and Qwen), and the informed forecasts also rank the verdicts of two human raters. Code is available at https://github.com/upwork/Payworthy

</details>

### 172. [ME^2FDiff: Multiscale Expert Enhancing Feature Diffusion in Residual Network for Brain Tumor Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05766v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05766

**作者：** Afrida Rahman、Bijoy Kundu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05766v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05766v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ME²FDiff，用图像条件化的隐扩散模块在瓶颈处迭代精化特征，配合时间步自适应的频谱带通滤波与多尺度专家共享 token 融合，用于 BraTS 2024 治疗后胶质瘤分割。

**相关性简评：** 其「扩散式瓶颈隐状态迭代精化＋按噪声水平选择频段的滤波」可能为 LVR 中扩散式 latent 状态更新与频段/信息筛选提供借鉴线索，但属医学分割应用，与多模态推理的联系需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ME^2FDiff: Multiscale Expert Enhancing Feature Diffusion in Residual Network for Brain Tumor Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Automated segmentation of post-treatment glioma from MRI is essential for accurate diagnosis and treatment planning, yet remains difficult. Surgery and chemoradiotherapy cause structural changes in brain like resection cavities, edema, and fibrosis, that alter imaging appearance and mimic tumor recurrence. Contemporary segmentation architectures attain high accuracy with two limitations. First, the bottleneck originates from the deepest encoder stage, so one representation must serve sub-regions that differ widely in size and appearance. Second, they produce the segmentation in a single forward pass with no mechanism for refinement. We propose ME^2FDiff, a single-model architecture, addressing both. An attention-conditioned spectral diffusion block refines the bottleneck. It uses the image-conditioned latent diffusion process with two processes: a spatial pre-attention gate and a spectral fusion block whose timestep-adaptive bandpass filter selects the informative frequency band at each noise level. A multiscale expert-enhanced feature block fuses the intermediate encoder scales into the bottleneck through expert-selected shared tokens, guaranteeing balanced utilization without an auxiliary loss. On the BraTS 2024 post-treatment glioma dataset, ME^2FDiff achieves the best results on every tumor sub-region among all single-model baselines (91.23% Dice, 84.52% IOU, 2.13 mm HD95 on whole-tumor) and surpasses a three-expert ensemble on both overlap metrics at roughly one-third of its inference cost. Against recent state-of-the-art methods retrained under identical conditions, it maintains the strongest performance with the fewest parameters of any competing model. These findings demonstrate that a single, compact network can absorb the specialization, usually sought through ensembling or larger architectures.

</details>

<a id="paper-173"></a>

### 173. [GREP: Grounding Generated Room Evaluation in Principles](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05802v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05802

**作者：** Mohan Cao、Yongsen Mao、Chucheng Xiang、Zhongyuan Liu、Zhihang Zhong、Haiping Wang、Yuan Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05802v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05802v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 GREP，以物理有效性、视觉一致性、人类使用逻辑三层原则指导一个评估 agent 自主从场景上下文中定位问题、收集证据并输出评分与报告，用于评价 LLM 生成的家具房间场景，并验证反馈报告可反哺场景改进。

**相关性简评：** 其「定原则—自主找问题—取证—给判断」的分层评估流程，可能与 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、何时继续获取证据与停止的决策有概念关联，但对象是 3D 房间场景而非模型内部推理状态，具体可借鉴程度需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GREP: Grounding Generated Room Evaluation in Principles

**abstract/A1：** Recent LLM-centric advances can generate attractive furnished rooms by selecting and arranging assets. Yet evaluation remains limited to specific perspectives or predefined questions, failing to reflect overall scene quality. To address this pressing need, we introduce GREP. Its core is a focus shift from defining specific questions to establishing a three-tier evaluation philosophy: basic physical validity checks whether a scene obeys physical constraints; visual coherence concerns compatibility in object scale, style, and composition; and, at the highest level, human-use logic requires object arrangements to align with everyday human habits. Guided by these principles, an evaluation agent autonomously and adaptively identifies issues from scene context, gathers evidence, and produces scores and reports. This supports scene-specific issue discovery and reliable judgments. Experiments show that (1) GREP achieves 87% agreement with human majority preferences, substantially outperforming baseline evaluators. (2) GREP reports improve scene quality through refinement, whereas score-only and no-feedback baselines show slight declines. (3) GREP suggests that as planning ability improves, recent models such as GPT-5.6 Sol can move from relying on pipelines and harnesses to challenging their guidance and constraints, but still lack the ability to resolve the resulting conflicts. Stronger models are therefore expected not only to plan well, but also to adapt to external guidance and constraints more flexibly. Project page: https://han-808.github.io/GREP/

**comment/C1：** 24 pages. Project page: https://han-808.github.io/GREP/

</details>

### 174. [DualCast: Dual-Image Conditioned Diffusion Transformers for Digital Human E-Commerce Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05825v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05825

**作者：** Lifan Jiang、Yibo Zhao、Senlin Cheng、Bolei Xu、Xiaobo Li、Can Ye、Dehai Zhao、Deng Cai、Boxi Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05825v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05825v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DualCast，用角色区分的双图条件框架生成数字人电商视频：将商品与人物图像分别 VAE 编码后置于 DiT 序列首尾边界，配合掩码区域加权损失与条件 dropout，并构建 10K 视频数据集。

**相关性简评：** 其「单参考坍缩」与按角色分离、并在序列前后边界定向注入 latent 的做法，可供核查 latent 位置与角色结构如何影响视觉证据被使用，可能与 LVR 中的视觉旁路或信息坍缩问题类比，但属视频生成设定，迁移性待验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DualCast: Dual-Image Conditioned Diffusion Transformers for Digital Human E-Commerce Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Digital human e-commerce video generation requires preserving product appearance, human identity, and overall video style during dynamic demonstrations. Although recent models support multiple reference images, their general conditioning schemes do not explicitly distinguish the asymmetric roles of "what is presented" and "who presents it." Consequently, generated videos may favor a single reference, resulting in product distortion, identity drift, or single-reference collapse. To address this issue, we propose DualCast, a role-addressed dual-image conditioning framework for video generation. By explicitly modeling the distinct roles of product and human conditions, DualCast reduces role ambiguity and feature competition between references. First, a dual-spatiotemporal slot injection mechanism VAE-encodes the product and human images separately and places their latent features at the front and rear boundaries of the DiT sequence, enabling sequence-level condition separation and directed injection. Second, a mask-guided region-weighted loss strengthens supervision over product, face, and product-face overlap regions, improving generation fidelity in critical areas. Third, image condition dropout enables a single model to support dual-image, product-only, and human-only generation. In addition, we construct a curated dataset of 10K e-commerce videos for model training. Experimental results demonstrate the strong performance of DualCast.

</details>

<a id="paper-175"></a>

### 175. [A low-rank decoder bottleneck bounds reliability in foundation-model perturbation prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05849v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05849

**作者：** Zheyu Zhang、Yuanhao Huang、Fan Feng、Jie Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05849v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05849v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文审计 STATE 模型中 state-transition 解码器在三种编码器与四条细胞系下的行为，发现其首层注意力输出呈低秩，且预测响应对最近训练响应的余弦距离可作为逐预测置信度，与 PDC 相关性接近使用真实响应时的水平。

**相关性简评：** 低秩注意力瓶颈限制预测可靠性的结论，可能与 LVR 中 latent 状态信息坍缩、表示容量不足的问题相通；以「距最近训练样本的距离」作逐预测置信度，或可为判断视觉证据是否被利用及停止/弃答决策提供评价思路，可供核查其跨任务迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A low-rank decoder bottleneck bounds reliability in foundation-model perturbation prediction

**abstract/A1：** Foundation-model predictors of perturbation response are evaluated primarily by aggregate accuracy. Using their predictions to prioritize experiments requires assessing each prediction's reliability. We audit the state-transition (ST) decoder used in STATE across three encoders (SE-600M, scGPT, and Tahoe-x1) and four cell lines under CRISPRi. The decoder's first attention layer has a low-rank output. Projecting this output onto 4-32 fixed directions estimated from training activations preserves mean per-perturbation Pearson delta correlation (PDC). This low-rank attention bottleneck bounds the decoder's reliability by distance to training responses: PDC declines as the cosine distance from a perturbation's observed response to its nearest training response increases. We propose predicted-NN-distance, the cosine distance from a predicted response to its nearest training response, as a per-prediction confidence score. The score has a mean absolute Spearman correlation of 0.81 with PDC, close to the 0.88 obtained with the observed response. Thresholds calibrated on this score transfer across encoders and cell lines, and the score remains predictive of PDC for drug perturbations.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 176. [Autonomous agents for electricity-system planning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05911v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05911

**作者：** Yoon Pyo Lee、Samrendra Roy、Jay Phil Yoo、Kazuma Kobayashi、Souvik Chakraborty、Syed Bahauddin Alam

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05911v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05911v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一个自主电力系统规划框架：多个 agent 提出投资上限、储能运行条件与馈线改造方案，经数值优化与校验后解读反馈、修订设计并决定何时停止，按固定验收准则保留成本最低的可行方案。

**相关性简评：** 其「提议—数值评估—反馈解读—重设计—停止」闭环与固定验收、候选留存机制，可供核查 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 决策与验证式评判的设计，但属领域工程应用，无 latent 表示或多模态状态，迁移价值有限。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Autonomous agents for electricity-system planning

**abstract/A1：** Planning electricity systems requires repeated decisions that connect regional resources, investment, operation and network constraints. Here we present an autonomous planning framework that carries prepared regional scenarios through proposal, numerical evaluation, feedback interpretation, redesign and final plan selection. Agents propose investment bounds, storage operating conditions and feeder interventions, interpret numerical feedback, and decide how to revise designs and when to stop. The framework automatically executes annual investment-and-dispatch optimization and representative-feeder verification, applies fixed acceptance criteria and retains the lowest-cost accepted candidate. We evaluate forty carbon-constrained trajectories across ten system-year scenarios in five electricity systems and four countries, using two model deployments and single- and multi-agent architectures. All forty retain candidates satisfying the implemented physical and fixed annual operational carbon-budget checks. Twenty-eight of forty matched reference designs exceed the new scenario ceilings. A separate archive of ninety-six trajectories under a common 1,000 Mt annual ceiling provides matched references and broader regional coverage. The results demonstrate autonomous planning under explicit emissions constraints using public regional data and published institutional demand outlooks.

</details>

### 177. [Graph-Mediated Large Language Model Systems in the Agentic Era: Reasoning, Knowledge, Retrieval, Execution, and Coordination](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05953v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05953

**作者：** Haihui Liu、Yang Chen、Zice Qiu、Yun Zhang、Jialun Zhong、Wenhua Wang、Yanzeng Li、Tian Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05953v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05953v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文是一篇综述，提出按「操作—角色—承担者」划分的图机制分类法，覆盖推理、知识与证据、检索与记忆、执行与协调五个环节，并整理基准、过程诊断与系统成本等评价框架。

**相关性简评：** 其关于中间观察与记忆表示如何约束后续决策、组件贡献区分及自适应计算与停止的讨论，可能为 LVR 中的状态组织、贡献分配和 THINK/LOOK/STOP 评价提供问题表述上的启发，但无视觉或多模态内容，需自行核查可迁移程度。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Graph-Mediated Large Language Model Systems in the Agentic Era: Reasoning, Knowledge, Retrieval, Execution, and Coordination

**abstract/A1：** Graphs play an increasingly important role in large language model (LLM) systems, supporting structural reasoning, knowledge grounding, retrieval, and memory. As these systems evolve toward the agentic era, graphs also organize execution dependencies and multi-agent communication, linking intermediate observations and retained information to subsequent decisions. However, understanding their contribution requires distinguishing the function of each graph operation, the component responsible for its computation, and the subsequent use of its output. This survey introduces an operation-level role--locus taxonomy for reasoning, knowledge and evidence, retrieval and memory, execution, and coordination. We compare how graph observations and external computations guide decisions, how memory representations and updates support later tasks, and how dependencies constrain execution and information delivery. These comparisons distinguish improvements in local metrics from downstream task performance while retaining the conditions under which results were reported. We then organize benchmarks, comparison designs, process diagnostics, and system costs into an evaluation framework. Finally, we identify open challenges in adaptive computation and stopping, memory maintenance, and dependency-aware recovery. The survey provides a basis for assessing when graph mechanisms are useful and for evaluating their effects across components and stages.

</details>

<a id="paper-178"></a>

### 178. [PathoS2VD: From 2D Histopathology to Pseudo Z-Stack Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05974v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05974

**作者：** Jiamu Wang、Keunho Byeon、Ji-Uk Kim、Jin Tae Kwak、Heounjeong Go

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05974v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05974v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作用 image-to-video 潜在扩散模型从单张 2D 病理切片生成伪 z-stack，学习 z 轴形态先验，并用保先验的混合微调迁移到新域，以提升下游前列腺癌分类。

**相关性简评：** 其「由单一视角合成未观测切面」与保先验微调，可能为 LVR 中补充缺失视觉证据、以及在微调中保持潜状态先验提供概念借鉴，但这里的 latent 是扩散潜空间而非推理状态，需核查是否真有状态或证据层面的对应。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PathoS2VD: From 2D Histopathology to Pseudo Z-Stack Representations

**abstract/A1：** For decades, diagnostic pathology has relied on thin two-dimensional (2D) tissue sections, despite biological tissues exhibiting inherently three-dimensional (3D) morphological organization. Because a single section captures only one focal plane, important morphological cues along the z-axis are inevitably discarded, limiting tissue representation for both human interpretation and computational analysis. In this work, we propose a generative framework that learns z-axis morphological priors from pathology z-stack data, offering a cost-effective alternative to dedicated volumetric imaging, and transfers this knowledge to conventional single-plane histopathology. Specifically, an image-to-video (I2V) latent diffusion model (LDM) is trained to synthesize a pseudo z-stack from a single 2D image, capturing plausible morphological variations across adjacent focal planes rather than reconstructing physical tissue volumes. To enable deployment on large-scale clinical datasets without native z-stack acquisition, we further introduce a prior-preserving mixture fine-tuning strategy that adapts the model to new domains while retaining the learned z-axis morphological prior. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the synthesized pseudo z-stacks exhibit realistic cross-plane morphological continuity and consistently improve downstream prostate cancer classification compared with conventional 2D representations. Our results suggest that learning transferable z-axis priors from limited z-stack data provides a practical way to enrich routine pathology images with complementary morphological information. Code: https://github.com/QuIIL/PathoS2VD.

**comment/C1：** Pre-review version. Accepted at ACCV 2026

</details>

### 179. [MotoVerse: A Benchmark for Predictive Perception of Motorized Two-Wheelers in Dense Unstructured Traffic](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06007v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06007

**作者：** Varun A. Paturkar、Shankar Gangisetty、C. V. Jawahar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06007v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06007v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建 MotoVerse 基准，从两轮机动车骑手的第一视角采集 949 段密集非结构化交通片段，标注三万多个周围交通体的轨迹，用于自车动作预判与交通体轨迹预测两项任务，并评测传统预测模型、通用与驾驶专用 VLM 及 VLA 模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「视频骨干在动作前五分之一即达到全观测精度、而零样本 VLM 需几乎完整序列」的观测，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据充分性与停止决策（LOOK/STOP 预算）提供可核查的外部线索，但与 latent 内部状态无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MotoVerse: A Benchmark for Predictive Perception of Motorized Two-Wheelers in Dense Unstructured Traffic

**abstract/A1：** Motorized Two-Wheelers (MTWs) dominate personal transport across much of the Global South, yet remain largely overlooked in predictive perception research for autonomous driving and ADAS. We introduce MotoVerse, a benchmark for predictive perception from an MTW's ego-centric perspective. MotoVerse comprises 949 maneuver clips collected in dense, unstructured traffic and trajectory annotations for over 31,000 surrounding road agents, including over 16,000 MTWs, enabling two complementary tasks: Ego-Rider Maneuver Anticipation (ERMA) and Traffic Agent Trajectory Prediction (TATP). Each clip is further annotated with an MTW-specific challenge taxonomy comprising dynamic ego-motion, weak traffic structure and contextual occlusion, enabling challenge-aware evaluation. We benchmark established conventional prediction models, general-purpose Vision-Language Models (VLMs), driving-specific VLMs and vision-language-action models. Our experiments reveal three key insights: (i) video backbones reach most of their full-observation accuracy from the first fifth of a maneuver, whereas zero-shot VLMs need most of the maneuver to be observed; (ii) zero-shot VLMs, whether general-purpose or driving-specific, largely remain close to simple baselines on both tasks; a preliminary LoRA study shows that adaptation lifts anticipation sharply but leaves trajectory prediction behind conventional models; and (iii) the rider's own motion drives forecasting difficulty: for every model, including a static baseline, errors are highest during left turns and rise under dynamic ego-motion. We believe MotoVerse will serve as a challenging benchmark for safer and more robust predictive AI for intelligent two-wheelers. The dataset and evaluation code are publicly available at https://github.com/VarunIIITH/MotoVerse.

</details>

<a id="paper-180"></a>

### 180. [METRIC-GS: Selective Metric-Scale Estimation for 3D Gaussian Splatting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06020v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06020

**作者：** Sooyeun Yang、Jongseong Brad Choi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06020v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06020v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 METRIC-GS，用冻结 2D 教师模型的可见性投票给 Gaussian 打标签，融合建筑与家具类尺寸先验做稳健最大似然估计，并以 inlier-mass 检验在证据不足时显式弃权、把弃权样本路由到重型度量模型。

**相关性简评：** 其“证据不足即弃权、只把弃权样本升级到昂贵模型”的选择性机制，可能为 LVR 中依据证据充分性做 STOP 决策与动态计算分配提供概念参考；但对象是 3DGS 的度量尺度而非推理状态，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** METRIC-GS: Selective Metric-Scale Estimation for 3D Gaussian Splatting

**abstract/A1：** A 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstruction inherits arbitrary units from structure-from-motion, so it cannot be measured or used to place AR content until someone supplies a scale. Both standard recipes are fragile on real captures: on five indoor scenes with tape-measured ceilings, a quantile floor-to-ceiling heuristic errs by 27% and metric-depth alignment by 35-71% across three networks. We present METRIC-GS, a selective scale estimator for real indoor photographs: it reads the scale off the reconstruction's own semantic content and, just as importantly, refuses when the evidence is thin. A frozen 2D teacher labels Gaussians by visibility-aware voting; instance-level size priors for architectural and furniture classes (doors, tables, the floor-to-ceiling gap) are fused by a robust maximum-likelihood step; and an inlier-mass test turns thin evidence into an explicit abstention that doubles as a router to a heavyweight metric model. On 47 ScanNet++ scenes with laser ground truth, with a configuration frozen before public evaluation, the estimator alone cuts the heuristic's mean error from 21.1% to 16.6% (9.3% on the 28 scenes it acts on) at ~11 s per scene. MASt3R remains the more accurate standalone estimator (9.9%), but routing only our refusals to it matches running it everywhere with 60% fewer invocations; this selective hybrid is the configuration we advocate. Visibility-aware voting raises the scenes the estimator can act on from 16 to 28 at unchanged deployed accuracy. Applied unchanged to 13 phone and drone captures of our own, on which MASt3R fails its own registration gate 12 times, the system improves all five captures it acts on and degrades none. We also report that a current feed-forward metric model scores 2.3% on a benchmark inside its training mixture and 28-35% on captures it has never seen: a published metric-scale number is a best case, not a deployment estimate.

</details>

<a id="paper-181"></a>

### 181. [Attraction-Ranking Mismatch in Out-of-Distribution Detection: Theory and Evidence from Hamiltonian Feature Dynamics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06021v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06021

**作者：** Zilin Wang、Lianghai Xiao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06021v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06021v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文以类条件 Hamiltonian 特征动力学为受控测试平台，研究将特征吸引到 ID 区域能否改善 OOD 检测，并提出吸引—排序错配，借 AUROC 分解与边界局部分析定位排序边界处的相对分数变化。

**相关性简评：** 其“局部分数对齐不等于整体排序改善、迁移后还可能退化”的评估视角，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 证据打分与筛选的评价设计，但任务与视觉推理相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Attraction-Ranking Mismatch in Out-of-Distribution Detection: Theory and Evidence from Hamiltonian Feature Dynamics

**abstract/A1：** Attracting features toward learned in-distribution (ID) regions is an intuitive strategy for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, but does such attraction improve detection? We investigate this question using class-conditioned Hamiltonian dynamics as a controlled testbed and identify an attraction-ranking mismatch: improving individual class-affinity scores need not improve the relative ordering of ID and OOD samples. Our analysis separates misalignment between the driving field and the detection score from ranking failure that persists even when they are aligned. An exact AUROC decomposition and a local boundary analysis identify relative score changes at the ID-OOD ranking boundary as the source of first-order AUROC change under regularity conditions. Experiments demonstrate that a score-aligned rule can improve performance on a held-out source validation split yet consistently degrade it after transfer, even without a single winning-class change. A separate perturbation study finds that near-boundary relative score changes track the direction of small AUROC changes in the tested settings. Finally, centroid approximation and shared scales connect the zero-step score to a static centroid family that includes the existing CTM detector. Together, these results explain why class attraction is insufficient to justify feature refinement and motivate evaluating its effect on the ranking boundary.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-182"></a>

### 182. [Who Pays for the Knowledge Models Copy? Model Collapse as Rogers' Paradox](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06022v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06022

**作者：** Nam H. Le

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL、q-bio.PE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06022v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06022v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把模型在自身输出上训练所导致的知识退化（稀有知识先丧失）重新表述为文化演化中的 Rogers 悖论，并用 trigram 到预训练 transformer 的合成/混合数据实验与均衡分析论证：关键在于外部新鲜信息的供给，筛选「看似合理」的文本会加速崩溃，只有携带新信息的核查才起纠偏作用。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关联，但其「自生成数据逐代丢失稀有信息、唯有引入新信息的校验有效」的论点，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 轨迹自训练与自验证监督的信息坍缩风险。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Who Pays for the Knowledge Models Copy? Model Collapse as Rogers' Paradox

**abstract/A1：** Generative models trained on the output of earlier models lose knowledge across generations, rare knowledge first. The remedies that work all bring in information from outside the loop, and the theory of model collapse treats that supply as given: a fixed archive, drawn from a fixed distribution. We argue that the supply is the problem, and that cultural evolution has the theory of it. We read model collapse as Rogers' paradox: a population that comes to rely on copying ends up no better off than one that never copied, because copies go stale unless someone keeps learning for themselves. We test the identification in three steps. First, Rogers' model, fitted only to fully synthetic training, predicts to within a few percentage points how much rare knowledge language models, from word trigrams to a pretrained transformer, retain under other mixtures of fresh data. Second, when the world itself changes, a lineage that copies its own past needs more fresh data to keep up the faster the world changes, as Rogers' model says it must and as analyses that assume a fixed target cannot; a lineage that restarts every generation from fixed pretrained weights is largely insulated, because those weights are knowledge that is never copied. Third, we close the loop: when the people who write a model's training data can ask the model instead of finding things out, the supply of fresh data becomes endogenous. At equilibrium, the model is exactly as reliable as it is costly for people to find out for themselves, the population is no better off than without the model, and every increase in the share of model-written text must be paid for by more people learning for themselves. Users who check what they are told and relearn when it is wrong break the paradox. Filters that select plausible text are conformist and accelerate collapse; only checks that carry new information act as critical learning.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 5 figures. Code: https://github.com/drnamlabs-gif/rogers-model-collapse

</details>

<a id="paper-183"></a>

### 183. [TRACK: Multiple Instance Learning for Anti-Money Laundering in Transaction Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06035v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06035

**作者：** Seonkyu Lim、Inwoo Tae、Gwangui Hong、Jaehoon Lee、Jeongwhan Choi、Taehoon Kim、Min-Soo Kim、Yongjae Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06035v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06035v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将账户级反洗钱检测重构为多示例学习：账户窗口是 bag、交易是 instance，提出 TRACK 用共享编码器逐笔编码再选择性池化成账户决策，训练中刻意不使用交易级标签。

**相关性简评：** 「粗粒度 bag 监督下从大量实例中挑出少数关键证据、并给出实例级分数」的问题表述，可能与 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选和 latent 贡献分配相呼应，但领域与模态相距较远，仅供概念核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TRACK: Multiple Instance Learning for Anti-Money Laundering in Transaction Networks

**abstract/A1：** Anti-money laundering (AML) investigations are organized around accounts, while the suspicious evidence for an account may reside in only a few of its transactions. Yet many account-level detectors summarize transactions before learning, and message-passing models may aggregate once more over neighboring accounts. We name this discrepancy the aggregation mismatch and study it under an account-window benchmark label defined by the presence of at least one flagged transaction. We recast account-level AML detection as multiple instance learning, in which an account-window is a bag and its transactions are instances. We propose TRACK, which encodes every transaction with a shared encoder and selectively pools the encodings into an account decision.
