To emulate coarse supervision, we withhold the transaction labels of five AML datasets from TRACK throughout training. Under a time-ordered protocol, TRACK achieves the highest average precision among the tabular and graph baselines trained from account labels on every dataset. Despite using only account-window supervision, it remains competitive with edge classifiers trained directly from transaction labels. Its instance scores also rank flagged transactions well above chance and approach or exceed directly supervised ranking performance across datasets.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 3 figures. Accepted at the 20th ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining (WSDM 2027)

</details>

<a id="paper-184"></a>

### 184. [Act-HiMotif: Hierarchical Daily Activity Schedule Generation via Learned Activity-Structure Motifs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06038v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06038

**作者：** Yushi Bai、Kang Liu、Ling Yin、Wei Wang、Yilin Lyu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06038v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06038v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向出行行为建模，提出分层生成模型 Act-HiMotif：先区分「仅居家日」与「活动日」，再从个体属性条件下的先验中采样可复用的活动结构 motif 作为中间表示，最后用预算约束的时长分配器生成总长恰为整天的活动时长序列。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关系，但其「可复用离散 motif 作为属性与具体序列之间的中间状态」及「固定预算下的资源分配」可能为 LVR 的抽象 latent 状态选择与计算预算/停止决策提供概念类比，值得少量核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Act-HiMotif: Hierarchical Daily Activity Schedule Generation via Learned Activity-Structure Motifs

**abstract/A1：** Generating daily activity schedules from individual attributes (e.g., age and income) is valuable for activity-based travel modeling and human behavior simulation. The task is challenging because exact activity structures follow a highly imbalanced, long-tailed distribution, while the same structure can admit multiple duration allocations under a fixed full-day budget. Direct generation must therefore learn many exact activity structures from highly uneven observations while modeling their possible temporal realizations. However, many exact structures share recurring activity organization. Based on this observation, we propose Act-HiMotif, a hierarchical motif-based generative model that introduces learned, reusable activity-structure motifs as an intermediate representation between individual profiles and specific activity structures. Act-HiMotif first distinguishes home-only days from active days, then samples a motif from a profile-conditioned prior to guide activity-structure generation, and finally applies a budget-constrained duration allocator to generate activity durations that sum exactly to the full-day horizon. This hierarchy models home-only routing, activity-structure generation, and duration allocation at their corresponding levels, while motifs allow related structures to share statistical support without losing their distinct sequences. Experiments on large-scale travel survey data show that Act-HiMotif outperforms strong baselines in both in-distribution and spatial generalization settings, demonstrating the effectiveness of hierarchical generation and reusable activity-structure motifs for producing realistic profile-conditioned daily activity schedules.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 17 figures, including supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-185"></a>

### 185. [SVGFaith: A Two-Layer Meta-Evaluation of Faithfulness Metrics for Text-to-SVG Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06048v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06048

**作者：** Guotao Liang、Haitao Zhou、Biao Liu、Huanxuan Liao、Lingfeng Qiao、Qian-Wen Zhang、Qian Yu、Di Yin、Xing Sun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06048v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06048v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SVGFaith 两层元评估框架，通过受控对比对和人工标注竞技场检验图像—文本指标对文本到 SVG 生成的细粒度忠实度，并提出基于需求依赖图与 VQA 判分的可解释指标。

**相关性简评：** 其原子视觉需求分解、前置条件折扣与逐项判分，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、细粒度忠实度评价和停止判据提供核查线索，但研究对象是 SVG 生成评估而非 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SVGFaith: A Two-Layer Meta-Evaluation of Faithfulness Metrics for Text-to-SVG Generation

**abstract/A1：** Text-to-SVG systems are commonly evaluated by applying raster-image metrics to rasterised outputs, yet whether these scores capture fine-grained prompt faithfulness remains unclear. We introduce SVGFaith, a two-layer meta-evaluation framework in which the more faithful candidate of each pair is determined by construction or by human judgment. SVGFaith-Diagnostic contains 1,842 controlled contrastive pairs across eight capability axes, split evenly between two directions: two renderings compared against one prompt, and two descriptions compared against one rendering. SVGFaith-Arena contains 1,327 human-annotated comparisons of real generator outputs. On SVGFaith-Diagnostic, CLIP and three other image--text embedding metrics reach at least 0.789 pairwise accuracy on object identity but at most 0.672 on spatial relations and attribute binding, where chance is 0.5. We further introduce SVGFaith-Score, an interpretable pointwise metric. A domain-tuned decomposer turns each prompt into a dependency graph of atomic visual requirements, a visual question answering model scores whether each requirement is satisfied, and a requirement's score is discounted when its prerequisites score low. In addition to the overall score, the per-requirement scores show which requirements are judged unsatisfied and which inherit discounts from their prerequisites. SVGFaith-Score significantly outperforms all nine baselines, including a Qwen3.5-122B-A10B judge, on Arena and in both Diagnostic directions. Code and data: https://github.com/Yukinonooo/SVGFaith.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-186"></a>

### 186. [Learning From Static Context in Federated Time Series Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06064v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06064

**作者：** Viktor Walter、Andreas Wagner、Fabian Stricker

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06064v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06064v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察联邦学习下的全局时间序列预测模型能否真正利用描述单条序列的静态输入，比较逐批 FedAdam、FedAvg、FedAdam+Prox 与 SCAFFOLD 在建筑能耗、风电及合成数据上的差异，并用另外两种静态条件架构复核该现象。

**相关性简评：** 其「静态条件通路是否被实际使用、何时失效（static pathway falls silent）」的诊断思路，可能可供核查 VLM 推理中视觉或静态条件信息是否被内部状态真正利用，即视觉旁路与信息坍缩问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning From Static Context in Federated Time Series Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Time series forecasting is shifting from locally trained, per-series models to global models trained across many heterogeneous series, which requires learning what sets the series apart. When the raw data cannot be centralized, such a global model is trained with federated learning (FL), one client per data owner. State-of-the-art forecasters such as the Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) tell series apart through a static input that describes each series and does not change over time. In the special case of a federated setting where each client holds a single series, this static input is constant within each client and varies only across clients. We ask whether a federated TFT can learn from it. Per-batch FedAdam, which aggregates gradients after every batch, uses the static input almost as well as central training, whereas communication-efficient schemes with local epochs (FedAvg, FedAdam+Prox) extract far less from it and gain nothing significant on either application dataset. At the same local computation, an Adam-compatible SCAFFOLD keeps the static pathway responsive on wind power and gains significantly there. On building energy its error still drops, but the static pathway falls silent, so drift and communication frequency cannot be fully separated as the cause. Only per-batch FedAdam matches or beats the per-client local models across all datasets. We confirm the effect on building-energy meter data, a wind-power dataset, and a controlled synthetic dataset. The loss is not specific to the TFT: on the synthetic and the wind data two further architectures with different static-conditioning mechanisms show it as well.

**comment/C1：** 4th IEEE International Conference on Federated Learning Technologies and Applications (FLTA 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-187"></a>

### 187. [RouTex: Multi-reference 3D Texture Generation by Reprogramming Rotary Position Encoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06070v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06070

**作者：** Chengyang Li、Yang Liu、Jiale Zhang、Qiang Rao、Wei Zhang、Taicheng Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06070v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06070v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RouTex，用 in-context DiT 完成多视角参考图像条件下的 3D 纹理生成，将 3D RoPE 沿空间与语义维度重编程为「位置路由」，并配合局部与全局双粒度注意力。

**相关性简评：** 该工作不研究 VLM 推理，但「用位置编码承担参考到视角的显式寻址」可能为 latent 状态的证据索引与视觉证据筛选提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RouTex: Multi-reference 3D Texture Generation by Reprogramming Rotary Position Encoding

**abstract/A1：** While single-image 3D texture generation has advanced significantly, conditioning texture synthesis on multi-view reference images remains largely under-explored, despite its necessity for resolving surface ambiguity in unobserved regions. In this paper, we propose RouTex, a novel multi-reference 3D texture generation framework based on in-context Diffusion Transformers (DiT). Our key insight is that native 3D Rotary Position Encoding (RoPE) inherently possesses spatial addressing capabilities. We introduce Positional Routing (PR) to reprogram 3D RoPE along spatial and semantic dimensions, establishing explicit reference-to-view correspondence without introducing additional parameters. To execute this routing efficiently, Dual-Granularity Attention (DGA) combines per-view local attention for fine texture fidelity with global joint attention over deduplicated references for cross-view consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RouTex achieves new state-of-the-art performance in multi-reference 3D texture generation.

</details>

<a id="paper-188"></a>

### 188. [GPUMAP: GPU-Accelerated Progressive Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06191v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06191

**作者：** Myeongwon Jung、Jaemin Jo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.GR、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06191v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06191v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 GPUMAP，将 UMAP 拆解为三个原子操作并重构为渐进式降维流水线，配合操作队列、实例级 local join 与调度策略及 GPU 并行，实现百万规模数据的交互式分析，并系统考察渐进投影随时间的演化与数据流偏差的影响。

**相关性简评：** 其渐进式、可流式更新的投影机制可能为在线观察 LVR 内部 latent 状态几何与相似度结构的演化提供工具线索，但论文本身不涉及视觉推理，需核查其增量更新是否适用于 latent 轨迹分析。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GPUMAP: GPU-Accelerated Progressive Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection

**abstract/A1：** Dimensionality reduction (DR) techniques are fundamental to the analysis of high-dimensional data, yet their substantial computational cost limits their use in interactive settings. To address this limitation, we present GPU-accelerated Progressive Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection (GPUMAP), an efficient progressive DR algorithm designed for interactive use. We identify three atomic operations within UMAP and restructure them into a progressive pipeline. As part of this adaptation, we introduce modifications to UMAP, including operation queues, instance-wise local join, and scheduling strategies. We further accelerate computation by leveraging GPU parallelism, enabling interactive analysis of million-scale datasets on commodity GPU hardware. Finally, we employ our implementation to systematically study the behavior of progressive DR, investigating how projections evolve over time relative to the ground-truth projection and how biased data streams influence the quality and stability of the resulting projections.

</details>

<a id="paper-189"></a>

### 189. [Cross-Asset Knowledge Transfer for Early Fault Detection of Data-Scarce Assets Without Target Fault Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06194v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06194

**作者：** Zekai Wang、Yuxuan Li、Zhangyue Shi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06194v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06194v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将跨资产早期故障检测形式化为「目标无故障样本」的零样本域适应，用 EWMA 控制图为源资产 run-to-failure 数据自动打标，提出 ADANN（域对抗 + MMD 惩罚 + 将分类器正常区锚定到目标自身正常数据的 ACD 损失），并用带预测时域的提前标注瞄准早期故障，在公开轴承数据上取得最高平均 F1。

**相关性简评：** 该工作「仅用目标正常数据校准判别边界」与「带预测时域的提前标注」可能为 LVR 中只用成功/正常轨迹校准 latent 异常检测与停止判据提供核查线索，域对抗加 MMD 的表示对齐也可作为跨域 latent 对齐的可比方案，但与本主线任务相距较远，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cross-Asset Knowledge Transfer for Early Fault Detection of Data-Scarce Assets Without Target Fault Data

**abstract/A1：** Reliability management of industrial fleets depends on early fault warnings, yet lifecycle data across similar assets are highly imbalanced. A few assets accumulate run-to-failure histories, while newly installed or rarely failing assets provide only normal-condition data. This paper asks how diagnostic knowledge can be transferred from a data-rich source asset to a data-scarce target asset when no fault data have been observed on the target. We formulate cross-asset early fault detection as a zero-shot domain adaptation problem, in which zero-shot refers to the absence of target fault samples, and propose a framework that requires only normal data from the target. The framework comprises (1) a statistical process control step that labels the source asset run-to-failure data with an exponentially weighted moving average chart, reducing manual annotation to verifying flagged segments, (2) an Adapted Domain-Adversarial Neural Network (ADANN) that combines domain-adversarial training and a Maximum Mean Discrepancy penalty with an Adapted Classification-Domain (ACD) loss, which anchors the classifier normal region to the target own normal data, and (3) an ahead-of-time labeling scheme with a prediction horizon that targets incipient faults. Under an exact-classification assumption, a theoretical analysis characterizes the domain-adversarial term for a normal-only target and the alignment state in which source fault features remain separated from target normal features. On a public run-to-failure bearing dataset, ADANN attains the highest mean F1-score among four transfer baselines, with a nominally significant precision gain over the strongest baseline, and its robustness is examined under synthetic noise.

</details>

<a id="paper-190"></a>

### 190. [BiCG-Net: Target-Specific Image Quality Assessment via Observation Modeling and In-Image Differencing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06203v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06203

**作者：** Yibu Yang、Tingqiong Cui、Liming Lei、Hao Xiong、Jun Xu、Siyuan Liu、Guanghui Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06203v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06203v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究图像中特定目标区域（而非整幅图像）的质量评估，提出 BiCG-Net：用由目标框向外扩展的嵌套 patch 采样序列、线性观测模型与图内差分机制蒸馏 11 个教师模型，并在多个 IQA 基准及零样本跨库迁移上评测。

**相关性简评：** 「由目标向外」的 patch 观测序列与差分消去目标项/教师偏差的设计，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、背景干扰诊断提供类比线索，但任务本身是 IQA，可否迁移需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BiCG-Net: Target-Specific Image Quality Assessment via Observation Modeling and In-Image Differencing

**abstract/A1：** We estimate the quality of a specific target region (e.g., a train body) in an image, rather than the whole image. This differs fundamentally from whole-image No-Reference Image Quality Assessment (NR-IQA): when the target occupies a small fraction of the frame, the whole-image score is dominated by background, and no public benchmark provides target-level human Mean Opinion Score (MOS). We estimate target quality via nested sampling - a sequence of patches expanding outward from the target box - and infer quality from the relative structure of this sequence. This motivates BiCG-Net, a student network that distills an 11-teacher ensemble through a linear observation model and an in-image differencing mechanism, which provably cancels the target term and teacher-specific bias. We train and test the student independently on each dataset, and evaluate zero-shot transfer across benchmarks. On CSIQ and LIVEC, the student reaches PLCCs of 0.9009 and 0.9133 against human MOS, exceeding the best single teacher and the teacher consensus. On a self-built MOS-free railway dataset, the student reaches a PLCC of 0.9855 against a weak label. The student exceeds its teachers because it learns a new mapping from the relative structure of teacher readings to the ground-truth label, rather than mimicking them. Residual analysis on CSIQ and LIVEC shows the prediction residual is significantly correlated with background contamination, teacher dispersion, and label extremity, demonstrating the student extracts information the consensus does not contain. Under CSIQ-to-LIVEC transfer, the frozen student reaches a PLCC of 0.8416, exceeding the consensus by 0.1947. We position BiCG-Net as an offline target-level quality auditing and label-generation engine; its 39,482-parameter student is compact by design, acting as a unifying layer mapping heterogeneous teacher readings onto a common quality scale.

**comment/C1：** 34 pages, 3 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-191"></a>

### 191. [Separation Is a Riesz Energy: How Many Environments Does ICA Need?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06228v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06228

**作者：** Yuda Bi、Yubo Guo、Jiaxiao Cai、Xiaonai Li、Zhuolin Li、Vince D. Calhoun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.IT、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06228v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06228v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究全高斯成分下的 ICA 可恢复性：利用成分方差跨环境变化，对 K 个协方差做联合对角化，给出分离单个成分所需的 Riesz 2-能量代价，以及随成分数 d 与环境数变化的样本复杂度维度律。

**相关性简评：** 「每成分分离能量 + 环境数/维度律」可能为 LVR 中潜在推理状态的可辨识性与所需异构条件、监督数量提供定量参照，但该框架针对线性高斯混合，与视觉或多模态 latent 状态的对应性可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Separation Is a Riesz Energy: How Many Environments Does ICA Need?

**abstract/A1：** Can independent components be recovered when all of them are Gaussian? Yes, if their variances change across environments; but how many environments, and how many samples as the number of components $d$ grows? We answer both for the joint diagonalization of $K$ covariances. Each component has a signature, its variances across the environments. Separating component $j$ costs the Riesz 2-energy $E\_j$ of this signature, a sum, over the other components, of inverse pairwise Fisher informations. For Gaussian sources no estimator in $O(d)$ beats $cE\_j/(n+E\_j)$ at any $n$ and $d$, and above an explicit sample size a spectral estimator attains $E\_j/n$ up to a constant, for every mixing and without the signatures (given a lower bound on the pairwise information). Since signatures live in $K-1$ dimensions, the rate obeys a dimension law: $d^2/n$, $d\\log d/n$ and $d/n$ for $K=2$, $3$ and $K\\ge4$ well-spread environments. What counts is the dimension the signatures span, not their number, and $\\log d$ well-designed environments make $d\\log d$ samples suffice. Projecting the covariances onto one linear combination pays $d^2/n$ at first order instead. In simulations up to $d=1000$ the predictions hold and joint diagonalization with $K\\ge4$ attains the rate once $n/d$ is in the hundreds; on task fMRI the energy says which components are hard to separate.

</details>

<a id="paper-192"></a>

### 192. [HarnessFlow: MCTS-Guided Harness Optimization for LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06254v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06254

**作者：** Zhi Hong、Qianlong Wang、Hongchen Wei、Aidan Wu、Daohong Ying、Mingze Kong、Bei Liu、Chong Luo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06254v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06254v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HarnessFlow，在固定模型与任务环境的前提下，用 MCTS 组织候选 harness 的改进树，并由编码智能体结合代码、执行反馈与可复用机制库迭代改写，在每基准 15 次尝试的预算内于六个基准上超过初始 harness 与 Meta-Harness。

**相关性简评：** harness 决定工具调用与环境交互流程，与 LVR 中「何时 LOOK、何时 STOP」的控制策略有概念对应；其预算受限的候选搜索与执行反馈改进，可供核查能否迁移到 latent 推理流程的自动配置与评价，但论文本身不涉及视觉输入或内部状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HarnessFlow: MCTS-Guided Harness Optimization for LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** The performance of large language model (LLM) agents depends on both the underlying model and the harness that governs interaction with tools and the environment. Yet harness design remains largely manual, and automating it requires navigating an open-ended search space through costly end-to-end evaluations. We introduce HarnessFlow, a framework for optimizing complete executable harnesses under a limited candidate-evaluation budget, with the model and task environment held fixed. HarnessFlow organizes candidates and their evaluation histories in a refinement tree, using a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-based policy to balance exploitation and exploration. A coding agent refines selected harnesses using archived code, execution feedback, and a library of reusable harness mechanisms. With 15 refinement attempts per benchmark, HarnessFlow achieves the highest mean test scores among the compared methods on six benchmarks spanning agentic tasks, coding, and web interaction. The optimized harnesses achieve an average score of 69.48%, outperforming the initial harnesses and Meta-Harness by 10.89 and 3.95 percentage points, respectively, while incurring lower average per-task execution costs than hand-engineered baselines. These results suggest that structured harness search offers a promising route toward automating the design of capable and efficient LLM agents.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 8 figures, 7 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-193"></a>

### 193. [Learning Pseudo-Riemannian Manifolds for Heterophilic Graphs via Graph Signature](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06265v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06265

**作者：** Yun Young Choi、Asung Kil、Sun Woo Park、Minho Lee、Seokhwan Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06265v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06265v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文针对异质图（边表示相异或结构排斥）中正定几何先验不匹配的问题，提出 Graph Signature Index 来量化类兼容矩阵的惯性，并据此设计 PRAT：以符号化的伪距离平方作为注意力 logit，在学习到的对角度量下进行训练。

**相关性简评：** 其「用不定度量表示相异/排斥关系」及「先诊断矩阵惯性、再约束学习」的思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的表示几何（尤其相似度是否需带符号结构、状态是否需编码反差信息）提供可核查的类比工具，但领域相距较远，需要较多迁移工作。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Pseudo-Riemannian Manifolds for Heterophilic Graphs via Graph Signature

**abstract/A1：** Several widely used families of Graph Neural Networks carry a positive-definite geometric prior. Graph convolution read as heat diffusion needs a positive semi-definite operator, hyperbolic models place features on positive-definite Riemannian manifolds, and dot-product and GAT-style attention scores use positive-definite inner products. On heterophilic graphs, where edges often signify dissimilarity or structural repulsion, that prior is a mismatch: no positive-definite form can assign a negative squared distance to a direction the labels separate. To measure the mismatch, we introduce the Graph Signature Index ($s\_{\\mathcal{G}}$), the inertia of a class-compatibility matrix built from label topology alone, and we prove that no value of the edge or node homophily index in $(0,1)$ rules out a negative eigenvalue. We then propose the Pseudo-Riemannian Attention Network (PRAT), whose attention logit is a signed squared pseudo-distance under a learnable diagonal metric initialized from $s\_{\\mathcal{G}}$. The design is diagnose-then-learn: the signature supplies a sign structure that unconstrained training keeps outside near-zero directions, and the projection learns the rest of the metric around it. Across fifteen node-classification benchmarks PRAT outperforms or matches the strongest heterophily-adapted models. A controlled ablation ties that performance to the sign structure itself: with architecture and protocol fixed, forbidding the metric to be indefinite costs accuracy on the graphs $s\_{\\mathcal{G}}$ marks as indefinite and leaves the others unchanged.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 41 pages, including appendix

</details>

<a id="paper-194"></a>

### 194. [DyPSI: Dynamic Physics Sensing via Joint Field and Sensor-Trajectory Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06280v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06280

**作者：** Yizhou Zhang、Panqi Chen、Lei Cheng、Ting Zhang、Jianlong Li、Jianfeng Shi、Shikai Fang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06280v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06280v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DyPSI，把动态物理感知表述为在历史观测条件下联合生成未来场与传感器轨迹：将离散传感器坐标提升为连续位置场，用共享 Functional Tucker 表示统一编码，并在该 latent 空间训练联合扩散模型，配合高斯过程时间核约束扰动以提升时间平滑性。

**相关性简评：** 其「在 latent 空间中联合预测未来状态与下一步观测位置」的建模，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK 的证据获取与停止决策提供参照；文中把训练目标联系到 A-最优设计准则与轨迹变分界，可供核查其是否能解释主动取证的收益与成本。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DyPSI: Dynamic Physics Sensing via Joint Field and Sensor-Trajectory Generation

**abstract/A1：** Physics sensing in evolving environments requires reconstructing dense spatiotemporal fields from sparse observations while adapting where future observations should be collected. Existing reconstruction and sensor-placement methods have made substantial progress, but they typically rely on static sensing assumptions: they either use a single layout across all frames or optimize placements independently at each frame, which limits their ability to produce temporally coherent sensing trajectories. We introduce DyPSI, a dynamic physics sensing framework that formulates this problem as the joint generation of future fields and sensor trajectories conditioned on historical observations. DyPSI lifts discrete sensor coordinates into continuous Sensor Position Fields, encodes fields and sensing configurations with a shared Functional Tucker representation, and trains a joint diffusion model to generate future fields and sensing trajectories simultaneously in the resulting latent space. A Gaussian-process temporal kernel correlates the perturbation injected during diffusion training, biasing the denoiser toward temporally smooth recoveries, and we further provide an analysis linking the EDM training objective to a sequence-aggregated A-optimality criterion and a kernel-induced bound on discrete trajectory variation. Experiments on turbulent flow, GLORYS12 sea-surface temperature, and 3D car aerodynamics show that DyPSI consistently outperforms static and frame-wise placement strategies, with substantial error reduction under tight sensor budgets. The code is available at https://github.com/mengqi132/DyPSI.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 35 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-195"></a>

### 195. [The Split Protocol Decides the Result: Re-Evaluating Encrypted Traffic Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06290v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06290

**作者：** Mayan Sharma、Kriti Saini、Devansh Behl

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06290v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06290v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文重新评估加密流量分类，指出随机划分会让同一抓包流量分居训练测试两侧从而虚高指标，并提出用单标识探针在训练前诊断应采用哪种划分轴，同时公开代码与负结果。

**相关性简评：** 虽属网络流量领域，其「泄漏控制划分」与「单标识探针」诊断思路可能可供核查 LVR 中以探针读取 latent 状态时，是否会因样本同源或捷径特征而高估信息保留与推理收益。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Split Protocol Decides the Result: Re-Evaluating Encrypted Traffic Classification

**abstract/A1：** Encrypted traffic classifiers are routinely reported at 95-99% accuracy. We show that on one of two public corpora, the same model and code score macro-F1 0.992 under the evaluation protocol standard in the literature and 0.567 under a protocol that keeps flows from one packet capture on one side of the split. Nothing changes but the partition (gap 0.426 over eight seeds, p = 0.008 after Holm-Bonferroni correction). The inflation is not a property of random splitting as such. On a month of backbone QUIC traffic, we evaluate five split protocols at eight seeds each: random, temporal and client-disjoint splitting are statistically indistinguishable from session-disjoint, while server-disjoint splitting costs 0.192 macro-F1 (d = 17.0, p = 0.008). Which axis matters depends on how the corpus was collected, in a way invisible from its label space. We give a diagnostic that identifies the right axis before any model is trained: single-identifier probes. A decision tree on the capture identifier alone reaches 0.983 under a random split and exactly 0.000 under session-disjoint splitting. Under this evaluation, a metric-trained flow embedding leads four deep baselines but does not beat gradient-boosted trees on twenty packet sizes. Five of our own pre-registered architectural claims fail: distance-based rejection of unknown applications beats softmax thresholding, but ablating every metric objective makes rejection better, because class separation correlates at r = -0.88 with rejection quality. Few-shot enrollment is flat within a corpus but, across corpora, five labelled flows per class recover 91% of the deficit left by failed zero-shot transfer. All splits, results, code and negative findings are released.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables Code and data splits: https://github.com/Mayan10/Flowconx

</details>

<a id="paper-196"></a>

### 196. [Multi-Resident Data Association in Smart Homes via MDP Modeling and Behavior Cloning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06296v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06296

**作者：** Xi Chen、Julien Cumin、Fano Ramparany、Dominique Vaufreydaz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.NE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06296v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06296v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文将智能家居多居民场景的「数据关联」问题重构为 MDP 序贯决策任务，用行为克隆训练策略，并分别实例化为轻量 Transformer 多标签分类器与微调 LLM 的条件文本生成。

**相关性简评：** 其核心是在匿名离散事件流上推断不可见实体的归属并保持全局一致性，与 LVR 中隐状态/信念状态的维护、证据归属与轨迹碎片化问题结构相似，可供核查状态抽象与一致性约束的借鉴价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multi-Resident Data Association in Smart Homes via MDP Modeling and Behavior Cloning

**abstract/A1：** Ambient sensors offer a privacy-preserving solution for activity recognition in smart homes, yet they face a critical challenge in multi-resident environments known as Data Association, which aims to identify the resident triggering a specific anonymous sensor event. Existing approaches fundamentally fail to maintain global consistency by often yielding conflicting associations for silent residents. Additionally, these methods frequently suffer from severe trajectory fragmentation caused by the ontological mismatch of applying continuous physical motion models to discrete logical behaviors. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework that reformulates data association as a sequential decision-making task within a Markov Decision Process (MDP). We solve the defined MDP via Behavior Cloning (BC) to avoid complex reward engineering by directly learning from expert demonstrations. We instantiate the policy using two complementary architectures, including a lightweight Transformer that treats association as a multi-label classification task over linearized event histories, and a fine-tuned Large Language Model (LLM) that reformulates the problem as conditional text generation to leverage pre-trained semantic knowledge. Extensive evaluations on three diverse datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, particularly in resolving concurrent multi-resident events. Furthermore, the LLM-based policy shows preliminary cross-environment transfer potential under benchmark settings, while its robustness in real deployments still requires further validation.

</details>

<a id="paper-197"></a>

### 197. [When Does Risk-Controlled Throughput Forecasting Actually Control Risk? A Reproduction Study on LEO Satellite Link](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06307v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06307

**作者：** Mayan Sharma、Kriti Saini、Devansh Behl、Suganya R、S. Vijayaragavan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.NI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06307v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06307v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 复现 LEO 卫星链路吞吐预测的风险预算方法 BG-CFQS，在原文三条 Starlink trace 与一个独立数据集上检验其风险保证是否成立，并比较随机划分与时序划分、风险预算下限、静态与在线 conformal 校准以及分组条件重校准的效果差异。

**相关性简评：** 「静态校准只移动工作点而不改善排序」「在线校准能贴合预算」「按协变量分组条件化反而更差」这些实证结论，可能为 LVR 中基于置信度的停止决策与证据筛选如何做风险控制提供可供核查的线索，但任务领域与视觉推理相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Does Risk-Controlled Throughput Forecasting Actually Control Risk? A Reproduction Study on LEO Satellite Link

**abstract/A1：** Risk-budgeted throughput forecasting converts a point forecast of LEO satellite capacity into a conservative bound whose overestimation rate is held at a user-specified budget, so that admission control can act on it safely. We reproduce the state of the art, BG-CFQS, on the three Starlink traces its authors used and on an independent fourth dataset, and report four findings. First, its risk guarantee is reproducible but conditional on the calibration split: under a random split we recover every published quantity, including risk-pass on 3 of 3 datasets, but under a contiguous temporal split, which is what a deployed terminal faces, it holds on 1 of 3. Second, independent of any split, the method cannot serve a risk budget below 0.15, the low end of its candidate quantile set. On all four datasets the achieved rate is identical at budgets of 0.05, 0.10 and 0.15, overshooting by 1.4x to 3.7x at the tightest. Third, static conformal calibration cannot improve a ranking, only shift an operating point; we confirm this exactly on latency spike detection, where online calibration improves AUPRC by up to 51%. Fourth, group-conditional online recalibration splits into two mechanisms. The online part replicates: it tracks the budget within 1% from 0.05 to 0.35, where static calibration is 16 to 21% off. The group-conditional part does not: on three of four datasets, conditioning on any available covariate, including measured serving-satellite geometry, is worse than not conditioning, and our attempt to predict which datasets benefit failed. What is new here is empirical, not algorithmic: we claim the findings, not the methods.

**comment/C1：** Code: https://github.com/Mayan10/Thalweg

</details>

<a id="paper-198"></a>

### 198. [Conformal Prediction for Distribution-to-Distribution Regression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06320v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06320

**作者：** Trung-Khang Tran、Tuan A. Hoang、Viet-Hoang Tran、Tan M. Nguyen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06320v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06320v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究分布到分布回归下的 Conformal Prediction，指出朴素 Split CP 虽边际有效但预测集缺乏可解释性，提出在正交基空间中构造带覆盖保证的预测集，并给出具渐近条件覆盖的自适应变体。

**相关性简评：** 其带有限样本覆盖保证的预测集与自适应条件覆盖思路，可能为 LVR 中 STOP 决策与答案验证提供校准的不确定性准则，但需核查分布级预测集能否迁移到 latent 状态或推理轨迹的置信评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Conformal Prediction for Distribution-to-Distribution Regression

**abstract/A1：** Conformal Prediction (CP) provides a model-agnostic framework for constructing finite-sample prediction sets with coverage guarantees and has become a tool for reliable decision-making in high-risk settings. Recent advances have extended Split Conformal Prediction to increasingly complex learning problems, including multilabel, high-dimensional, and functional outputs. In this work, we study CP for Distribution-to-Distribution Regression, a setting of growing interest across many application domains. We show that a naive application of Split CP yields prediction sets that, while marginally valid, are non-interpretable, e.g., lacking a well-defined volume or a tractable sampling procedure, and of limited practical value. To overcome this, we propose a new conformal framework that operates in an orthogonal basis space, producing interpretable prediction sets while preserving coverage guarantees. We further develop an adaptive variant with asymptotic conditional coverage under mild assumptions. Empirical results on synthetic and real-world datasets validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/tuanhacs/conformal\_d2d.

</details>

<a id="paper-199"></a>

### 199. [EnzymeRSI: A dual-loop recursive self-improvement system for enzyme design](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06321v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06321

**作者：** Genoria AI Team、Yuejie Hou、Lixiang Yang、Yisi Liu、Weiting Tang、Yueyuxiao Yang、Meng Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06321v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06321v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EnzymeRSI 双环递归自我改进系统用于酶工程：外环在不改变模型权重的前提下演化实验流程与预测、筛选策略，内环在受控条件下开发算法，并规划云端实验室闭环验证。

**相关性简评：** 该工作与视觉推理无直接关系，但其「外环改流程而非权重、内环改预测与筛选」的分层自改进结构，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 等策略编排与证据筛选的元层优化提供类比，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EnzymeRSI: A dual-loop recursive self-improvement system for enzyme design

**abstract/A1：** Improvement in enzyme engineering is usually pursued by optimising which candidates to assay within a fixed model and workflow; when the bottleneck arises from a mismatched method or a failing workflow, that route is difficult to escape. We present EnzymeRSI, a dual-loop system for recursive self-improvement (RSI). The outer loop evolves harness assets without changing model weights, the inner loop develops prediction and selection algorithms under controlled conditions, and a cloud laboratory system is specified to close the physical loop with quality-controlled feedback. By updating its prediction strategy through iterative comparison, the outer loop raised the mean development-set Spearman correlation on ProteinGym by 0.044 across five assays, reaching 0.335 on CD19 and 0.683 on OPSD (rhodopsin); of the two first places, only CD19 was produced by the outer loop itself. In the self-improvement of the ThermoMPNN algorithm the inner loop reached a PCC of 0.849, and on TadA-Bench it reached a Spearman correlation of 0.307 in 1.13 h. In three prospective wet-lab tests of the system's experimental decision-making, 10 of the 32 candidates that had not been pre-specified for the luciferase RLuc showed at least twice the wild-type activity; a recommended variant of the cytidine deaminase A3A raised 5mC conversion to 90.2%; and path selection in evolution raised the catalytic efficiency of the DNA polymerase KOD-E on a modified substrate to 31.1 times that of the parent. By treating the organisation of the research workflow and the development of algorithms as a single falsifiable dry-to-wet loop, this work offers an evolutionary paradigm for moving beyond the blind spots of a fixed method and for autonomous enzyme engineering.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 8 figures, 7 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-200"></a>

### 200. [Flexible Intensities Matter: A Comprehensive Re-evaluation of Hawkes Processes and Neural Temporal Point Processes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06348v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06348

**作者：** Hendrik Mehrtens、Florian Buettner、Oliver Stegle

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06348v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06348v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文系统比较不同灵活度的 Poisson、Hawkes 过程与神经 TPP，在五个基准数据集及两个大规模医疗事件序列上做自回归下一事件预测，发现 Hawkes 加上可学习基线与核后与神经 TPP 的差距基本消失，且灵活度带来的增益几乎全部来自时间密度而非类型项，并开源统一实现的 TorchPP。

**相关性简评：** 其把似然分解为「何时发生」与「发生什么」并把收益归因到时间项的做法，可能为 LVR 中把 STOP 决策与 latent 内容生成分开建模、分开评价提供类比线索；自激励强度结构也可供核查是否可作循环计算或动态触发的信号，迁移性需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Flexible Intensities Matter: A Comprehensive Re-evaluation of Hawkes Processes and Neural Temporal Point Processes

**abstract/A1：** Temporal point processes (TPPs) are the canonical framework for event sequence data, with applications spanning seismology, social media, and electronic health records. Recently, neural TPPs have been reported to substantially outperform classical TPPs, such as Hawkes processes, but these comparisons pitted flexible neural models against Hawkes processes in their most restricted form, and did not consider large-scale medical data. Here, we revisit this comparison through a systematic evaluation of Poisson and Hawkes processes of increasing flexibility against neural TPPs, on five established benchmark datasets and on two large-scale medical evaluation tasks derived from UK Biobank disease histories and MIMIC-IV ICU records, under autoregressive next-event prediction. First, the previously reported gap between Hawkes processes and neural TPPs largely disappears once Hawkes processes are equipped with flexible, learnable baselines and kernels. Second, the flexibility of the intensity parametrization also limits prior neural TPPs: a Transformer with a spline-based intensity head outperforms previous intensity-based neural TPPs and is on par with the intensity-free model. Decomposing the log-likelihood into a time term $\\log p(t)$ and a type term $\\log p(d \\mid t)$, we find that this flexibility advantage lies almost entirely in the time density: more flexibility does not improve the type term. On these benchmarks, the gains of neural TPPs thus lie mostly in modeling when events occur, not which. Third, our results underscore the value of complex datasets for evaluating TPPs: only on the MIMIC-IV ICU records do neural TPPs retain an advantage in the type term. Our results inform both future method development and model choice. We release our framework, TorchPP, as an open-source package with consistent implementations of classical and neural TPPs.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 36 pages, 3 figures, 25 tables. Code: https://github.com/PMBio/torchPP (paper reproduction on branch neurips2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-201"></a>

### 201. [MA-Mesh: Resilient Multi-Agent Collaboration via Role-based Self-Healing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06389v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06389

**作者：** JunYong Lee、Woo Kyung Kim、Honguk Woo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06389v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06389v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MA-Mesh，在现有多智能体协作模式上构建基于角色的自愈层，自动生成韧性感知的系统规格并合成为可执行的多智能体策略代码，配合跨智能体反思与角色故障转移，在软件开发、工具使用、科学推理等任务上评测性能与鲁棒性。

**相关性简评：** 其「同伴互评＋角色接管」的运行时失效恢复思路，可能为 LVR 中多 latent 假设的验证与失败回退、证据不足时的策略切换提供概念线索，但论文为纯文本多智能体编排，与视觉状态表示无直接联系，迁移价值需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MA-Mesh: Resilient Multi-Agent Collaboration via Role-based Self-Healing

**abstract/A1：** Effective collaboration in multi-agent systems requires mechanisms for coordination and adaptability in dynamic and uncertain environments. However, existing multi-agent systems rarely model resilience explicitly, instead relying on ad-hoc output correction that limits systematic, runtime management of robustness. In this work, we propose Multi-Agent Mesh (MA-Mesh), a framework that constructs a role-based self-healing layer on top of a collaboration pattern. Specifically, MA-Mesh generates resilience-aware system specifications and synthesizes them into executable multi-agent policy code. To enhance resilience, we introduce two self-healing mechanisms: Cross-agent reflection, a mechanism that allows agents to evaluate not only their own outcomes but also the performance of their peers, enabling informed behavioral adjustments and collective policy refinement; Role-based failover, a strategy which dynamically reassigns roles and enables agents to substitute for one another when sub-tasks fail or agents underperform, ensuring continuity of task execution. Experimental results across diverse tasks (e.g., software development, tool use, scientific reasoning) demonstrate that MA-Mesh significantly outperforms existing multi-agent systems in both task performance and robustness under system uncertainty.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at EMNLP 2026 (Main)

</details>

<a id="paper-202"></a>

### 202. [KolTa: A Physics-Informed Active Learning Framework for Formulation Discovery with Limited Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06394v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06394

**作者：** Boyong Tang、Tao Wang、Lei Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06394v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06394v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 KolTa 主动学习框架，用三阶段预测器结合软物理约束，并以融合预测效用、集成分歧与物理残差的 PRC-UCB 规则在固定查询预算下批量挑选配方，以找出目标温度下前 5% 的高性能配方。

**相关性简评：** 固定预算下的批式证据选择、分歧驱动探索与残差校准评分，可能为 LVR 中「何时继续 LOOK、何时 STOP」及视觉证据筛选与获取成本权衡提供可核查的借鉴；但该工作属材料配方发现，与多模态推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** KolTa: A Physics-Informed Active Learning Framework for Formulation Discovery with Limited Data

**abstract/A1：** Finding high-performing formulations is difficult when few have been measured and experimental budgets are limited. We propose KolTa, an active learning framework that selects batches to discover the top 5% of formulations at a target temperature under a fixed query budget. KolTa combines a three-stage predictor of component contributions, role-conditioned interactions, and anchored temperature response with task-specific soft physics constraints. Its Physics-Residual-Calibrated Upper Confidence Bound (PRC-UCB) acquisition rule combines predicted utility, ensemble disagreement, and a target-temperature physics residual, with nonnegative exploration weights fitted to out-of-fold signed errors. We evaluate the framework on three measured tasks and one simulated task covering binary-mixture viscosity, drug solubility, ionic-liquid viscosity, and electrolyte conductivity. KolTa ranks first or second in mean area under the learning curve (AULC) on every task and outperforms seven of the eight comparator strategies in paired tests, winning 75 of 78 paired comparisons across tasks and seeds against those seven strategies. Across the three measured tasks, its largest gap to the best mean AULC is 0.032, compared with at least 0.056 for every comparator. On Drug, reaching 80% average recovery of the remaining targets across drugs requires an estimated median of 348 additional formulation queries, about 18% fewer than the fastest comparator. Researchers can adapt the framework to diverse formulation problems through component roles and physical constraints.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, including 11 pages of supplementary material; 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-203"></a>

### 203. [Zero-Shot Coordination among LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06409v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06409

**作者：** Adrian Hayler、Shashank Reddy Chirra、Andrei Lupu、Johannes Forkel、Bidipta Sarkar、Siheng Feng、Jakob Foerster

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06409v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06409v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在刻意简化的最小环境中考察独立开发的前沿 LLM agent 的零样本协作，指出其失败主要源于对协作问题理解不足与对伙伴信念推理较弱，并提出可自动发现的「Coordination-friendly definitions」作为共享约定来提升协作鲁棒性。

**相关性简评：** 其「伙伴信念推理失败」与自动发现共享定义的做法，可能为 LVR 中把伙伴或环境信念编码进内部状态、以及用共享语义约定缓解 latent 通信歧义提供问题表述上的启发，但无视觉与 latent 状态证据，具体联系需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Zero-Shot Coordination among LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** We study zero-shot coordination (ZSC), where independently developed agents must coordinate at test time. While ZSC has been well studied in the RL literature, far less is known about the performance of LLM agents despite their increasing deployment in such settings. Existing work on LLM coordination often relies on specialised scaffolding that independently developed agents are unlikely to share in practice. Moreover, evaluations on complex environments (e.g., Hanabi) make it difficult to pinpoint the sources of coordination failure. In contrast, we focus on simple, general-purpose scaffolds in minimal environments designed to isolate specific coordination challenges. Our results show that even in these controlled settings, frontier LLM agents struggle to coordinate, largely due to limited understanding of the coordination problem and weak reasoning about their partner's beliefs. While LLMs introduce semantic information as an additional axis for coordination, they nonetheless fail to exploit this structure effectively. Towards this, we propose Coordination-friendly definitions (CFDs) as a principled approach for enabling robust coordination among LLM agents. Finally, we show that CFDs can be discovered automatically, removing the need for manual engineering. Code is available at https://github.com/ahayler/zsc-llm-agents.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 Poster. ICLR 26 MALGAI Workshop Oral. The first two authors contributed equally

</details>

<a id="paper-204"></a>

### 204. [Partially Interpretable Decision Trees](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06428v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06428

**作者：** Ludovic Schwartz、Federico Berton、Tim van Erven

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06428v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06428v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究带「推迟」选项的部分可解释决策树：提出三种算法，包括 Branches 的精确搜索扩展、在标准 CART 上后处理的转换算法，以及生长时就考虑推迟的广义 CART，并给出黑箱准确率在域内较均匀时精巧建树增益有限的理论界，另在准确率刻意不均匀的合成数据和概念瓶颈模型上评估。

**相关性简评：** 推迟/路由到黑箱的机制与 LVR 中「内部推理是否足够、何时调用外部视觉证据或更多计算」的停止决策同构；其关于黑箱收益空间不均匀性决定推迟价值的界，可能可供核查作为何时 LOOK 的判据线索，概念瓶颈部分也与视觉概念通路相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Partially Interpretable Decision Trees

**abstract/A1：** Partially interpretable models extend simple interpretable models with the option to defer complicated inputs to an accurate but uninterpretable black-box model. By deferring more inputs, the model gets more accurate; by deferring fewer, it gets more interpretable. Existing procedures to train such partially interpretable models build on linear models or decision rules. We propose three algorithms for decision trees: a partially interpretable extension of the existing Branches algorithm, which performs exact search to optimize the tree; a conversion algorithm that we run on top of standard CART, which is computationally more efficient; and a generalized CART whose splitting criterion takes into account the option to defer while growing the tree. Perhaps surprisingly, the performance of the three algorithms is similar across many realworld datasets. We can explain this through a formal bound on the potential gain of smart tree construction, provided the black box accuracy is sufficiently homogeneous across the domain. We also construct a synthetic dataset where the accuracy of the black box is deliberately inhomogeneous. There, our deferral-aware generalization of CART does outperform regular CART and matches the performance of exact search. Finally, we evaluate an application to concept bottleneck models. Partially interpretable decision trees can replace uninterpretable neural networks after the bottleneck. On undeferred inputs, the model is fully interpretable, and deferring preserves accuracy.

</details>

<a id="paper-205"></a>

### 205. [AmbiHTMLBench: A Benchmark for HTML-based Visually Rich Document parsing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06449v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06449

**作者：** Boyu Niu、Yingjie Xiong、Shurui Li、Linhan Pang、Yuntong Zhang、Han Wang、Hao Wu、Kai Zuo、Xu Tang、Xuanhe Zhou、Fan Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06449v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06449v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 AmbiHTMLBench：1,115 页真实文档要求模型重建可执行 HTML，用「渲染块对齐的客观指标」与「LLM 判断对齐人类偏好」两套协议评测十个多模态系统，并微调小模型验证任务适配。

**相关性简评：** 「用 HTML 替代 Markdown 以保留细粒度空间结构与版面几何」这一表示选择问题与 LVR 关心的视觉细节保真相通，其客观＋主观互补的评测协议可供核查用于诊断视觉证据保留；但全文聚焦输出格式与文档解析，不涉及推理期 latent 状态，联系较外围。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AmbiHTMLBench: A Benchmark for HTML-based Visually Rich Document parsing

**abstract/A1：** Traditional document parsing systems use Markdown as their output representation, which is effective for linear text but does not fully preserve fine-grained spatial structure, table geometry, visual hierarchy, or rendering semantics. We show that HTML is advantageous for heterogeneous and layout-sensitive documents whose representations must remain editable, inspectable, and reusable, and introduce AmbiHTMLBench to systematically study this setting. In this benchmark, given a real-world document, a model must recover executable HTML that preserves its content, organization, and visual appearance. The benchmark contains 1,115 diverse pages with structured annotations and evaluates predictions in two different ways: The objective protocol aligns ground-truth and predicted rendered blocks and measures table fidelity, page-level text error, and a two-dimensional layout consistency; a complementary reference-anchored protocol measures perceived fidelity by aligning LLM judges with human preferences. Across ten primary multimodal systems, the best objective score is 0.8092, while the subjective winner is different, demonstrating that automatic decomposition and human-perceived fidelity capture complementary properties. We further fine-tune a small-parameter expert model and find that task-specific adaptation yields good performance on document-to-HTML reconstruction task. These results position AmbiHTMLBench as a diagnostic testbed for building smarter and more logical document parsing systems.

**comment/C1：** Check latest benchmark info on https://ambihtmlbench.tech

</details>

<a id="paper-206"></a>

### 206. [CoDecide: Content-Adaptive Video Decoding for High-Throughput Video Analytics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06455v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06455

**作者：** Shristy Shah、Thomas B. Moeslund、Kamal Nasrollahi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06455v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06455v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CoDecide：用压缩码流信号驱动轻量学生模型判断哪些帧包含「语义上新」的内容，只解码这些帧及其区域，并把已解码帧的检测结果借助运动矢量传播到被跳过的帧。

**相关性简评：** 其「按需获取视觉证据」与跳过帧状态传播，可能启发 LVR 中 LOOK 决策与在有限解码预算下的证据筛选，以及未观测部分如何用传播状态补齐，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoDecide: Content-Adaptive Video Decoding for High-Throughput Video Analytics

**abstract/A1：** Analytics on video archives are bottlenecked by decoding. In a dense pipeline that runs a detector on every frame, decoding is a small fraction of the runtime, but once the detector is restricted to selected frames, decoding takes over as the main cost. We introduce CoDecide, a retrospective analytics system that only decodes the frames where new content appears, using signals from the compressed bitstream to find them. Motion tells us something is moving, but not whether it is new to the scene or has been there the whole time. To make that distinction, we supervise a lightweight compressed-domain student on a label-free novelty signal from a frozen DINOv2 teacher, which fires on semantically new regions rather than on any motion. Consequently, the student model generalizes to new camera deployments without requiring per-camera retraining. At inference, it decides which frames to decode and which regions to reconstruct. Detections from the decoded frames are propagated to the skipped frames using motion vectors. We evaluate CoDecide with a leave-one-dataset-out protocol on VIRAT, MEVA, and ShanghaiTech. Against a dense per-frame detector, CoDecide recovers more moving-object detections than prior compressed-domain selectors at an equivalent decoding budget, outperforming even a competitor trained directly on the target camera. CoDecide runs 15 to 24x faster than a pipeline that decodes and detects every frame. Code is available at https://github.com/shristy51/CoDecide.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at WACV 2027. 12 pages including supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-207"></a>

### 207. [A 3D foundation model unifying CT interpretation and segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06471v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06471

**作者：** Yulu Xie、Zilong Li、Shen Xu、Yifan Bai、Liuhong Shi、Jiao Chen、Xianglei Kong、Wenchao Zhu、Na Shen、Xianghua Ye、Yang Yan、Jiaju Zheng、Yunsong Peng、Yanzhou Su、Chaohui Yu、Hao Luo、Fan Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06471v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06471v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HoloCT 三维 CT 基础模型，将全卷上下文与细粒度解剖细节联合建模，用一个模型同时完成 CT 解读与体素级分割，并构建大规模多任务指令与掩码配对数据集，在多中心数据上验证效果。

**相关性简评：** 其「以 3D 掩码作为文本结论的空间证据」及语言引导分割与生成掩码互惠的现象，可能与 LVR 中视觉证据获取、答案是否依赖内部视觉内容的核查相关，但属医学影像远领域，迁移需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A 3D foundation model unifying CT interpretation and segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Radiologists interpret computed tomography (CT) scans by jointly determining what a finding represents and where it lies in three-dimensional (3D) anatomy. However, CT models have treated CT interpretation and 3D segmentation as separate tasks, leaving textual findings spatially unverifiable and overlooking potential synergy between clinical understanding and spatial perception. Here we present HoloCT, a generalist 3D foundation model unifying CT interpretation and voxel-level segmentation by jointly modelling whole-volume context and fine-grained anatomical detail, providing 3D masks as evidence. We curated HoloDATA, comprising 195,921 CT scans from 73 public datasets and six clinical cohorts, with 6.2 million instruction dialogues and 2.8 million CT-mask pairs. Across 346 tasks spanning segmentation, lesion screening, visual question answering, and report generation on 61 internal and 12 external datasets, HoloCT outperformed specialist and foundation models. In multicentre validation across 11,455 clinical CT scans from five hospitals, HoloCT outperformed competing models in lesion segmentation and report generation by 18.2 and 15.3 points. It also achieved 85.1% mean accuracy in screening thyroid tumours and colon lesions, exceeding all four participating radiologists in a retrospective reader study. Further analyses revealed bidirectional semantic-spatial synergy: language-guided segmentation delineated unseen anatomical structures and lesions as well as diffuse free-text findings, outperforming the leading approach by 22.1 points, whereas prompting the model to generate corresponding 3D masks alongside its textual outputs significantly improved interpretation performance. HoloCT shows that clinical understanding and spatial perception can be learned together within a single foundation model and offers a path towards more accurate and reliable CT analysis.

**comment/C1：** 114 pages, including Supplementary Information; 7 main figures and 2 Extended Data figures

</details>

<a id="paper-208"></a>

### 208. [Model Merging Does Not Require Uniform LoRA Ranks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06486v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06486

**作者：** Zirong Xie、Zainab Afolabi、Nikita Kozodoi、Jack Butler

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06486v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06486v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究不同 LoRA rank 的专家能否直接合并：在三个领域、四个 rank、三种模型规模上训练 36 个专家，用六种合并方法比较统一 rank 与各领域最优 rank，发现异构 rank 对平均与效用型选择无害甚至有益，并指出按奇异值大小筛选谱组件会系统性丢弃低幅度专家。

**相关性简评：** 其「按对 loss 的实际影响而非参数幅度选择/保留组件」的思路，可能为 latent 模块与多分支专家间的贡献分配、以及判断 latent 内容是否真正被使用提供类比线索，可核查其是否可迁移到推理期状态评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Model Merging Does Not Require Uniform LoRA Ranks

**abstract/A1：** Model merging combines separately fine-tuned experts into a single model without retraining. Standard practice trains every expert at the same LoRA rank, which couples the rank choice across experts even though each domain has its own best rank. We show this coupling is a property of the merge method, not of merging. We train 36 experts on three domains (mathematics, instruction following, multilingual) at four ranks and three model scales, then merge with six methods at uniform ranks and at each domain's own best rank. Giving experts different ranks costs averaging and utility-based selection nothing, and gains them up to 1.8 pp of multi-domain accuracy, while the conflict-resolution methods are inconsistent and can lose up to 4 pp. The split has a simple cause. Merging keeps only a fixed share of the pooled spectral components, and methods that choose them by size discard many that would have reduced loss. They also tend to discard the same expert: the instruction adapter has the smallest singular values, so it is dropped almost entirely and IFEval falls by 16 to 25 pp. Choosing components by their effect on loss instead keeps all three experts, and leaves accuracy nearly flat across an 8$\\times$ rank range (0.7 pp, against 6.7 pp for TIES at 4B scale). Rank can therefore be chosen per expert whenever the merge operator does not select by magnitude.

</details>

<a id="paper-209"></a>

### 209. [FPTR: Feasibility-Preserving Transactional Refinement for Deadline-Constrained Joint Beam and Resource Allocation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06491v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06491

**作者：** Minghao Kong、Zirong Liu、Jianhao Wu、Muyao Chen、Jianxin Wu、Rongjie Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.AR、cs.PF

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06491v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06491v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向短间隔无线调度，提出 FPTR：始终保留已验证可行的当前解，每个精炼阶段独立构造候选，只有完整、按时通过可行性校验且严格改进目标时才替换，否则丢弃；在合成实例上与六种搜索基线比较，并在 100 ms 截止下报告可行率与收益。

**相关性简评：** 其「保留可行现任解、按截止时间与校验结果决定是否提交新状态」的事务式精炼与随时中断机制，可能为 LVR 中动态计算分配、候选 latent 轨迹的选择性替换与停止决策提供概念线索，需核查其截止控制与改进判据能否对应推理期状态更新。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FPTR: Feasibility-Preserving Transactional Refinement for Deadline-Constrained Joint Beam and Resource Allocation

**abstract/A1：** Short-interval wireless schedulers must return a complete, feasible beam-and-resource allocation before each execution deadline, and a late or invalid output gives no utility in the current control cycle. Searching longer can increase transmitted traffic, but it may also miss the deadline or leave only an infeasible intermediate solution. We propose Feasibility-Preserving Transactional Refinement (FPTR), which always keeps a validated feasible allocation, the incumbent. Each refinement stage builds a separate candidate and leaves the incumbent unchanged. A candidate replaces the incumbent only if it is complete, passes the feasibility validator before its stage cutoff, and strictly improves the objective; otherwise it is discarded. Four refinement stages use this rule: buffer-aware resource selection, compatibility-group (CG) user sharing, beam-mask rebuilding from remaining demand, and joint reassignment of two resources. On 150 fixed synthetic instances, the full pipeline (Full) raises traffic by 3.83% on average over a lightweight beam-first reference and by 1.79% over its Base constructor. In a separate suite with matched inputs, Full exceeds six independent search baselines (ALNS, tabu search, GA, simulated annealing, iterated local search, and GRASP) by 6.68-8.35%, where outputs later than 100 ms receive zero utility. All 750 Full runs in the internal suite returned feasible allocations, and none exceeded 100 ms. Separating mandatory output construction from optional refinement lets spare computation time improve the allocation without endangering the incumbent. The open-source implementation supports independent reproduction.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 4 figures; includes appendix. Code: https://github.com/rudykon/FPTR\_Scheduler

</details>

<a id="paper-210"></a>

### 210. [ElecLoad: Adaptive Electricity Load Forecasting Guided by Multiple Events](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06508v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06508

**作者：** Huajian Zhang、Mingyue Cheng、Xingpeng Gao、Xiaoyu Tao、Zhiding Liu、Jiqian Yang、Zhi Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06508v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06508v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 ElecLoad，一个面向多事件影响的电力负荷预测智能体：先分析各事件与目标负荷的相关性、作用时机及事件间相互作用，据此决定整体直接预测还是按时段切分，并由子智能体独立初预测、主智能体审阅、子智能体按反馈选择性精修。

**相关性简评：** 其「先判断证据相关性再决定是否分段及计算分配、并由上层审阅触发选择性精修」的流程，可能为 LVR 中的动态计算分配、证据筛选与停止决策提供概念类比，但领域与模态相距较远，需核查其分段准则是否具可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ElecLoad: Adaptive Electricity Load Forecasting Guided by Multiple Events

**abstract/A1：** Accurate electricity load forecasting provides critical decision support for energy procurement, generation scheduling, and supply-demand balancing, contributing to the economic efficiency and operational reliability of power systems. However, future electricity demand can be affected by the complex interplay of multiple events described in Web notices, weather alerts, and event schedules. Their effects vary over time and can reinforce or offset one another, making electricity load difficult to forecast accurately. While existing methods can incorporate context as additional input, how to use the effects of multiple events and their interactions to guide the load forecasting process itself remains to be explored. To address this gap, we propose ElecLoad, a context-aware agent for multi-event electricity load forecasting that adaptively organizes the forecasting process. Specifically, we train ElecLoad to combine historical load with context to analyze each event's relevance to the target load, the timing of its effects, and potential interactions among events. Guided by this analysis, ElecLoad adaptively chooses to forecast the entire forecast horizon directly or to partition it into segments according to changes in event effects. When the horizon is partitioned, sub-agents independently generate initial forecasts for each segment. The main agent then reviews these forecasts, and the sub-agents perform selective refinement guided by its feedback. Experiments on multiple electricity load datasets show that ElecLoad achieves state-of-the-art performance in most evaluation scenarios. Code is available at https://github.com/zhjai/ElecLoad.

</details>

<a id="paper-211"></a>

### 211. [EmoReward: Self-Evolving Reward Calibration for Emotional Video Generation RL](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06518v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06518

**作者：** Mingzhe Zheng、Yue Wu、Jing Tan、Chang Zou、Dengyang Jiang、Zeyue Tian、Xuanhua He、Ryan Xu、Yunyang Ge、Sihui Ji、Yunlong Lin、Kaihang Pan、Hongru Yan、Sixiang Chen、Dianyi Wang、Julian Zhang、Weijie Kong、Changlin Li、Lanqing Guo、Zhao Zhong、Qifeng Chen、Harry Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06518v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06518v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 EmoReward，一种自演化奖励校准机制：从实际训练 rollout 的奖励轨迹中估计各类情感证据信号的可靠性，并用实例级门控分配与重归一化多奖励权重，用于情感视频生成的 RL 后训练。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线只是间接关联：按置信度、冲突等对异构信号做门控，并据历史可靠性重标定各信号贡献的做法，可能可供核查多源视觉证据的筛选与贡献分配，以及如何抑制奖励劫持。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EmoReward: Self-Evolving Reward Calibration for Emotional Video Generation RL

**abstract/A1：** Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has advanced generative model post-training in verifiable domains such as code and mathematics, but evaluating video quality, especially emotional expression, often depends on subjective aesthetics and has no uniquely correct target. Existing approaches typically rely on fixed rewards for visual quality, motion quality, and text alignment that poorly capture emotional intent and can invite reward hacking, while RLHF is difficult to scale and subject to individual aesthetic differences that complicate the calibration of facial, body motion, and chromatic cues across narrative contexts. We introduce EmoReward, a new self-evolving training mechanism that distills evidence reliability from reward traces collected during an actual training run to adaptively guide multi-reward weight allocation in subsequent training by determining which emotional evidence to trust and how much to weight it. EmoReward first plans specific grounding cues from the emotional prompt to guide evaluation, then uses heterogeneous signal primitives to score the generated video during the act stage. Reflective Reward Gating calibrates their contributions through instance-level gates for phase, face confidence, conflict, and subtlety, then renormalizes weights over the active set to produce the sample's fused reward. RewardMemory then estimates each primitive's reliability from rollout scores and outcomes within each emotional regime in the finished training run, then fixes bounded multipliers that calibrate fusion weights in the next training run without updating reward models. Experiments on HunyuanVideo 1.5 show improvements across multiple RL algorithms and compare EmoReward with other agentic reward methods under SAGE-GRPO. Ablations and transfer experiments on SD 3.5 and FLUX further support adaptive reward calibration for visual generation.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, 15 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-212"></a>

### 212. [IDSISA: Influence-Distilled Sharded Training for Efficient Machine Unlearning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06519v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06519

**作者：** Deeraj SK、Sadhana Devarajan、Tanmoy Hazra、Anupam Shukla

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06519v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06519v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 IDSISA：用影响函数把易遗忘样本集中到少数分片以减少重训范围，并从未受影响分片做蒸馏以弥补隔离带来的效用损失，同时形式化重复删除请求下的级联重训认证。

**相关性简评：** 其用影响函数量化单个样本贡献并配合分片隔离与蒸馏回收效用的做法，或可供核查 latent 推理中轨迹级贡献分配与监督信号的思路；论文本身与视觉多模态推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** IDSISA: Influence-Distilled Sharded Training for Efficient Machine Unlearning

**abstract/A1：** Machine unlearning removes the influence of specific training examples to comply with privacy regulations. Full retraining gives strong guarantees but is computationally prohibitive at scale; approximate methods are efficient but lack formal guarantees. SISA (Sharded, Isolated, Sliced, and Aggregated training) achieves certified deletion through data sharding and independent retraining, but random partitioning scatters forget requests across most shards, and isolated retraining degrades utility. We introduce IDSISA (Influence-Distilled SISA), which uses influence functions to concentrate forget-prone samples into few shards and distillation from unaffected shards to recover utility lost to isolation. The deletion guarantee inherited from SISA's retraining step remains exact and requires no probabilistic qualification; the distillation-based utility recovery is a separately quantified, empirically bounded approximation ($ε\_{\\text{cert}} \\leq 0.015$ in expectation). We further formalize certification under sequences of repeated deletion requests, where cross-shard distillation can create dependencies between shards; we prove that a closure-based cascading retraining procedure restores the deletion guarantee to the full aggregated model under any deletion sequence. Experiments on ResNet-18/CIFAR-10, ResNet-18/CIFAR-100, BiLSTM/IMDB, TCN/IMDB, and a fully connected network on Fashion-MNIST show $14-91\\%$ better selective forgetting, up to $50\\%$ better utility retention in relative terms (with consistent percentage-point gains on the other architectures, where a relative baseline is undefined), and unlearning-time changes ranging from $-6.7\\%$ to $+6.4\\%$ depending on architecture, relative to SISA.

**comment/C1：** This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication. Copyright may be transferred without notice, after which this version may no longer be accessible

</details>

<a id="paper-213"></a>

### 213. [LungMend: A Healthy-Only Latent Diffusion Shape Prior for Lung Mask Correction in Severe Tuberculosis CT](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06556v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06556

**作者：** Rezkellah Noureddine Khiat、Pierre-Yves Brillet、Catalin Fetita

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06556v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06556v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LungMend：在健康肺解剖上训练 latent diffusion 先验，以带合成损坏的肺掩码和骨骼解剖为条件，作为后处理修复严重结核 CT 中被分割模型漏掉的空洞与实变区域。

**相关性简评：** 「用生成先验补回被漏掉的视觉证据、并以人工损坏样本训练恢复」的思路，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节丢失后的 latent 状态补全与监督方式提供可核查线索，但该工作本身是医学分割后处理，与多模态推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LungMend: A Healthy-Only Latent Diffusion Shape Prior for Lung Mask Correction in Severe Tuberculosis CT

**abstract/A1：** Lung segmentation models such as R231 are widely used, but they fail on severe tuberculosis (TB) CT: cavities and consolidations look like non-lung tissue and are left out of the mask. We present LungMend, a post-processing model that corrects such masks using only healthy anatomy. A latent diffusion model, trained on healthy lungs with synthetically corrupted masks, learns to recover the complete lung from the faulty mask and the bone anatomy, without any pathological data or CT intensities. LungMend recovers cavities and consolidations that R231 misses and improves accuracy on expert-annotated slices. Code: https://github.com/noureddinekhiati/LungMend

**comment/C1：** 5 pages (4 + 1 references), 2 figures, 2 tables. Submitted to IEEE ISBI 2027. Code: https://github.com/noureddinekhiati/LungMend

</details>

<a id="paper-214"></a>

### 214. [From Fixed Defaults to Calibrated Activation Policies for Long-Term Time-Series Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06559v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06559

**作者：** Shijie Xu、Boyan Tang、Jianghua Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06559v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06559v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究面向长期时间序列预测，提出一种校准式激活选择策略：从既往实验中学习可靠默认配置，按任务与预测跨度调整激活函数及其位置，并在证据不足时保留默认；在 1225 次 Informer 训练上平均降低 MSE 7.86%。

**相关性简评：** 主题属预测任务配置选择，与 LVR 无直接关联，但其「以证据强度决定是否偏离默认配置」的校准策略，可能为 latent 推理中何时追加计算、何时停止或切换策略的门控设计提供概念参照，需核查其判据形式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Fixed Defaults to Calibrated Activation Policies for Long-Term Time-Series Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Long-term time-series forecasting supports decisions in energy, transportation, and other applications where reliable predictions are needed beyond the observed data. Although forecasting research has advanced model architectures, fixed activation configurations provide limited guidance on how activation choices should vary across datasets and prediction horizons. This study aims to establish when task-specific activation choices improve forecasting accuracy and how previous experiments can guide the selection of suitable configurations for new tasks. We develop a calibrated selection policy that learns a reliable default from prior experiments, adapts the activation function and its location to each task, and retains the default when evidence for a change is insufficient. In an evaluation supported by 1,225 Informer training runs across 17 dataset variants and 51 forecasting tasks, the policy reduces mean squared error by 7.86% on average relative to the conventional fixed-activation baseline and improves 72.55% of tasks. These findings establish activation choice and placement as practical opportunities for improving existing forecasting models and show how accumulated experimental evidence can guide configuration decisions without repeating an exhaustive search for every task.

**comment/C1：** 36 pages, 2 figures. Shijie Xu and Boyan Tang contributed equally. Code: https://github.com/Tt200411/From-Fixed-Defaults-to-Calibrated-Activation-Policies-for-Long-Term-Time-Series-Forecasting

</details>

<a id="paper-215"></a>

### 215. [One Scale, Four Texts: Gaps, Overlaps and Silent Repairs in Published Grading Schemes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06569v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06569

**作者：** Elisa Rovai、Fabio Rovai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06569v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06569v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文把四份糖尿病视网膜病变分级文本转写为统一规则语言，用穷举与 SMT 检验文本缺口、类别重叠及版本间无法映射之处，并在 927 张标注图像和两项预注册实验中比较不同文本标签下的分类器行为，以及语言模型有无审议时的「静默修补」。

**相关性简评：** 其「规范缺口」与「静默修补」概念可能启发 LVR 评价：可供核查生成的状态是在补全输入未指定的信息、还是依赖视觉证据，以及 deliberation 能否减少这种无证据填充。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** One Scale, Four Texts: Gaps, Overlaps and Silent Repairs in Published Grading Schemes

**abstract/A1：** Category systems often reach their users as text, and readers apply them with tacit repairs. We ask what such a text determines once the repairs are removed, using diabetic retinopathy grading, where one international scale circulates in several documents. We transcribe four texts (the English screening definitions of 2025, the international scale as tabulated in 2017 and as published in 2003, and an Italian guideline of 2015) into one rule language over 24 finding variables, and make every point a text leaves open a named precisification. Exhaustive enumeration of 1,425,807,360 finding profiles, re-derived by an SMT solver and spot-checked by an OWL reasoner, shows that every text that assigns actions leaves some eyes without a category under every reading; that the 2017 tabulation has a proliferative class that is empty when read literally and a macular gap that the 2003 text lacks, so that no function maps the 2003 grades onto the 2017 grades; and that on image findings alone the English and Italian texts order some pairs of eyes in opposite ways. On 927 annotated photographs from three public datasets, with retinal thickening assumed absent, the English text and the 2017 tabulation disagree on referral for 26.8% of the eyes of one dataset (DDR) on which both decide, and in a pre-registered experiment classifiers trained on each text's labels side with their own text. Thickening cannot be seen on a colour photograph; left free, every disagreement involving England becomes undecidable, so it rests on what the camera cannot show. In a second pre-registered study, language models that answered without deliberating repaired most gap and overlap items even when told to read literally, while Claude Sonnet 5.5 and Claude Opus 5.5 reported the defects far more often; within two models, deliberation reduced silent repair without removing it.

</details>
