### 216. [BudgetSplat: Budget-Controllable Generalizable Gaussian Splatting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06593v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06593

**作者：** Yuxi Hu、Jun Zhang、Wei Mao、Yuchen Rao、Friedrich Fraundorfer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06593v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06593v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BudgetSplat，一种可用用户指定保留比例控制输出规模的 feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting 框架，通过重要性估计、空间覆盖与跨视角冗余的联合建模，以及 budget 条件调制，使单一模型支持多个测试期预算而无需重训练。

**相关性简评：** 论文本身属三维重建，但其“按预算压缩表示、重要性×覆盖度×冗余联合选择、预算条件调制”的思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态或视觉证据的预算约束与筛选提供问题表述层面的借鉴，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BudgetSplat: Budget-Controllable Generalizable Gaussian Splatting

**abstract/A1：** Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting enables efficient generalizable novel view synthesis, but existing pixel-aligned methods often generate highly redundant Gaussian primitives, causing the final representation size and rendering cost to grow rapidly with image resolution and the number of input views. We propose BudgetSplat, a budget-controllable feed-forward framework that adapts the final Gaussian representation to a user-specified preservation ratio. Rather than treating compaction as simple importance ranking, we formulate it as a joint problem of importance estimation, spatial coverage, and cross-view redundancy. A single trained model supports varying numbers of context views and multiple test-time budgets without per-budget retraining or per-scene compression. To realize this formulation, Intra-Cross Mask Supervision provides structure-, coverage-, and redundancy-aware guidance for learning Gaussian importance, while budget-conditioned feature modulation adapts Gaussian opacity and attributes to the requested preservation ratio. Coverage-Aware Budget Selection further combines patch-wise pre-selection, view-wise opacity ranking, and 3D redundancy suppression to construct compact yet spatially distributed Gaussian representations. Experiments on indoor reconstruction and cross-dataset generalization demonstrate consistent quality--size trade-offs across different input view counts and preservation ratios, substantially reducing final representation storage and rendering cost while preserving rendering quality.

</details>

### 217. [A trust layer for fast pedestal-stability surrogates: calibrated error bars, out-of-distribution alarms and few-shot recalibration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06597v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06597

**作者：** Amin Vakhshouri

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、physics.plasm-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06597v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06597v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向聚变剥离-气球模稳定性代理模型，提出与模型无关的「信任层」：共形预测误差棒、把可疑查询判为分布外并路由回物理代码的警报，以及用少量新设计模拟做少样本再校准，并以预注册假设检验其覆盖率与筛选用时。

**相关性简评：** 「校准误差棒＋越界报警＋转交慢速高保真求解器」这套费用与风险门控，可能可供核查 LVR 中何时停止 latent 推理、何时改去取视觉证据；与该文的聚变场景本身无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A trust layer for fast pedestal-stability surrogates: calibrated error bars, out-of-distribution alarms and few-shot recalibration

**abstract/A1：** Machine-learning surrogates of linear peeling-ballooning stability now run in milliseconds, but they are mostly deployed as point estimators: they do not say how wrong they may be, or when a query lies outside what they were trained on. For a fusion company designing a new device, every equilibrium of interest is outside the training data of existing surrogates, so this is the case that matters. We describe a model-agnostic trust layer for stability surrogates, made of conformal error bars, out-of-distribution alarms that route doubtful cases to the physics code, and recalibration from a few simulations of a new design, and we test it with pre-registered hypotheses. On a large public dataset of JET-like equilibria with ideal-MHD growth rates, the error bars hold their nominal coverage in distribution and fall short under a shift in plasma shape; a few dozen simulations of the new shape, or routing the cases the alarms flag, restore it. On resistive BOUT++ surrogates, the bars stay calibrated on held-out runs, the alarm flags tokamaks absent from training, and a scenario scan is screened in seconds instead of hours. Across equilibria from the SPARC library and the DIII-D coil set, a surrogate trained on our own graded BOUT++ labels beats the baseline on unseen equilibria, while a surrogate trained elsewhere ranks the same cases backwards and its alarm declines them. Building our own neural-operator surrogate, the harness exposed two input defects whose fix closed its gap to a simple network. For every surrogate tested, the trust layer keeps coverage near target, with a bar width set by that surrogate's own error. Not every pre-registered hypothesis held, and we report those results and the limits of all of them.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 6 figures

</details>

### 218. [Interactive World Simulation with Cosmos for Physical AI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06627v1>)

Wang、Hao Wang、Shijie Wang、Zhonghao Wang、David Wehr、Shumian Xin、Mengyao Xu、Yao Xu、Shuai Yang、Sophia Zalewski、Pengcuo Zeren、Cindy Zha、Jing Zhang、Yuliya Zhautouskaya、Kaiwen Zheng、Andrew Zhu、Rick Zhuang

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一个共享框架，将 Cosmos 世界基础模型改造为面向 Physical AI 的实时交互式世界模拟器，可依据动作与场景控制流式输出视觉观测，也支持从外部模拟器维护的物理状态生成观测，并在相机导航、机器人操作与多视角自动驾驶仿真中演示。

**相关性简评：** 其「外部模拟器维护的物理状态」与「视觉观测生成」相分离、并按动作流式预测视觉后果的组织方式，可能与 LVR 中内部状态表示和观测/证据生成的分工形成类比，可供核查；但属较远领域，未涉及 latent 推理机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interactive World Simulation with Cosmos for Physical AI

**abstract/A1：** We present a shared framework that adapts Cosmos world foundation models for real-time interactive world simulation in Physical AI. By streaming visual observations in response to actions and scene controls, the framework enables users and embodied agents to interact with evolving virtual environments. It supports both predicting the visual consequences of actions and generating observations from physical state maintained by an external simulator. We demonstrate this approach across camera navigation and robot manipulation, controllable video transfer, and multi-view autonomous vehicle (AV) simulation. These examples provide a shared foundation for developing interactive virtual environments for Physical AI training and evaluation. Our code and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/nvidia/cosmos and https://huggingface.co/collections/nvidia/cosmos3-examples. The project website is available at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/cosmos-lab/cosmos-simulation.

**comment/C1：** Project website: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/cosmos-lab/cosmos-simulation

### 219. [From Certified Pairs to a Certified Hub: Reasoner-Checked Crosswalks for the Information Exchange Standard](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06628v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06628

**作者：** Fabio Rovai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.DB、cs.LO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06628v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06628v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为数字孪生生态的本体映射认证给出形式化判据（合并本体的连贯性与保守性），将 IES 到 13 个本体的 crosswalk 经匹配、人工审查、SKOS 对应认证与 HermiT 推理逐对及成 hub 检验，发现成对认证不可组合。

**相关性简评：** 「成对认证不能组合、认证单元应是 hub」与多步推理轨迹局部正确却整体不一致的评价难题可能同构，其逻辑一致性判据可供核查 latent 推理链验证与组合式监督的借鉴线索；但领域距离较远，仅为概念启发。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Certified Pairs to a Certified Hub: Reasoner-Checked Crosswalks for the Information Exchange Standard

**abstract/A1：** A national digital twin was conceived as an ecosystem of twins connected through mappings to a common foundation, but the roadmap that promises to confirm those mappings are valid never says what valid means. We give the test a precise form, coherence and conservativity of the merged ontology, borrowed from logic-based alignment repair, and apply it to the Information Exchange Standard (IES), first pair by pair and then to whole hubs. Crosswalks from IES into thirteen ontologies across seven sector groups, anchored on the building ontologies BOT, Brick, and SAREF4BLDG, pass through separate stages of matching, human review, certification of each SKOS correspondence against the two class hierarchies, and reasoning with HermiT. Pair by pair, the stages catch different failures: certification alone removed all 526 conservativity violations of the raw crosswalks, review alone left 73, and together they made eleven of twelve overlapping pairs coherent and conservative. Pairwise certification does not compose. Merging the three certified building crosswalks into one hub made five Brick classes unsatisfiable, through a homonym between the IES and Brick notions of a floor that only BOT's disjointness axioms expose; rejecting that one row gave a coherent and conservative hub that entails 290 cross-ontology links nobody wrote down. The core hub is inconsistent before any crosswalk is added, because two published standards, the Open Energy Ontology and BFO 2020, contradict each other; with either one left out it is coherent and conservative. A hub, not a pair, is the unit an ecosystem of twins has to certify.

</details>

### 220. [Self-Directed Apple Tasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06677v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06677

**作者：** Tanmay Devale、Reed Phillips、Arvind Ramaswami

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06677v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06677v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究在线学习中的「apple tasting」部分反馈模型：仅在预测为正时才能看到真实标签；论文分析学习者可自适应选择预测顺序的 self-directed 设定，给出可实现情形下错误数的四分类速率刻画，以及 agnostic 情形的 regret 界。

**相关性简评：** 其中「仅在主动查询时才获得标签、且可自行决定查询顺序」的设定，可能与 LVR 中 LOOK 的证据获取时机与停止决策的理论成本刻画有概念联系，可供核查其自定序与速率结果能否类比推理时的选择性观测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Self-Directed Apple Tasting

**abstract/A1：** We study the classical apple-tasting setting, a partial feedback model in which the learner observes the true label only when it predicts $1$. A combinatorial characterization for the rates of this problem in the online adversarial setting has been studied by Raman et al. (2024). We study the self-directed setting, in which the learner can adaptively choose the order of the points it predicts on. In the realizable setting, we establish a tetrachotomy on the expected minimax number of mistakes made by any learner. Depending on the VC dimension, the $\\emptyset$-centered star number, and the self-directed dimension, we show that the rates could possibly be $Θ(1)$, $O(\\log n) \\cap ω(1)$, $\\tilde Θ(\\sqrt{n})$, or $Θ(n)$. In the agnostic setting, the minimax regret is $\\tilde Θ(\\sqrt{n})$ or $Θ(n)$, depending on the VC dimension.

</details>

<a id="paper-221"></a>

### 221. [Do unlearning algorithms learn the right features?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06681v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06681

**作者：** Polina Dolgova、Amartya Sanyal、Sebastian U. Stich

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06681v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06681v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究机器遗忘的评估方式：指出仅比较输出快照会掩盖特征差异，证明输出一致但特征不同的模型一次梯度更新即会分歧，并提出区分「缺失特征」与「冗余特征」的特征恢复度量，在八种遗忘方法上显示标准指标良好者常未真正恢复重训练特征。

**相关性简评：** 其特征恢复度量把「缺失」与「冗余」成分分开打分，与诊断 latent 推理状态是否保留任务必要视觉信息、抑或夹带无关内容的问题在评价形式上有潜在呼应，可供核查其指标构造能否移植到 LVR 状态评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do unlearning algorithms learn the right features?

**abstract/A1：** Machine unlearning is commonly evaluated with standard post-deletion metrics that compare an unlearned model to one retrained on the remaining data. Such snapshot evaluations can conceal consequential differences in the features the two models encode. We prove that if an unlearned and a retrained model agree on their outputs but encode different features, a single gradient step on their prediction heads can break this agreement. We further show that deleting an arbitrarily small fraction of the data can cause retraining to select qualitatively different features, so matching retraining can require learning features that the full model never had. We then introduce a measure of feature recovery that separates missing from extraneous features. Across eight unlearning methods, models that perform well on standard metrics often fail to recover the retrained features. This mismatch predicts deviations from retraining under subsequent relearning and downstream adaptation, and directly correcting it systematically reduces these deviations.

</details>

### 222. [SecureGARD: Dynamic Ontology-Guided Governance for Secure Tool-Using LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06682v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06682

**作者：** Guoqin Xiong、Shengtao Wen、Xiang Chen、Xinyi Zeng、Zhaoxin Fan、Wei Ye、Xiaofeng Cao、Sheng-Jun Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06682v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06682v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 SecureGARD，用可执行本体结合 episode 级任务状态，把工具调用归入统一语义表示并给出 ready／waiting／forbidden 的确定性决策，本体则从经过验证的执行轨迹离线演化，在 AgentDojo 等基准的间接提示注入设置下评测安全性。

**相关性简评：** 其显式任务状态、未满足义务与「继续／等待／禁止」判定，可能与 LVR 中基于证据充分性和状态依赖的停止决策及动作门控形成类比线索，但无视觉或多模态推理内容，需核查这种状态判定的实际形式是否可借用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SecureGARD: Dynamic Ontology-Guided Governance for Secure Tool-Using LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents translate model outputs into consequential actions, turning indirect prompt injection into an execution-security problem. Existing defenses constrain tool calls, trajectories, or information flows, but often leave action semantics, state dependencies, and evidence relations implicit, limiting generalization across heterogeneous tools and complicating safe updates. We present \\ours{}, a dynamic ontology-guided governance framework. \\ours{} grounds heterogeneous calls in a shared semantic representation and combines an executable ontology with episode-level task state to derive deterministic ready, waiting, and forbidden decisions. A waiting decision identifies an unmet but recoverable obligation, enabling bounded replanning without expanding the agent's authorization boundary. \\ours{} evolves its ontology offline from verified training executions. An offline LLM proposes instance-independent action--state axioms, which are accepted only if they address ontology errors exposed by training failures, preserve logical consistency and protected entailments, and pass held-out validation before the ontology is frozen. We evaluate \\ours{} on AgentDojo, ASB, and AgentDyn under benign and indirect prompt-injection settings. \\ours{} achieves a 0\\% attack success rate (ASR) across all three benchmarks while improving task utility under attack compared with existing baselines. These results show that explicit semantic governance can strengthen execution security without indiscriminate blocking.

</details>

<a id="paper-223"></a>

### 223. [PINR-ESI: Physics-Informed Self-Supervised Implicit Neural Representation Framework for EEG Extended-Source Imaging](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06700v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06700

**作者：** Zhiyong Zhou、Sheng Liao、Youshen Xiao、Xiaolin Li、Yakang Dai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.NC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06700v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06700v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出基于物理信息的自监督隐式神经表示（INR）框架用于 EEG 扩展源成像，不依赖配对源标签或仿真预训练，以 lead field 约束 INR，将皮层电流密度建模为连续坐标条件场，并用经典逆解做热启动与空间约束。

**相关性简评：** 连续坐标条件的 INR 作为承载空间细节与范围的隐式状态，以及用前向测量一致性替代标签的自监督约束，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据的空间表示与 latent 状态一致性监督提供概念线索，尚需核查其与 VLM 内部表示的关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PINR-ESI: Physics-Informed Self-Supervised Implicit Neural Representation Framework for EEG Extended-Source Imaging

**abstract/A1：** EEG extended-source imaging aims to localize active cortical regions and characterize their spatial extent, yet supervised inverse networks typically depend on synthetic EEG-source pairs and may be sensitive to synthetic-to-real mismatch. We propose a physics-informed self-supervised implicit neural representation (INR) framework for spatial source reconstruction without paired source labels or simulation-based supervised pretraining. The lead field matrix encodes the source-to-sensor volume-conduction model and constrains the INR through agreement between predicted and measured EEG. Cortical current density is modeled as a continuous coordinate-conditioned field. A classical inverse estimate provides warm initialization and spatial constraints on source activity, while patch-integrated projection maps the field to discrete source coefficients for forward modeling. Simulations with varying noise levels, source counts, and source extents showed the clearest advantage in identifying active cortical regions, particularly for multiple and larger sources. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations using real EEG recordings from epilepsy patients and intracranial stimulation experiments further supported the feasibility of the framework for source imaging. These findings suggest the potential of physics-informed self-supervised INR for extended-source imaging.

**comment/C1：** Preprint, 10 pages, 11 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-224"></a>

### 224. [Coupled Scene Graph Anticipation: When With-Constraint Recall Hides Structural Inconsistency](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06738v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06738

**作者：** Zeno Nongmaithem、Anushttha Srivastava、Anshit Sinha、Apoorv Sisodia、Suman Chowdhury、Prerana Mukherjee、Rajesh P. Barnwal

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06738v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06738v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究未来视频帧的场景图预测：用关系交互图在 Neural ODE 向量场中耦合共享实体的关系，并加入基于互斥谓词矩阵的结构一致性损失，同时论证 With-Constraint 评测无法捕捉组内矛盾。

**相关性简评：** 其对约束式指标掩盖结构不一致、一致性损失梯度取决于模型分数而非真值的分析，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据一致性的评价设计与监督贡献分配提供参照线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Coupled Scene Graph Anticipation: When With-Constraint Recall Hides Structural Inconsistency

**abstract/A1：** Scene Graph Anticipation (SGA) predicts object relationships in future video frames. Continuous-time SGA models evolve each subject-object pair with an independent Neural ODE, so relationships sharing an entity cannot influence one another during integration, and nothing prevents mutually exclusive predictions, such as "sitting on" and "standing on", for the same pair. We introduce CoupledSGA, which links such relationships through a Relationship Interaction Graph with graph attention inside the ODE vector field, and adds a Structural Consistency Loss ($\\mathcal{L}\_{SC}$) based on a matrix of mutually exclusive predicates. Developing CoupledSGA revealed a limitation of the evaluation protocol. With-Constraint evaluation keeps one predicate per group for each subject-object pair, so its R@K and mR@K cannot capture the within-group violations targeted by $\\mathcal{L}\_{SC}$: we prove they are unchanged whenever each group's winning predicate and its score are held fixed. We further show that the gradient of $\\mathcal{L}\_{SC}$ depends on predicate scores rather than ground-truth labels: for two-predicate rules it amplifies the ordering the model already holds, and when all scores in a group are equal it suppresses predicates in more exclusion rules more strongly, explaining why the penalty can lower recall while improving consistency. On Action Genome, activating $\\mathcal{L}\_{SC}$ after two warm-up epochs reduces R@20 from 42.21% to 35.18% while lowering the No-Constraint contradiction rate from 45.92% to 4.07% (matched-budget R@20: 40.48% CoupledSGA vs. 44.78% SceneSayerODE). CoupledSGA is a testbed, not a state-of-the-art claim: With-Constraint recall captures the cost of enforcing structural consistency but not the improvement. We recommend reporting No-Constraint results alongside a contradiction rate for methods that constrain predicate co-activation.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at ICVGIP 2026 (Indian Conference on Computer Vision, Graphics, and Image Processing), December 21-24, 2026, Kolkata, India. 9 pages, 2 figures, 4 tables. Code: https://github.com/zenoNong/CoupledSGA

</details>

<a id="paper-225"></a>

### 225. [EvoWorld: Towards Out-of-Sight World Evolution in Generative Video Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06748v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06748

**作者：** Yequan Bie、Dengyang Jiang、Zhixuan Chen、Xiaozhuan Liang、Haoxuan Che、Quande Liu、Xi Chen、Hao Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06748v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06748v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「视野外因果演化」任务，要求视频模型渲染事件后推断其对世界实体的后果，并给出 EvoWorld 基线：由 VLM 充当「世界大脑」观察已生成片段、维护世界状态并推理状态转移，段自回归渲染器负责实现，另配套数据与评测基准。

**相关性简评：** 其把「维护并更新显式世界状态」与「像素渲染」分离的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的持续维护与条件化生成提供问题表述参照；事件—后果—再现的评测设计也可供核查状态更新与因果一致性评价时参考。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EvoWorld: Towards Out-of-Sight World Evolution in Generative Video Models

**abstract/A1：** Generative video models are increasingly used as visual world simulators, yet their primary strength lies in rendering plausible pixels rather than reasoning about how a world should evolve. Coherent world evolution requires more than visual fidelity: when an event occurs, a model must infer its consequences for the entities in the world and then render the resulting state. For example, after a wild beast appears, the model should not only depict the wild beast, but also infer whether a nearby person should flee, take cover, or fight back based on the realized scene. Existing diffusion-based video models lack an explicit mechanism for maintaining world state and reasoning over such event-conditioned consequences, often producing visually plausible but behaviorally static or causally inconsistent outputs. To make this reasoning gap concrete and measurable, we propose a new problem, Out-of-Sight Causal Evolution, which requires a model to render an event, reason about its consequences for the evolving world state, and reveal a causally plausible outcome as the world continues to evolve, even out of sight. As a baseline reference solution, we introduce EvoWorld, a brain-renderer collaboration framework in which a vision-language model serves as the world brain: it observes generated segments, maintains an evolving account of the world, and reasons about plausible state transitions, while a segment-autoregressive video renderer realizes them visually. We further introduce EvoWorld-Data and EvoWorld-Bench for training and evaluating event realization, causal reactions, and revisit consistency. Experiments show that EvoWorld substantially improves event-conditioned causal evolution over recent models while preserving visual quality and consistency, establishing a reference point for reasoning-guided, autonomous world evolution beyond the current field of view.

</details>

<a id="paper-226"></a>

### 226. [Continual Classification Beyond the Kernel Regime: Harnessing the Sparsity Bias of L2 Regularization in Diagonal Linear Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06759v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06759

**作者：** Gilad Karpel、Itay Evron、Ron Meir、Daniel Soudry

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06759v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06759v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在高维统计设定下研究线性预测器的持续学习逻辑回归，先推导持续线性分类的最优 L2 正则强度并刻画泛化表现，再以两层对角线性网络分析深层模型中 L2 的稀疏偏置在持续学习下失效的问题，提出加权 L2 变体以恢复该偏置，并从理论与实验说明其在稀疏 teacher 下收敛更快。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接联系，但「以先前任务权重为中心的加权 L2」这一保持稀疏偏置的持续训练思路，可能为 LVR 模型在多任务/持续训练中兼顾新任务适应与旧推理能力保留、避免状态表征坍缩提供可核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Continual Classification Beyond the Kernel Regime: Harnessing the Sparsity Bias of L2 Regularization in Diagonal Linear Networks

**abstract/A1：** We study logistic regression for continual learning with linear predictors in a high-dimensional statistical setting. Existing theoretical work has largely focused on continual linear regression with variants of L2 regularization, motivated by their analytical simplicity and connection to the kernel regime of neural networks. We first derive the optimal L2 regularization strength for continual linear classification and characterize its generalization performance, recovering behavior analogous to prior results for continual linear regression. Next, we examine the effect of L2 regularization in deeper models using a minimal two-layer diagonal linear network. In the single-task setting, L2 regularization on the weights of such models is known to promote sparsity in the resulting predictor, yielding a rich, sparse feature-learning regime beyond the kernel regime (a similar effect arises in two-layer ReLU networks). However, in continual learning, where L2 regularization is typically centered on previous-task weights, this sparsity bias is no longer preserved. We therefore propose a weighted variant of L2 regularization that extends this beneficial sparsity bias to continual learning. We show theoretically and empirically that, for a sufficiently sparse teacher, our variant converges substantially faster than directly learning a linear predictor with conventional continual L2 or L1 regularization, trading some long-horizon bias for markedly improved performance over moderate task horizons.

</details>

<a id="paper-227"></a>

### 227. [Message-passing graph persistence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06801v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06801

**作者：** Jorge Luiz Franco、Arthur Queiroz Moura、Moacir Ponti、Amauri Souza

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06801v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06801v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Message-Passing Graph Persistence，将消息传递嵌入图过滤过程，用节点嵌入关联持久事件并决定连通分量代表，证明其同构不变且表达力强于所扩展的 PH 描述子，并在图分类与回归任务上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其按过滤步骤更新状态、并以嵌入记录结构持续性与代表元的思路，可能为刻画 latent 推理轨迹的结构稳定性与动态状态摘要提供概念借鉴，但迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Message-passing graph persistence

**abstract/A1：** Message-passing graph neural networks (GNNs) are limited in expressive power by the one-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman test. Persistent homology (PH) provides topological descriptors that can complement GNN representations, but existing approaches typically compute persistence separately from the representation learning process. In this paper, we introduce Message-Passing Graph Persistence (MGP), a family of topological descriptors that incorporates message passing into graph filtrations. At each filtration step, node embeddings are updated using the current subgraph and associated with persistence events. These embeddings also determine the representatives of connected components during persistence computation. We prove that MGP is invariant under graph isomorphism and that it is strictly more expressive than the PH-based descriptors it extends. We evaluate MGP on an expressivity benchmark and on several graph classification and regression datasets. Experiments show that MGP outperforms other PH-based descriptors on most of these datasets.

</details>

<a id="paper-228"></a>

### 228. [Navigating Disagreement Among Criteria for Evaluating Explanation Faithfulness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06846v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06846

**作者：** Grant Shanklin、Jiří Němeček、Asia Biega

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06846v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06846v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究解释忠实性（faithfulness）评估准则之间为何会给出相互冲突的判定，提出一个「faithfulness functional」框架，把分歧定位在解释对模型作出的断言、哪些比较与断言相关、以及如何聚合这些比较三处，并将已有准则重构于其中，再用不利处分通知的案例说明具体情境如何为这些选择提供辩护。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 中「答案是否真在使用 latent 内容」的贡献分配和因果归因评价有概念呼应，其把忠实性拆为断言、比较与聚合的做法，可能可供核查用于梳理 latent 归因评估中多套判据不一致的问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Navigating Disagreement Among Criteria for Evaluating Explanation Faithfulness

**abstract/A1：** Faithfulness requires that algorithmic explanations accurately reflect the model's behavior. Yet existing operationalizations of faithfulness can disagree about whether the same explanation is faithful, leaving developers and auditors to choose among conflicting evaluations. In this paper, we investigate disagreement among evaluation criteria and offer a practical path forward for evaluating explanation faithfulness. We first identify how operationalizations of faithfulness differ. We introduce a faithfulness functional that locates their disagreements in what an explanation claims about the model, which comparisons bear on that claim, and how agreement across those comparisons should be aggregated, and we reconstruct existing evaluation criteria within it. We argue these reconstructions support understanding faithfulness as a common though incompletely specified relation between the model and explanation, so the differences reflect choices left open by the general requirement. Justifying an operationalization then comes down to justifying these choices, which, on our view, draws on explanatory, institutional, and normative considerations. We support this argument with a case study of adverse action notices, reconstructing the evaluation implicit in an industry-proposed procedure and identifying which of its choices the setting justifies and which remain open. Our work gives auditors and practitioners a way to navigate disagreeing evaluations of faithfulness. It also explains why the literature should expect multiple operationalizations, while showing how concrete settings can narrow the choice among them. More broadly, our analysis shows how a shared requirement of faithfulness can support contextually justified evaluations despite disagreement among existing metrics.

</details>

<a id="paper-229"></a>

### 229. [LongBanana: An Expert-Verified Benchmark for Long-Context Multi-Reference Image Synthesis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06856v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06856

**作者：** Haoxiang Cao、Yuxuan Zhang、Penghui Du、Bo Li、Jiajiong Cao、Yingying Fan、Zimeng Wu、Yifu Luo、Mingzhe Zheng、Junwen Miao、Huaisong Zhang、Yang Yao、Yu Wang、Dongfu Jiang、Ping Nie、Wenhu Chen、Chaoqun Wang、Changqian Yu、Kelsey R. Allen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06856v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06856v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 LongBanana，一个面向长上下文多参考图像合成的专家设计并核验基准，包含 90 个任务、每任务约 32 张参考图与近 4,670 条原子评价项，用实体-关系图把节点、边与全局约束转成清单式评分；并给出 LongBanana-Gen 智能体框架，比较直接生成与含 RPR 流程的 harness 在证据落地与修复上的差异。

**相关性简评：** 「分散视觉证据检索、干扰项排除、实体关系绑定」的任务表述与图约束式评测，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、关系保持与评价设计提供参照，但其最终产物是合成图像而非可检查的 latent 推理状态，与主线仅为间接关联，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LongBanana: An Expert-Verified Benchmark for Long-Context Multi-Reference Image Synthesis

**abstract/A1：** We introduce LongBanana, to our knowledge the first expert-designed and expert-verified benchmark for long-context multi-reference image synthesis in the age of agent-driven image generation. The task requires retrieving dispersed visual evidence, rejecting distractors, binding entities and relations, and composing one coherent final image from multiple references. LongBanana contains 90 carefully designed tasks organized by a 3-by-3 taxonomy; each task includes about 32 reference images, 20 constraints, and 50 evaluation items, yielding 4,670 expert-specified atomic evaluation items in total. Its graph-grounded evaluation design instantiates each task with reference images and an entity-relation graph, then converts node, edge, and whole-graph obligations into checklist items for Content Fidelity, Relational Binding, and Global Constraint Satisfaction. Reviewed expert labels include a 15% double-labeled subset with 89.4% exact agreement and 0.83 quadratic weighted kappa. We further introduce LongBanana-Gen, an agent-harness framework for long-horizon image generation; experiments show that minimal agent harnesses improve over direct generation, while a ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect (RPR) workflow yields harness-dependent gains by strengthening evidence grounding and repair. Together, LongBanana provides an auditable testbed for measuring dense visual evidence use and a shared target for developing stronger agent-driven image-generation systems. Code and data are available at the project page: https://long-banana.com/.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 Evaluations and Datasets Track. Project page: https://long-banana.com/

</details>

<a id="paper-230"></a>

### 230. [CounterFactual Retrieval for Evidence-Aware Individual Animal Identification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06864v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06864

**作者：** Ankit K. Upadhyay、Aleksandr Algasov、Ekaterina Nepovinnykh、Fedor Zolotarev、Tuomas Eerola、Heikki Kälviäinen、Charles V. Stewart、Lukas Picek

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06864v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06864v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CounterFactual Foreground Retrieval（CFR）评测协议，在同一 query–gallery 检索任务下分别用全图、仅前景、仅捷径（背景或轮廓）三种视图考察匹配所依据的证据，把单一检索分数转成「证据画像」，并发布约 0.5M 掩码覆盖 34 个数据集。

**相关性简评：** 其用反事实视图把任务相关视觉证据与捷径分开考察的评测思路，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否真的依赖实体与前景细节、以及视觉旁路与信息坍缩的诊断设计；但研究对象是个体动物检索而非 VLM 推理，迁移需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CounterFactual Retrieval for Evidence-Aware Individual Animal Identification

**abstract/A1：** Automated identification of individual animals is moving from small curated studies toward shared benchmarks and reusable foundation models. This progress is useful, but current evaluation leaves the evidence behind a match unresolved, i.e., high retrieval accuracy does not show whether a model used animal features or identity-correlated shortcuts. We address this with Counterfactual Foreground Retrieval (CFR), an evidence-aware evaluation protocol. CFR evaluates the same query-gallery task under different views: full image, foreground-only, and shortcut-only. The full-image measures standard retrieval performance; the foreground-only measures foreground-supported retrieval; and shortcut-only view measure how much identity signal is recoverable from background, or silhouette. This turns a retrieval score into an evidence profile: how well the model performs on the original images, how much of that performance remains when only the animal is visible, and how much identity signal remains recoverable when the animal is removed. We release around 0.5M masks for 34 datasets and code for mask generation and CFR evaluation.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 5 figures; accepted at WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-231"></a>

### 231. [Adaptive Multi Mode: Budget-Aware Routing of Optimizers By Online Landscape Probes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06879v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06879

**作者：** Benjamin Jacob Bodner

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.NE、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06879v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06879v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 AMM，在给定预算下用对曲率、平台、可分性、尖点与盆地等的在线探测，把预算分配给拟牛顿、信赖域、坐标、多起点与进化策略等优化引擎，并在 bbob 与 MA-BBOB 上与多个优化器比较。

**相关性简评：** 其「在线探测并在预算内计费、按景观性质切换求解模式」的框架，可能为 LVR 中按需获取证据、动态算力分配与停止决策提供类比线索；但该工作属纯黑盒数值优化，与视觉表示无直接联系，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adaptive Multi Mode: Budget-Aware Routing of Optimizers By Online Landscape Probes

**abstract/A1：** The best classical optimizer for a continuous black-box problem depends on its budget and landscape. Existing routers decide from the dimension and budget, past improvement or features fitted to one benchmark, and few test an optimizer's implicit assumptions online. AMM (Adaptive Multi Mode) routes a known budget among quasi-Newton, trust-region, coordinate, multistart and evolution-strategy optimization engines with online probes of curvature, plateaus, separability, kinks, basins and fractal structure. Each probe has a default, a dimension-by-budget validity range and a cost charged to the budget. In its pre-registered development loop on bbob, 32 of 36 predictions held over the last 16 confirmations. AMM was then tested once on held-out bbob instances 101-110 (D=2-40, 10\*D-1000\*D) against up to seven competitors. It has the highest target share in 382 of 600 function-dimension-budget cells (236 outright, 146 tied) and, by a descriptive posterior rule, is credibly behind in 35, mostly at 10\*D and 30\*D. At every budget it also leads the controls built from its own engines (the best engine alone, the best engine per dimension and budget, and a greedy schedule), narrowly at 10\*D and 30\*D, and nevergrad's NgIohTuned. In pre-registered runs its lead over IPOP-CMA-ES holds under affine and box transformations, on box-constrained bbob and, pooled, on rotated f2-f4, but rotating the separable f3 and f4 removes most of the lead there. On held-out MA-BBOB its final share at 2000\*D is level with IPOP-CMA-ES's and its anytime area lower. We published AMM as an open-source Python package (pip install amm-optimizer; https://github.com/BjBodner/amm-optimizer).

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 8 figures, 19 tables. Code: https://github.com/BjBodner/amm-optimizer

</details>

<a id="paper-232"></a>

### 232. [Procedural Adversarial Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06890v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06890

**作者：** Jack Nugent、Jia Deng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06890v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06890v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出程序化对抗学习（PAL）框架，在可解释程序生成器的低维控制参数空间中用零阶优化（Simple-PAT）按需搜索困难样本，并构建 PAL-Dojo 生成器集合，用于表面法向、分割与人体姿态估计的对抗训练与评估。

**相关性简评：** 其「由程序参数控制布局、光照、位姿并生成带真值图像」的设定，可能为检验 LVR 是否保留任务必要视觉细节提供可控反事实诊断手段，低维参数搜索困难实例的思路也可供核查评价设计，但论文本身不涉及多模态推理或 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Procedural Adversarial Learning

**abstract/A1：** For many computer vision tasks, neural networks can be trained to produce a low average error, yet they still fail on seemingly simple scenes. Reducing these failures requires first finding them, but they may not be in a fixed dataset. We study procedural adversarial learning (PAL), a framework of minimax training and adversarial evaluation which searches the input space of a procedural generator for difficult instances. The procedural generator is an interpretable program mapping a low-dimensional vector of control parameters (e.g. layout, lighting, pose) to a rendered image and its label. We introduce the Simple-PAT algorithm, which uses zeroth-order optimization in the procedural generator's parameter space to propose adversarial training samples that are generated on demand. We also create PAL-Dojo, a collection of efficient procedural generators for surface normal estimation, image segmentation, and human pose estimation. Against a cross-model black-box adversary with 50M aggregate queries, Simple-PAT reduces worst-case error by a geometric mean of 3.15x compared to standard training and 2.63x compared to the strongest robust training baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/princeton-vl/procedural-adversarial-learning.

</details>

<a id="paper-233"></a>

### 233. [Occlusion for Free: Detecting Occlusion from the Disagreement Between Two Monocular Distance Cues](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06899v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06899

**作者：** Chibueze Praise Onyenweaku、Toyibat Adele、Sikirulahi Opeyemi Abdulkareem、Obed Honour Eje

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06899v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06899v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一种无需训练、无需遮挡标注的遮挡检测方法：利用「视高距离」在遮挡下基本不变、而单目度量深度会被遮挡物拉近这一分歧，仅凭两者排序差异打分，并在 KITTI（AUC 0.909）与 CARLA 合成场景上验证。

**相关性简评：** 该工作给出一个不依赖标注的「视觉证据是否被遮蔽」信号，可能为 LVR 中视觉旁路何时不可信、是否需要再取证据（LOOK/STOP）提供廉价线索，但它本身不做推理状态建模，与内部表示的联系需进一步核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Occlusion for Free: Detecting Occlusion from the Disagreement Between Two Monocular Distance Cues

**abstract/A1：** Knowing that a pedestrian is occluded is useful to a robot even when the detector cannot recover the hidden pixels, but occlusion estimators are normally trained on occlusion annotations that deployment settings rarely have. We show that occlusion can be read off two distance estimates a camera-only pipeline already computes. Apparent height is largely unchanged by occlusion, because the detected box still spans the whole person; monocular metric depth is pulled toward the occluder, because the nearest surface inside the box is what a depth map reports. Only their ordering matters, so no focal length, stature, or depth scale is needed, provided the depth model's error is close to a pure gain, a condition that can be checked without labels. The score detects heavy occlusion on KITTI with AUC 0.909 (0.888 over all annotated pedestrians, including those the detector misses), requires no training and no occlusion labels, and is not a proxy for range: within fixed distance bands it holds AUC 0.894-0.925 while true distance alone sits near chance. On synthetic CARLA scenes with measured visibility it reaches 0.819, against 0.644 for distance alone. We arrived here after measuring three standard architectural responses to occlusion, depth fusion, occlusion-simulating augmentation, and attention, none of which improved detection outside its training distribution in the configurations we tested, and after finding that a detector trained on German roads loses half its accuracy on self-annotated street scenes in Lagos, Nigeria. Signals that need no local annotation are worth more in settings that benchmarks do not represent.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 GlobalSouthAI Workshop

</details>

### 234. [FrontierPhysics: Evaluating Agents for End-to-End Physics Research](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06910v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06910

**作者：** Bingran You、Wenjun Ke、Ria Rosenauer、Bo Liu、Wenbo Chen、Min Chen、Shuqi Xu、Yue Ma、Jiechao Feng、Bo Chen、Xiangkai Sun、Jiarui Liu、Jinen Guo、Zeyu Zhou、Xu Huang、Zhuohan Li、Haoyu Li、Heguang Lin、Lijie Ding、Yadong Zeng、Kangqi Zhang、Xinge Liu、Hanwen Liu、Zhenghong Li、Alexander von Recum、Yuchen Zhu、Felix Schwarzfischer、Yizhou Zhao、Jicheng Wang、Binxu Li、Qian Yu、Chan Li、Cheng Dai、Di Wang、Haoyu Zhang、Jiankai Sun、Kaituo Feng、Mian Qin、Ruihong Yin、Yiqun Luo、Weiwei Wu、Shihong Pan、Tsung Yeh Hsieh、Xiao Huang、Yan Zhang、Sihan Li、Zirui Ren、Zengji Tu、Kyoung Whan Choe、Ke Sun、Vincenzo Galia、Vaisakhi Mishra、Jiashu He、Hongyang Wang、Zhiheng Yang、Shubham Gaur、Yifan Gu、Shuyi Wang、Zihao Zuo、Steven Dillmann、Yiyou Sun、Xinyang Han、Hanchung Lee、Molei Tao、Yue Zhao、Shilong Liu、Yuchen Wu、Xuandong Zhao、Xiangyi Li、Florian Brand、Gerbrand Ceder、Hartmut Haeffner

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06910v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06910v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FrontierPhysics，一个由物理学家基于自身研究设计的 56 个端到端物理研究任务基准，要求智能体完成文献调研、制定计划、开展实验并撰写结构化输出与手稿，并用确定性验证器加专家评分表同时考察正确性与可发表性。

**相关性简评：** 虽不涉及视觉或 latent 状态，但其对长程轨迹中证据获取、计划推进与「正确性 vs 研究质量」双重评价的设计，可能为 LVR 的推理过程评价与停止决策提供参照，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FrontierPhysics: Evaluating Agents for End-to-End Physics Research

**abstract/A1：** AI agents are playing an increasingly important role in physics research. However, existing physics benchmarks only evaluate fixed questions, isolated sub-tasks, or paper reproduction with given solutions, and rarely check research quality alongside correctness. We introduce FrontierPhysics, an end-to-end benchmark with 56 tasks made by 42 physicists based on their own research, covering nine domains across theoretical, computational, and experimental physics. Each task requires agents to review the literature, develop a research plan, conduct research and experiments, and produce structured outputs and scientific manuscripts. Combining deterministic verifiers with expert-authored rubrics, FrontierPhysics evaluates whether agents' research is correct and publishable. Across 22 models in 26 configurations, no configuration passes more than 46 of its 168 rollouts. Manual review of agent trajectories shows that frontier agents' main weaknesses are insufficiently rigorous reasoning and limited expert intuition, rather than gaps in physics knowledge or execution errors.

**comment/C1：** 41 pages (10 pages main text), 19 figures, 11 tables

</details>

### 235. [MvBody: Multi-View-Based Hybrid Transformer Using Optical 3D Body Scan for Explainable Cesarean Section Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2511.03212v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2511.03212

**作者：** Ruting Cheng、Boyuan Feng、Yijiang Zheng、Chuhui Qiu、Aizierjiang Aiersilan、Joaquin A. Calderon、Wentao Zhao、Qing Pan、James K. Hahn

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.GR、cs.LG、cs.MM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2511.03212v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2511.03212v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MvBody，用孕期自报医学数据与 3D 光学体扫描的多视角 Transformer 预测剖宫产风险，并结合度量学习损失与 Integrated Gradients 解释。

**排除原因：** 属三维体形医学风险分类任务，模型仅输出风险分数，没有 latent 推理状态、证据筛选或动态计算，事后归因也不涉及 latent 内容被答案使用，缺少可供 LVR 核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MvBody: Multi-View-Based Hybrid Transformer Using Optical 3D Body Scan for Explainable Cesarean Section Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Accurately assessing the risk of cesarean section (CS) delivery is critical, especially in settings with limited medical resources, where access to healthcare is often restricted. Early and reliable risk prediction allows better-informed prenatal care decisions and can improve maternal and neonatal outcomes. However, most existing predictive models are tailored for in-hospital use during labor and rely on parameters that are often unavailable in resource-limited or home-based settings. In this study, we conduct a pilot investigation to examine the feasibility of using 3D body shape for CS risk assessment for future applications with more affordable general devices. We propose a novel multi-view-based Transformer network, MvBody, which predicts CS risk using only self-reported medical data and 3D optical body scans obtained between the 31st and 38th weeks of gestation. To enhance training efficiency and model generalizability in data-scarce environments, we incorporate a metric learning loss into the network. Compared to widely used machine learning models and the latest advanced 3D analysis methods, our method demonstrates superior performance, achieving an accuracy of 84.62% and an Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve (AUC-ROC) of 0.724 on the independent test set. To improve transparency and trust in the model's predictions, we apply the Integrated Gradients algorithm to provide theoretically grounded explanations of the model's decision-making process. Our results indicate that pre-pregnancy weight, maternal age, obstetric history, previous CS history, and body shape, particularly around the head and shoulders, are key contributors to CS risk prediction.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 4 figures

</details>

### 236. [Visual Forensic Detection of Fabricated Bangla News Photocards using Deep Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05403v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05403

**作者：** Anindya Kundu、Kabya Mithun Saha、Md. Muhaiminul Islam、Fayek Ahmed、Aminul Kader Bulbul、Muhammad Ibrahim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05403v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05403v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建孟加拉语假新闻「图片卡」数据集，用小型全批归一化 CNN 与 ImageNet 预训练 YOLOv8n-cls 做伪造图像二分类，并给出分组交叉验证与误差分析。

**排除原因：** 该工作是静态图像伪造分类加数据增强与评估协议的工程研究，不涉及 latent 状态生成、证据筛选或推理期计算决策，也未见可供核查的 LVR 方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Visual Forensic Detection of Fabricated Bangla News Photocards using Deep Learning

**abstract/A1：** In Bangladesh, much localized misinformation spreads as photocards: single-image news summaries that imitate the branding of well-known news organizations. Because the misleading message lives in the pixels rather than in machine-readable text, text-based fact-checking tools cannot see it. We recast photocard fake-news detection as visual image forgery detection. We build a dataset of 969 authentic and 1,077 fabricated Bangla photocards scraped from public Facebook pages: authentic cards from the verified pages of seventeen national television broadcasters, fabricated ones from pseudo-broadcaster pages, anonymous profiles, and public groups. Sixteen photometric and morphological transformations per image grow the corpus to 32,736 samples. A fully connected ANN reaches only about 52% accuracy, its loss stuck at ln(2) ~ 0.693, showing that flattening the image destroys the spatial cues forgery detection needs. A compact, fully batch-normalized four-block CNN with 489,954 parameters, about 1,644x smaller than the ANN, is evaluated with leakage-free stratified group five-fold cross-validation, which keeps every augmented version of a card in one fold. Under this protocol the CNN reaches 87.45% +/- 0.53% pooled accuracy from scratch and 88.75% +/- 1.55% when its classifier head is warm-started from the ANN. An ImageNet-pretrained YOLOv8n-cls backbone (1.44M parameters) fine-tuned on the same folds reaches 97.36% +/- 0.42%, marking the headroom above the compact model. We report per-class precision, recall, F1, and pooled confusion matrices, diagnose and fix a one-class collapse caused by an unnormalized classifier head, and give a manual error analysis and bias-variance diagnosis showing that the compact CNNs under-fit.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 9 figures, 22 tables

</details>

### 237. [mmHand: Toward Pixel-Level-Accuracy Hand Localization Using a Single Commodity mmWave Device](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05404v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05404

**作者：** Xiaoyu Zhang、Zhengxiong Li、Chenhan Xu、Luchuan Song、Huining Li、Hongfei Xue、Yingxiao Wu、Wenyao Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05404v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05404v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 mmHand：将单台商用毫米波雷达的原始数据转换为二维「mmImage」表示，并设计质量评估与像素空间标注流程，训练跨模态空间特征增强模型，在 U-V 像素坐标系下实现无穿戴、保护隐私的手部像素级定位。

**排除原因：** 属毫米波感知定位的系统与应用工作，虽涉及将非视觉信号转成图像式空间表示，但没有推理状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策等与 LVR 有关的机制，缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** mmHand: Toward Pixel-Level-Accuracy Hand Localization Using a Single Commodity mmWave Device

**abstract/A1：** The hand localization problem has been a longstanding focus due to its many applications. The task involves modeling the hand as a singular point and determining its position within a defined coordinate system. However, due to data modality limitations, existing hand localization technologies face several challenges. For example, vision-based localization raises privacy concerns, while wearable-based methods compromise user comfort. In this paper, we introduce mmHand, a new device-free, privacy-preserving dynamic hand localization system with pixel-level accuracy, using a single commodity mmWave device. We first propose a mmImage generation tool to fully extract spatial information from raw mmWave data and introduce a novel 2D image-format representation of mmWave data. Next, we design a framework that provides a new quality evaluation method and pixel space labeling for the mmWave data. Finally, we present a cross-modality spatial feature-enhanced model with high spatial feature extraction capabilities, which can accurately localize hand positions at the pixel level in the mmWave radar U-V pixel coordinate system. We evaluate the system with experiments on 12 subjects in three scenarios, and the results across four metrics demonstrate the effectiveness of our hand localization system.

</details>

### 238. [When Does Sharing Work? Scaling a Shared SigLIP2 Across Image, Video, Audio, and Audio-Visual Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05406v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05406

**作者：** Xiaofei Sun、Peng Wang、Huayao Liu、Xiaoxiao Xu、Jun Song、Deqing Li、Lin Qu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05406v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05406v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Omni-Encoder，用一套全共享参数的 SigLIP-2 ViT 统一编码图像、视频、音频与音视频输入，通过模态原生分词、绝对时间戳参数化的多模态 RoPE 与交替模态训练实现端到端联合优化。

**排除原因：** 属于多模态表示预训练与编码器共享的工作，不涉及推理状态生成、视觉证据筛选、思考/查看/停止决策或 latent 使用与监督，也没有可迁移到 LVR 的具体机制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Does Sharing Work? Scaling a Shared SigLIP2 Across Image, Video, Audio, and Audio-Visual Representations

**abstract/A1：** Contrastive pretraining has advanced image-text understanding, yet image, video, and audio encoders are still trained independently, with cross-modal alignment realized mainly through post-hoc binding to a frozen anchor space. We present Omni-Encoder, a general multimodal encoder that processes image, video, audio, and audio-visual inputs within a single SigLIP-2 Vision Transformer with fully shared parameters. Unlike anchor-centric binding or per-modality adapters, all modalities are optimized jointly end-to-end, and the framework is organized from input to training. A modality-native input stage first maps each signal into the shared embedding space, including a 1D-convolutional audio tokenizer that converts mel-spectrograms into tokens aligned with visual patches. A multimodal rotary position embedding parameterized by absolute timestamps then places visual frames and audio segments in a unified spatiotemporal coordinate system. Finally, alternating-modality training interleaves image, video, audio, and temporally aligned audio-visual batches and accumulates their gradients into one update, which we find central to stable joint optimization. Omni-Encoder achieves competitive results on 16 benchmarks across all four modalities with one shared parameter set, and joint training improves each modality over its separately trained counterpart. Code and models will be publicly released.

</details>

<a id="paper-239"></a>

### 239. [PointNDF: Cascaded Neural Displacement Fields for Point Cloud Denoising](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05411v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05411

**作者：** Michael Y. Wang、Pingping Cai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05411v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05411v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究点云去噪的级联神经位移场回归，目标是几何质量与点分布均匀性，与 LVR 的 latent 状态生成、视觉证据筛选、计算分配与停止决策均无具体联系，也未见可供迁移的推理状态或监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PointNDF: Cascaded Neural Displacement Fields for Point Cloud Denoising

**abstract/A1：** Point cloud denoising aims to recover clean geometry from noisy 3D observations while preserving both fine surface details and the underlying spatial distribution of the points. Many learning-based methods adopt a two-stage pipeline that first extracts point-wise features from the noisy input and subsequently predicts point-wise displacements via simple regressions. However, these approaches often lack robust feature guidance and distribution-aware geometric refinement, causing them to fail at maintaining optimal point uniformity and geometric quality. To address these limitations, we propose PointNDF, a denoising framework that combines patch-level geometric context with local displacement prediction. Specifically, a Point Transformer V3 (PTv3) encoder aggregates multiscale geometric information into a global patch descriptor. Rather than directly regressing an unconstrained displacement vector, a Neural Displacement Field (NDF) fuses this global patch descriptor with per-point neighborhood features. It then predicts each displacement through bounded-vector and scale factor branches, thereby achieving a superior, more uniform point distribution. Three cascaded NDF stages propagate local features and progressively refine the point positions. Experiments on PU-Net and ModelNet10 demonstrate competitive denoising accuracy and favorable inference efficiency. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/peridous/PointNDF.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 3 figures, 7 tables. Accepted to MMM 2027 (33rd International Conference on Multimedia Modeling)

</details>

### 240. [Constraint-Preserving Flow Matching for Spectral Graph Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05436v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05436

**作者：** Anita Yang、Ken-ichi Kawarabayashi、Yohan Jung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05436v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05436v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 Laplacian-Constrained Stiefel Flow Matching（LaSFM），在流匹配生成中同时保持特征向量正交性与 Laplacian 零空间约束，并扩展到性质引导的谱图生成。

**排除原因：** 主题为谱图生成与流形几何约束，未涉及视觉或多模态 latent 推理、证据获取训练或停止决策，缺少可迁移的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Constraint-Preserving Flow Matching for Spectral Graph Generation

**abstract/A1：** Spectral graph generation models graphs through the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of their Laplacians, providing a compact and continuous alternative to direct graph construction. However, these eigensystems must retain the structural properties of graph Laplacians. Existing approaches preserve eigenvector orthonormality but do not enforce the Laplacian nullspace constraint, potentially producing eigensystems inconsistent with Laplacian structure. To address this, we introduce Laplacian-Constrained Stiefel Flow Matching (LaSFM), which preserves the nullspace condition alongside eigenvector orthonormality and positive eigenvalues during generation. We characterize the underlying constrained Stiefel manifold and formulate flow matching on it. We further extend LaSFM to property-guided generation, steering samples toward desired graph properties while maintaining these constraints. Experiments demonstrate improved performance on both spectral and graph-level metrics, along with better alignment with target properties.

</details>

<a id="paper-241"></a>

### 241. [Matched but Not Better: An Equivalence-Tested Benchmark of Quantum Machine Learning on Six Clinical Datasets and the QML-Health-READY Reporting Framework](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05442v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05442

**作者：** Mohammed Siraj B、Gousia Habib、Ruban S

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05442v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05442v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在六个公开临床数据集上，将量子核 SVM 与变分量子分类器同五个调优后的经典模型进行配对比较，并检验其 AUROC 等价性，同时提出一个八领域的 QML 报告框架。

**排除原因：** 内容属于量子机器学习在临床预测上的局限与报告规范，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部表示、证据使用或停止决策；其配对比较与等价检验只是一般统计做法，没有可供核查的具体迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Matched but Not Better: An Equivalence-Tested Benchmark of Quantum Machine Learning on Six Clinical Datasets and the QML-Health-READY Reporting Framework

**abstract/A1：** Background: Quantum machine learning (QML) is promoted for clinical prediction, but comparisons with classical models are rarely paired or tested for equivalence. Methods: We compared a quantum-kernel support vector machine (QSVM; 4 qubits, tuned bandwidth) and a variational quantum classifier (VQC) with five tuned classical models across six public clinical datasets. All models used identical four-component inputs and 25 cross-validation splits. Paired AUROC differences were assessed using a corrected resampled t-test, Holm-adjusted equivalence tests (margins: +/-0.02 and +/-0.05), and random-effects pooling. Ablation, noise, finite-shot, and implementation-fault analyses were conducted. We propose QML-Health-READY, an eight-domain reporting framework. Results: Neither quantum model significantly outperformed any classical comparator. Pooled across datasets, QSVM differed from the kernel-matched radial basis function (RBF) SVM by -0.006 AUROC (95% CI: -0.017 to +0.004), equivalent to +/-0.02. VQC differed from the best classical model by -0.007 (95% CI: -0.019 to +0.004). QSVM was equivalent to RBF SVM within +/-0.05 in four of six datasets but was significantly worse than the best classical model on NHANES 2017-18 (-0.076). A classically trivial product-state kernel performed as well as the entangling kernel. With depolarizing noise (p = 0.01) and 1,024 shots per kernel entry, QSVM AUROC decreased by 0.054 (95% CI: 0.036 to 0.071). Untuned bandwidth produced near-chance AUROC (0.48-0.59). Conclusions: Correctly configured quantum classifiers approached but did not exceed tuned classical models. Finite-shot estimation, rather than gate noise, was their main practical cost, while configuration errors could reverse conclusions. QML-Health-READY defines reporting requirements for credible claims.

</details>

### 242. [FedSharp: Byzantine Robust Decentralized Federated Learning Under Noisy Channel](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05546v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05546

**作者：** Ojaswi Sinha、Amin Farajzadeh、Animesh Yadav、Melike Erol-Kantarci

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05546v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05546v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FedSharp，一种面向去中心化联邦学习的鲁棒聚合框架：节点保留 2b 个前驱模型，用坐标中位数偏差筛除可疑模型，再对损失最低的 b 个候选做近端平均，并结合噪声自适应梯度范数正则，以应对信道噪声累积、Byzantine 攻击与非 IID 漂移。

**排除原因：** 该工作研究分布式训练中的拜占庭鲁棒聚合与信道噪声，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部表示、证据使用或推理期状态生成，其模型筛选机制也难以具体对应 LVR 的假设选择问题，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FedSharp: Byzantine Robust Decentralized Federated Learning Under Noisy Channel

**abstract/A1：** Decentralized federated learning (DFL) eliminates the central aggregation server but remains vulnerable to data heterogeneity, Byzantine attacks, and channel noise. We show that noise accumulates as models propagate across nodes, becoming increasingly significant as the learning rate decays. Moreover, accumulated noise enables Byzantine attackers to exploit loss-based model selection by mimicking honest model averages. To address these challenges, we propose screened, hop-aware, ranked, proximal averaging, a robust DFL framework (FedSharp). In this approach, each node maintains models from its 2b predecessors, ensuring at least b honest candidates when at most b nodes are Byzantine. FedSharp screens suspicious models using coordinate-wise median deviations, averages the b lowest-loss candidates to reduce fresh channel-noise variance by a factor of b, and applies noise-adaptive gradient-norm regularization and proximal training to mitigate accumulated noise and non-IID drift. Experiments on a 100-node ring with 33 Byzantine nodes show that FedSharp achieves 93.5-94.9% accuracy on MNIST, compared with 29.4-38.1% for a state-of-the-art single-model-selection benchmark, and 32.8-48.8% on CIFAR-10, compared with 10.0-30.5%, across Gaussian and hidden attacks under IID and non-IID data. On MNIST, FedSharp also achieves accuracy within approximately one percentage point of the benchmark operating without channel noise.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 4 figures, submitted for possible publication

</details>

### 243. [Whose Values Are Generated? Measuring Visual Value Leakage in Multilingual Text-to-Image Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05578v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05578

**作者：** Zucan Lyu、Binwei Yao、Rheeya Uppaal、Jing Yao、Xiaoyuan Yi、Junjie Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05578v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05578v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 VisionValueBench，一个多语言基准，用 Hofstede 的六个文化价值维度检验 T2I 系统在价值中立的社会场景提示下是否输出偏向特定价值取向的图像，即「视觉价值泄漏」，并比较提示语言与地理线索的影响。

**排除原因：** 研究重点是图像生成中的文化价值偏向与提示语言效应，不涉及 VLM 的推理状态表示、视觉证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 的方法或评价线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Whose Values Are Generated? Measuring Visual Value Leakage in Multilingual Text-to-Image Generation

**abstract/A1：** Text-to-image (T2I) systems serve users across languages and cultural contexts. However, evaluations of cultural representation focus mainly on recognizable elements such as landmarks, clothing, and activities, overlooking the cultural values that generated images convey. To bridge this gap, we introduce VisionValueBench, a multilingual benchmark that operationalizes six of Hofstede's cultural value dimensions (e.g., individualism versus collectivism) to investigate how T2I systems frame neutral social situations through a particular cultural orientation. We find that value-neutral prompts, which admit diverse cultural interpretations, nonetheless yield images that favor particular value orientations, a phenomenon we call visual value leakage. We evaluate prompts varying in language and geographic cues across 960 social scenarios and six T2I configurations. Generated images consistently favor the same value orientations across all six dimensions, for example, equality over hierarchy (low power distance), individual autonomy over group obligation (individualism), and enjoyment over restraint (indulgence). Prompt language has limited influence on these orientations, whereas geographic cues shift some dimensions toward the associated cultural values while largely preserving the prevailing orientations. These findings reveal substantial homogeneity in the cultural values conveyed by T2I systems worldwide.

**comment/C1：** 38 pages

</details>

### 244. [BrainBind: Bridging Synchronized EEG and fMRI through Long-Form Naturalistic Video Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05581v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05581

**作者：** Shixiao Wang、Rining Wu、Jian K. Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05581v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05581v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 BrainBind，将同步采集的 EEG 与 fMRI 映射到冻结的长视频嵌入空间，在 CineBrain 数据集上做跨模态检索，并比较不同代理空间与系统配置的检索表现。

**排除原因：** 属脑信号解码与跨模态检索研究，不涉及模型内部推理状态、视觉证据使用或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 等决策过程；其检索评估与 episode-disjoint 划分虽涉及语义相近候选和情境混淆，但与 LVR 的表示形成和生成机制缺少可迁移的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BrainBind: Bridging Synchronized EEG and fMRI through Long-Form Naturalistic Video Representations

**abstract/A1：** Real-world vision is dynamic and scene-rich, while EEG and fMRI resolve it at complementary temporal and spatial scales. Most neural decoding systems, however, align one modality in isolation and mostly against static images or short clips. We study the synchronized case: EEG and fMRI are recorded during the same long-form naturalistic viewing, giving a shared stimulus but also recurring narrative context and many semantically similar candidates. We present BrainBind, a framework that maps synchronized EEG and fMRI into frozen long-form video embedding spaces and treats those spaces as common visual-semantic references, not literal neural codes. On CineBrain, a five-seed Recall@K 2 x 4 matrix across two video anchors and four system configurations shows distinct retrieval profiles. With Omni, fMRI organizes the full candidate list most strongly (MRR = 23.82, nDCG@10 = 25.31), while Joint-Dual gives the highest mean label-aware R@1 (13.52%) with a distinct rank-wide profile. The complementary episode-disjoint evaluation quantifies the contribution of repeated stimulus identity and narrative context to the random split. Across these experiments, proxy choice and system configuration produce distinct top-rank and rank-wide retrieval profiles within the present synchronized dataset. The code is available at https://github.com/Forainest789/Brainbind.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at BMVC 2026. 26 pages, 4 figures, including supplementary material. Code: https://github.com/Forainest789/Brainbind

</details>

### 245. [Exploring the Privacy--Size Tradeoff for Differentially Private Self-Attentive Variational Autoencoders](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05582v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05582

**作者：** Rohit Sadavarte、Dhruvi Kothari、Sparsh Gupta、Eric Van Oeveren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CE、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05582v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05582v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SAVANT——一个在 DP-SGD 下训练的表格数据自注意力 VAE 生成器，在两个公开数据集上联合考察隐私预算与数据规模对下游分类效用的影响，并用两种成员推断攻击检验隐私泄漏。

**排除原因：** 研究主题是表格合成数据的差分隐私与规模权衡，与视觉/多模态推理中的内部表征、证据使用或停止决策没有实质联系；注意力与 VAE 在此仅作为表格生成器结构，未涉及推理期状态生成或视觉信息保留，故不入选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Exploring the Privacy--Size Tradeoff for Differentially Private Self-Attentive Variational Autoencoders

**abstract/A1：** Data tends to be most useful when it is shared, and the data most worth sharing is often the data that privacy obligations restrict. Synthetic data from a generator trained under differential privacy (DP) is one way to reconcile the two, but its utility depends on the privacy budget $\\varepsilon$ and on how much data the generator sees - a parameter that most data sharing applications of synthetic data control. We present SAVANT, a self-attentive variational autoencoder for tabular data trained with DP-SGD that, to our knowledge, is the first to combine attention, a VAE, and DP in one tabular model. We evaluate it against two marginal mechanisms, PrivBayes and MST, jointly over the privacy budget and the dataset size on two public datasets, measuring the utility a downstream classifier loses by training on synthetic rather than real data. We find that, under the same DP guarantee, more data generally means more utility. For the marginal mechanisms it absorbs most of what a strict budget costs, and a noise-scaling argument suggests DP-SGD exchanges data fo budget at a worse rate. Two independent membership-inference attacks, judged against a measured null, found no aggregate membership signal.

**comment/C1：** ICAIF 2026, Financial AI Security, Privacy and Safety workshop. Poster session

</details>

### 246. [Anduin: A sybil-resistant agentic marketplace reputation system](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05586v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05586

**作者：** Reza Ramji、Preston Vander Vos、Ziming Zeng、Duc V. Le

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05586v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05586v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究链上智能体服务市场的声誉系统：以 6600 万笔支付记录构建买卖双方二部图，用与可信网络相连的图路径推导服务商评分，并提出三种抗 Sybil 评分算法，在模拟对手下评估操纵成本。

**排除原因：** 主题是去中心化市场的身份与激励安全，未涉及多模态推理中的视觉表示、latent 状态生成或证据使用；其图路径信任传播虽属加权聚合，但与 LVR 的贡献分配或证据筛选缺乏可核查的具体接口，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Anduin: A sybil-resistant agentic marketplace reputation system

**abstract/A1：** As autonomous AI agents enter permissionless onchain markets, selecting new service providers remains a core challenge. Existing reputation systems depend on scarce resources like human curation, identity credentials, or voluntary reviews, while naive volume rankings are vulnerable to Sybil attacks. We evaluate whether existing onchain payment histories can yield provider ratings that are resilient against Sybil attacks. Using a 157-day dataset comprising 66M agentic transactions worth $6.1M across 16 blockchains, we model payment flows as a bipartite graph between buyers and providers. A provider's rating is derived from graph paths linked to a buyer's existing trusted network. We propose three Sybil-resistant rating algorithms: a max-flow rating and two relaxed algorithms optimized for stronger rating performance. Evaluating these algorithms against simulated adversaries across 813 buyers, we show that rating manipulation costs 20% of those buyers' total spend and is around 23x harder to manipulate than the standard similarity rule (collaborative filtering).

</details>

### 247. [ViBench 2.0: A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Software Building](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05620v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05620

**作者：** Daniel Furman、Preeya Kirani、Ibrahim Sheikh、Zhen Li、Peter Zhong、Michele Catasta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05620v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05620v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作是长程软件构建的编码智能体基准，只按最终产品成败判分，未涉及视觉或多模态内部状态、证据筛选、latent 生成或训练监督，与 LVR 主线缺乏可迁移的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ViBench 2.0: A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Software Building

**abstract/A1：** On Replit, our agentic engineering platform, users now build entire products without writing code, through successive feature requests to an agent. Yet existing coding benchmarks evaluate single, fully specified tasks, and many are near saturation. We introduce ViBench 2.0, a benchmark for long-horizon software building that we use to select our platform's models. Its 17 full-stack web products, modeled on widely used software, grow over 241 feature requests and are judged only when finished, as a user would judge them: a browser agent runs 544 test plans, and a build passes only if every plan does, under a grading protocol validated to keep this strict score reliable. Evaluating nine models, we find a reliability gap: the three strongest pass up to 98% of test plans yet deliver a fully working product in at most 65% of builds, and no open-weight model delivers one. The gap is closing fast: across three releases of one model family, pass@1 on the public products rises from 3% to 53% while the cost per build falls by more than half. We release eight products with the harness and grader, hold out nine, and report lessons from production use.

</details>

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究视频动作识别的时空表示，用纯深度可分离卷积与隐式门控聚合实现线性的多阶时序建模，并提出空间-时间双交互以在不增加开销的情况下改进表示，在多个视频基准上取得与 SOTA 相当的结果。

**排除原因：** 这是纯视觉骨干的效率与表示阶数问题，既无 LVR 式的内部推理状态生成与更新，也不涉及证据筛选、停止决策或答案对 latent 内容的使用，未提供可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spatio-Temporal Multi-Order Interaction for Video Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Many temporal approaches for video recognition rely on explicit cross-channel aggregation projections, which have quadratic complexity in the number of channels, introducing significant computational overhead. In addition, the spatio-temporal representation used for video recognition is biased either toward local order interactions (for CNNs) or toward global order interactions (for transformers). Starting from the multi-order spatial representation of MogaNet, our method introduces two complementary contributions to tackle these problems. First, for the temporal modelling, we introduce parallel multi-order processing with depthwise-only convolutions and implicit gating-based aggregation, which is linear in the number of channels, inducing a considerable computational reduction. Second, we propose a dual interaction between spatial and temporal dimensions, which allows a better spatio-temporal representation without increasing the computational cost. By combining these two contributions, our proposed VideoMogaNet achieves performance on par with or better than state-of-the-art action recognition approaches across multiple video benchmarks (UCF101, HMDB51, Kinetics-400, Diving48), while considerably reducing the cost of the temporal branch.

</details>

### 249. [NitroFusion2: Bringing Adversarial Loss to the Text Stream of Multimodal Diffusion Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05648v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05648

**作者：** Dar-Yen Chen、Yi-Zhe Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05648v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05648v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作面向多模态扩散 Transformer 的少步图像生成，提出以文本流中间 token 状态作为对抗判别表征，并结合按属性条件化的判别器，把蒸馏步数降到 1–2 步。

**排除原因：** 虽涉及「中间状态作为表征」和少步计算，但文本流状态仅服务于图像生成的对抗蒸馏与细节质量，不涉及推理状态生成、证据筛选或答案使用，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NitroFusion2: Bringing Adversarial Loss to the Text Stream of Multimodal Diffusion Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Adversarial diffusion distillation has conventionally relied on image features for discrimination. We revisit this choice for multimodal diffusion transformers (MMDiTs), whose text stream becomes image-dependent through joint attention. We introduce Text-Stream Adversarial Distillation (TSAD), which exploits intermediate text-token states as a new adversarial representation. Because the text stream has already read the image while remaining tied to the text condition, changing that condition changes what information it extracts from the image. Building on this property, we introduce Aspect-Cued Discrimination (ACD), which conditions the discriminator on different aspects of the sample, such as typography, objects, or style, to steer which properties are emphasized during adversarial training. Building on the expert-discriminator design of NitroFusion, we integrate TSAD and ACD in NitroFusion2. NitroFusion2 brings few-step distillation from the conventional 4-8-step regime down to just 1-2 steps at comparable quality. At one step, it produces sharp rendered text and rich fine-grained details, making one-step generation practical rather than merely a stress test for distillation. At two steps, it remains competitive across the evaluated benchmarks. These results establish the MMDiT text stream as a powerful and steerable representation for adversarial diffusion distillation.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://chendaryen.github.io/NitroFusion2.github.io/

</details>

### 250. [Adaptive Domain Decomposition for Finite Basis Physics-Informed Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05659v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05659

**作者：** Sarah Helfert、Wanxin Chen、Amanda A. Howard、Panos Stinis、Alexander Heinlein

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05659v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05659v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作将 POUNets 学习到的可分离域划分与有限基 PINNs 结合，提出 ADD-FBPINNs，在多个二维 PDE（Helmholtz、多尺度 Poisson、Burgers）上自动学习稳定且几何可解释的子域划分以提升精度。

**排除原因：** 论文属于 PDE 数值求解中的自适应区域分解与基函数构造，未涉及多模态推理、latent 状态生成或证据筛选，与 LVR 主线缺乏具体可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adaptive Domain Decomposition for Finite Basis Physics-Informed Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** In this work, we present adaptive domain decomposition finite basis physics-informed neural networks (ADD-FBPINNs). The proposed method integrates Partition of Unity Networks (POUNets), a data-driven approach for learning domain partitions, with finite Basis physics-informed neural networks (FBPINNs) to perform more efficient domain decomposition and achieve more accurate approximations. FBPINNs improve upon the accuracy of standard physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) through user-defined subdivisions of the computational domain. By adapting the domain decomposition based on the solution's features using a separable POUNet, the framework can automatically learn partitions that are stable and can be geometrically interpretable. We test this approach on the two-dimensional Helmholtz equation, two-dimensional Poisson equation with multiscale features, and viscous Burgers equation and observe improved accuracy compared with FBPINNs with fixed domain decomposition.

</details>
