### 251. [Self-Supervised Pretraining for Label-Efficient Facial Expression Recognition using Smart Glasses](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05711v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05711

**作者：** Zhengtao Gao、Francesco Bombassei De Bona、Leonardo Alchieri、Ivana Kiprijanovska、Simon Stankoski、Bojan Sofronievski、Hristijan Gjoreski、Marc Langheinrich、Martin Gjoreski

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05711v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05711v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在 OCOsense 光学肌电智能眼镜数据上以掩码自编码目标预训练 ViT，并微调做五类表情识别，以降低标注需求。

**排除原因：** 属传感器信号上的自监督标签效率研究，不涉及视觉/多模态推理中的内部状态、证据获取或停止决策，也无 latent 推理或状态更新的可迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Self-Supervised Pretraining for Label-Efficient Facial Expression Recognition using Smart Glasses

**abstract/A1：** Facial Expression Recognition (FER) on optomyographic (OMG) smart glasses such as OCOsense enables privacy-preserving, camera-free affect sensing for health and well-being. Most FER systems are fully supervised, i.e., they rely either on hand-crafted expert features with classical machine learning, or on end-to-end deep models trained from labelled data. Both depend on large quantities of labelled expressions, which are costly to collect and limit deployment in real-world, personalized settings. We investigate Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) as a route to label-efficient FER on these glasses. We pretrain a Vision Transformer with a masked-autoencoder objective on unlabelled OCOsense recordings and finetune it for five-class FER under person-independent cross-validation. Across label budgets from 5% to 100%, pretraining consistently improves performance; with only 5% of the labels, the pretrained model outperforms the from-scratch model trained with all labels. Our results show that SSL pretraining is a practical way to reduce labelling cost for smart eyewear FER, and we discuss the implications for future personalization and continual adaptation.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 5 figures, 2 tables. Best Paper Award at the SEPH 2026 workshop, UbiComp Companion '26

</details>

### 252. [C-WFDM: A ControlNet-Guided Wavelet-Fusion Diffusion Model for Multimodal Brain MRI Completion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05737v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05737

**作者：** Muhammad Nabi Yasinzai、Remika Mito、Mangor Pedersen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05737v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05737v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文属于脑 MRI 缺失模态的图像补全与生成任务，虽以已有对比度作为条件，但不涉及 VLM 推理状态、证据获取、停止决策或 latent 表示使用，缺乏可供核查的 LVR 迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** C-WFDM: A ControlNet-Guided Wavelet-Fusion Diffusion Model for Multimodal Brain MRI Completion

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal brain MRI provides complementary anatomical, tissue, and pathological information to support downstream artificial intelligence (AI) applications. However, modality availability is uneven across public and pooled neuroimaging datasets: T1-weighted (T1w) MRI is widely represented, T2-weighted (T2w) and fluid-attenuated inversion recovery (FLAIR) MRI are less consistently available, and gadolinium-enhanced T1-weighted (T1Gd) MRI is relatively scarce. Pooled datasets therefore contain participants with different combinations of acquired MRI contrasts, reflecting variation in the study designs, acquisition protocols, and clinical or research objectives of the contributing studies. Many missing-modality benchmarks simulate incomplete MRI by withholding contrasts from complete four-modality cases, which may not represent the observed availability patterns in pooled datasets. To address this gap, we introduce C-WFDM, a ControlNet extension of our Wavelet-Fusion Diffusion Model (WFDM), for subject-specific missing-modality completion. C-WFDM is built on an earlier BrainScape snapshot comprising 157 public MRI datasets and 45,880 preprocessed scans. The pretrained WFDM backbone retains target-modality and metadata conditioning, while target-specific ControlNet adapters use MRI contrasts from the same participant to guide synthesis. C-WFDM synthesizes T2w from T1w, FLAIR from T1w and T2w, and T1Gd from T1w, T2w, and FLAIR. Acquired conditioning contrasts are used when available, and generated intermediate contrasts are used only when required. We compare C-WFDM with ALDM, cWDM, and Pix2Pix using paired structural similarity index (SSIM), peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), and mean absolute error (MAE). In the BrainScape completion experiment, C-WFDM achieves the best T2w and FLAIR results among the evaluated models, with SSIM values of 0.9232 and 0.8904, respectively.

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05739

**作者：** Haixin Wang、Jianheng Hou、Hongyu Shen、Wenzhe Fan、Wei-Chieh Huang、Qianyu Zhu、Weichen Li、Taoran Li、Yuhang Yao、Ruohan Wang、Zhejian Peng、Wanting Jiang、Yifan Chen、Shushu Liang、Haoming Li、Defu Cao、Xu Hu、Siyang Wu、Chen Yang、Haodi Hu、Haichao Zhang、Cong Wang、Zhiyuan Yu、Weizhi Zhang、Yifan Wang、Jintao Huang、Yizhou Sun、Yijun Wang、Yuexing Hao、Xiaomin Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05739v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05739v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PersonaCodeBench，将用户画像转化为可验证要求，在多轮交互式编程会话中评测编码代理的个性化需求满足与正确性。

**排除原因：** 该工作聚焦编码代理的用户画像与软件交付评测，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态，也未提供可用于 LVR 的状态表示、证据筛选或停止决策的具体方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PersonaCodeBench: Evaluating Coding Agents in Persona-driven Human-Interactive Sessions

**abstract/A1：** Coding agents must deliver correct software while respecting users' expertise, preferences, and working conditions. Existing benchmarks increasingly incorporate interaction, yet personalized requirement fulfillment remains underexplored. We introduce PersonaCodeBench, which turns user characteristics into verifiable obligations in persona-driven multi-turn coding sessions. Our persona pool spans roles, technical abilities, interaction preferences, and practical constraints, yielding task-grounded evolved requirements for implementation, permissions, testing, explanation, and delivery. At runtime, a controller governs user simulator responses and requirement disclosure, using online requirement evaluation to provide feedback and advance the interaction. Requirements emerge progressively, with their content, disclosure conditions, and acceptance criteria fixed before evaluation. A completion verifier and a personalization verifier separately assess native correctness and persona fulfillment. Joint pass requires both adherence to the declared budget and compliance with validity conditions. We evaluate all major frontier agents, establish a comprehensive leaderboard, and conduct detailed experimental analyses to validate effectiveness. PersonaCodeBench supports reproducible evaluation of coding agents' ability to meet explicit, personalized user expectations alongside coding objectives.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages, 9 figures

</details>

### 254. [WayPOP: A Panoramic Open-Set Panoptic Tracking Benchmark](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05801v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05801

**作者：** Rohit Mohan、Maximilian Luz、Swarnava Chowdhury、Yao Lu、Florian Drews、Thomas Nürnberg、Yakov Miron、Federico Tombari、Stefano Gasperini、Abhinav Valada

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05801v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05801v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 WayPOP，一个基于 Waymo 全景序列并注入 Objaverse 未知三维资产的开放集全景 panoptic tracking 基准，含跨视角、跨时间一致的标注与评测协议。

**排除原因：** 该工作是自动驾驶全景分割与跟踪的基准构建，关注未知类别掩码与身份关联，未涉及 VLM 内部表示、latent 推理状态或证据使用，缺少可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WayPOP: A Panoramic Open-Set Panoptic Tracking Benchmark

**abstract/A1：** Holistic perception for autonomous agents requires reliably segmenting and associating scene elements across time and camera views, including objects outside a fixed semantic taxonomy. Existing open-set panoptic benchmarks focus on individual images or views, while panoramic tracking benchmarks assume closed-set taxonomies. Consequently, these benchmarks do not evaluate whether unknown objects can be consistently segmented and tracked across synchronized multi-camera sequences. We introduce WayPOP, a panoramic open-set panoptic tracking benchmark built on real Waymo PVPS sequences augmented with diverse out-of-taxonomy 3D assets from Objaverse. Inserted objects are placed at fixed locations in the 3D scene and rendered using calibrated vehicle-mounted cameras as the ego vehicle and surrounding traffic move, providing temporally and cross-view consistent panoptic annotations and auxiliary LiDAR range observations. WayPOP contains approximately 670 curated assets instantiated as 980 unique unknown-object tracks, yielding 53,452 unknown-object mask annotations across 21,300 camera images. Our evaluation protocol defines a hierarchy of tasks from view-independent open-set panoptic segmentation to full panoramic open-set panoptic tracking, using known/unknown PQ and (w)STQ metrics. We benchmark representative open-set and open-vocabulary segmentation methods with temporal and cross-camera association. Our results reveal substantial limitations of current methods in truly open-set scenarios, highlighting robust unknown-object localization and identity association across time and views as major open challenges.

</details>

### 255. [Navigational Safety of Marine Surface Vehicles in Restricted Visibility: From Intelligent Vision to Vision-Centered Multi-Sensor Fusion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05818v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05818

**作者：** Ryan Wen Liu、Fengchuan Song、Tianxiang Wang、Guobei Peng、Youngrong Kim、Xian Zhong、Hooi-Siang Kang、Maohan Liang、Junzhi Yu、Shanliang Yao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05818v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05818v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文综述受限能见度下海上水面载具的智能视觉方法，包括船载视频稳像、视觉增强、目标检测跟踪与可航区域分割，以及以视觉为中心的红外、雷达、LiDAR、AIS 多传感器融合与相关数据集和应用。

**排除原因：** 属航海感知工程的应用综述，既无 latent 推理状态，也未讨论推理期证据筛选、停止决策或训练监督，与 LVR 主线缺乏具体可核查的借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Navigational Safety of Marine Surface Vehicles in Restricted Visibility: From Intelligent Vision to Vision-Centered Multi-Sensor Fusion

**abstract/A1：** Marine surface vehicles (MSVs) have attracted considerable attention for their commercial, scientific, and military applications. Owing to their favorable performance-to-cost ratio, shipboard cameras are widely used to detect and recognize potentially hazardous situations across diverse operational scenarios. However, visual perception is inevitably degraded by restricted conditions, including irregular motion of the imaging platform, adverse weather, and long imaging distances. To improve situational awareness and navigation capabilities, complementary sensors, such as the automatic identification system (AIS), radar, and light detection and ranging (LiDAR), etc., have become essential shipboard equipment. Multi-sensor fusion can provide richer perceptual information and help ensure navigational safety of MSVs in restricted visibility. This article reviews recent progress from intelligent vision to vision-centered multi-sensor fusion for newcomers and researchers in intelligent ship navigation and intelligent waterborne transportation system (IWTS). After introducing the generation of quality-degraded images in restricted visibility, we will review intelligent vision methods for four key issues, i.e., shipboard video stabilization, visual perception enhancement, object detection and tracking, and navigable area segmentation. We then examine vision-centered multi-sensor fusion methods, including infrared-visible image fusion, radar-vision fusion, LiDAR-vision fusion, AIS-vision fusion, and other multi-sensor fusion scenarios. Open-source datasets and safety-critical applications are also summarized and discussed. Finally, we highlight open challenges and future research directions. The collected maritime datasets are available at https://github.com/MAPGroup/Datasets and will be regularly updated.

**comment/C1：** 42 pages, 16 figures

</details>

### 256. [Autonomy and Coordination in Intelligent Manufacturing: A Recursive Autonomous Agent Framework](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05828v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05828

**作者：** Yuning Chen、Hongda Zhang、Linqiang Hu、Ziqing Zhou、Yi Liu、Zhao Huang、Ding Zhou、Haining Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05828v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05828v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从复杂适应系统视角分析智能制造中的个体自主性与集体协调，提出以目标契约、能力契约和递归组织结构组合自主智能体的 RAA 工程框架。

**排除原因：** 这是面向生产组织的多智能体工程架构，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部表示、latent 状态生成、证据使用与训练监督，也缺少可供核查的状态抽象或推理机制线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Autonomy and Coordination in Intelligent Manufacturing: A Recursive Autonomous Agent Framework

**abstract/A1：** Intelligent production requires systems to make decisions and organize work according to production goals and operating conditions. It also requires continuous adjustment through feedback. This paper analyzes intelligence in human production organizations from a complex adaptive systems (CAS) perspective. The analysis identifies two coupled mechanisms: individual autonomy and collective coordination. Individual autonomy enables actors to form local decisions using available information. Collective coordination aligns these decisions with shared goals and joint constraints. Learning and adaptation improve both mechanisms over time. A production-system model formalizes how local decision rules and coordination jointly form system decisions. On this basis, we propose the Recursive Autonomous Agent (RAA) framework, which combines goal-driven autonomous agents with a recursive organizational structure. Goal contracts define local objectives and operating boundaries, while capability contracts support the selection and composition of production units. Common interfaces allow coordinated groups to participate as units in larger organizations, connecting equipment, production lines, and factories. RAA thus provides an engineering approach to next-generation intelligent manufacturing, translating the requirements of intelligence, openness, and system-wide integration into mechanisms for autonomous and coordinated production.

</details>

### 257. [Vision Paper: Why Distributed LLM Post-Training and Decentralized Alignment Are Now Essential](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05841v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05841

**作者：** Zhanhong Jiang、Qisai Liu、Soumik Sarkar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05841v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05841v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该立场论文主张将分布式（联邦与去中心化）学习作为 LLM 后训练的一等范式，从隐私与数据主权、多元性、鲁棒性三方面论证 RLHF/DPO 集中偏好数据假设不可持续，并梳理 reference drift、偏好异质性、DP-效用权衡等挑战与研究议程。

**排除原因：** 内容聚焦偏好数据的分布式收集与对齐治理，不涉及多模态视觉推理、latent 状态生成或证据使用，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 的状态更新或监督线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Vision Paper: Why Distributed LLM Post-Training and Decentralized Alignment Are Now Essential

**abstract/A1：** This vision paper argues that distributed learning, including federated and decentralized, should become a first-class paradigm in LLM post-training, not merely a privacy add-on. Current methods like RLHF and DPO rest on an invisible assumption: preference data should be centralized. We contend this assumption is scientifically unjustified, practically unsustainable, and ethically problematic. As LLMs deploy into healthcare, law, finance, and multilingual communities, preference data originates from populations that cannot or will not centralize. We develop a three-pillar argument grounded in (1) privacy and data sovereignty, (2) pluralism and representational equity, and (3) robustness and scalability. We formalize key technical challenges unique to this intersection, reference drift, preference heterogeneity, and the DP-utility trade-off for contrastive losses, and propose concrete algorithmic directions, and sketch a research agenda. Our analysis shows distributed post-training is not only necessary but tractable, and ignoring it risks AI that is legally noncompliant, representationally narrow, and brittle at scale.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 4 figures, IEEE BigData 2026

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DualControlNet，一个受文本提示和多维条件控制的城市土地利用图与卫星影像联合生成模型，并用土地利用、空间一致性和图像质量指标评估。

**排除原因：** 属于城市规划和遥感影像生成的应用型工作，其多模态控制与生成质量评估未涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、视觉证据选取或 latent 推理过程，缺少可迁移到 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Generative AI for urban planning: synthesizing land-use maps with satellite imagery via DualControlNet

**abstract/A1：** Various land-use models characterize land-use patterns and facilitate scenario planning. However, existing models are largely predictive, forecasting transitions based on numeric data without synthesizing new land-use maps from simple text prompts. Meanwhile, generative AI in urban systems remains unimodal, weakly controllable, and evaluated mostly by generic image quality rather than domain-specific metrics. To address these gaps, we introduce DualControlNet, a generative land-use model that jointly synthesizes high-resolution land-use maps and satellite imagery, addressing multimodality, controllability, and verification challenges. Trained on over 40,000 paired tiles from Orlando and Chicago, DualControlNet is evaluated using land-use, spatial consistency, and image quality metrics. Relative to unimodal baselines, DualControlNet improves land-use diversity consistency by 62.8% and building coverage ratio consistency by 16.3%, more than doubles cross-modal spatial consistency, and triples visual quality. Scenario-based experiments demonstrate that DualControlNet supports sustainable development through multidimensional controls, increasing density and diversity, and responding to text prompts like "high-density development." Overall, this work advances land-use modeling via a multimodal, controllable, and verifiable generative framework, lowering technical barriers and enabling intuitive exploration of urban futures.

</details>

### 259. [Auditable Unlearning for LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05866v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05866

**作者：** Piyush Tiwary、Anushka Srivastava、Prathosh A. P

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05866v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05866v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出把「可审计性」作为机器遗忘的第四个评价维度，用 GapWiden 铰链损失拉开遗忘集与保留集的审计统计差距，并用推理期 GapVeil 拒绝采样强化该差距。

**排除原因：** 纯文本遗忘与隐私审计工作，不涉及视觉信息保留、latent 推理状态或证据获取，其统计分离与拒绝采样也难以对应 LVR 的状态生成或贡献分配问题，缺少实质借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Auditable Unlearning for LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Machine unlearning aims to remove a user's data from a trained model after a deletion request. Existing methods are majorly evaluated on three axes - how well they forget, how much capability they retain, and how robust they are to membership inference attacks, but not on whether the provider can offer the user any evidence that the deletion occurred. To fill this gap, we introduce auditability as a fourth axis, defined as the separation between requested (forget set) and retained (retain set) data under a statistic the provider computes. We show that across eleven unlearning methods on TOFU, MUSE and RWKU no method satisfies all four axes, with different methods falling short on different axes. To address this, we propose a two stage method that can be used on top of any unlearning method, in the the first stage we propose ${GapWiden}$ which uses a hinge loss to increase the difference between audit statistics of forget set and retain set. In the second stage we propose ${GapVeil}$, an inference time rejection-sampler which uses the base model as the drafter and re-samples based on the audit statistics of the drafted response. We observe that using our method, auditability and leakage reach oracle parity for every unlearning method, and forget performance converges to the oracle; retain performance improves for nine of eleven methods but remains short of parity. Our results show that evidence of deletion or auditability can be constructed by design, and that treating it as an explicit requirement changes the viability of existing unlearning methods.

</details>

### 260. [Feature Puzzles: Single-Image Supervision for Multi-View 3D-Aware Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05874v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05874

**作者：** Jiahao Ma、Qiang Zhang、Miaomiao Liu、David Ahmedt-Aristizabal、Chuong Nguyen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05874v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05874v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文用单图语义监督加裁剪/几何变换构造多视角一致的伪标签，并借预训练 3D 基础模型特征做前馈式三维分割；属于三维感知与分割任务方法，既无推理期 latent 状态生成，也不涉及证据获取、停止决策或 VLM 推理，未提供可核查的状态或监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Feature Puzzles: Single-Image Supervision for Multi-View 3D-Aware Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Humans can easily recognize the same objects across different viewpoints of a 3D scene, whereas teaching models such multi-view-consistent perception typically requires videos or calibrated multi-view captures, which are considerably more expensive and less diverse than large-scale monocular data.~In this paper, we introduce Feature Puzzles, a novel framework for learning multi-view-consistent segmentation using only single-image semantic segmentation supervision. Specifically, we propose a simple yet effective pipeline that constructs multi-view-consistent segmentation pseudo-labels through image cropping and geometric transformations. We further introduce a lightweight, plug-and-play network that leverages the multi-view-consistent image features learned by existing pretrained 3D foundation models and the generated pseudo-labels to perform multi-view-consistent segmentation. Importantly, our flexible framework supports various types of prompts provided from one or multiple views and can be readily extended to open-vocabulary 3D segmentation. Under this single-image supervision, our feed-forward model achieves the best mIoU on NVOS, remains competitive on SPIn-NeRF and ScanNet++ against baselines using per-scene optimization or in-domain multi-view training, and demonstrates zero-shot transfer to open-vocabulary 3D segmentation. Visual comparisons are available on our project page: https://featurepuzzles.github.io/

**comment/C1：** Feed Forward 3D Reconstruction and Spatial Understanding, Multi-view Promptable Segmentation, Instance Tracking

</details>

### 261. [MIGONet: a multiple-input graph neural operator with solver-free discrete manufactured-solution training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05876v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05876

**作者：** Muhammad Hasnain、N. K. Anand、Arun Srinivasa

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05876v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05876v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是稳态热传导的 PDE 代理建模工作，核心是多输入图神经算子与免求解的离散制造解训练数据生成，不涉及多模态推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策；其整场编码与边界条件作为算子输入的做法仅属算子条件化设计，缺乏可供核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MIGONet: a multiple-input graph neural operator with solver-free discrete manufactured-solution training

**abstract/A1：** Surrogate modeling of transport requires operators that capture the combined influence of spatially distributed sources and Dirichlet and Neumann boundary conditions, together with training datasets that can be generated at low computational cost. This work presents MIGONet, a multiple-input graph neural operator, and a discrete method of manufactured solutions (MMS) that trains it without solving a partial differential equation, using steady heat conduction as the model problem. In the discrete MMS procedure, temperature fields are prescribed a priori, and the source terms and Dirichlet or Neumann boundary data are evaluated with the same discrete operators as the reference finite-difference method (FDM), so every training sample satisfies the discrete governing equations by construction. Because the boundary data are derived from each field, a Dirichlet or Neumann condition can be assigned to every boundary segment of every sample, and the boundary specification becomes an operator input. MIGONet supplies representations of the complete source field and boundary data to every node before local message passing. On the rectangular domain, it is compared with a version without the complete-source encoder (MIGONet-local) and with the multiple-input operator network MIONet, using the same training data. MIGONet achieves mean relative temperature errors of 10.31% and 10.64% for curved-wire and Gaussian-spot heating, compared with 45.97% and 48.34% for MIONet and 72.25% and 67.82% for MIGONet-local. For 10,000 samples, discrete MMS reduces data-generation time from 486.65 s to 3.15 s relative to matched iterative FDM labeling. Separately trained MIGONet models are also evaluated on L-shaped, disc-shaped, and ring-shaped domains.

**comment/C1：** Accepted for publication in Transport Phenomena by De Gruyter Brill. 35 pages, 16 figures, 13 tables

</details>

### 262. [Chronos: Sequential Identity-Preserving Video Generation by Temporal Anchoring](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05893v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05893

**作者：** Mengqi Huang、Wenchuan Wang、Zhendong Mao、Yijing Tu、Zhike Liu、Luoni Wang、Ning Tong、Xiaoxiao Sheng、Tianyu Huang、Xuecheng Qi、Yongdong Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.MM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05893v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05893v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 Chronos 框架，通过语义提示锚定、视觉帧锚定和多维评测的候选路由，在单段人脸身份保持与多段连续动作生成中缓解身份漂移与运动抑制。

**排除原因：** 属于身份保持视频生成的应用工作，其身份漂移与分段动作问题不涉及内部推理状态、视觉证据筛选或停止决策，候选路由也只是生成质量选优，未见与 LVR 问题的具体可迁移线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Chronos: Sequential Identity-Preserving Video Generation by Temporal Anchoring

**abstract/A1：** Identity-preserving video generation aims to synthesize high-quality and visually realistic videos from textual and visual conditions while faithfully maintaining the subject's identity across diverse motions. Prior methods have extensively introduced reference-guided control signals and identity-aware conditioning to improve facial alignment from reference images. However, improving static identity alignment alone creates a fundamental tension between identity fidelity and temporal controllability, which frequently causes identity drift or motion suppression under large pose variations and multi-segment sequential actions. To address this, we propose Chronos, a framework that unifies single-segment facial identity preservation and multi-segment sequential action generation under a common temporal formulation. Specifically, Chronos integrates Semantic Prompt Anchoring to adaptively refine the user prompt, Visual Frame Anchoring to transform conditions into visually grounded keyframe anchors for visual guidance, and Metric-Guided Candidate Routing to select the highest-quality videos from heterogeneous generators via multi-dimensional evaluation. Extensive evaluations demonstrate the superiority of our approach, which achieved first place in both Track 1 and Track 2 of the ACM MM 2026 Identity-Preserving Video Generation Challenge, demonstrating its effectiveness across both facial identity preservation and sequential action generation settings.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by ACM MM 2026. 1st place in Tracks 1 and 2 of the ACM MM 2026 Identity-Preserving Video Generation Challenge

</details>

### 263. [CHARM+: Cross-Hardware Attention with Re-Merge Multistream Mechanism](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05906v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05906

**作者：** Xinhang Zhang、Boning Zhang、Chengchun Liu、Chunpu Li、Lei Sun、Weifeng Zhang、Limin Xiao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.AR、cs.DC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05906v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05906v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 CHARM+，通过分层重组长序列注意力的通信、解耦跨硬件与硬件内通信并使用多流重叠，加速分布式注意力在 NUMA 与多节点架构上的前向与反向计算。

**排除原因：** 属分布式注意力通信与并行调度层面的系统优化，不涉及视觉细节保留、latent 推理状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，材料中也无状态更新或监督机制的具体线索可供借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CHARM+: Cross-Hardware Attention with Re-Merge Multistream Mechanism

**abstract/A1：** As sequence lengths continue to grow, distributed attention based on sequence parallel (e.g., Ring-Attention) has become a mainstream solution for large language models (LLMs). Nevertheless, inherent communication dependencies limit computation-communication overlap, reducing GPU utilization. Moreover, in cross-hardware (NUMA or node) architectures, due to imbalanced communication bandwidth, frequent communication synchronization will limit overall performance. Based on these insights, we propose CHARM+, Cross-Hardware Attention with Re-Merge Multistream Mechanism. We reorganize sequential communications into several levels and achieve intra-level parallelism and computation overlap. Furthermore, we decouple cross-hardware communication from intra-hardware communication, eliminating synchronization overhead caused by bandwidth imbalance. Forward and backward algorithms were evaluated on multiple hardware architectures and consistently outperformed state-of-the-art methods. Compared to Ring-Attention, our forward and backward algorithms achieve average speedups of 1.3x and 1.7x on a dual-NUMA PCIe architecture, and 2.8x and 2.9x on a dual-node NVLink architecture, respectively.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026 (Poster)

</details>

### 264. [3D Banff Glomerulitis Scoring for Kidney Transplant Pathology: Feasibility Study for Volumetric Quantification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05925v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05925

**作者：** Yanfan Zhu、Junchao Zhu、Ruining Deng、Shilin Zhao、Yu Wang、Yaohong Wang、Yuechen Yang、Chongyu Qu、Zhengyi Lu、Juming Xiong、Mengmeng Yin、Yihan Wang、Andrew J. Rauch、Haichun Yang、Yuankai Huo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05925v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05925v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 3D Banff 病变评分框架，对肾移植活检连续切片进行配准、跨深度重建肾小球身份并做三维炎症细胞计数，比较三种评分策略与传统 2D 评分的一致性及与临床生物标志物的相关性。

**排除原因：** 属病理切片三维量化与临床可行性研究，虽涉及视觉证据的聚合，但不涉及多模态推理、内部表示或计算决策，缺少可供 LVR 借鉴的状态更新或证据筛选线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** 3D Banff Glomerulitis Scoring for Kidney Transplant Pathology: Feasibility Study for Volumetric Quantification

**abstract/A1：** Quantifying glomerular inflammation in kidney-transplant biopsies is traditionally performed on single whole-slide sections using the Banff "most-severe section" rule, despite the inherently three-dimensional nature of lesion distribution. This 2D based assessment might lead to instability when inflammation varies across slices. This paper reports a feasibility study examining the technical feasibility and clinical relevance of extending Banff scoring into three dimensions. To this end, we propose a 3D Banff lesion-scoring framework that aligns serial sections, reconstructs glomerular identities across depth, and integrates inflammatory-cell counts in 3D under three scoring strategies: Compatible, Volumetric, and Deduplicated Volumetric. In the experiments, glomerulitis (g-scores) was used as an example Banff metric and applied to multi-section renal allograft biopsies. Our findings indicate that 3D Banff g-scores are more consistent across sections and, under the semi-automatic setting, the Volumetric strategy correlates more strongly with clinical biomarkers than traditional 2D scores. These results show that 3D volumetric quantification offers promising added value, underscoring the potential benefit of 3D-aware Banff scoring for kidney transplant pathology.

</details>

### 265. [Deep Meets Classical: A CPU-Efficient Decision-Fusion Model for Automatic Modulation Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05937v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05937

**作者：** Zhiyi Yin、Zhizhao Wen、Ruoxin Zhang、Chao Wang、Puyang Xu、Yifan Jiang、Yusi Gong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05937v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05937v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究无线信号自动调制分类，提出将小型残差 CNN 与基于高阶累积量的梯度提升树分类器按 SNR 互补性进行后验概率决策融合的轻量 CPU 方案。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于射频信号分类与模型融合，不涉及视觉或多模态推理、latent 状态生成与使用、证据筛选或停止决策，其逻辑回归元融合也只是分类后验拼接，难以为 LVR 提供具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Deep Meets Classical: A CPU-Efficient Decision-Fusion Model for Automatic Modulation Classification

**abstract/A1：** Automatic modulation classification (AMC) underpins spectrum sensing and non-cooperative signal identification in cognitive radio, yet the two standard CPU-feasible approaches to it, a compact convolutional neural network (CNN) on raw in-phase and quadrature (I/Q) samples and a classical higher-order-cumulant feature classifier, are rarely compared against each other conditioned on signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). We show that they are, in fact, complementary: the CNN is markedly more robust at low SNR, where hand-crafted statistics collapse toward chance, while the cumulant classifier is more accurate at high SNR, where higher-order statistics cleanly separate dense constellations. We exploit this complementarity with FuseAMC, a lightweight decision-fusion model that trains a logistic-regression meta-combiner over the concatenated class-posteriors of an approximately 198k-parameter residual CNN and a gradient-boosted-tree cumulant classifier, on a validation split disjoint from test. On RadioML 2016.10a (5 seeds, 44k-frame held-out test set), FuseAMC reaches 55.8% overall accuracy, improving on the best cumulant-feature baseline by 5.1 points and on the CNN alone by 3.2 points (paired-bootstrap 95% confidence-interval lower bounds of 5.0 and 3.1 points, both positive), while matching the best classical baseline in its own strong high-SNR regime and the CNN in its strong low-SNR regime. With about 0.2 million parameters, a model footprint of a few megabytes, and roughly 3 MFLOPs per frame, the whole pipeline trains and runs on one CPU core, targeting general-purpose and embedded processors without hardware acceleration.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables. Accepted at the 2026 6th International Conference on Artificial Intelligence, Automation and High Performance Computing (AIAHPC)

</details>

### 266. [BAM: Bivariate Additive Models for Interpretable Tabular Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05940v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05940

**作者：** Atsushi Koike

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05940v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05940v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 BAM（双变量加性模型），将 GA²M 中的成对形状函数约束为非对称二元高斯形式，使每个成分可解释为单峰等高线图，并在训练中自动选择特征对，在六个表格基准上评估精度与可审计性之间的权衡。

**排除原因：** 工作属于表格数据的可解释加性建模，不涉及视觉或多模态推理、latent 状态生成与使用、证据获取或停止决策，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 的具体方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BAM: Bivariate Additive Models for Interpretable Tabular Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Generalized Additive Models with pairwise interactions (GA$^2$Ms) are regarded as glass-box models, since each pairwise shape function can be visualized as a contour plot. In practice, the shape functions learned by methods such as EBM or NODE-GAM are so complex (irregular contours and sharp local fluctuations) that a practitioner cannot meaningfully interpret them or check them against domain knowledge. Auditability in this stronger sense requires substantially simpler shape functions. We argue that such simplification can match the accuracy of standard GAMs if the right feature pairs are captured. We propose Bivariate Additive Models (BAMs), a class of GA$^2$Ms in which every pairwise shape function is constrained to a simple parametric form, instantiated as an asymmetric bivariate Gaussian. Each component is visualizable as a unimodal contour map, and its feature pair is selected automatically during training. Experiments on six tabular benchmarks show that BAM attains predictive accuracy comparable to standard GAMs and approaches that of GA$^2$Ms. We also show the trade-off between accuracy and the number of components to inspect.

</details>

### 267. [From Waste to Discovery: Recycling High-Scored Unexposed Candidates in Taobao Feed Recommendation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05997v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.05997

**作者：** Ge Fan、Kai Meng、Huiping Chu、Yuning Jiang、Han Zhu、Bo Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05997v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.05997v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是淘宝推荐系统的召回工程实践，通过回收高分未曝光候选缓解探索-利用困境，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据筛选或 latent 生成，也没有可供核查的方法迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Waste to Discovery: Recycling High-Scored Unexposed Candidates in Taobao Feed Recommendation

**abstract/A1：** Helping users discover new interests is vital to the long-term health of industrial recommender systems, yet the usual remedy, boosting exposure of under-explored (discovery) content, often backfires in production. We report URED (Unexposed Recycling for Efficient Discovery), a training-free recall-stage system serving Taobao feed recommendation at Alibaba for over six months to tens of millions of daily active users. It rests on a production-validated insight: naive boosting fails because it injects cold, information-poor candidates that rankers systematically over-estimate, buying no discovery gain while risking engagement. Items scored highly but never exposed (signals industrial pipelines routinely discard) form a pool that is both relevant and reliably predictable. Over a 30-day horizon URED aggregates them and re-injects at recall time through a lightweight pipeline spanning offline storage, distributed aggregation, and low-latency online serving, adding under 1% latency via a single O(1) lookup per request with no model retraining. Online A/B tests show +3.47% discovered categories and +8.47% content richness with engagement preserved: completion video views rise significantly (+2.62%) and total views move positive (+0.36%, not significant). Ablations confirm both design pillars are necessary: a one-day window collapses the discovery gain to +0.55% (not significant), and random injection without score filtering yields no gain while trending negative on engagement (-0.17%). Across production recall channels, URED reaches 13.9x the platform-average discovery rate (6.3x the strongest nearline baseline) at engagement quality on par with the platform average. We share deployment details and lessons for practitioners facing the exploration-exploitation dilemma under tight resource constraints.

</details>

### 268. [Intra-Architecture Source Attribution for Stable Diffusion: A New Task with Benchmark Dataset and Baseline](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06000v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06000

**作者：** Ashutosh Dayal、Mallika Garg、Debashis Sen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06000v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06000v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出「IntrAA-SD」任务，即区分同一 SDXL 架构下仅经不同微调得到的生成器，并构建 14 万张图像的基准数据集，给出结合 CLIP 风格门控与 LoRA 微调 DINOv2 特征的 MoE 基线。

**排除原因：** 该工作是生成图像来源取证与数据集基准，不涉及内部推理状态、视觉证据获取、停止决策或 VLM 推理训练；其风格门控与指纹特征也无明确可迁移到 LVR 的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Intra-Architecture Source Attribution for Stable Diffusion: A New Task with Benchmark Dataset and Baseline

**abstract/A1：** Designing systems that attribute a generated image to its source model has lately become a major area of AI research. Most existing datasets and systems related to image source attribution are targeted towards the detection of architectural signatures of the source models. Distinct from this, focusing on stable diffusion, we introduce a novel task, IntrAA-SD, Intra-Architecture source Attribution for Stable Diffusion, where the source models to be differentiated share the same architectural backbone and differ only due to fine-tuning. The proposed task aligns with the emergence of fine-tuned generators having the same architecture, like Juggernaut XL and RealVis XL, which are fine-tuned versions of Stable Diffusion XL (SDXL). We provide a first-of-its-kind $140$K-image benchmark dataset containing images generated from different fine-tunes of SDXL, along with in-domain and out-of-distribution prompts. We also provide a baseline framework for IntrAA-SD, which leverages a style-conditioned Mixture-of-Experts network. It uses a frozen CLIP backbone to capture the image style and gate the MoE, which processes fine-tuned trace features from a high-resolution LoRA-adapted DINOv2 network. The fine-tuned models used, the prompting strategies considered, and the formation details employed in the novel dataset are elaborated. Our baseline intra-architecture source attribution method is evaluated in terms of attribution accuracy on our dataset, and ablation studies are also performed. Code and dataset is available at https://github.com/mallikagarg/IntrAA-SD.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at IEEE/CVF Winter Conference on Applications of Computer Vision(WACV 2027)

</details>

### 269. [Practical Estimation of the Bayes Optimal Fairness-Accuracy Tradeoff with Soft Labels](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06027v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06027

**作者：** Mohit Sharma、Okan Koç、Amit Deshpande、Takashi Ishida、Gang Niu、Rajiv Ratn Shah、Masashi Sugiyama

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06027v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06027v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究带公平性约束的分类问题，给出公平贝叶斯误差的显式表达，并用软标签与群体信息构造无实例依赖的一致估计量，同时给出偏差与样本复杂度界。

**排除原因：** 内容属于公平性—准确率权衡的理论估计，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据使用或停止决策，也无可供迁移的 latent 推理方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Practical Estimation of the Bayes Optimal Fairness-Accuracy Tradeoff with Soft Labels

**abstract/A1：** There is a fundamental limit to the best prediction error of any model for a given data distribution. In classification, the Bayes error quantifies this limit and serves as both a benchmark for trained models and a criterion to detect overfitting. The state-of-the-art Bayes error estimation techniques for binary classification use only soft labels (positive class probabilities) and require no input features or auxiliary training. We extend such notions to constrained classification problems, such as fairness. Fair classification is an important case of constrained or multi-objective classification. It requires a model not only to be accurate but also to yield fair outcomes (equal or near-equal true positive rates) across different demographic groups (e.g., race and gender). This leads to an inherent fairness-accuracy tradeoff or a Pareto frontier that is used to compare fair classifiers against each other. The focus of our work is to estimate the fundamental limit for this fairness-accuracy tradeoff. We derive an explicit expression for the fair Bayes error rate (i.e., best achievable error rate subject to given fairness constraints), and construct consistent, instance-free estimators for it using only soft labels and group information. We provably bound the bias and the sample complexity of our estimators. Empirically, our method produces a robust, low-variance estimate of the optimal fairness-accuracy tradeoff curve, even when the soft labels are noisy and derived from a classification model on hard labels.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at Neurips 2026

</details>

### 270. [UniRestorer++: Scalable Universal Image Restoration with Multi-Granularity Degradation Modeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06042v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06042

**作者：** Jingbo Lin、Zhilu Zhang、Fan Li、Renjing Pei、Wenbo Li、Hang Xu、Hongzhi Zhang、Wangmeng Zuo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06042v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06042v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 UniRestorer++，通过多粒度退化建模、退化分组专家与基于退化/粒度估计的路由，实现可扩展的通用图像复原，并构建 CDD-50 基准。

**排除原因：** 研究的是低层图像复原中的专家路由与容量扩展，虽涉及估计驱动的层级路由，但不考察多模态推理状态表示、证据使用或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系，也无具体可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** UniRestorer++: Scalable Universal Image Restoration with Multi-Granularity Degradation Modeling

**abstract/A1：** Universal image restoration aims to handle diverse degradations within a single model, but a reliable scaling strategy remains underexplored. Increasing backbone depth and width can lead to performance saturation, while degradation-aware specialization depends on accurate degradation estimation and is vulnerable to errors on out-of-distribution and real-world inputs. We propose UniRestorer++, a scalable framework based on multi-granularity degradation modeling. It progressively refines degradation groups and introduces group-specific experts, coupling capacity growth with specialization. Degradation-estimation-based routing selects a root-to-leaf path, while granularity-estimation-based routing chooses a level along that path to balance specialization and robustness. Spatially dense degradation representation maps further enable region-aware routing for spatially non-uniform corruptions. We develop CDD-50, a benchmark covering 50 degradation categories from single to penta-degradation. Experiments show approximately log-linear PSNR trends over the evaluated model-size range and stronger scaling gains on out-of-distribution and real-world data than vanilla MoE scaling. UniRestorer++ achieves state-of-the-art performance across benchmarks and demonstrates zero-shot generalization and few-shot transfer on real-world images. Code: https://github.com/mrluin/UniRestorer\_plusplus

</details>

### 271. [DeepfakeGenome: Toward Next-Generation Deepfake Attribution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06046v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06046

**作者：** Shunli Wang、Xinyu Zhou、Yandan Zhao、Wenbin Liang、Taiping Yao、Ke-Yue Zhang、Zhiyuan Yan、Shouhong Ding、Lizhuang Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06046v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06046v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建包含 100 种人脸伪造算法、约 200 万张图像的 deepfake 检测与归因基准，并设计 4 种评估协议（含基于检索的归因范式）以考察归因任务性能上限。

**排除原因：** 属于图像取证中的真伪分类与生成源归因，不涉及 VLM 内部视觉状态、latent 推理生成或证据筛选决策，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DeepfakeGenome: Toward Next-Generation Deepfake Attribution

**abstract/A1：** In recent years, AIGC technologies are capable of generating hyper-realistic forged facial images, posing severe threats to facial security safety. To tackle these challenges, early Deepfake research primarily centered on real-fake classification. Recently, growing efforts have been devoted to the Deepfake Attribution (DFA) of generated content. Nevertheless, existing research on Deepfake detection and attribution faces saturated performance in binary classification, limited diversity in datasets and algorithms, and imperfect evaluation protocols, which severely impede practical application. To address these limitations, we propose a comprehensive deepfake detection and attribution benchmark named DeepfakeGenome (DFG). It contains 100 facial forgery algorithms and 2M images in total, achieving 4x to 100x larger than prior DFA benchmarks. We further design 4 protocols for practical evaluation, including a novel retrieval-based attribution paradigm. Unlike previous open-set evaluation metrics, the proposed retrieval metrics are more aligned with the real-world active defense situation of blacklist registration mechanisms. Based on these elaborate designs, we investigate the performance ceiling of deepfake attribution task. Over 2k experimental evaluations are conducted, and 10 insightful findings are derived. We believe this work can provide new insights into the DFA research field. The Code and Benchmark of DFG are avaliable on https://huggingface.co/spaces/DeepfakeGenome/DFG\_Page.

**comment/C1：** 28 Pages

</details>

### 272. [Reglos: A Self-Evolving Personal Memory Agent with Recursive Self-Improvement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06049v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06049

**作者：** Sen Yang、Jiaduo Lin、Zehong Huang、Wen Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06049v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06049v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** Reglos 是一个免训练的个人记忆智能体：用追加式用户记忆记录偏好与事实的更新，并让编码智能体根据失败轨迹改写上下文构建流程（harness），再由另一模型家族的批评者筛查是否在刷榜。

**排除原因：** 论文面向个性化对话记忆与 agent 工程自改进，其状态是用户事实而非推理期 latent 状态，不涉及视觉证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，自查机制也仅针对基准刷分，暂无可供核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reglos: A Self-Evolving Personal Memory Agent with Recursive Self-Improvement

**abstract/A1：** Personal agents draw on past conversations to tailor their responses to each user. Their memory systems store and update what each user says, and coding agents now also rewrite the harness that turns this memory into the model's context. Such patches risk overfitting, because one tested only on the failures it targets can fix those cases and help nowhere else. We present Reglos, a training-free personal memory agent that tests whether harness changes help beyond the failures they were written to fix. Its self-evolving memory adds to each user's append-only record with every conversation, as new preferences supersede old ones and facts can be forgotten. Its harness improves through recursive self-improvement, in which a coding agent proposes patches from failure traces and a critic from a second model family screens each one for benchmark gaming. After the harness is frozen, a fixed evaluator decides which further patches to accept. With the same backbone behind every system, Reglos outperforms state-of-the-art memory systems such as Mem0, MemOS and EverOS, reaching 61.3% and 84.0% on PersonaMem-v2 and LongMemEval-S with Qwen3.8-27B, and 57.5% and 87.2% with GPT-5.6 Luna.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 7 figures, 8 tables. Website: https://reglos.ai

</details>

### 273. [Alleviating Limited Effectiveness and Multi-Class Collapse in Deep Graph-level Clustering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06052v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06052

**作者：** Xiaoyang Lin、Renchi Yang、Yuqi Chen、Xin Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06052v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06052v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 S^3GLC 图级聚类框架，用谱签名刻画全局拓扑并结合局部空间表示，配合基于最优传输的平衡聚类目标以缓解多类塌缩。

**排除原因：** 主题为图级无监督聚类与类别塌缩，不涉及视觉语言推理、latent 状态生成或证据获取，也无可迁移至 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Alleviating Limited Effectiveness and Multi-Class Collapse in Deep Graph-level Clustering

**abstract/A1：** Given a collection of $N$ graphs, graph-level clustering (GLC) seeks to partition the $N$ graphs into semantically meaningful groups, which serves as a fundamental task in analyzing graph-structured data. While Graph Isomorphism Networks (GINs) have become the de facto encoders for GLC, they still fail to distinguish graphs due to their neglect of global topology. On top of that, existing deep clustering frameworks suffer from severe "clustering collapse" issues, particularly in imbalanced multi-class scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose S$^3$GLC, a novel framework that integrates global topological characteristics with local neighborhood features for balanced GLC. Theoretically, we leverage the Hoffman-Wielandt inequality to construct Spectral Signature as a rigorous lower bound for graph edit distance (GED), providing a computationally efficient proxy for global topological features. We additionally propose a joint manifold-preserving sparse regression module to fuse Spectral Signature with Spatial Representation, eliminating noise while preserving the complementary information. Furthermore, to mitigate clustering collapse, we introduce a balanced clustering objective based on optimal transport, harnessing the Sinkhorn-Knopp normalization to enforce equipartition constraints on cluster assignments. Extensive experiments on 10 real-world datasets demonstrate that S$^3$GLC significantly outperforms 15 state-of-the-art baselines, achieving remarkable improvements on benchmark datasets such as REDDIT-12K and OGBG-PPA.

</details>

### 274. [TouchéWorld tech report : Towards Online Unified Camera--Hand Exploration in Generative Worlds](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06061v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06061

**作者：** Jiaao Yu、Yanjian Zhang、Haoyang Jiang、Guillaume Wisniewski、Nadi Tomeh、Shijie Jia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06061v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06061v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作构建了一个可接收连续相机与手部动作控制的视频世界模型，并配套在线分块生成与评测基准。

**排除原因：** 论文聚焦生成式世界模型的手势—相机交互与视频生成，未涉及视觉推理中的内部表征、证据获取或停止决策，在线分块生成也不构成对 latent 状态更新的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TouchéWorld tech report : Towards Online Unified Camera--Hand Exploration in Generative Worlds

**abstract/A1：** Recent video world models allow users to explore generated environments through camera navigation. Continuous hand motion provides a fine-grained action channel for interacting with the people and objects encountered during exploration. We present TouchéWorld, a framework that integrates gesture-driven interaction into a camera-controlled video world model. Given an initial image and a scene-description prompt, the model generates video conditioned on temporally aligned camera and hand controls. We construct joint camera--hand training data from heterogeneous egocentric videos through annotation completion, consistency-based filtering, and temporal alignment. Building on a pretrained camera-controlled world model, we combine spatial hand-skeleton conditioning with numerical hand-motion features through parameter-efficient adaptation. Causal adaptation and few-step distillation support chunk-level online generation, allowing users to update their controls based on generated visual feedback. We further introduce TouchéBench to evaluate hand-motion following, camera--hand coordination, online interaction, and object response. Together, these components establish a framework for studying continuous exploration and hand-driven interaction within generative worlds.

</details>

### 275. [UniCompress: From Multi-LoRA Experts to One Compact Model for Edge Intelligence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06075v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06075

**作者：** Mingkang Liu、Peipei Jiao、Weiming Meng、Tianyuan Liu、Lichen Pang、Junyan Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06075v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06075v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 UniCompress，将多任务 LoRA 专家整合为一个无适配器的 INT2 紧凑 VLM，并用 GPTQ 初始化与教师引导的量化恢复来压缩视觉编码器和语言主干，面向边缘具身部署。

**排除原因：** 工作重心是多任务 VLM 的存储压缩与部署，未涉及推理期 latent 状态生成、视觉证据使用或停止决策，也未提供与 LVR 表示或训练监督相关的具体方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** UniCompress: From Multi-LoRA Experts to One Compact Model for Edge Intelligence

**abstract/A1：** Embodied and edge systems increasingly rely on vision-language models (VLMs) to support heterogeneous skills, yet deploying a separate task adapter for each skill while retaining a high-precision backbone creates substantial memory and management costs. We present UniCompress, a deployment-oriented framework that converts multi-task expertise into one compact, adapter-free model. Starting from a consolidated BF16 Qwen3-VL-4B teacher, UniCompress constructs an asymmetric INT2 student using GPTQ initialization and teacher-guided quantization recovery. The approach jointly quantizes and recovers the projection weights in the vision encoder and language backbone, enabling aggressive compression of the complete vision-language model while preserving its multi-task behavior. We evaluate UniCompress on four heterogeneous embodied tasks: posture recognition, household-safety understanding, multimodal dialogue routing, and mobile-GUI action prediction. UniCompress reduces the approximate weight storage from 8.9 GB in BF16 to 2.1 GB in INT2, corresponding to a reduction of approximately 76%. It improves the INT2 macro task score by 34.3 percentage points over GPTQ-INT2 while remaining within 0.9 points of the consolidated BF16 teacher. These results demonstrate the potential of teacher-guided joint vision-language INT2 recovery for deploying a single multi-skill VLM on resource-constrained embodied platforms.

</details>

### 276. [Catastrophic Overfitting is Weight Drift the Training Loss Cannot See](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06082v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06082

**作者：** Chao Pan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06082v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06082v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究快速对抗训练中的灾难性过拟合（CO），指出其本质是训练损失几乎不可见的权重漂移，并提出在验证鲁棒性下降时把权重回滚到 EMA 的防护做法。

**排除原因：** 内容是图像分类器的对抗鲁棒训练与优化动力学，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据使用或推理期决策，EMA 回滚也只是通用训练稳定性技巧，缺乏可供 LVR 核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Catastrophic Overfitting is Weight Drift the Training Loss Cannot See

**abstract/A1：** Fast adversarial training (FAT) makes robust training affordable by replacing the multi-step attack with a single-step one, but it suffers from catastrophic overfitting (CO): partway through training, robustness to multi-step attacks collapses to near zero while the single-step attack used for training keeps being repelled. We show that CO is a quiet escape of the weights. Across the event the network's clean behavior quietly improves, and moving from the weights just before CO to those just after it changes the training objective only marginally while destroying most of the robustness. The training objective is therefore nearly blind to the displacement it causes, so it exerts no force to undo it. The exponential moving average (EMA) of the weights, which FAT protocols already maintain, does not accumulate this drift and stays robust through the entire event. This observation suggests a nearly free fix: when validation robustness drops, reset the weights to their EMA. We build this guard on top of a coupling-based base method designed so that its average stays inside the robust region. The resulting training removes CO in every one of our runs and attains the strongest AutoAttack accuracy among fast methods on all three benchmark datasets, including a clear margin on CIFAR-100, where it also clears the multi-step reference at roughly half its cost. Code is available at https://github.com/fzjcdt/co-weight-drift.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages (including appendix), 7 figures, 6 tables. Code available at https://github.com/fzjcdt/co-weight-drift

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06089

**作者：** Kento Tokura、Ryosuke Korekata、Daichi Yashima、Kei Katsumata、Kanon Amemiya、Miyu Goko、Komei Sugiura

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06089v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06089v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 STARE 方法，面向服务机器人依据包含视觉特征与场景文字的自由指令检索日常物体，用 Crosslingual Visual Prompt 增强场景文字理解并建模指令实体与物体关系，并构建 GoGetIt、TextCaps-test 两个基准。

**排除原因：** 属于机器人视觉语言检索任务，主要解决跨模态匹配与场景文字识别，未涉及 latent 推理状态、证据筛选、停止决策或训练监督机制，缺乏可供核查的 LVR 迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** STARE: Scene Text-Aware multimodal Retrieval for Everyday objects

**abstract/A1：** In this study, we focus on service robots that retrieve and transport everyday objects based on free-form instructions that may refer to visual features and scene text. This is challenging because it requires a multimodal understanding of the visual features and spatial relationships of objects, as well as scene text embedded in the environment. To address this, we propose Scene Text-Aware multimodal Retrieval for Everyday objects (STARE), which leverages visual and textual cues in response to free-form instructions. STARE introduces Crosslingual Visual Prompt to enhance scene text interpretation, and models the complex relationships between named entities in the instructions and their corresponding objects. We evaluate STARE using two novel benchmarks, GoGetIt and TextCaps-test, both of which consist of real-world indoor/outdoor images and instructions involving object manipulation and navigation. The results show that STARE outperforms all baseline methods on standard multimodal retrieval metrics. Furthermore, in physical experiments conducted under a zero-shot transfer setting, STARE achieves an overall success rate of 80%. Our project page and code are provided in the Supplementary Materials.

**comment/C1：** ACCV 2026 MM4SciReal Workshop

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06096

**作者：** Eleftheria Tetoula-Tsonga、George Arvanitakis、Theodoros Giannakas

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06096v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06096v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文用高光谱像素级自监督编码器加注意力回归头预测标本元素组成，属遥感/地质回归任务，不涉及推理状态生成、视觉证据筛选或停止决策；其“多样本表示聚合到单一标签”的做法与 LVR 关注的问题仅有泛化层面的相似，缺乏可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Many Pixels, One Composition: Self-Supervised Spectra for Specimen-Level Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Can hyperspectral (HS) imaging be used to predict the elemental composition of physical specimens found on the Earth's surface? We approach this problem as a supervised regression task, using an open dataset of specimens, each comprising a HS cube and elemental characterization. In particular, we train an encoder in a self-supervised fashion that operates on pixel-level; then freeze the encoder and train a lightweight attention-based regression head in a supervised manner, by pairing many representations of a given specimen with its corresponding elemental label. In test specimens, our approach significantly outperforms traditional spectral matching and unmixing baselines. It also outperforms supervised learning models based on the mean spectrum (of similar model capacity), while full model fine-tuning achieves the strongest performance, at substantially higher model capacity.

</details>

### 279. [Rohbau3D-Semantics: Dense Semantic and Instance Labels for Point Clouds of Indoor Shell Construction Scenes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06180v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06180

**作者：** Lukas Rauch、Thomas Braml

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06180v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06180v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作发布 Rohbau3D-Semantics 数据集，为 14 个工地、504 个激光扫描点云场景补充 17 类建筑构件的密集语义与实例标注，并提供全景/立方图派生表示、基准划分与语义分割基线。

**排除原因：** 这是面向建筑工地 3D 场景理解的标注数据集，未涉及 VLM 推理、latent 状态生成、证据获取或停止决策，缺少与本主线的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rohbau3D-Semantics: Dense Semantic and Instance Labels for Point Clouds of Indoor Shell Construction Scenes

**abstract/A1：** We present the Rohbau3D-Semantics dataset, which adds dense semantic and instance-level labels to laser scans of the previously released Rohbau3D domain data from shell construction sites. The dataset comprises 504 point cloud scenes from 14 survey sites, each captured with a terrestrial laser scanner and stored in a scanner-centered local coordinate system. Unlike point clouds sampled from reconstructed meshes or synthetic building models, the data preserve the characteristic properties of real laser scanning, including non-uniform sampling, measurement artifacts, reflections, and fine geometric detail. Combined with manual labels, expert oversight during pointwise annotation, and full-scene review, this yields a high-quality benchmark for semantic scene understanding in complex built environments. The dataset includes 17 class labels of building components and one background class, along with instance annotations for object-level analysis. In addition to the 3D point clouds, the release also provides scripts for deriving geometrically aligned panoramic and cube-map representations of color, LiDAR reflection intensity, depth, surface normals, semantic labels, and instance labels from the 3D arrays. We describe the acquisition pipeline, annotation protocol, quality-control process, and panorama-generation procedure, and provide benchmark splits and baseline experiments for semantic segmentation. By combining precise terrestrial laser scans, dense annotations, and a wide variety of shell-construction scenes, Rohbau3D-Semantics bridges the gap among synthetic indoor datasets, mesh-derived benchmarks, and smaller construction-specific resources. The dataset is intended to support research on 3D scene understanding, construction-site analytics, and multimodal scene analysis in the built environment. Access and download the full dataset via https://github.com/RauchLukas/rohbau3d

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the IEEE/CVF Winter Conference on Applications of Computer Vision (WACV 2027)

</details>

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出「灾难性表现」的形式化定义，用「基线价值」与「正交价值」两个参照值刻画 AI 代理撤销人类既有收益的情形，属于对齐目标设定的理论研究。

**排除原因：** 全文围绕价值目标与灾难性风险的定义，未涉及视觉细节表示、latent 推理状态生成、证据使用或停止决策，也没有可供迁移的具体方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Definition of Catastrophic Performance

**abstract/A1：** A central challenge in AI is specifying objectives that align with human preferences. While existing theoretical results elucidate how misspecification impacts performance, they typically do not distinguish between merely suboptimal and catastrophic outcomes. To enable rigorous analysis of catastrophic risk from AI misalignment, we introduce a formal definition of catastrophic performance. Our definition is based on two reference values. The first, baseline value, captures the value attained if no agent is deployed. The second, orthogonal value, captures the value of a trajectory that is unrelated to human preferences, in a sense we describe. Orthogonal value is typically far worse than baseline value. We interpret these reference values through examples in which past human activity has altered the state of the environment to human benefit. Starting at the resulting state, an AI agent is deployed with the goal of accelerating further progress. Catastrophic performance represents this agent's undoing of benefits afforded by human activity.

</details>

### 281. [FedXCrop: Privacy-Preserving Crop Disease Detection via Federated Learning with Post-Aggregation Explainability under Non-IID Farm Distributions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06221v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06221

**作者：** Kriti Saini、Manikandan P

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06221v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06221v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** FedXCrop 构建联邦学习作物病害分类流程，在非 IID 客户端分布下比较 FedAvg 与 FedProx，并用 Grad-CAM、SmoothGrad 定量评估聚合全局模型的解释质量。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于农业图像分类的隐私保护训练与归因可视化，不涉及 VLM 推理、latent 状态生成或证据使用，Grad-CAM 解释也未触及内部推理状态，缺乏可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FedXCrop: Privacy-Preserving Crop Disease Detection via Federated Learning with Post-Aggregation Explainability under Non-IID Farm Distributions

**abstract/A1：** Deep learning can classify crop diseases accurately from benchmark leaf images, but centralized training requires farms to share raw data, and predictions without explanations offer limited support for treatment decisions. We present FedXCrop, a federated crop disease classification pipeline that quantitatively evaluates explanations of the aggregated global model. A MobileNetV2 model trained centrally on PlantVillage achieved 99.38% test accuracy (standard deviation 0.05 over three seeds) on a fixed test set of 5,428 images across 38 classes. Federated models initialized with ImageNet weights were trained for 30 rounds across five clients. FedAvg achieved 98.81% accuracy with identically distributed clients and 98.81%, 98.59%, and 97.78% under Dirichlet label skew (alpha = 1, 0.5, and 0.1, respectively). FedProx was not significantly different from FedAvg at any heterogeneity level (McNemar p &gt; 0.3). Grad-CAM and SmoothGrad explanations were evaluated on 380 test images. Both attributed more energy to the leaf than chance (0.70-0.78 versus 0.60), while Grad-CAM maps from federated and centralized models showed Spearman correlations of 0.74-0.76. Agreement decreased as heterogeneity increased, lesion localization was only modestly above chance, and a model-randomization test indicated that SmoothGrad maps depended substantially on image structure rather than learned parameters. In this setting, label skew reduced federated accuracy by 0.6-1.6 percentage points relative to centralized training. FedAvg largely preserved Grad-CAM behavior, while FedProx provided no measurable benefit.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages. Includes 38-class crop disease classification experiments, federated learning evaluation under non-IID client distributions, and quantitative explainability analysis using Grad-CAM and SmoothGrad. Code and experimental results: https://github.com/Kriti2709/fedxcrp

</details>

### 282. [Homology Features for X-Ray Hyperspectral Images Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06230v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06230

**作者：** Fethi Harkat、Guillaume Gey、Valérie Perrier、Kévin Polisano

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06230v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06230v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 论文研究 XRT 高光谱图像的像素级分割，用 UMAP 投影上的持续同调拓扑特征与 CNN/GNN 特征融合提升分类精度；不涉及 VLM 推理的 latent 状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，拓扑描述符作用于输入数据流形而非推理内部表示，缺少可供核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Homology Features for X-Ray Hyperspectral Images Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Characterizing Multi-Energy X-ray Transmission (XRT) hyperspectral images presents significant challenges for traditional classification frameworks due to high-dimensional redundancy and non-linear spectral overlaps. Although manifold learning techniques such as UMAP enhance spectral representation by preserving non-linear correlations, they do not explicitly utilize the geometric structure of the embedded manifold for segmentation. This study introduces a hybrid architecture that integrates topological features derived from persistent homology with convolutional and graph neural networks. The approach computes Vietoris-Rips persistent homology on UMAP-projected superpixel point clouds to capture H 1 cyclic structures that encode spectral heterogeneity. These topological descriptors are combined with pixel-level CNN features through a broadcasting mechanism that propagates superpixel-level graph representations to full spatial resolution. Experimental results on XRT multi-energy imagery demonstrate that the proposed hybrid model significantly outperforms both CNN-only and superpixel-pooling fusion baselines, suggesting that topological features provide complementary discriminative information to local spectralspatial descriptors.

</details>

### 283. [NextGAN: Towards Simple and Scalable GAN Training for Image and Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06270v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06270

**作者：** Xinhao Lou、Dongye Xie、Peng Sun、Chenyi Zhuang、Kaicheng Yu、Tao Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06270v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06270v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 NEXTGAN，通过在冻结表示空间中回归 critic 的输入梯度到参考特征与生成特征之间的位移，实现无需辅助损失的一步或少步图像与视频生成对抗训练。

**排除原因：** 论文关注生成质量与采样步数的对抗训练加速，不涉及推理期 latent 状态、视觉证据获取、停止决策或推理轨迹监督，缺乏可供 LVR 核查的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NextGAN: Towards Simple and Scalable GAN Training for Image and Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Adversarial training enables one- and few-step image and video generation, but conventional adversarial losses supervise critic values without directly specifying the gradients used to update the generator. We present NEXTGAN, which trains a scalar critic by regressing its input gradient onto displacements between independently paired reference and generated features at interpolated locations in frozen representation spaces. For a fixed generator, this regression is equivalent to an adversarial objective with a gradient-energy penalty, while the generator retains the standard potential-based GAN update. The same objective adapts pre-trained image and video generators with up to 14B parameters, without auxiliary losses, online teacher scores, or trajectories. On ImageNet $256 \\times 256$, NEXTGAN achieves one-step FIDs of 2.87 with pixel-space JiT-B and 1.54 with latent-space DiT-XL/1; its adversarial form further reaches 1.36. For text-to-video generation, a single trained Wan2.1-1.3B model supports one-, two-, and four-step sampling, achieving the highest VBench total scores among the compared 1.3B methods at one and two steps. At two steps, NEXTGAN scores 83.13, exceeding rCM and AnyFlow by 1.25 and 0.47 points, respectively. Code is available at https://github.com/LINs-lab/NextGAN.git.

</details>

### 284. [Quo Vadis, Driving Scene Reconstruction? Occlusion-Aware Gaussian Densification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06273v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06273

**作者：** Dung Do Tien、Kien Nguyen Do Trung、Kiet Dang Vu、Trung Thai Tran、Duc Dung Nguyen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06273v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06273v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** OccSplat 是一个前馈 3D Gaussian Splatting 框架，用高斯遮挡映射定位缺乏支撑的遮挡区域，并为这些区域生成新的 Gaussian primitives，以补全驾驶场景几何、改善新视角合成。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向驾驶场景三维重建与渲染质量，在 3D 表示层面补全被遮挡几何，不涉及 VLM 的内部推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线的状态生成、使用和评价缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Quo Vadis, Driving Scene Reconstruction? Occlusion-Aware Gaussian Densification

**abstract/A1：** While recent feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting methods enable efficient driving-scene reconstruction from surround-view images, their reliance on a single observation fundamentally hinders novel-view synthesis. Regions occluded in the input can become visible from novel viewpoints, leaving the reconstructed Gaussians under-constrained and incomplete. Moving beyond image-space inpainting for rendered views, we introduce OccSplat, a feed-forward framework whose core contribution is Occlusion-Aware Gaussian Densification, explicitly targeting missing scene geometry rather than missing pixels. By predicting new Gaussian primitives for unsupported regions, OccSplat completes the scene directly in the 3D representation. Specifically, OccSplat first identifies occluded regions that lack Gaussian support through Gaussian Occlusion Mapping. Gaussian Occlusion Completion then generates new primitives for these regions. The predicted primitives are integrated into the original representation to improve novel-view synthesis while retaining feed-forward inference without per-scene optimization. Experiments on the nuScenes benchmark demonstrate that OccSplat improves on the compared methods and yields particularly large gains in occluded regions, leading to more accurate and visually coherent driving-scene reconstruction.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the Workshop on Computer Vision for Developing Countries (CV4DC), ACCV 2026 (Proceedings Track)

</details>

### 285. [Learning to Tune with Biased Gradients: Iterative Feedback Tuning of Model-Free Control on Throttle Valves](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06338v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06338

**作者：** Félix Martinez-Jiménez、Sander Miller-Murphy、Tobie Ondoa-Essomba、Emmanuel Witrant

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06338v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06338v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究汽车电子节气门的迭代反馈整定（IFT），分析摩擦与爬行导致两次闭环实验初始状态不匹配、进而使梯度估计有偏的问题，并在数字孪生与两个硬件阀门上比较 Adam 与 Gauss–Newton 等更新规则的表现。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是模型无关控制器的参数整定，与视觉/多模态推理中的内部表示、证据获取和 latent 状态生成无实质联系；文中「梯度噪声结构决定更新规则优劣」仅属泛化优化经验，难以形成针对 LVR 训练监督或贡献分配的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Tune with Biased Gradients: Iterative Feedback Tuning of Model-Free Control on Throttle Valves

**abstract/A1：** Iterative feedback tuning (IFT) learns the parameters of a fixed-structure controller by stochastic gradient descent. At each iteration, two closed-loop experiments are run on the plant: the first one measures the tracking error, and the second one, driven by this error, provides the sensitivities from which the gradient of the cost is estimated. This estimate is unbiased for linear plants only. We study the biased case on automotive electronic throttle valves, whose friction and stiction vary between valves and runs, controlled by an intelligent proportional (model-free) controller, which is an incremental PI controller. We show that the mismatch of the controller state at the start of the two experiments, which stiction makes unavoidable, adds an error inversely proportional to the amplitude of the second experiment. On digital twins of ten valves, the IFT gradient is a descent direction in only 69\\% of the low-cost region, friction being the cause, and all update rules plateau and then drift. On two hardware valves, the best update rule depends on the noise structure of the gradient. On the first valve, where one gradient component is buried in noise, Adam outperforms Gauss--Newton, its normalisation and its momentum each improving one parameter. On the second valve, where both components are consistent, the same normalisation amplifies a direction that Gauss--Newton rightly discounts, and Adam drifts to a controller worse than the initial one. The twins reproduce the mechanism but not this ranking.

</details>

### 286. [FlashRelight: Portrait Video Relighting with Dynamic Lighting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06355v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06355

**作者：** Jiahui Sheng、Le Li、Limin Lin、Jiahao Li、Wansen Feng、Hong Gu、Shuhan Chen、Xiaorun Li、Rui Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06355v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06355v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除
