摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作是肖像视频动态重光照的两阶段生成框架，属图像/视频生成应用，未涉及视觉推理的内部状态、证据筛选或推理决策，缺少可迁移到 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FlashRelight: Portrait Video Relighting with Dynamic Lighting

**abstract/A1：** Portrait video relighting aims to modify the illumination of a portrait video while preserving photorealism and temporal stability. The task becomes particularly challenging under dynamic lighting, where illumination direction, intensity, and color vary over time. To tackle this problem, we introduce FlashRelight, a novel two-stage framework for dynamic portrait video relighting. FlashRelight first suppresses the source illumination through delighting, and then synthesizes the target dynamic lighting on an illumination-neutral portrait video. FlashRelight supports three complementary control modalities: envmap sequences for explicit frame-level lighting control, dynamic lighting text for semantic illumination editing, and lighting reference videos for visual lighting transfer. Training follows a low-cost hybrid strategy that combines static one-light-at-a-time (OLAT) data with generated dynamic-lighting portrait videos, covering both controlled illumination variation and realistic portrait motion. Extensive experiments show that FlashRelight achieves temporally coherent and photorealistic dynamic relighting while enabling flexible cross-video lighting transfer. Code is available at https://github.com/vivoCameraResearch/FlashRelight

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 23 pages, including appendix

</details>

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文发布 eegdash-corpus-v0：整合 77 个公开 EEG 数据集（约 1.4 万小时），统一转换为 REVE 预训练格式，并用重训 REVE-Base 验证该语料可支撑预训练。

**排除原因：** 属 EEG 基础模型的数据语料与许可/复现工程工作，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的 latent 状态、证据筛选、停止决策或训练监督等问题，也无可具体核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EEGDash Corpus: A Training-Ready Public Corpus for EEG Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Foundation models for electroencephalography (EEG) are pretrained on tens of thousands of hours of recordings, and their accuracy grows with that amount. Such data is hard to assemble. The largest sources are clinical archives that cannot be redistributed, and the public sources are spread over several archives, file formats, and licences. We release eegdash-corpus-v0, a training-ready corpus of 77 public EEG datasets, as the gated Hugging Face dataset EEGDash/eegdash-corpus-v0 with the SHA-256 of every file. REVE, the largest published EEG foundation model, names 89 datasets as its pretraining corpus. Twelve cannot be redistributed: four clinical archives that hold 82% of its hours, and eight under no-derivatives licences. The corpus holds the 76 others that REVE's sampling rate allows and one more from REVE's release card: 77 datasets, 14,075 hours from 7,272 subjects, 5.0 million 10-second samples and 6.21 billion tokens per epoch. Every dataset is fetched through EEGDash, converted once with REVE's recipe, and stored in its own buckets of a chunked Zarr store with its licence recorded beside it, so a model can train on the whole store or on any subset of datasets. To test that the store pretrains a model, we retrain REVE-Base from scratch twice with the authors' code, changed only in how it reads data, and score it on seven public OpenEEGBench tasks. It reaches 0.331 under linear probing and 0.273 after fine-tuning, against 0.333 and 0.325 for the released model and 0.200 and 0.205 for an untrained encoder. The converter, the conversion reports, and the loader changes are released with the corpus.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 6 figures, 7 tables. Accepted at the Workshop on Machine Learning for EEG Signal Processing (MLESP 2026), IEEE BIBM 2026

</details>

### 288. [Corralling AI Risks with Grassroots Contracts](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06374v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06374

**作者：** Ehud Shapiro

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.DC、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06374v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06374v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文讨论如何用「草根契约」及其实现在智能手机上的应用，应对平台被 AI 控制、匿名不可问责的 AI 代理和无来源标注的深度伪造三类风险。

**排除原因：** 主题属 AI 治理、平台问责与内容溯源的社会技术方案，未涉及多模态推理的内部状态、视觉证据使用或推理计算决策，缺少可借鉴的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Corralling AI Risks with Grassroots Contracts

**abstract/A1：** AI's inflection point is a fountain of alarming risks. Digital platforms in particular are vulnerable to three major AI risks, as those in control of global platforms cede control to AI, of the algorithms, of membership and of content: (1) AI control of global platforms, social and economic, used by the majority of humanity, resulting in the majority of humanity becoming vulnerable to AI control. (2) Unaccountable AI agents operating anonymously on social and economic digital platforms, side-by-side with humans, possibly masquerading as humans, serving unknown masters with unknown goals, operating under unknown restraints without accountability or culpability. (3) Unattributed deepfakes generated by anonymous AI, with people unable to distinguish them from genuine content and with their producers evading accountability and culpability. Here, we propose to corral these AI risks with grassroots contracts, a digital incarnation of a two-centuries old notion of Pierre-Joseph Proudhon. We present grassroots contracts for a social graph with accountable AI agents and for a social network with digital provenance, and working smartphone apps that implement the two and show how they corral these major AI risks: (1) AI control of global platforms. (2) Unaccountable AI agents. (3) Unattributed deepfakes.

</details>

### 289. [From hydrological laws to catchment models: A process-based model matches deep-learning accuracy on three continents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06382v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06382

**作者：** Xilin Xia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、math.DS、physics.geo-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06382v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06382v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究物理定律约束下的低维水文流域模型与 LSTM 的精度对比，不涉及多模态推理、latent 推理状态或视觉证据使用；其「状态」指水量储量而非推理表示，未提供可供核查的 LVR 方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From hydrological laws to catchment models: A process-based model matches deep-learning accuracy on three continents

**abstract/A1：** Dooge asked whether hydrology has laws at the catchment scale. We ask whether a low-dimensional system built under physical laws can match the accuracy of neural networks that outperform process-based models on the same inputs. Three laws constrain a lumped catchment model. Conservation makes it a flux-connected storage network, dissipation fixes the sign of every flux and aggregation determines how the unresolved composition enters the fluxes, through effective laws over threshold distributions and a memory of the forcing. These laws admit four kinds of refinement. A parsimonious model under them, CaRT (11 parameters per catchment), is refined in a loop assisted by AI agents. Each refinement addresses an observed deficit through a hypothesis from the literature. The result, CaRT+, has fourteen states, 35 parameters per catchment and no daily neural network. Most refinements follow from aggregation over thresholds or from variables that carry no water but modulate fluxes. On 602 British and 593 US gauged catchments, single CaRT+ models reach median Nash-Sutcliffe efficiencies of 0.863 and 0.765 in held-out years. A stateful LSTM reaches 0.869 and 0.751 and an entity-aware LSTM 0.862 and 0.754, while three-seed LSTM ensembles remain ahead of the CaRT+ ensemble by 0.003-0.012 in median paired NSE. On 505 drier Australian gauged catchments unused in development, single CaRT+ models reach 0.778 against 0.762 and 0.753 for the two LSTMs. The results suggest that catchment-scale laws exist as laws of form, with the constitutive families shared and only their parameters learned.

**comment/C1：** 61 pages, 12 figures, 13 tables, plus 19 pages of Supporting Information

</details>

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该技术报告针对人脸识别流水线在训练数据可能被污染场景下的后门攻击，提出一种训练期投毒身份检测方法与两种推理期可疑输入过滤方法，并评估其计算开销。

**排除原因：** 主题属于人脸识别系统的安全加固与数据投毒防御，不涉及视觉细节表示、latent 推理状态生成或答案对视觉证据的使用，也未提供可迁移至 LVR 的状态更新或贡献分配线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Bag of Tricks for Defending Deep Learning-based Face Recognition Pipelines against Backdoor Attacks

**abstract/A1：** The protection of mature, multi-stage Face Recognition Systems is crucial to system providers. This technical report targets the threat model most relevant to industry practitioners: although model training is performed in-house, training data is sourced from possibly-compromised third parties. Given this setting, we present three practical defense "tricks" against data poisoning-based backdoor attacks. The first operates at training-time, detecting poisoned identities during training or prior to a pipeline's deployment. The last two operate at inference-time, filtering suspicious inputs before an answer can be sent back to a potential attacker. Each method is presented with their motivation, algorithms and computational impact, and evaluated on realistic face recognition workloads. Our goal is to provide actionable hardening techniques to system providers.

</details>

### 291. [Graph Foundation Models Can Become Effective Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detectors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06441v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06441

**作者：** Zhuangyan Hu、Zhuoxuan Liang、Wei Li、Xiaohua Jia、Moustafa Youssef、Sumi Helal

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06441v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06441v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Gfm4Mtsad 适配框架，通过结构—信息对齐（SICA）、伪异常筛选（MAPS）与尾部证据校准（TGEC）三个模块，把预训练图基础模型用于多变量时间序列异常检测。

**排除原因：** 论文研究传感器拓扑建模与窗口级异常评分校准，属时间序列异常检测任务，未涉及 VLM 内部表示、推理状态生成或视觉证据使用，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的状态、证据或决策联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Graph Foundation Models Can Become Effective Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detectors

**abstract/A1：** Multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTSAD) relies on modeling both temporal dynamics and inter-sensor dependencies to identify complex system faults. Although graph-based detectors explicitly model sensor topologies, they train task-specific networks from scratch on limited target-domain data, remaining vulnerable to spurious correlations and unable to reuse transferable priors. While pretrained Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) offer transferable topological priors and anomaly discriminability, directly adapting them to MTSAD is hindered by cross-modal feature-topology mismatch, unreliable pseudo-anomaly supervision, and decision distortion during node-to-window aggregation. To fill these gaps, we propose a novel adaptation framework, Gfm4Mtsad, which unleashes pretrained GFMs for MTSAD. We introduce the Structure-and-Information Contract Alignment (SICA) module to bridge continuous time series and the pretrained GFM space. This module harmonizes principal subspace token projection, normal-prior-guided graph construction, and residual recovery to suppress spurious edges while retaining fine-grained temporal details. We propose a Mechanism-Aligned Pseudo-Anomaly Screening strategy (MAPS) to steer unsupervised learning. It applies localized dependency breaks while preserving empirical residuals, selecting hard negatives near the normal boundary for targeted supervision. We develop a Tail-Gated Evidence Calibration mechanism (TGEC) to eliminate node-to-window aggregation distortion. It calibrates multi-granular graph responses against normal operational references, strictly preserving benign score rankings while selectively refining extreme anomaly candidates in the tail distribution for reliable window detection and interpretable sensor evidence. Extensive evaluations with two GFM backbones demonstrate that Gfm4Mtsad achieves superior detection performance over existing baselines.

</details>

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在固定 224×224 分辨率下比较 EfficientNet-B0–B7 在 SD-OCT 四分类视网膜疾病任务上的精度与效率权衡，发现精度随模型规模非单调变化。

**排除原因：** 属于医学图像分类的模型缩放刷分研究，不涉及视觉推理状态生成、证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，也无可迁移的 latent 或训练监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Within-Family EfficientNet Scaling under Fixed-Resolution Transfer Learning for Spectral-Domain Optical Coherence Tomography Retinal Disease Classification

**abstract/A1：** Background/Objectives: CNNs perform well in retinal disease classification from spectral-domain optical coherence tomography (SD-OCT), but whether EfficientNet's natural-image scaling hierarchy transfers to medical-image transfer learning at fixed resolution remains unclear. This study evaluates EfficientNet-B0-B7 for four-class SD-OCT classification and accuracy-efficiency trade-offs. Methods: Eight ImageNet-pretrained variants (4.05-64.10M instantiated backbone parameters) were evaluated at 224x224 on the Kermany dataset. Grayscale images were replicated to RGB and processed using model-embedded preprocessing and augmentation. Training comprised frozen-backbone head training followed by partial fine-tuning. Metrics covered classification, discrimination, efficiency, and visual analyses. Results: Test accuracy varied non-monotonically from 95.93% (B0) to 96.95% (B1); B7 achieved 96.73%. B1 had the highest accuracy and Drusen F1 (0.8780), while B7 had Drusen F1 of 0.8720 and the highest Drusen recall (0.9177). Drusen had the lowest F1 across all variants and contributed 82.2% of the summed squared deviation in class-specific F1; class ranking remained Normal &gt; CNV &gt; DME &gt; Drusen. The OCT accuracy ranking differed from the reported ImageNet hierarchy, where B7 ranked first. The accuracy-to-parameter ratio fell from 19.82 to 1.48 percentage points per million total parameters. The final train-test gap had a weak, non-significant association with test accuracy (r = 0.26, p = 0.53). Conclusions: Larger EfficientNet variants did not consistently improve SD-OCT classification. B1 achieved the highest accuracy and Drusen F1, whereas B7 showed a secondary accuracy recovery at greater model size and CPU latency, indicating task and protocol dependent scaling effects. As cross-partition duplicates have not been independently audited, results are conditional on the supplied split.

**comment/C1：** 46 pages, 20 figures, 18 tables

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06514

**作者：** Yousef Nejatbakhsh、Malihe Aliasgari、Parsa Safaee、Sina Shokoohyar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.AP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06514v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06514v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 Spider Network Filtering Framework，把五个维度的交互规则编码为图特征并接入逻辑回归，用于判断是否收购 FDIC 接管的困境银行，并在 55 例交易上做交叉验证评估。

**排除原因：** 属金融并购决策与表格数据分类研究，不涉及视觉或多模态表示、latent 推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，其交互规则与可解释特征构造也未提供可核查的 LVR 方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** An Interaction-Aware Decision Framework for FDIC-Assisted Distressed Bank Acquisitions: Empirical Evidence from the FDIC Failed-Bank Panel and the SVB-First Citizens Case

**abstract/A1：** Acquirers of failed banks weigh acquisition value, regulatory protection, risk, buyer strength, and execution difficulty jointly, because each signal's meaning depends on the others: high non-performing loans matter less when government loss sharing absorbs the losses. The proposed Spider Network Filtering Framework encodes such dependencies as four interpretable interaction rules among five dimensions, yielding graph-inspired features for a logistic model and a Pursue/Caution/Avoid decision rule. We evaluate it on 55 acquisitions assisted by the Federal Deposit Insurance Corporation in 2008-2024 by acquirers with a parent listed in the United States. Six financial inputs come from regulatory reports published before each failure; two deal terms, which the winning bidder knows when it bids but investors learn at the announcement, come from the regulator's press releases. The outcome, a positive three-day abnormal return around the announcement, records the market's initial reception, not realized success. Under grouped, nested cross-validation, the Spider-enhanced model reaches a mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.594, against 0.615 for logistic regression on the same inputs and 0.632 for ridge regression; permutation tests that repeat the full model selection give at most weak evidence that any of them ranks better than chance. The Spider score used directly does not rank better than chance, and an apparent link between target distress and higher returns disappears against a bank-sector benchmark. Thresholds tuned for the F1 score approve most acquisitions with negative returns and realize negative utility when each costs three times what a positively received acquisition gains.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 2 figures, 15 tables. Research code, datasets, and reproducibility materials are available at https://github.com/malihealiasgari20/FDIC-SNFF-Decision-Framework

</details>

<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spatially and Compositionally Controlled Multimodal Glioblastoma MRI Synthesis with Latent Diffusion

**abstract/A1：** Glioblastoma exhibits substantial heterogeneity in tumour morphology and composition across MRI modalities, often making available clinical datasets highly unbalanced in terms of patient pathologies. We propose a two-stage latent diffusion model (LDM) framework for the generation of explicitly controllable 2D glioblastoma MRI scans. First, a segmentation LDM generates tumour maps conditioned on location, spatial extent, axial position, and subregion composition. Second, a modality-conditioned LDM with ControlNet generates T1, T1Gd, T2, and FLAIR images adhering to the requested segmentation. On a held-out test set, generated segmentations achieved a mean centroid error of 0.842 pixels and a subregion proportion error of 0.795 percentage points. ControlNet improved Fréchet inception distance (FID) across all four modalities, reducing pooled FID from 42.59 to 36.12. An expert reader study yielded mean quality ratings of 6.60 for real images, 6.83 for ControlNet-generated images, and 6.77 for fully synthetic images.

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RAGAM-AI: A Hybrid Reinforcement Learning based Agentic approach powered by Generative AI for Edge Resource Management

**abstract/A1：** Resource management for distributed edge networks hosting different services has been handled efficiently using Reinforcement learning (RL). Reward generalization in complex workflows is still an open issue within the RL realm. Therefore, this work proposes RAGAM-AI, a hybrid framework that combines the capabilities of RL algorithms and Generative-AI (Gen-AI) for powering an Agentic AI workflow that optimizes resource allocation at the Multi-access Edge Computing (MEC) units. This framework further resolves the reward generalization issue by utilizing Large Language Model (LLM) for feedback. A Multi- Agent Soft-Actor Critic (MASAC) using MathΣtral as a reward oracle is implemented for managing resource allocations at the MEC. RAGAM-AI interprets system state information and evaluates multi-objective trade-offs guiding the resource orchestration loop over distributed edge server agents. The complexity of the framework is further brought down by incorporating surrogate reward model. The overall performance is evaluated against the single agent SAC, REINFORCE and random policies for benchmarking. On using RAGAM-AI, lower allocation deviations for CPU, memory and bandwidth resources are reported while minimizing the latency and data rate costs. A scalable AI as a feedback (AIAF) solution optimizing network resources with significant performance improvement is thus presented.

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Thermodynamics-Informed Learning Captures Hierarchical Hydrogen-Bond Networks in Deep Eutectic Solvents

**abstract/A1：** Deep eutectic solvents (DESs) offer a tunable platform for lignocellulose refining, but their solvation behavior depends on multicomponent hydrogen-bond networks rather than individual constituent properties. We develop TiNN, a thermodynamics-informed neural network that hierarchically represents HBA-HBD association and solute accommodation through a learned free-energy pathway. Trained on 938 records, TiNN achieves a test-set $R^2$ of 0.917 and generalizes to new HBA-HBD combinations and sparse conditions. Ablation identifies solute-specific stabilization as the largest contributor to prediction accuracy, while the importance of hydrogen-bond compatibility depends on the dissolution mechanism. Experimental measurements of glucose, xylan, and Kraft lignin in acidic, basic, and neutral DESs show that TiNN reduces the geometric mean solubility error from more than 23-fold with COSMO-RS to approximately 1.5-fold. Without training on fractionation data, TiNN also distinguishes DES formulations with high and low lignin and hemicellulose removal from corn stover. These results support chemically grounded solvent screening for lignocellulose fractionation.

</details>

**作者：** Mario Mallea、Angela Nebot、Francisco Mugica

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06610v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06610v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究协同过滤推荐，在 EASE 浅层自编码器基础上引入用户-用户信息并提出用户-物品邻接分布混合模型，以提升排序精度。

**排除原因：** 内容属于推荐系统中的线性协同过滤建模，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，也未提供可核查的 latent reasoning 方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Alternatives to Shallow Autoencoders for Collaborative Filtering

**abstract/A1：** Collaborative filtering (CF) is a cornerstone of recommender systems and plays a relevant role in many modern applications. CF uses user-item interaction data to discover future preferences. Although deep learning models have shown promise in CF, Embarrassingly Shallow Autoencoders for Sparse Data (EASE) has gained attention for its outstanding ranking accuracy provided by its closed-form solution. EASE relies primarily on relationships among items to fit a full-rank high-dimensional linear mapping. We hypothesize that this design limits its capacity to capture similar but not equivalent fine-grained user relationships, consequently limiting its recommendation accuracy. This paper introduces an alternative formulation based on EASE that takes advantage of user-user information. Furthermore, we propose a hybrid model that combines both user-user and item-item adjacency distributions. Our experiments reveal that the proposed models outperform EASE on well-known recommendation benchmarks, highlighting the significance of including the user alternative in shallow autoencoder studies for CF.

</details>

### 298. [Dimension-Free Private Mean Estimation is Impossible Without the Covariance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06615v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06615

**作者：** Renaud Gaucher、Ephraim Linder、Adam Smith、Jonathan Ullman、Lydia Zakynthinou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06615v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06615v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究协方差未知时差分隐私高斯均值估计的误差下界，属理论统计与隐私学习，与 LVR/VLM 推理的状态表示、证据使用或计算分配均无具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dimension-Free Private Mean Estimation is Impossible Without the Covariance

**abstract/A1：** We study the limits of differentially private Gaussian mean estimation when the covariance matrix is unknown. Prior work shows that when the covariance $Σ$ is known, the optimal sample complexity can depend only on its spectrum, rather than on the ambient dimension $d$. We establish new lower bounds for Gaussian mean estimation with Euclidean error which show that private estimators cannot achieve similar guarantees when the covariance is unknown. First, when the sample size $n$ is smaller than the effective dimension $\\mathrm{trace}(Σ)/\\\|Σ\\\|\_{\\text{op}}$, the optimal error when the covariance is unknown can be larger than the optimal error when it is known by a factor of $\\sqrt{d/n}$. Second, when the sample size is larger than the effective dimension, even for diagonal covariance matrices with effective dimension bounded by $\\log d$, the optimal error when the covariance is unknown can be larger than the optimal error when it is known by a factor of $\\sqrt{d}/n^{3/2}$. Together, these results show that in the unknown covariance setting, exploiting anisotropic structure under differential privacy can require a dimension-dependent error or sample complexity.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06624v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06624v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**内容简述：** 该论文面向3D延时显微成像中的细胞谱系追踪，提出在稀疏标注下整合检测、候选连接校准与谱系图事件联合选择的流程。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是生物影像中的追踪图构建，不涉及VLM内部表示、latent推理状态或证据获取与停止决策；稀疏标注与联合选择仅为领域内训练/后处理技巧，未见可供LVR核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Track Cells from Sparse Annotations in 3D Time-Lapse Microscopy

**abstract/A1：** Reconstructing cell lineages from 3D time-lapse microscopy requires distinguishing cell motion from division and inferring temporally consistent connections. When only a subset of cells is annotated, however, training procedures that treat unannotated regions as background or incorrect correspondences can be problematic. We propose a cell tracking pipeline that integrates detection from sparse annotations, calibration of candidate connections using image features, and joint selection of lineage graph events. For detection, we combine the outputs of 2.5D and 3D models trained to suppress erroneous background supervision at unannotated cells. We then calibrate ordinary link and division probabilities using motion features from dense flow, correspondence features from a learned cell-matching model, and features from models that identify cells immediately before division, dividing parents, and daughters immediately after division. Ordinary links, divisions represented as daughter pairs, and node inclusion are selected jointly under a surrogate objective derived from the evaluation metric, followed by gap filling and temporal coordinate refinement. The pipeline achieves an overall score of 0.9762 in 5-fold cross-validation on 199 videos from 2 zebrafish embryos. With candidates and calibrated values held fixed, joint selection outperforms a sequential method that allows replacement of existing divisions and reoptimization of ordinary links by 0.0105. The improvement arises primarily from division recovery.

</details>

### 300. [Fingerprinting the Dispatchers behind Distributed Model Extraction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06661v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06661

**作者：** Snigdha Chandan Khilar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06661v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06661v1>)

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文利用请求到达与流完成时间戳，对分布式模型窃取（API 蒸馏）活动中的负载均衡账号进行取证排序，并分析抖动、key pinning 等规避手段的代价。

**排除原因：** 研究属于模型窃取检测与流量取证，关注账号归因和吞吐，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部表示、latent 状态生成与使用，也无可迁移的状态更新或证据筛选线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fingerprinting the Dispatchers behind Distributed Model Extraction

**abstract/A1：** API distillation campaigns try to hide model-stealing traffic by splitting requests across thousands of load-balanced accounts, which standard content classifiers often miss. However, request timestamps, specifically arrival time and stream completion time, reveal the underlying dispatching strategy, such as round-robin counters or key-shuffling pools. Using a 20 percent seed set, this timing analysis can forensically rank the remaining accounts of a campaign with 0.92 to 1.00 AUC within 20 minutes, even across thousands of real production accounts. While adaptive attackers struggle to hide rotating dispatchers using jitter, pinning keys successfully evades detection, though it can severely throttle throughput when quotas bind. Ultimately, timing exposes shared infrastructure patterns even when content filters fail.

</details>

<a id="paper-301"></a>

### 301. [CardioDrift: Automatic Cardiac Motion Tracking on a 3D Atlas Mesh via Shape-aware Coherent Point Drift with Slice Shift Correction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06664v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06664

**作者：** Reza Akbari Movahed、Abuzar Rezaee、Shatha Alamri、Edmond S. L. Ho、Fani Deligianni、Ali Gooya

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06664v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06664v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** CardioDrift 提出一种基于图集网格的全自动心脏运动跟踪框架，将 Coherent Point Drift 概率配准扩展到多心区表面联合对齐，并内嵌切片偏移校正，在 UK Biobank 与 M&amp;M 数据上取得更高的分割重叠与距离指标。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于cine CMR 医学图像配准与图集拟合，关注解剖对应点与切片伪影校正，与 LVR 的内部表示、latent 状态生成、证据获取与停止决策缺乏实质联系，也未提供可核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CardioDrift: Automatic Cardiac Motion Tracking on a 3D Atlas Mesh via Shape-aware Coherent Point Drift with Slice Shift Correction

**abstract/A1：** Automatic cardiac motion tracking from cine cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging is essential for quantifying myocardial motion abnormalities associated with cardiovascular disease. Existing methods fall into two groups: those that represent motion without anatomical correspondence across subjects and therefore limit inter-subject motion assessment; and those that represent motion with globally corresponding points, which typically require a time-consuming and error-prone semi-automatic step to fit a cardiac atlas to the subject space. To address these limitations, this study proposes CardioDrift, a fully automatic, atlas-based probabilistic framework that represents subject-specific cardiac motion on a 3D atlas mesh with globally corresponding points. The Coherent Point Drift (CPD) formulation is generalised to jointly register the atlas surfaces of multiple cardiac regions to the anatomically corresponding contours extracted from cine CMR segmentation maps throughout the cardiac cycle. Slice-shift correction is embedded in the same probabilistic model, estimating slice-wise translations that suppress the misalignment artefact limiting atlas-fitting accuracy. Evaluation on 1301 randomly selected UK Biobank subjects and the M&amp;M dataset, CardioDrift attains Dice similarity coefficients (DSCs) of $92.23\\pm8.70\\%$, $82.11\\pm7.56\\%$, and $88.69\\pm4.13\\%$ for the left ventricle (LV), myocardium (Myo), and right ventricle (RV), exceeding the strongest baseline on every distance and overlap metric of both datasets.

</details>

### 302. [LifeVal: Evaluating AI Model Performance on Personalized Agentic Consumer Tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06687v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06687

**作者：** Jenny Yang、Elizabeth Proehl、Anjan Bharadwaj、Emily Fan、Charlotte Cole、James Kim、Noam Brown、Phoebe Thacker、Amelia Glaese、Tejal Patwardhan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06687v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06687v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LifeVal 基准，用调查与真实对话记忆构建个性化消费级智能体任务及评分细则，评估模型在个人化生活事务上的完成质量。

**排除原因：** 属于一般智能体应用评测，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部表示、latent 状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，也无可借鉴的状态或监督机制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LifeVal: Evaluating AI Model Performance on Personalized Agentic Consumer Tasks

**abstract/A1：** We introduce LifeVal, a benchmark evaluating AI model performance on personalized agentic consumer tasks. LifeVal covers the most valuable life tasks that a personalized digital superassistant could automate, representing over 1.7 trillion US dollars in annual willingness-to-pay across 148 consumer task categories (e.g., managing finances, moving apartments, searching for a job, finding childcare). To construct our activity distribution, we conducted a new survey of willingness-to-pay for a digital superassistant across a demographically representative sample of over 3,800 consumers, cross-referencing our results against the American Time Use Survey (Bureau of Labor Statistics, 2025) and the Multinational Time Use Study (Fisher et al., 2025). For the most valuable consumer activities, we recruited task creators to create AI evaluation tasks personalized using real ChatGPT conversations and memories, reference files, and personal context. Task creators had to pass our expertise threshold (including personally completing the task multiple times and seeing the results through in their real lives for over a year) and also write a rubric, ensuring that our evaluation measured the best personalized completion for that task. All tasks had to pass at least three rounds of human quality-control before acceptance into our final evaluation sets.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 6 figures

</details>

### 303. [Agent-Driven Generative Priors for Pathology Image Synthesis and Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06701v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06701

**作者：** Sicheng Yang、Rui Tang、Haipeng Zhou、Hongqiu Wang、Zhaohu Xing、Jianda Ma、Lie Dai、Guang Yang、Lei Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06701v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06701v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作提出三阶段病理图像框架：用 agent 系统筛选图像质量并构建 TCGA-200K，训练生成模型，再将其骨干迁移到细胞语义分割。

**排除原因：** 属于病理图像合成与分割的任务应用，agent 仅用于数据质量筛选，未涉及 VLM 推理中的 latent 状态、证据使用或停止决策，缺乏可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Agent-Driven Generative Priors for Pathology Image Synthesis and Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Deep learning methods for pathology are constrained by the limited availability and high cost of large-scale pixel-level annotations. Synthetic data can alleviate this limitation, but existing pipelines often lack reliable quality control and remain weakly connected to downstream analysis. To address these issues, we propose a three-stage framework that integrates automated data screening, generative representation learning, and efficient adaptation to downstream tasks. First, we develop a pathology agent system to assess image quality and filter samples, resulting in TCGA-200K, a dataset containing 217,030 pathology patches selected from the public TCGA repository. Second, we train a generative model on the screened data. In addition to image synthesis, its backbone learns structural and semantic representations that can be transferred to downstream tasks. Third, we adapt the pretrained generative backbone to semantic segmentation using a lightweight task-specific head. We further introduce PathSeg-380K, a semantic segmentation dataset with expert annotations for approximately 380,000 cells across 12 cell types. Experiments on multiple pathology datasets show that the proposed framework achieves state-of-the-art segmentation performance. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/script-Yang/Agent-GenPath.

**comment/C1：** Code available at: https://github.com/script-Yang/Agent-GenPath

</details>

### 304. [Symmetry Breaking at the Edge of Stability via Central Flow Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06718v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06718

**作者：** Naima Elosegui Borras、Georgios Arvanitidis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06718v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06718v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文分析有限步梯度下降在 edge of stability 下参数空间守恒量（如层间平衡性）的漂移，属纯优化动力学理论，与视觉细节保留、latent 状态生成/使用、证据获取或停止决策均无可核查的具体联系，其离散更新偏差结论也难为 LVR 提供方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Symmetry Breaking at the Edge of Stability via Central Flow Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Continuous symmetries in neural networks lead to conserved quantities that are invariant under gradient flow, such as layer balancedness in linear and ReLU networks. Finite-step gradient descent need not preserve these laws, particularly at the edge of stability (EOS), where the iterates oscillate around a smoother trajectory, which can be described by the central flow dynamics (Cohen et al., 2025). We study how gradient flow conservation laws evolve under the central flow description of this regime. We show that the curvature correction induces a finite-dimensional drift determined by the oscillation covariance along critical parameter directions. We thus take the first steps to analyse which conservation laws remain and are broken at the EOS according to the central flow dynamics by analysing a scalar two-layer model, two-layer matrix factorisation, and a two-layer ReLU network, where the known conservation laws are shown to be complete (Marcotte et al., 2023).

</details>

### 305. [FedFiOT: Fisher-Weighted Optimal Transport for Model Merging in One-Shot and Few-Round Federated Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06783v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06783

**作者：** Quan Minh Nguyen、Hoang M. Ngo、Bhakti Chowkwale、Yasaman Keshtkarjahromi、My T. Thai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06783v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06783v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** FedFiOT 面向单轮或极少轮联邦学习，用 Fisher 信息衡量参数重要性，并以加权最优传输联合对齐神经元与聚合全局模型。

**排除原因：** 其神经元对齐与重要性加权仅发生在跨客户端参数聚合层面，不涉及视觉证据筛选、推理期 latent 状态或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FedFiOT: Fisher-Weighted Optimal Transport for Model Merging in One-Shot and Few-Round Federated Learning

**abstract/A1：** One-shot and few-round federated learning (FL) reduce communication burden by constructing a global model within one or a few communication rounds, but often achieve lower performance than conventional multi-round FL. Without frequent synchronization, client models may drift apart, causing neurons that learn similar features to occupy different positions across clients. At the same time, local data heterogeneity can cause corresponding parameters to differ in their importance to each client's predictions. Together, these challenges cause direct averaging to combine mismatched neurons and assign equal weight to parameters despite differences in their importance. We propose FedFiOT, a Fisher-weighted optimal transport framework for one-shot and few-round FL that leverages Fisher information to quantify parameter importance and guide neuron alignment and model aggregation. FedFiOT jointly optimizes neuron correspondences and global model parameters through alternating minimization of a shared Fisher-weighted objective. Theoretically, we show that FedFiOT's optimization procedure monotonically decreases the layer-wise merging objective and establish a stationarity bound that quantifies the effects of client heterogeneity and nonuniform Fisher weighting.Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over competing baselines in both one-shot and few-round federated learning settings.

</details>

### 306. [VA-TDM: A Unified View for Improving Few-Step Diffusion Models by Velocity Adversarial Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06796v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06796

**作者：** Yihong Luo、Tianyu Pang、Tianyang Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06796v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06796v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向少步扩散/流模型生成，提出速度对抗蒸馏 VAD 与轨迹分布匹配方法 VA-TDM，用学习到的 critic 比较学生与教师速度场并监督学生轨迹上的部分去噪。

**排除原因：** 这是图像与视频生成的采样加速蒸馏，不涉及多模态推理的内部表示、视觉证据筛选或停止决策，其「轨迹匹配」指扩散采样轨迹而非推理状态轨迹，缺乏具体可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VA-TDM: A Unified View for Improving Few-Step Diffusion Models by Velocity Adversarial Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Few-step distillation offers efficient generation with diffusion and flow models, but preserving quality with only a few sampling steps remains challenging. Distribution-matching methods via score distillation provide a strong foundation, yet they rely on accurately tracking the student's changing distribution. At intermediate generation steps in few-step sampling, student-score estimation can be difficult, and commonly used estimators may describe a different distribution from the one used to update the student. Flow matching allows a more flexible approach to supervision through velocity fields constructed from the student's sampling process. Building on this perspective, we introduce Velocity Adversarial Distillation (VAD), which uses a learned critic to compare student and teacher fields and turn their disagreement into feedback for learning. VAD unifies score distillation, Fisher-based matching, and moment matching, also explaining existing heuristic MMD updates and delivering new training objectives. We further develop VA-TDM, which follows trajectory distribution matching: providing supervision for partial denoising along the student trajectory. Beyond this, VA-TDM combines transition-aware online velocity learning and dual-form VAD supervision across intermediate student transitions. Extensive results show that VA-TDM is powerful for learning few-step generators, achieving state-of-the-art four-step performance in both text-to-image and text-to-video generation across various backbones and scaling effectively to 33B MiniMax-H3. In particular, our VA-TDM-H3 notably outperforms the few-step Fast-H3 and is closer to the original many-step H3 according to human preference.

**comment/C1：** Project Page: https://luo-yihong.github.io/VA-TDM/ Code: https://github.com/Luo-Yihong/VA-TDM

</details>

### 307. [Open-HumanScale: An Open Recipe for Scaling Human and Robot Experience for Embodied Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06817v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06817

**作者：** Juncheng Ma、Jiaxin Li、Jiankai Tu、Bo Liang、Guodong Lv、Yufan Deng、Junzhe Tang、Liangzhu Yang、Chen Bian、Zicong Cao、Baohao Zhu、Jianxin Bi、Tengfei Yan、Yinyuan Zhang、Kewei Zhang、Xinglong Chen、Kaiqi Chen、Duomin Wang、Xiaotian Tang、Liang Tan、Li Li、Yuan Xu、Haosen Yang、Yuqi Wang、Jingfei Xia、Songyan Huang、Zhiyang Dou、Zhen Dong、Enze Xie、Tat-Seng Chua、Daquan Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06817v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06817v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作给出以 5 万小时自我中心人类视频预训练具身基础模型的开放流程，提出 HumanWAM 世界-动作模型并考察数据规模与下游机器人成功率之间的对数线性缩放规律。

**排除原因：** 属于机器人策略与世界-动作模型的数据缩放研究，未涉及 latent 推理状态的生成与更新、视觉证据筛选或停止决策等机制，未见可迁移的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Open-HumanScale: An Open Recipe for Scaling Human and Robot Experience for Embodied Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Egocentric human video offers a scalable source of manipulation priors for robot learning, yet translating raw data scale into generalizable robot policies demands rigorous data curation and training recipes. We introduce Open-HumanScale, the first fully open recipe for scaling egocentric pretraining to 50,000 hours, anchored by Open-HumanScale-50K---a curated egocentric video corpus densely annotated with hand motion and natural language. Leveraging this corpus, we systematically investigate pure human pretraining via closed-loop simulation evaluations and uncover an empirical log-linear scaling law linking pretraining data hours to downstream robot success rates. Grounded in these findings, we develop HumanWAM-Vanilla, a world--action model pretrained exclusively on 50k hours of egocentric human video. Upon robot post-training, HumanWAM-Vanilla reaches 92.08% success on RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Clean and 68.88% on Clean2Random, alongside a 14.17% mean success rate and a 19.00 mean progress score on RoboDojo. To push the capability frontier further, we explore joint human--robot pretraining with HumanWAM-Goal, a visual-goal-conditioned world--action model. Hybrid data scaling unlocks broad zero-shot generalization across both simulation and physical benchmarks, propelling post-trained RoboTwin 2.0 performance to new state-of-the-art levels: 92.70% on Clean2Clean and a substantial leap to 84.60% on Clean2Random. On physical robots, HumanWAM-Goal further exhibits emergent embodied in-context learning, dynamically adapting its manipulation behavior to unseen visual goals without parameter updates. To support reproducible scaling research in embodied AI, we open-source the Open-HumanScale-50K corpus, data curation and pretraining pipelines, alongside the pretrained and post-trained HumanWAM checkpoints, inference code, and simulation evaluation suites.

**comment/C1：** GitHub: https://github.com/DAGroup-PKU/HumanNet/tree/open-humanscale Project website: https://dagroup-pku.github.io/open-humanscale/

</details>

### 308. [ServerBench: How can your coding agents design specialized LLM serving systems?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06841v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06841

**作者：** Xinrui Zhong、Zhiming Wang、Yongqi Liu、Zhaohui Wang、Shanglin Yang、Zikun Li、Zhuoming Chen、Beidi Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06841v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06841v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ServerBench 提出一个基准，让 coding agent 在会话依赖、延迟与正确性约束下，自行实验并设计完整的 LLM 推理服务系统，以「每美元 GPU 成本所换取的延迟达标 token 价值」为统一目标，并用独立验证检查提交的系统。

**排除原因：** 该工作关注服务系统层面的工程实现、吞吐与成本优化，未涉及视觉细节在内部状态中的保留、latent 推理状态的生成与使用或证据筛选与停止决策，其成本目标与验证环节与 LVR 的距离过远，难以形成具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ServerBench: How can your coding agents design specialized LLM serving systems?

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) coding agents develop software through experimentation, offering an opportunity to design the serving systems supporting their own operation. Effective serving requires coordinating implementation and operating decisions under session dependencies, latency constraints, and correctness requirements. We introduce ServerBench, a benchmark for agentic design of complete, specialized LLM serving systems. Agents experiment against a common objective, dollars per dollar: latency-qualified token value per dollar of GPU cost. Stateful replay preserves session dependencies, and independent verification checks the submitted system. Across 11 coding-agent models and three deployments, Claude Sonnet 5.5 achieves 2.97 dollars per dollar on Gemma E4B, $4.87\\times$ the vLLM reference; 22 of 33 runs yield verified systems, and 13 exceed the strongest baseline. Our analysis identifies three findings: high-value designs coordinate capacity improvements with load selection; agents interpret performance feedback through workload-relevant experiments; and reliable delivery requires verification of the execution paths introduced by optimization. Overall, ServerBench supports systematic study of how agents translate service objectives and feedback into effective, verified serving systems for given task specifications.

</details>

### 309. [Trainspotting: Learning Object-Centric 3D Reconstruction from Found Photos](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06849v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06849

**作者：** Hanyu Chen、Noah Snavely

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06849v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06849v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作构建了来自 Wikimedia Commons 的「found photos」数据集（12,068 个物体、311,886 张跨年份与环境拍摄的照片），并用于评测与微调前馈式三维重建模型在相机位姿、深度与几何上的表现。

**排除原因：** 论文核心是三维重建数据与基准，评价指标为位姿、深度和几何精度，不涉及 latent 推理状态、证据使用或停止决策等主线问题，也未见可供核查的 latent 视觉状态或推理机制线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Trainspotting: Learning Object-Centric 3D Reconstruction from Found Photos

**abstract/A1：** State-of-the-art feedforward reconstruction models perform poorly on in-the-wild object-centric photo collections. They are trained largely on single-session object captures whose images vary in viewpoint while the object and its environment remain nearly fixed. Public photo archives contain a different form of multi-view data. They hold found photos of the same object or design: photos taken independently by many people across years, environments, cameras, and sometimes physical units. In this work, we introduce Trainspotting, a dataset of 12,068 objects and 311,886 photos from Wikimedia Commons. We reconstruct each object to recover camera poses, multi-view stereo depth, and object masks. We categorize the objects into three regimes: objects fixed at one site, objects that move between sites, and designs shared by many physical units. Evaluating recent feedforward reconstruction models on Trainspotting shows that they fail most severely in the last two regimes. Fine-tuning VGGT-$Ω$ on Trainspotting significantly improves camera pose, depth, and 3D geometry on held-out objects. We observe similar improvements on the independent NAVI in-the-wild benchmark, which contains photos of each object in different environments. Performance on conventional benchmarks remains largely unchanged.

**comment/C1：** project page: https://hanyuc.com/trainspotting

</details>

### 310. [CompressionBench: How can your coding agents compress LLMs?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06858v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06858

**作者：** Xinrui Zhong、Zhifeng Li、Zhiming Wang、Yongqi Liu、Shanglin Yang、Yang Zhou、Zikun Li、Zhuoming Chen、Beidi Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06858v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06858v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 CompressionBench，将 LLM 压缩建模为编码智能体自主完成的终端任务，考察其在存储与时间预算下压缩权重并给出推理代码的能力。

**排除原因：** 内容集中在模型压缩的智能体评测与 perplexity 搜索，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据使用或停止决策，也未给出可迁移到 LVR 的具体方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CompressionBench: How can your coding agents compress LLMs?

**abstract/A1：** Recursive self-improvement requires AI systems to improve the models and infrastructure used by subsequent systems. LLM compression offers a concrete efficiency objective: retain a pretrained model's behavior within a smaller model storage budget. We study whether coding agents can carry out this step autonomously. We formulate compression as a terminal task in which an agent receives a source checkpoint, storage limit, inference environment, and time budget, then constructs compressed weights and executable inference code. The agent controls its algorithm and experiments; perplexity feedback guides search, and independent verification checks the final model. Across six agents and six model--size settings, 29 of 36 submissions pass verification. Selecting the lowest-perplexity agent design per task yields an average 3.4% perplexity increase over BF16 and 77.33% downstream accuracy, compared with 79.56% for BF16. Recorded experiments reveal how agents achieve these outcomes and where improvement breaks down. Agents lower PPL in 21 of 24 revisions at nearly fixed storage; complete-model feedback reverses 17 of 79 prior local rankings; and generation and inference tests expose failures that likelihood alone misses. The results identify representation design, experimental judgment, and capability validation as central to agent-driven model compression, a measurable component of efficiency-oriented self-improvement.

</details>

<a id="paper-311"></a>

### 311. [Dull, Dirty, Dangerous? Working Conditions and the Limits of Occupational AI Exposure](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06888v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.06888

**作者：** Siyi Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.HC、econ.GN

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06888v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.06888v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文用 O\*NET 的 21 个条目考察 754 个美国职业的「枯燥、肮脏、危险」工作条件与职业 AI 暴露度指标之间的关系，并分析条目分组与职业特征如何影响暴露度估计的解释。

**排除原因：** 属劳动经济学层面的职业 AI 暴露度统计分析，不涉及模型内部表示、推理状态生成、视觉证据使用或训练监督，与 LVR/VLM 推理主线无实质联系，也无可借鉴的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dull, Dirty, Dangerous? Working Conditions and the Limits of Occupational AI Exposure

**abstract/A1：** Automation is often justified as relief from dull, dirty, and dangerous work. I examine how these conditions relate to occupational AI exposure using 21 O\*NET items across 754 US occupations. Higher exposure is associated with lower condition scores under a large language model (LLM) measure and AI Occupational Exposure (AIOE), with equal occupation weights and employment weights. Adjustment for physical demands, required education, training and experience, and wages reduces the composite association. The adjusted confidence intervals include zero for LLM exposure and remain below zero for AIOE. Separating repetition from restricted discretion also changes what the combined Dull score conveys. These comparisons show how item groupings and occupational characteristics shape the interpretation of exposure estimates. Occupational profiles offer a starting point for selecting contrasting research settings. Establishing desired automation or realized relief requires evidence about workers' preferences and specific interventions.

</details>
